<section class="category">
<h1>Vandanā</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-vande-ham-shri-guroh-shri-yuta-pada-kamalam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Vandanā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>vande ’haṁ śrī-guroḥ śrī-yuta-pada-kamalaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-gurūn vaiṣṇavāṁś cha</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-rūpaṁ sāgrajātaṁ saha-gaṇa-raghunāthānvitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;taṁ sa-jīvam</strong><br>
<strong>sādvaitaṁ sāvadhūtaṁ parijana-sahitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-chaitanya-devaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-rādhā-kṛṣṇa-pādān saha-gaṇa-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lalitā-śrī-viśākhānvitāṁś cha</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrī Chaitanya-charitāmṛta: Antya-līlā, 2.1)</i><br>
Śrīla Kṛṣṇadās Kavirāj prays, “First I offer my respects unto the lotus feet of my initiating spiritual masters and the recruiting spiritual masters and advanced Vaiṣṇavas. Next I offer my obeisances to my śāstra gurus, Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī, his elder brother, Śrīla Sanātan Goswāmī, Śrīla Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, and Śrīla Jīva Goswāmī. On a higher platform I offer my respects unto Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu, who came with His paraphernalia and associates, along with those great personalities Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu and Śrī Advaita Āchārya. And in the highest stage, I offer my obeisances unto the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Govinda, and all of the gopīs, headed by Lalitā Devī and Viśākhā Devī.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>om ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā</strong><br>
<strong>chakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Applying the soothing salve of sambandha-jñāna, a proper acquaintance with the environment, my spiritual master has opened my inner eye and thereby rescued me from the darkness of ignorance, fulfilling my life’s aspirations. I offer my respects unto Śrī Gurudev.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gurvabhishta-supurakam-guru-ganair" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>gurv-ābhīṣṭa-supūrakaṁ guru-gaṇair āśīṣa-saṁbhūṣitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>chintyāchintya-samasta-veda-nipuṇaṁ śrī-rūpa-panthānugam</strong><br>
<strong>govindābhidham ujjvalaṁ vara-tanuṁ bhakty-anvitaṁ sundaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande viśva-guruñ cha divya-bhagavat-premṇo hi bīja-pradam</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj)</i><br>
His Guru’s most cherished wishes, he’s truly fulfilling,<br />adorned with his guru-varga’s affectionate blessing.<br />In knowledge both conceivable and inconceivable,<br />his expertise illumines the ontological whole.<br />He’s the foremost follower of Śrī Rūpa’s divine aim,<br />Śrī Bhakti Sundar Govinda is his celebrated name.<br />His beautiful figure stands out, clean, clear and effulgent,<br />devotionally endowed with transcendent sentiment.<br />I worship him, the world-wide guru from high above,<br />the empowered bestower of the seed of Divine Love.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-devam-divya-tanum-suchanda-vadanam-balarka-chelanchitam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>devaṁ divya-tanuṁ suchanda-vadanaṁ bālārka-chelāñchitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>sāndrānanda-puraṁ sad-eka-varaṇaṁ vairāgya-vidyāmbudhim</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-siddhānta-nidhiṁ subhakti-lasitaṁ sārasvatānām varaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande taṁ śubhadaṁ mad-eka-śaraṇaṁ nyāsīśvaraṁ śrīdharam</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj)</i><br>
I fall at the feet of Śrīla Śrīdhar-Deva,<br />who with charming expression sings his songs of sweet nectar.<br />Dressed with fine garments like sun newly-risen,<br />he’s the true saint the devotees have chosen.<br />His detachment and knowledge are just like an ocean,<br />the treasure-house of complete, perfect conclusion.<br />Radiant in Pastimes of loving devotion,<br />the abode of pure ecstasy, bestower of good fortune.<br />Foremost true follower of Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta,<br />foremost great general of the whole renounced order,<br />my lord and my master, my only shelter,<br />I worship his feet, Śrīla Śrīdhar-Deva.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-siddhanta-sarasvatiti-vidito" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Bhagavān Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-siddhānta-sarasvatīti-vidito gauḍīya-gurv-anvaye</strong><br>
<strong>bhāto bhānur iva prabhāta-gagane yo gaura-saṅkīrtanaiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>māyāvāda-timiṅgilodara-gatān uddhṛtya jīvanimān</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-prema-sudhābdhi-gāhana-sukhaṁ prādāt prabhuṁ taṁ bhaje</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj)</i><br>
In the great Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava teachers’ line,<br />as Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī he’s renowned.<br />Like the radiant sun in the morning sky,<br />he appeared to rescue all souls swallowed by<br />the all-devouring impersonal philosophy.<br />By spreading the teachings of Lord Gaurāṅga<br />to sing the Holy Name of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa,<br />he gave all the chance to dive in the ocean<br />of love of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Person.<br />Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta, my lord, divine master – <br />at his feet do I pray to serve him forever.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-namo-gaurakishoraya-bhaktavadhuta-murtaye" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrīla Gaura Kiśor Dās Bābājī <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>namo gaura-kiśorāya bhaktāvadhūta-mūrtaye</strong><br>
<strong>gaurāṅghri-padma-bhṛṅgāya rādhā-bhāva-niṣeviṇe</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj)</i><br>
I bow to our Guru, Śrīla Gaura Kiśor Dās,<br />the pure devotee beyond social class;<br />the bee in the lotus of the feet of Śrī Gaurāṅga,<br />who deep in his heart serves Śrī Rādhā forever.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-vande-bhaktivinodam-shri-gaura-shiakti-svarupakam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrīla Sachchidānanda Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>vande bhaktivinodaṁ śrī-gaura-śakti-svarūpakam</strong><br>
<strong>bhakti-śāstrajña-samrājaṁ rādhā-rasa-sudhā-nidhim</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj)</i><br>
I bow to Śrī Ṭhākur Bhakti Vinod,<br />Mahāprabhu’s love divine personified.<br />He’s the king of all knowers<br />of the purpose of the scriptures,<br />and he is the ocean<br />of Śrī Rādhā’s devotion.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gaura-vrajashritashieshair-vaishnavair-vandya-vigraham" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrīla Jagannāth Dās Bābājī <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>gauḍa-vrajāśritāśeṣair vaiṣṇavair vandya-vigraham</strong><br>
<strong>jagannātha-prabhuṁ vande premābdhiṁ vṛddha-vaiṣṇavam</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj)</i><br>
The great venerable Grandsire of pure devotion,<br />loved by all Vaiṣṇavas of both Nabadwīp and Vṛndāvan – <br />I worship the feet of that great Vaiṣṇava Guru,<br />the ocean of love, Śrīla Jagannātha Prabhu.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-vancha-kalpatarubhyas-cha" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Vāñchhā-kalpa-tarubhyaś cha <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>vāñchhā-kalpa-tarubhyaś cha kṛpā-sindhubhya eva cha</strong><br>
<strong>patitānāṁ pāvanebhyo vaiṣṇavebhyo namo namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I bow down unto the holy feet of the pure Vaiṣṇavas, who are like desire trees able to fulfil all the devotees’ aspirations. The Lord’s devotees are comparable to a vast ocean of causeless mercy, as they deliver the fallen souls from material existence. I offer all respects unto them.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>pañcha-tattvātmakaṁ kṛṣṇaṁ bhakta-rūpa-svarūpakam</strong><br>
<strong>bhaktāvatāraṁ bhaktākhyaṁ namāmi bhakta-śaktikam</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrī Chaitanya-charitāmṛta: Ādi-līlā, 1.14)</i><br>
I offer my obeisances unto Lord Kṛṣṇa in His fivefold aspect known as the pañcha-tattva: I offer my respects unto Śrī Gaurāṅgadev, who appears as a devotee of the Lord relishing His own internal mellows. Furthermore I bow down before Lord Nityānanda Prabhu, who is the self-same form and elder brother of Śrī Gaurāṅga. Next, I pay homage unto that great personality Śrī Advaita Prabhu, who is an incarnation of the Lord’s devotee, and unto the Lord’s eternal associates like Śrīvās Paṇḍit, who serve the Lord in loving devotional relationships. Finally, I bow down at the feet of those devotees who hold the actual potency of unalloyed dedication in their hands: Śrī Rāmānanda Rāya, Śrī Gadādhar Paṇḍit, and Śrī Svarūp Dāmodar.<br /><i>(Śrīla Kṛṣṇadās Kavirāj Goswāmī)</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>namo mahāvadānyāya kṛṣṇa-prema-pradāya te</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇāya kṛṣṇa-chaitanya-nāmne gaura-tviṣe namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrī Chaitanya-charitāmṛta: Madhya-līlā, 19.53)</i><br>
I offer my obeisances unto Lord Gaurāṅga, whose divine complexion is a radiant golden hue and who possesses the qualities of the most benevolent incarnation. His spiritual Pastimes freely grant pure love for Lord Kṛṣṇa to all. He is none other than Lord Kṛṣṇa known in the age of Kali as Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya.<br /><i>(Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī)</i></p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jayatam-suratau-pangor" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Guru Gaurāṅga Rādhā Madana-Mohanjīu <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Kolkata, Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswata Kṛṣṇānuśīlana Sangha</h3>
<p class="verse"><strong>jayatāṁ suratau paṅgor mama manda-mater gatī</strong><br>
<strong>mat-sarvasva-padāmbhojau rādhā-madana-mohanau</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrī Chaitanya-charitāmṛta: Ādi-līlā, 1.15)</i><br>
O Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Madana-mohan, I am a helpless cripple and my meagre intelligence is absorbed in vile material sense objects. Kindly let deep devotional attachment arise for the exclusive wealth of my life which is Your lotus feet. May Your Lordships, who are so compassionate and affectionate, be forever victorious!<br>
<i>(Śrīla Kṛṣṇadās Kavirāj Goswāmī)</i></p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-divyad-vrindaranya-kalpa-drumadhah" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Guru-Gaurāṅga-Gāndharvā-Govindasundarjīu <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Nabadwīp, Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh</h3>
<p class="verse"><strong>dīvyad-vṛndāraṇya-kalpa-drumādhaḥ-</strong><br>
<strong>śrīmad-ratnāgāra-siṁhāsana-sthau</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-gāndharvā-śrīla-govinda-devau</strong><br>
<strong>preṣṭhālībhiḥ sevyamānau smarāmi</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrī Chaitanya-charitāmṛta: Ādi-līlā, 1.16)</i><br>
I constantly meditate upon that Divine Couple Śrī Śrī Gāndharvā-Govinda, who are seated upon a wonderful throne highly decorated with brilliant jewels. They are sitting within the illustrious forest of Vraja, beneath a mind-attracting desire tree, accompanied by Their dedicated servitors such as Śrī Lalitā Devī, Śrī Rūpa Mañjarī and other intimate servants like the priya-narmā sakhīs.<br>
<i>(Śrīla Kṛṣṇadās Kavirāj Goswāmī)</i></p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shriman-rasa-rasarambhi" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī-Śrī-Guru-Gaurāṅga-Rādhā-Gopīnāthjīu <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Hāpāniyā, Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Āśram</h3>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrīmān rāsa-rasārambhī vaṁśī-vaṭa-taṭa-sthitaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>karṣan veṇu-svanair gopīr gopīnāthaḥ śriye ’stu naḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrī Chaitanya-charitāmṛta: Ādi-līlā, 1.17)</i><br>
Śrī Gopīnāth, who instigates the exchange of devotional mellows, is standing upon the banks of the Yamunā River at the special place known as Vaṁśīvaṭa. There, the sweet tune of His flute is naturally attracting the hearts of the cowherd maidens of Vraja Dhām. May that charming Lord bring us within His auspicious embrace.<br>
<i>(Śrīla Kṛṣṇadās Kavirāj Goswāmī)</i></p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-vrindayai-tulasi-devyai" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Vṛndāyai tulasī-devyai <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛndāyai tulasī-devyai priyāyai keśavasya cha</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-bhakti-prade devi satyavatyai namo namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I eternally offer my respects unto Śrī Vṛndā Devī, Śrīmatī Tulasī Mahārāṇī, who is the dearmost object of Lord Keśava’s affection. I bow down before Satyavatī who can bestow pure dedication unto Lord Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>atha natvā mantra-gurūn gurūn bhāgavatārthadān</strong><br>
<strong>vyāsān jagad-gurūn natvā tato jayam udīrayet</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Then, let me bow down before all my spiritual masters: the guru who gave me the divine mantra, as well as all those who have taught me the meaning of <i>Śrīmad Bhāgavatam</i>. Let me offer my respectful obeisances unto Śrīla Vyāsadev, the spiritual master of the entire universe and the giver of the all-conquering conclusions of <i>Śrīmad Bhāgavatam</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>jayaḥ sa-parikara-śrī-śrī-guru-gaurāṅga-</strong><br>
<strong>gāndharvā-govindasundara-pāda-padmānāṁ jayastu!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Let the holy lotus feet of Śrī Gurudev, Śrīman Mahāprabhu, and Śrī Śrī Gāndharvā-Govindasundar, along with Their eternal associates, be victorious over all!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-namah-om-vishnupadaya-krishna" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrīla A.C. Bhaktivedānta Swāmī Prabhupād <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>namaḥ om viṣṇupādaya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhūtale</strong><br>
<strong>swāmī śrī bhaktivedānta prabhupādāya te namaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>gurvajñaṁ śīrasi-dhṛtvā śaktyāveśa sva-rūpiṇe</strong><br>
<strong>hare-kṛṣṇeti mantreṇa pāśchatya-prāchya-tāriṇe</strong><br>
<strong>viśvāchārya prabaryāya divya kāruṇya mūrtaye</strong><br>
<strong>śrī bhāgavata-mādhurya-gīta-jñāna pradāyine</strong><br>
<strong>gaura-śrī-rūpa-siddhānta-sarasvatī niṣeviṇe</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-kṛṣṇa-padāmbhoja-bhṛṅgāya gurave namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj)</i><br>
I offer my humble obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhakti­vedānta Swāmī Prabhupād, who is Kṛṣṇa’s beloved associate and who came down to this plane from Goloka. Taking the order of his guru on his head, he is the śaktyāveśa (empowered) avatār of Nityānanda Prabhu personified. He distributed the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra all over the Eastern and Western world, delivering and uplifting all fallen souls. He is the best of millions of jagat-gurus, because he is the personification of divine mercy. He has distributed the sweet nectar of <i>Śrīmad Bhāgavatam</i> and the transcendental knowledge of <i>Bhagavad-gītā</i> all over the world. He is constantly engaged in exclusive devotional service to Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur, Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī, and Śrī Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu. I offer my humble obeisances unto Śrīla Prabhupād, who is like a bumblebee always tasting the nectar of the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Govinda.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shrimach-chaitanya-sarasvata-mathavara-udgita-kirtir" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>The Glory of Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrīmach-chaitanya-sārasvata-maṭhavara-udgīta-kīrtir jaya-śrīṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bibhrat sambhāti gaṅgā-taṭa-nikaṭa-navadvīpa-kolādri-rāje</strong><br>
<strong>yatra śrī-gaura-sārasvata-mata-niratā-gaura-gāthā gṛṇanti</strong><br>
<strong>nityaṁ rūpānuga śrī-kṛtamati-guru-gaurāṅga-rādhā-jitāśāḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>Composed by Śrīla B.R. Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj while residing in a hut on the banks of the Ganges in 1941. Śrīla B.S. Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj crafted this translation, based upon his own Bengali translation.</i><br>
In the Holy Abode of transcendental touchstone,<br />Śrī Nabadwīp Dham – the selfsame Śrī Vṛndāvan,<br />in the land of Koladwīp, whose inner identity<br />is the King of mountains Śrī Govardhan,<br />near the charming shores of the holy River Bhāgīrathī<br />who delivers all the fallen souls,<br />stands that grand and glorious King of all Temples – <br />Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh.<br>
In that beautiful retreat, the surrendered devotees<br />whose lives are the message of Śrī Gaura,<br />are eternally absorbed in the loving service<br />of the Divine Master, the Golden Lord Gaurāṅga,<br />and the Loving Lord Śrī Govindasundar<br />with His consort Śrīmatī Gāndharvā,<br />accompanied by all Their beloved associates.<br>
With hearts always filled with good hope<br />to attain the grace of the Lord,<br />following faithfully in the line<br />of Śrī Śrī Rūpa and Raghunāth – <br />the storekeepers of the unlimited treasure<br />of pure, sweet love divine – <br />those surrendered souls ever sing the unending glories<br />of the transcendental Name and qualities<br />of the most magnanimous, original Supreme Lord,<br />Śrī Śrī Gaurāṅgasundar.<br>
Indeed the hearts of all beings, moving and still,<br />marvel in the glory of Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh<br />as they take shelter in the soothing, affectionate shade<br />of the victory flag that flies aloft<br />and sings wholeheartedly to the world the ever-expanding<br />renown of Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh;<br />bearing that banner of all transcendental benedictions,<br />Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh ever shines resplendent<br />in all its divine magnificence.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-sa-parikara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Jaya Dhvani <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="translation"><i>This is not a comprehensive list, but a guideline to the glorifications chanted at the end of each ārati in the Temple. It is flexible.</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>Jaya Saparikar Śrī Śrī Guru Gaurāṅga Gāndharvā Govindasundar-jīu kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Saparikar Śrī Śrī </strong>(the Names of the local Temple Deities)<strong> kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Om Viṣṇupād Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakāchārya-varya Aṣṭottara-śata-śrī Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Om Viṣṇupād Paramahaṁsa Parivrājakāchārya-varya Aṣṭottara-śata-śrī Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Om Viṣṇupād Bhagavān Śrī Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Om Viṣṇupād Śrīla Gaura Kiśor Dās Bābājī Mahārāj kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Om Viṣṇupād Śrīla Sachchidānanda Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Om Viṣṇupād Śrīla Jagannātha Dās Bābājī Mahārāj kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śrīla Baladev Vidyābhūṣaṇ kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śrīla Viśvanāth Chakravartī Ṭhākur kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śrī Śrī Narottam, Śyāmānanda, Śrīnivās – Prabhu-traya kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śrīla Kṛṣṇa Dās Kavirāj Goswāmī kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śrīla Vṛndāvan Dās Ṭhākur kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śrī Rūpa, Sanātan, Bhaṭṭa Raghunāth, Śrī Jīva, Gopāl Bhaṭṭa, Dās Raghunāth – Śrī Ṣaḍ Goswāmī Prabhu kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śrīla Svarūp Dāmodar, Śrīla Rāy Rāmānanda kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śrī Rūpānuga Guru-varga kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Namāchārya Śrīla Hari Dās Ṭhākur kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Viśva-vareṇya Śrīla A.C. Bhaktivedānta Swāmī Prabhupād kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya, Prabhu Nityānanda, Śrī Advaita, Gadādhar, Śrīvās-ādi Gaura-bhakta-vṛnda kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Sapārṣada Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Sapārṣada Śrīman Mahāprabhu kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śrī Nabadwīp Dhām kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śrī Gaṅgā-mayī, Śrī Yamunā-mayī kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śrī Vṛndāvan Dhām kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, Gopa, Gopī, Gopīnāth, Śyāma Kuṇḍa, Rādhā Kuṇḍa, Girirāj Govardhan kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Bhakti Devī, Vṛndā Devī, Tulasī Devī, kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śrī Puruṣottam Dhām kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Baladev, Subhadrā, Jagannātha-jīu kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Grantharāj Śrīmad Bhāgavatam kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Grantharāj Śrī Chaitanya Charitāmṛta kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Ākara Maṭh Rāja Śrī Chaitanya Maṭh kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Tadīya Śākhā Maṭh kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Viśva-vyāpī Tadīya Śākhā Maṭh kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śuddha Bhakti Vighna Vināśāya Śrī Nṛsiṁhadeva kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Bhakta-pravara Prahlād Mahārāj kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Ananta-koṭī Vaiṣṇava-vṛnda kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Samaveta Vaiṣṇava-maṇḍala kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Samaveta Bhakta-vṛnda kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Āchārya-vṛnda kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Śrī Sannyāsa-vṛnda kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
(Names of sannyasīs/senior Vaiṣṇavas present in the assembly, kī jaya!)<br>
<br>
<strong>Jaya Śrī Harināma-saṅkīrtan kī jaya!</strong><br>
<br>
<strong>Nitāi Gaura Premānande! Haribol!</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h3>Translations of key phrases</h3>
<p class="translation"><i class="translation__term">Āchārya-varya</i> – The best, preferred, beloved teacher<br>
<i class="translation__term">Ananta-koṭī Vaiṣṇava-vṛnda</i> – An assembly of innumerable Vaiṣṇavas<br>
<i class="translation__term">Ākara Maṭh Rāja Śrī Chaitanya Maṭh</i> – Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur’s headquarters maṭh, Śrī Gauḍīya Maṭh in Māyāpur<br>
<i class="translation__term">Aṣṭottara-śata-śrī</i> – Highly respectful and auspicious form of address<br>
<i class="translation__term">Bhakta-pravara</i> – The best, topmost, devotee<br>
<i class="translation__term">Grantharāj</i> – The king of scriptures<br>
<i class="translation__term">Jaya</i> – All glories, victory, and triumph<br>
<i class="translation__term">Jīu</i> – An affectionate and reverential term for a Deity<br>
<i class="translation__term">Nitāi Gaura Premānande</i> – May the divine Names of Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu and Śrī Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu be ecstatically, lovingly sung<br>
<i class="translation__term">Namāchārya</i> – Āchārya of the Holy Name<br>
<i class="translation__term">Om Viṣṇupād</i> – At the lotus feet of Lord Viṣṇu<br>
<i class="translation__term">Paramahaṁsa</i> – Sage or saint who sees divinity everywhere; extracts the essence; (literally) supreme swan<br>
<i class="translation__term">Parivrājak</i> – Constantly moving<br>
<i class="translation__term">Prabhu-traya</i> – Three Prabhus<br>
<i class="translation__term">Samaveta Bhakta-vṛnda</i> – All the assembled devotees<br>
<i class="translation__term">Samaveta Vaiṣṇava-maṇḍala</i> – All the circle, group, of Vaiṣṇavas<br>
<i class="translation__term">Saparikar; Sapārṣada</i> – (Accompanied by) Their paraphernalia; Their eternal associates<br>
<i class="translation__term">Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh</i> – the Temple and ashram in Nabadwīp established by Śrīla Guru Mahārāj and developed by Śrīla Gurudev<br>
<i class="translation__term">Śrī Harināma-saṅkīrtan</i> – The congregational chanting of the Holy Names of Śrī Kṛṣṇa<br>
<i class="translation__term">Śrīla</i> – One who is endowed with divine qualities, (literally) one who possesses ‘Śrī’ (all good qualities/fortune/beauty)<br>
<i class="translation__term">Śrī Rūpānuga Guru-varga</i> – The spiritual masters in the line of Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī<br>
<i class="translation__term">Śuddha Bhakti Vighna Vināśāya</i> – Destroyer of all obstacles on the path of pure devotion; (literally) pure devotion obstacles destroyer<br>
<i class="translation__term">Tadīya Śākhā Maṭh</i> – All the branches, missions, and ashrams that originated from the main Maṭh; (literally) related branch maṭhs<br>
<i class="translation__term">Viśva-vareṇya</i> – Universally worshipable<br>
<i class="translation__term">Viśva-vyāpī Tadīya Śākhā Maṭh</i> – (literally) universally expanded related branch maṭhs</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-krishna-chaitanya-prabhu-nityananda" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Pañcha Tattva Mantra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya prabhu nityānanda</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-advaita gadādhara śrīvāsādi gaura-bhakta-vṛnda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The five features of the Absolute Truth: Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya Mahāprabhu, Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu, Śrī Advaita Āchārya, Śrī Gadādhar Paṇḍit, and Śrī Śrīvās Paṇḍit heading all the devotees of Gaurāṅga (Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya Mahāprabhu).</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hare-krishna-hare-krishna-krishna-krishna-hare-hare" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Hare Kṛṣṇa Mahāmantra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa hare hare</strong><br>
<strong>hare rāma hare rāma rāma rāma hare hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>Illumination by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Mahārāj:</i><br>
Before chanting the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa, we must first chant the Pañcha Tattva mantra. The Pañcha Tattva, or five features of the Absolute Truth, came to give the Hare Kṛṣṇa Mahāmantra to the fallen devotees of this age, so they are the general representation of Guru for us. They help us to enter into the domain of Kṛṣṇa and also the plane of Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu.<br>
After chanting the Pañcha Tattva mantra, we should count on the beads of the japa-mālā and chant the Mahāmantra.<br>
In the Name of Rāma within the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra, the Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇavas will find Rādhā-ramaṇa Rāma. That means, “Kṛṣṇa, who gives pleasure (ramaṇ) to Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī.” In our conception, the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra is wholesale Kṛṣṇa consciousness, not Rāma (Lord Rāmachandra) consciousness. Śrī Chaitanya’s highest conception of things is always Svayaṁ Bhagavān, Kṛṣṇa-līlā, Rādhā-Govinda-līlā. That is the real purpose of Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu’s advent and teachings. In that consideration, the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra does not mention the Rāma-līlā of Āyodhya at all. There is no connection with that in the highest conception of the Hare Kṛṣṇa mantra. The inner conception of the mantra is responsible for our spiritual attainment. The inner conception of the devotee will guide them to their destination.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hari-nama-mahamantra-sarva-mantra-sara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Daśa-vidha Nāmāparādha <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>The Ten Offences to the Holy Name</h3>
<h4>Bengali composition by</h4>
<h4>Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-nāma mahāmantra sarva-mantra-sāra</strong><br>
<strong>yā̐dera karuṇā-bale jagate prachāra</strong><br>
<strong>sei nāma-parāyaṇa sādhu, mahājana</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐hādera nindā nā kariha kadāchana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Hare Kṛṣṇa Mahāmantra – of all mantras best,<br />throughout the world is preached by saints’ potent grace behest;<br />such saints to the Name devoted, such pure souls great:<br />never dare offend them – never show them hate.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vrajendra-nandana kṛṣṇa sarveśvareśvara</strong><br>
<strong>maheśvara ādi tā̐ra sevana-tatpara</strong><br>
<strong>nāma chintāmaṇi kṛṣṇa-chaitanya-svarūpa</strong><br>
<strong>bheda-jñāna nā karibe līlā-guṇa-rūpa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Kṛṣṇa, son of Nanda, of all lords the Leader – <br />the great Śiva and all the gods serve His feet forever;<br />all-fulfilling Name of Kṛṣṇa glorious and great – <br />His Pastimes, Nature, Form, also think not separate.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘‘guru kṛṣṇa-rūpa hana śāstrera pramāṇe</strong><br>
<strong>guru-rūpe kṛṣṇa kṛpā kare bhāgyavāne’’</strong><br>
<strong>se gurute martya-buddhi avajñādi tyaji’</strong><br>
<strong>iṣṭa-lābha kara, nirantara nāma bhaji’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Guru’s a form of Kṛṣṇa – the scriptures corroborate;<br />in the form of Guru, Kṛṣṇa blesses the fortunate.”<br />Never offend that Guru by thinking him mere mortal;<br />gain your highest goal and serve the Name for time eternal.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śruti, śruti-mātā-saha sātvata purāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-nāma-charaṇa-padma kare nīrājana</strong><br>
<strong>sei śruti-śāstra yebā karaye nindana</strong><br>
<strong>se aparadhīra saṅga karibe varjana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Vedas, Mother Gāyatrī, and <i>Śrīmad Bhāgavatam</i><br />illuminate the lotus feet of Śrī Harināma;<br />whoever dares to vilify those holy Vedic scriptures – <br />never keep their company, know them as offenders.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāmera mahimā sarva-śāstrete vākhāne</strong><br>
<strong>atistuti, hena kabhu nā bhāviha mane</strong><br>
<strong>agastya, ananta, brahmā, śivādi satata</strong><br>
<strong>ye nāma-mahimā-gāthā saṅkīrtana-rata</strong><br>
<strong>se nāma-mahimā-sindhu ke pāibe pāra?</strong><br>
<strong>atistuti bale yei – sei durāchāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The glories of the Name – all scriptures’ exaltation;<br />dare not think their praise to be exaggeration.<br />Agastya, Ananta, Brahmā, Śiva, all forever<br />sing the glories of that Name with full-hearted fervour.<br />Who can cross the ocean of the glories of that Name?<br />Whoever says ‘exaggeration’ have their sin to blame.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa-nāmāvali nitya golokera dhana</strong><br>
<strong>kalpita, prākṛta, bhāve – aparādhi-jana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Holy Names of Kṛṣṇa – supreme wealth of Goloka:<br />who thinks those Names mundane, imagined – he is an offender.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāme sarva-pāpa-kṣaya sarva-śāstre kaya</strong><br>
<strong>sārā-dina pāpa kari’ sei bharasāya –</strong><br>
<strong>emata durbuddhi yāra sei aparādhī</strong><br>
<strong>māyā-pravañchita, duḥkha bhuñje niravadhi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All scriptures claim the Name all sin it can destroy,<br />but those who spend their time in sin, making it a ploy – <br />such a wicked attitude is that of an offender<br />deceived by illusion, perpetually to suffer.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>atulya śrī-kṛṣṇa-nāma pūrṇa-rasa-nidhi</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐ra sama nā bhāviha śubha-karma ādi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Incomparable Name of Kṛṣṇa – ocean of ecstasy:<br />never dare compare it with auspicious piety.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāme śraddhā-hīna-jana – vidhātā vāñchita</strong><br>
<strong>tāre nāma dāne aparādha suniśchita</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Those who’re faithless t’ward the Name – deceived by Providence:<br />giving them the Holy Name is surely an offence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuniyāo kṛṣṇa-nāma-māhātmya apāra</strong><br>
<strong>ye prīti-rahita, sei narādhama chhāra</strong><br>
<strong>ahaṁtā mamatā yāra antare bāhire</strong><br>
<strong>śuddha kṛṣṇa-nāma tāra kabhu nāhi sphure</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Though hearing glories infinite of Kṛṣṇa’s Holy Name,<br />those whose hearts don’t melt in love are rascals of ill fame;<br />only pride and avarice their thoughts and deeds do yield – <br />the Pure Name of Kṛṣṇa is to them never revealed.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei daśa aparādha kariyā varjana</strong><br>
<strong>ye sujana kare hari-nāma-saṅkīrtana</strong><br>
<strong>apūrva śrī-kṛṣṇa-prema labhya tāre haya</strong><br>
<strong>nāma-prabhu tāra hṛde nitya vilasaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Casting off these ten offences, leaving no exception,<br />those pure souls who chant the Name in Holy Congregation – <br />the miracle of love for Kṛṣṇa they will surely savour,<br />the Name Divine Himself will shine within their hearts forever.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nija-pati-bhuja-danda-chchhatra-bhavam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Govardhana-vāsa-prārthanā-daśakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Ten Prayers for a Dwelling near Śrī Govardhan</h3>
<h4>Composed by Śrīla Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī Prabhu in Sanskrit.</h4>
<h4>The preferred prayer to Śrī Govardhan of</h4>
<h4>Bhagavān Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nija-pati-bhuja-daṇḍa-chchhatra-bhāvaṁ prapadya</strong><br>
<strong>pratihata-mada-dhṛṣṭoddaṇḍa-devendra-garva</strong><br>
<strong>atula-pṛthula-śaila-śreṇi-bhūpa priyaṁ me</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You became the umbrella atop the handle of the arm of your Lord and crushed the pride of proud, arrogant, impudent Indra. You are the incomparable king of all great mountains. Please give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pramada-madana-līlāḥ kandare kandare te</strong><br>
<strong>rachayati nava-yūnor dvandvam asminn amandam</strong><br>
<strong>iti kila kalanārthaṁ lagnakas tad-dvayor me</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Young Couple liberally enact Pastimes of mad love in your caves. So that I can see Their Pastimes, please give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>anupama-maṇi-vedī-ratna-siṁhāsanorvī-</strong><br>
<strong>ruha-jhara-dara-sānu-droṇi-saṅgheṣu raṅgaiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>saha bala-sakhibhiḥ saṅkhelayan sva-priyaṁ me</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You induce your beloved to joyfully play with Balarām and His friends amidst your trees, brooks, caves, plateaus, valleys, and incomparable jeweled terraces, which resemble jeweled thrones. Please give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rasa-nidhi-nava-yūnoḥ sākṣiṇīṁ dāna-keler</strong><br>
<strong>dyuti-parimala-viddhāṁ śyāma-vedīṁ prakāśya</strong><br>
<strong>rasika-vara-kulānāṁ modam āsphālayan me</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You provide the dark, lustrous, fragrant terrace that witnesses the fair Pastime of the Young Couple, who are an ocean of rasa, and you delight the best amongst those who relish rasa. Please give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-dayitam apūrvaṁ rādhikā-kuṇḍam ātma-</strong><br>
<strong>priya-sakham iha kaṇṭhe narmaṇāliṅgya guptaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>nava-yuva-yuga-khelās tatra paśyan raho me</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Playfully embracing around the neck your dear friend Rādhā Kuṇḍa, the incomparable favourite of the Lord, you hide in a secluded place beside her and watch the Pastimes of the Young Couple. Please give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sthala-jala-tala-śaṣpair bhūruhach-chhāyayā cha</strong><br>
<strong>prati-padam anukālaṁ hanta samvardhayan gāḥ</strong><br>
<strong>tri-jagati nija-gotraṁ sārthakaṁ khyāpayan me</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You always fully nourish the cows everywhere with your land, water, plains, grass, and the shade of your trees, and thus proclaim the fulfilment of your Name throughout the three worlds. Please give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!<br>
<i class="translation__note">Note:</i><br>
 <i>Govardhana</i> means “He who nourishes (vardhana) the cows (go).”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sura-pati-kṛta-dīrgha-drohato goṣṭha-rakṣāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>tava nava-gṛha-rūpasyāntare kurvataiva</strong><br>
<strong>agha-baka-ripuṇochchhair datta-māna drutaṁ me</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are highly honoured by Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the enemy of Agha and Baka. You protected His community from the onslaught of Indra beneath your form as their new home. Please quickly give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>giri-nṛpa hari-dāsa-śreṇi-varyeti nāmā-</strong><br>
<strong>mṛtam idam uditaṁ śrī-rādhikā-vaktra-chandrāt</strong><br>
<strong>vraja-nava-tilakatve kḷpta vedaiḥ sphuṭaṁ me</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O king of mountains! When the nectar of your Name “Hari-dāsa-varya: the best of the Lord’s servants” came forth from the moon-like face of Śrī Rādhikā, you were clearly established by the Vedas as the new tilak of Vraja. Please give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nija-jana-yuta-rādhā-kṛṣṇa-maitrī-rasākta-</strong><br>
<strong>vraja-nara-paśu-pakṣi-vrāta-saukhyaika-dātaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>agaṇita-karuṇatvān mām urīkṛtya tāntaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You alone give happiness to the people, animals, and birds of Vraja, who are filled with the mood of friendship towards Rādhā, Kṛṣṇa, and Their companions. You are immeasurably merciful. So, please accept me, a wearied soul, and give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nirupadhi-karuṇena śrī-śachī-nandanena</strong><br>
<strong>tvayi kapaṭi-śaṭho ’pi tvat-priyeṇārpito ’smi</strong><br>
<strong>iti khalu mama yogyāyogyatāṁ tām agṛhṇan</strong><br>
<strong>nija-nikaṭa-nivāsaṁ dehi govardhana tvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Although I am insincere and deceitful, I have been given to You by Your dearmost, Śachī’s Divine Son, who is unconditionally merciful. So, please do not judge whether I am qualified or unqualified, and give me a dwelling near you, O Govardhan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rasada-daśakam asya śrīla-govardhanasya</strong><br>
<strong>kṣiti-dhara-kula-bhartur yaḥ prayatnād adhīte</strong><br>
<strong>sa sapadi sukhade ’smin vāsam āsādya sākṣāch</strong><br>
<strong>chhubhada-yugala-sevā-ratnam āpnoti tūrṇam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who carefully studies these ten nectarean verses describing the king of mountains, Śrī Govardhan, will soon attain a dwelling at this joyous place and quickly attain the precious jewel of personal service to the Divine Couple.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Ārati</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-jaya-girirajer-arati-vishiala" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Govinda Kuṇḍa Gupta-Govardhan Ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h4>for the āratis in our Nabadwīp Girirāj-Govardhan Temple in Govinda Kuṇḍa</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya jaya girirājer ārati viśāla</strong><br>
<strong>śrī gaura-maṇḍala-mājhe bhuvana-maṅgala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories, all glories, to the grand ārati of Śrī Girirāj! In the midst of Śrī Gaura Maṇḍal, it is benefitting the entire universe.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>koladvīpe śobhe gupta-govardhanarūpa</strong><br>
<strong>vyakta hoilā śrī govinda kuṇḍe aparūpa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The hidden Govardhan graces Koladwīp with His beauty. Now He has manifested His exquisite beauty in Śrī Govinda Kuṇḍa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mālatī-mādhavī-kuñja kandare kandare</strong><br>
<strong>nigūḍha-nikuñja-līlā hari-manohare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There are groves of mālatī and mādhavī flowers in its various caves. In these groves, Lord Hari plays His hidden, mysterious, and enchanting Pastimes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>apūrva kuṇḍera śobhā jena sudhābhāṇḍa</strong><br>
<strong>surendra-surabhī-sevā-saubhāgya-mārtaṇḍa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The incomparable beauty of the kuṇḍa is like a reservoir of honey. Even Lord Indra and the divine Surabhī cow are rendering their service under the shining sun of good fortune.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī bhakti-rakṣaka deva-gosvāmī śrīdhara</strong><br>
<strong>prakāśilā tava sevā dīpta manohara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj has manifested the effulgent and enchanting devotional service to You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī chaitanya-sārasvata maṭha suśobhana</strong><br>
<strong>yathā nitya sevā kare sārasvata-gaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh is a beautiful place where devotees of the Maṭh are always rendering their devotional service.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gosvāmī śrī-raghunātha dāsere smariyā</strong><br>
<strong>nirantara sevā kare antarmaṇā haiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Remembering Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, they are continuously serving in an introspective mood.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya gupta-govardhana divya-rasālaya</strong><br>
<strong>rūpa-raghunāthānuga-bhaktera āśraya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to the hidden Govardhan, which is a reservoir of transcendental rasa. He is the refuge of the devotees who are following in the footsteps of Śrī Rūpa and Śrī Raghunāth.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kuliyā-prakāśa tava achintya-mahimā</strong><br>
<strong>divāniśi bhakta-vṛnda kare parikramā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By Your inconceivable glories and magnanimity, You have manifested Kuliyā (where all offences are forgiven). Day and night, the multitudes of devotees are circumambulating this place.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sevya o sevaka-rūpe nitya parakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>sānande ārati dekhe e adhama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are eternally manifested both as the worshipable Lord and as the servitor Lord. This fallen servant sees Your ārati with great joy.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-jaya-girirajer-arotiko-shobha" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Girirāj-Govardhan Ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h4>for the āratis in our Govardhan Temple</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya jaya girirājer āratika śobhā</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-vraja-maṇḍala mājhe jaga mana lobhā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to the beautiful ārati of Śrī Girirāj which is going on in Śrī Vraja Maṇḍal, attracting the minds of all living entities of the universe.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pramoda-madana-līla śrī-rādhā-ramaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>yathā nitya-līlā kare laye sakhī-gaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Śrī Rādhā-Ramaṇa, Kṛṣṇa, who gives pleasure to his beloved (Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī) is eternally playing His joyful Loving Pastimes there in the association of the sakhīs of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mālatī-mādhavī-kuñja kandare kandare</strong><br>
<strong>nigūḍha nikuñja-līla hari manohare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Here deep within the groves of mālatī flowers and mādhavī creepers, the confidential Pastimes of the charming Lord Hari are going on.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śyāma-kuṇḍa rādhā-kuṇḍa kusum sarovara</strong><br>
<strong>mānasa gaṅgā dāna-ghāṭī jāra abhyantara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Within this holy place are Śyāma Kuṇḍa, Rādhā Kuṇḍa, Kusum Sarovar, Mānasa Gaṅgā and Dāna-ghāṭī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-govinda-kuṇḍa līlāsthalī nāhi sīmā</strong><br>
<strong>divāniśi-bhakta-vṛnda kare parikramā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">With Śrī Govinda Kuṇḍa, these holy places of divine Pastimes are without limit. Day and night the devotees are performing circumambulation in worship.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘śrīdhar-svāmī-sevāśrama’ parama śobhana</strong><br>
<strong>yathā nitya-sevā kare sārasvata-gaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Here there is the supremely beautiful Śrīdhar Swāmī Sevāśram where devotees of Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh are performing their eternal service.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>abhinna śrī kṛṣṇa jñāne bhajibāra tare</strong><br>
<strong>mahāprabhu samarpilā raghunātha kare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrīman Mahāprabhu gave Śrī Śrī Girirāj-jīu to Śrī Raghunāth Goswāmī so that Śrī Dās Goswāmī could worship His Lordship knowing Him to be non-different from Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gosvāmī śrī-raghunātha dāsere smariyā</strong><br>
<strong>nirantara sevā kare antarmanā haiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The devotees of Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh worship Śrī Śrī Girirāj-jīu in a deep mood of devotion in remembrance of Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya giri-govardhana divya-rasālaya</strong><br>
<strong>rūpa-sanātana-jīva-raghunāthāśraya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Śrī Giri Govardhan which is the abode of divine rasa and the shelter of Śrī Rūpa, Sanātan, Jīva, and Raghunāth Goswāmīs.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>surendra-munīndra-śiva-śuka-mahājana</strong><br>
<strong>dekhena ārati-śobhā durllabha-darśana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The beauty of the ārati of Śrī Girirāj-jīu which is rare and precious is being seen by Lord Indra, Nārada Muni, Lord Śiva, Śukadeva Goswāmī, and all the great personalities.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sevya o sevaka-rūpe nitya parakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>sānande ārati dekhe e adhama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Śrī Girirāj-jīu is eternally manifested as both the worshipable Lord and the servitor Lord. This fallen servant sees the ārati of Śrī Girirāj-jīu with great ecstasy.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-guru-maharaja-karuna-sagara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Chaitanya Ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h4>for the āratis in our Jagannāth Purī Temple</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya guru mahārāja karuṇā-sāgara</strong><br>
<strong>śrī bhakti rakṣaka deva-gosvāmī śrīdhara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Śrīla Guru Mahārāj, Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj, who is like an ocean of mercy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prakāśile nīlāchale bhuvana-maṅgala</strong><br>
<strong>nitāi-chaitanya-deva-sevā samujjvala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By his mercy, Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Chaitanyadev have manifested in Puri Dhām bestowing auspiciousness upon the whole world and revealing Their effulgent devotional service.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gorāpreme mātoyārā nityānanda-rūpa</strong><br>
<strong>rasarāja mahābhāva chaitanya-svarūpa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu’s form is dancing ecstatically, intoxicated with divine love for Śrī Gaura, whose manifestation is Rasarāj Śrī Kṛṣṇa taking the heart and halo of Mahābhāva, Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kasita-kāñchana jini śrī-aṅga labaṇi</strong><br>
<strong>du̐hu̐gale vanamālā bhāvera dolanī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Both Lords are standing fully effulgent, glowing just like freshly cut gold, as garlands of various beautiful forest flowers play on their necks.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>murachita koṭikāma rūparāsaraṅge</strong><br>
<strong>madhura nartana-bhāva varābhaya bhaṅge</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Millions and millions of Cupids fall unconscious before the divine feet of Their Lordships, seeing Their beauty as Their divine forms playfully sway to and fro. They are dancing, touching the hearts of everyone and freely bestowing mercy upon the conditioned souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>koṭi-chandra-bhānuśobhā ratna-siṁhāsane</strong><br>
<strong>prema netre dekhe mahā-bhāgyavāna jane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Brilliant like millions of moons, They stand effulgent upon Their jewelled throne. The fortunate souls, their devotional eyes full of love and affection, can see Their divine forms.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mṛdaṅga mandirā bāje suśaṅkha-dhvanita</strong><br>
<strong>śruti-mauli-ratnamālā-dīpa nirājita</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the temple during the ārati, the mṛdaṅgas resound sweetly as conchshells are blown and the ghee lamp is offered. The different parts of the Vedas within bhakti yoga have taken form in each light, and through this form they are worshipping Their Lordships in the ārati.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhuvana-mohana du̐hu̐ rūpera ārati</strong><br>
<strong>gupta-vṛndāraṇya-vāsī dekhe niravadhi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The enchanting beauty of the Two (Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Chaitanya) captivates all the worlds. The residents of hidden Vṛndāvan behold it without end.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī svarūpa rāmānanda rūpa-sanātana</strong><br>
<strong>raghunātha haridāsa gadādhara dhana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">They are the treasure of Śrī Svarūp Dāmodar, Rāmānanda Rāya, Rūpa Goswāmī, Sanātan Goswāmī, Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Haridās Ṭhākur and Gadādhar Paṇḍit.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sārvabhauma gopīnātha jīvānuga-jana</strong><br>
<strong>dekhena ārati-śobha durllabha-darśana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Sārvabhauma Bhaṭṭāchārya, Gopīnāth Āchārya and the followers in the sampradāya of Jīva Goswāmī behold the splendour of the ārati – a vision rarely attained.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nadīyā prakāśe nityānanda-gauranidhi</strong><br>
<strong>patita-pāvana-kṣetre milāila vidhi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Nityānanda and Śrī Gaura Mahāprabhu have manifested eternally in Nabadwīp Dhām, Nadia. But now Their divine forms have manifested in Puri Dhām, mercifully given by our fortune-maker, Śrī Gurudev.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>avichintya nityānanda-chaitanya-prakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>śrī guru-prasāde dekhe ei adhama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Inconceivably Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Chaitanya have manifested, and through the mercy of Śrī Gurudev this fallen soul sees Their ārati and joins within the saṅkīrtan.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-jaya-gurudever-arati-ujjvala" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Guru Ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Glorification of Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h4>by Śrīpād B.A. Paramahaṁsa Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya jaya gurudever ārati ujjvala</strong><br>
<strong>govardhana-pāda-pīṭhe bhuvana-maṅgala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to the effulgent ārati of Śrī Gurudev, which is going on at the foot of Govardhan, benefitting the whole world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-bhakti-sundara deva prabhu śiromaṇi</strong><br>
<strong>gosvāmī govinda jaya ānandera khani</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Śrī Bhakti Sundar Govinda Goswāmī, who is our supreme master and is like a reservoir of joy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ājānu lambita bhuja divya kalevara</strong><br>
<strong>ananta pratibhā bharā divya guṇadhara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His graceful long hands touch his knees and his body is beautiful and transcendental. He is an ocean of infinite genius and transcendental qualities.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-kṛṣṇe jāni tava abhinna svarūpa</strong><br>
<strong>saṁsāra tarite ebe śuddha-bhakta-rūpa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">We consider your real identity to be non-different from Kṛṣṇa and Gaurāṅga. But now, to deliver this material world, you have assumed the form of a pure devotee.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpānuga-dhārā tumi kara ālokita</strong><br>
<strong>prabhākara sama prabhā bhuvana-vidita</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are brightening the line of the followers of Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī. Your effulgence, which is comparable to that of the sun, is known throughout the whole world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuddha-bhakti prachārite tomā sama nāi</strong><br>
<strong>akalaṅka indu yena dayāla nitāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There is no one like you to preach pure devotion. You are the spotless moon, and you are merciful like Lord Nityānanda.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ullasita viśva-vāsī labhe prema-dhana</strong><br>
<strong>ānande nāchiyā gāhe tava guṇa-gaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As ecstatic people all over the world are attaining the treasure of love of Kṛṣṇa, they are dancing and singing your glories.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sthāpilā āśrama bahu jagata mājhāre</strong><br>
<strong>pāramahaṁsa-dharma-jñāna śikṣāra prachāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To teach the essence of the highest knowledge and to preach it to all, you have established many ashrams all over the world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chintyāchintya veda-jñāne tumi adhikārī</strong><br>
<strong>sakala saṁśaya chhettvā susiddhānta-dhārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You have mastery over the knowledge of the Vedas, both conceivable and inconceivable. You are the treasurer of bona fide conclusions, which can destroy all doubt.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra mahimā gāhe goloka maṇḍale</strong><br>
<strong>nitya-siddha parikare tava līlā-sthale</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your glories are being sung in the spiritual world of Goloka, the realm of your Pastimes, by your servants who are eternally liberated.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>patita pāvana tumi dayāra samīra</strong><br>
<strong>sarva-kārye sunipuṇa satya-sugambhīra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To deliver the fallen souls, you are like a breeze of mercy. You are expert in all service, and you are established in truth and gravity.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>apūrva lekhanī-dhārā pragāḍha pāṇḍitya</strong><br>
<strong>sadā hāsya miṣṭa-bhāṣī suśīla kavitva</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your beautiful and unprecedented writing reflects your deep scholarship. You are always smiling and speaking sweet words, which remind one of your soothing poetry.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sādhu-saṅge sadānandī sarala vinayī</strong><br>
<strong>sabhā-madhye vaktā śreṣṭha sarvatra vijayī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are always straightforward and humble, and you are always joyful in the association of pure devotees. In a spiritual gathering, you are always the best speaker. In every field of life, you always come out victorious.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gauḍīya-gagane tumi āchārya-bhāskara</strong><br>
<strong>nirantara sevā-priya miṣṭa kaṇṭhasvara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the sky of the Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava world, you are a preceptor who is prominent like the sun. You are always fond of service, and your sweet voice inspires others to do service also.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra karuṇā māge trikāla vilāse</strong><br>
<strong>gāndharvikā-giridhārī sevā-mātra āśe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Past, present, and future – for all eternity, we beg your mercy. Only by your mercy will one receive devotional service to Śrī Śrī Gāndharvikā-Giridhārī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛpā kara ohe prabhu śrī-gaura-prakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>ārati karaye sadā e adhama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my lord, who is the manifestation of the mercy of Śrī Gaurāṅga, please be merciful to me. This insignificant servant of yours is always praying this way while worshiping you.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-guru-maharaja-jati-rajeshvara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Guru-ārati-stuti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h4>in glorification of Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya ‘guru-mahārāja’ yati-rājeśvara</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-bhakti-rakṣaka deva-gosvāmī śrīdhara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to you, ‘Guru Mahārāj,’<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;of sannyāsī kings, the Emperor:<br />the glorious Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Dev-Goswāmī Śrīdhar.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>patita-pāvana-līlā vistāri’ bhuvane</strong><br>
<strong>nistārilā dīna-hīna āpāmara jane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Extending in this world your<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Pastimes as saviour of the fallen,<br />you delivered all the suffering souls,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;including the most forsaken.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra karuṇā-ghana murati heriyā</strong><br>
<strong>preme bhāgyavāna jīva paḍe murachiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Beholding your holy form<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;of concentrated mercy<br />the fortunate souls fall in a swoon<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;of divine love’s ecstasy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sudīrgha supībya deha divya-bhāvāśraya</strong><br>
<strong>divya-jñāna-dīpta-netra divya-jyotirmaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your lofty form full of divine emotion<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;is nectar for our eyes;<br />with divine knowledge and shining eyes,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;your effulgence fills the skies.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>suvarṇa-sūraja-kānti aruṇa-vasana</strong><br>
<strong>tilaka, tulasī-mālā, chandana-bhūṣaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">A golden Sun resplendent,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;in robes of saffron dressed;<br />adorned with tilak, Tulasī beads,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;and with sandal fragrance blessed.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>apūrva śrī-aṅga-śobhā kare jhalamala</strong><br>
<strong>audārya-unnata-bhāva mādhurya-ujjvala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The matchless beauty of your holy form,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dazzling in its brightness;<br />your benevolence of most noble heart,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;in the moonglow of love’s sweetness.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>achintya pratibhā, snigdha, gambhīra, udāra</strong><br>
<strong>jaḍa-jñāna-giri-vajra divya-dīkṣādhāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Inconceivable genius, affection,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gravity, magnanimity – <br />a thunderbolt crushing mundane knowledge,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the reservoir bestowing divinity.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-saṅkīrtana-rāsa-rasera āśraya</strong><br>
<strong>“dayāla nitāi” nāme nitya premamaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the Golden Lord’s saṅkīrtan dance,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;you revel ecstatically;<br />“Dayāl Nitāi, Dayāl Nitāi!”<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;you ever call so lovingly.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sāṅgopāṅge gaura-dhāme nitya-parakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>gupta-govardhane divya-līlāra-vilāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Eternally present in Nabadwīp Dhām<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;with your associates, by your sweet will,<br />you enjoy your holy Pastimes<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;on hidden Govardhan Hill.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gauḍīya-āchārya-goṣṭhī-gaurava-bhājana</strong><br>
<strong>gauḍīya-siddhānta-maṇi kaṇṭha-vibhūṣaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You’re the fit recipient of your dignity<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;in the Gauḍīya Āchārya assembly,<br />the jewel of the Gauḍīya-siddhānta<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;adorning your chest so nobly.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-sarasvatī-sphūrta siddhāntera khani</strong><br>
<strong>āviṣkṛta gāyatrīra artha-chintāmaṇi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You’re the living message of Śrī Gaura,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;you’re the mine of perfect conclusions;<br />you revealed the Gāyatrī’s inner purport:<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the gem fulfilling all aspirations.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>eka-tattva varṇanete nitya-nava-bhāva</strong><br>
<strong>susaṅgati, sāmañjasya, e-saba prabhāva</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When you speak, one subject alone<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;is revealed in ever-new light;<br />proper adjustment, and harmony – <br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;all these are your spiritual might.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra satīrtha-varga sabe eka-mate</strong><br>
<strong>rūpa-sarasvatī-dhārā dekhena tomāte</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All your dear Godbrothers<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;declare unanimously<br />they see within your Holiness<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;the line of Rūpa-Saraswatī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tulasī-mālikā-haste śrī-nāma-grahaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>dekhi’ sakalera haya ‘prabhu’ uddīpana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Seeing you take the Holy Name,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;with Tulasī beads in hand,<br />awakens the thought of Saraswatī Ṭhākur<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;in everybody’s mind.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>koṭī-chandra-suśītala o-pada bharasā</strong><br>
<strong>gāndharvā-govinda-līlāmṛta-lābha-āśā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your feet, cooling like ten million moons – <br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;in their service all faith do I place;<br />the nectar of Rādhā-Govinda’s Pastimes<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;one day we may drink, by your grace.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>avichintya-bhedābheda-siddhānta-prakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>sānande ārati stuti kare dīna-dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O personification of perfect conclusions<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;inconceivably one, yet different!<br />I sing this prayer at your ārati,<br />&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;with joy – your humble servant.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-kali-kukkura-kadana-jadi-chao-he" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Prabhāti Gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kali-kukkura-kadana yadi chāo he</strong><br>
<strong>kali-yuga-pāvana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kali-bhaya-nāśana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-śachī-nandana gāo he</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O mind, you are like a miserable dog of Kali-yuga! If you want deliverance, then sing the Holy Name of the son of Śachī, for He is the saviour and destroyer of fear in this dark age of Kali.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gadādhara-mādana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitāi-era prāṇa-dhana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;advaitera prapūjita gorā</strong><br>
<strong>nimāi viśvambhara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrīnivāsa-īśvara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakata-samūha-chita-chorā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the transcendental beloved of Gadādhar, the life and soul of Nityānanda Prabhu, and the worshipable Lord of Śrī Advaita Āchārya. He is known as Nimai as He was born under a neem tree; Viśvambhara as He is the sustainer of the universe; He is the master of Śrīvās Ṭhākur, and He steals the hearts of all of the devotees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nadīyā-śaśadhara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;māyāpura-īśvara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāma-pravartana-śūra</strong><br>
<strong>gṛhī-jana-śikṣaka&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nyāsi-kula-nāyaka</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mādhava rādhā-bhāva-pūra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the moon of Nadia, the Lord of Māyāpur, and heroic saviour who inaugurated the chanting of the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa. He is the teacher of all householders and the commander-in-chief of all sannyāsīs. Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu is Kṛṣṇa, filled with the ecstatic heart’s sentiment of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sārvabhauma-śodhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gajapati-tāraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rāmānanda-poṣaṇa vīra</strong><br>
<strong>rūpānanda-vardhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sanātana-pālana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;haridāsa-modana dhīra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He purified Sārvabhauma Bhaṭṭāchārya and rescued Pratāparudra Mahārāj, the great king of Orissa. He is the hero and protector of Rāmānanda Rāy. He increases the ecstasy of Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī and He is the guardian of Śrīla Sanātan Goswāmī. He delights the sober Haridās Ṭhākur.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vraja-rasa-bhāvana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;duṣṭa-mata-śātana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kapaṭī-vighātana kāma</strong><br>
<strong>śuddha-bhakta-pālana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuṣka-jñāna-tāḍana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhala-bhakti-dūṣaṇa rāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya is absorbed in the mellows of Vṛndāvan. He removes the filthy thoughts of the mind, destroying lust and deceit. He drives away dry empiric knowledge which obscures the beauty of pure devotion. He is the protector, maintainer, and constant guardian of His pure devotees.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jashomati-nandana-vraja-vara-nagara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Nāma-kīrtan <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yaśomatī-nandana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vraja-vara-nāgara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gokula-rañjana kāna</strong><br>
<strong>gopī-parāṇa-dhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;madana-manohara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāliya-damana-vidhāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Kṛṣṇa is the beloved son of Mother Yaśodā, the transcendental lover of the land of Vraja, and the delight of Gokula. One of His nicknames is Kāna. He is the life and soul of the gopīs. He is the enchanter of Cupid and the conqueror of the Kālīya serpent.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;amala hari-nāma amiya-vilāsā</strong><br>
<strong>vipina-purandara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;navīna nāgara-vara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vaṁśī-vadana suvāsā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Holy Names of Kṛṣṇa are spotless and full of sweetness and beauty. Kṛṣṇa is the king of the forest of Vṛndāvan. He is the ever-fresh youth, an excellent dresser, and the best of lovers. He always plays sweet melodies upon His flute.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vraja-jana-pālana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;asura-kula-nāśana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nanda-godhana-rākhoyālā</strong><br>
<strong>govinda mādhava&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;navanīta-taṣkara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sundara nanda-gopālā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kṛṣṇa is the guardian of all who live in Vṛndāvan, and the destroyer of demons. He tends the cows and is known as Govinda, for He delights the cows and the senses. He is Mādhava, the husband of the goddess of fortune. He loves to steal butter, and He is the beautiful cowherd boy of Nanda Mahārāj.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yamunā-taṭa-chara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopī-vasana-hara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rāsa-rasika kṛpāmaya</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-rādhā-vallabha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndāvana-naṭa-vara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakati-vinodāśraya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Wandering on the banks of the Yamunā River, Kṛṣṇa stole the garments of the gopīs while they were bathing. He enjoys the mellows of the Rāsa Dance. He is the most merciful, beloved lover of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī. He is the great dancer of Vṛndāvan, and the supreme shelter of Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-shiachinandana-sura-muni-vandana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śachīnandana-vandanā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya śachī-nandana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sura-muni-vandana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhava-bhaya-khaṇḍana jaya he</strong><br>
<strong>jaya hari-kīrtana-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nartanāvartana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kali-mala-kartana jaya he</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya Mahāprabhu, the son of Śachī Devī. Great sages and demigods always sing His praise, and by doing so, the fear of repeated birth and death is cut to pieces. All glories to the kīrtan of the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa, for when one chants the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa and dances in ecstasy, the filth of this age of Kali disappears.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nayana-purandara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśvarūpa-snehadhara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśvambhara viśvera kalyāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>jaya lakṣmī-viṣṇu-priyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśvambhara-priya-hiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya priya kiṅkara īśāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Chaitanya delights all eyes with His charming beauty. He is the most dearly beloved Lord of all and the intimate brother of Viśvarūp. He brings the highest auspiciousness to everyone, for He floods the entire universe with the nectar of love of Godhead and thus saves all living beings. All glories to Lakṣmī Priyā and Viṣṇu Priyā Devī, who are very dear to Śrī Nimāi Paṇḍit. All glories to Their family servant, Īśāna.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-sītā advaita rāya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mālinī śrīvāsa jaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya chandraśekhara āchārya</strong><br>
<strong>jaya nityānanda rāya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gadādhara jaya jaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya haridāsa nāmāchārya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Sītā Ṭhākurāṇī and Śrī Advaita Āchārya, all glories to Mālinī Devī and Śrīvās Ṭhākur, and all glories to Āchārya Chandra­śekhara. All glories to Śrīla Nityānanda Prabhu, Śrīla Gadādhar Paṇḍit, and Śrīla Haridās Ṭhākur, the Nāmāchārya.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>murāri mukunda jaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;premanidhi mahāśaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya yata prabhu-pāriṣada</strong><br>
<strong>vandi sabākāra pāya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;adhamere kṛpā haya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakti sapārṣada-prabhupāda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Murāri Gupta, and all glories to Mukunda Datta. All glories to all the other eternal associates of Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu. By singing the glories of Śrīla Prabhupād and all those great souls who are eternal associates of Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu, even the lowest of men can obtain the highest treasure of love.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gurudeva-kripa-bindu-diya-kara-ei-dase" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Gurudeva! <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gurudeva!</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpā-bindu diyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kara’ ei dāse</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tṛṇāpekṣā ati dīna</strong><br>
<strong>sakala-sahane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bala diyā kara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nija-māne spṛhā-hīna</strong></p>
