<p class="translation">Gurudev, please give to this servant just one drop of mercy, and make me more humble than a blade of grass. Grant me the strength to endure all things, and make me free from personal honour.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sakale sammāna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karite śakati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deha’ nātha! yathā yatha</strong><br>
<strong>tabe ta’ gāiba&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-nāma sukhe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;aparādha habe hata</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Give me energy so that I can properly give respect and honour to all. Then I shall be able to happily chant the Holy Name, and my offences will go away.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabe hena kṛpā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;labhiyā e jana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛtārtha ha-ibe nātha!</strong><br>
<strong>śakti-buddhi-hīna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmi ati dīna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kara more ātma-sātha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my Lord! When will such mercy fall to this person. I am weak, bereft, and devoid of intelligence. Please allow me to be with you.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yogyatā-vichāre&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kichhu nāhi pāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra karuṇā sāra</strong><br>
<strong>karuṇā nā haile&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐diyā kā̐diyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇa nā rākhiba āra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If you examine me, you will find no qualities. Your mercy is everything to me. If you are not merciful unto me, I can only weep, and I will not be able to maintain my life.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-udilo-aruna-puraba-bhage" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Aruṇodaya-kīrtan <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Part One</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;udila aruṇa pūrava-bhāge</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dvija-maṇi gorā amani jāge</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakata-samūha la-iyā sāthe</strong><br>
(gorā)<strong> gelā nagara-vrāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Just before sunrise, as the eastern sky turned crimson, the jewel of the twice-born, Śrī Gaurahari, awoke. Taking His devotees with Him, He began the kīrtan, wandering through the countryside towns and villages.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘tāthai’ ‘tāthai’ bājala khola</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ghana ghana tāhe jhā̐jera rola</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;preme ḍhala ḍhala sonāra aṅga</strong><br>
(gorār)<strong> charaṇe nūpura bāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The mṛdaṅgas went “tāthai tāthai” to the rhyming chiming of the cymbals, as Mahāprabhu’s golden form shivered in ecstasy and His footbells jingled.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mukunda mādhava yādava hari</strong><br>
<strong>balare balare vadana bhari’</strong><br>
<strong>michhe nida-vaśe gela re rāti</strong><br>
<strong>divasa śarīra-sāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Mahāprabhu would call out to the still sleeping people, “Don’t waste your nights uselessly sleeping and your days decorating your bodies! Cry out the Names Mukunda, Mādhava, Jādava, and Hari!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>emana durlabha mānava-deha</strong><br>
<strong>pāiyā ki kara bhāvanā keha</strong><br>
<strong>ebe nā bhajile yaśodā-suta</strong><br>
<strong>charame paḍibe lāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“You have attained this rare human form of life. What are you doing? Don’t you care for this gift? If you don’t worship the darling of Mother Yaśodā now, then great sorrow awaits you at the time of death.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>udita tapana ha-ile asta</strong><br>
<strong>dina gela bali’ ha-ibe vyasta</strong><br>
<strong>tabe kena ebe alasa ha-i’</strong><br>
<strong>nā bhaja hṛdaya-rāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“With every rising and setting of the sun, a day passes and is lost. Why then do you remain idle, refusing to serve the Lord of the heart?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jīvana anitya jānaha sāra</strong><br>
<strong>tāhe nānā-vidha vipada-bhāra</strong><br>
<strong>nāmāśraya kari’ yatane tumi</strong><br>
<strong>thākaha āpana kāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Know for certain that this life is full of various miseries. Take shelter of the Holy Name as your only business.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa-nāma-sudhā kariyā pāna</strong><br>
<strong>juḍāo bhakati-vinoda-prāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>nāma vinā kichhu nāhika āra</strong><br>
<strong>chaudda-bhuvana-mājhe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Drink the pure nectar of the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa, and thus satisfy the soul of Ṭhākur Bhakti Vinod. There is nothing but the Name to be had in all the fourteen worlds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jīvera kalyāṇa-sādhana-kāma</strong><br>
<strong>jagate āsi’ e madhura nāma</strong><br>
<strong>avidyā-timira-tapana-rūpe</strong><br>
<strong>hṛd-gagane virāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To penetrate the darkness of ignorance and bless all souls within this universe, the beautiful Name of Kṛṣṇa has arisen like the shining sun.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jiv-jago-jiv-jago-gaurachanda-bole" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Aruṇodaya-kīrtan <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Part Two</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jīva jāga, jīva jāga, gorāchā̐da bale</strong><br>
<strong>kata nidrā yāo māyā-piśāchīra kole</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Gaurāṅga calls, “Wake up, sleeping souls! Wake up, sleeping souls! You have slept so long in the lap of the witch Māyā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse">(ār kata kāl ghumāibe?)<br>
(māyā-piśāchīra kole ār kata kāl ghumāibe?)<br>
(uṭha jīva, kṛṣṇa bhaja ār kata kāl ghumāibe?)</p>
<p class="translation">(“How much longer will you sleep? How much longer will you sleep in the lap of the witch Māyā? Arise soul! Serve Kṛṣṇa! How much longer will you sleep?)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhajiba baliyā ese saṁsāra-bhitare</strong><br>
<strong>bhuliyā rahile tumi avidyāra bhare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“You came into this world saying, ‘O my Lord, I will certainly worship You’, but having forgotten this promise, you have remained in great ignorance.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse">(bhuliyā rahile)<br>
(avidyāra mohe paḍe tumi bhuliyā rahile)</p>
<p class="translation">(“Having forgotten, you have remained there. You have fallen into the bewilderment of ignorance, and, having forgotten your promise, you have remained there.)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāre la-ite āmi ha-inu avatāra</strong><br>
<strong>āmi vinā bandhu āra ke āchhe tomāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“I have descended just to save you. Other than Myself, who else is your friend?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse">(eman bandhu ār ke āchhe?)<br>
(māyā ha-ite uddhārite eman bandhu ār ke āchhe?)</p>
<p class="translation">(“Who else is such a friend? Who else is such a friend to rescue you from Māyā?)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>enechhi auṣadhi māyā nāśibāra lāgi’</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāma mahāmantra lao tumi māgi’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“I have brought the medicine for destroying the illusion of māyā. Now pray for this Hari-nāma Mahāmantra and take it.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse">(māgiyā lao re)<br>
(sudhā-mākhā ei hari-nāma māgiyā lao re)</p>
<p class="translation">(“O pray and take it. O pray and take this Name of Hari which is coated with nectar.”)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakati-vinoda prabhura charaṇe paḍiyā</strong><br>
<strong>sei hari-nāma mantra la-ila māgiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Ṭhākur Bhakti Vinod fell at the lotus feet of Lord Gaurāṅga, and after begging for the Holy Name he received that Mahāmantra.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-samsara-davanala-lidha-loka" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Gurvāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Sanskrit composition by Śrīla Viśvanāth Chakravartī Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saṁsāra-dāvānala-līḍha-loka-</strong><br>
<strong>trāṇāya kāruṇya-ghanāghanatvam</strong><br>
<strong>prāptasya kalyāṇa-guṇārṇavasya</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I worship the lotus feet of the Divine Master, who is the ocean of auspicious qualities. Just to deliver all planes of life that are suffering in the forest fire of material existence, he, as the rain-cloud of mercy, showers down the waters of compassion.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mahāprabhoḥ kīrtana-nṛtya-gīta-</strong><br>
<strong>vāditra-mādyan manaso rasena</strong><br>
<strong>romāñcha-kampāśru-taraṅga-bhājo</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I worship the lotus feet of the Divine Master, whose heart is maddened in nectarean love for Śrīman Mahāprabhu by the grand congregational chanting of the Holy Name. Dancing, singing, and playing the khol and karatāls, etc., His body thrills with joy, trembles, and waves of tears flow from his eyes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-vigrahārādhana-nitya-nānā-</strong><br>
<strong>śṛṅgāra-tan-mandira-mārjanādau</strong><br>
<strong>yuktasya bhaktāṁś cha niyuñjato ’pi</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I worship the lotus feet of the Divine Master, who personally engages in many types of worshipful services such as dressing the deity and cleansing the Lord’s holy temple, and similarly engages the devotees (in his retinue).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chatur-vidha-śrī-bhagavat-prasāda-</strong><br>
<strong>svādv-anna-tṛptān hari-bhakta-saṅghān</strong><br>
<strong>kṛtvaiva tṛptiṁ bhajataḥ sadaiva</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I worship the lotus feet of the Divine Master, who fully satisfies the devotees of Śrī Kṛṣṇa with four types of nectarean prasādam foodstuffs that are chewed, sucked, licked, and drunk (that is, he brings about the eradication of mundanity and the awakening of the joy of love that develops from respecting prasādam), and he is thereby himself satisfied.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-rādhikā-mādhavayor apāra-</strong><br>
<strong>mādhurya-līlā-guṇa-rūpa-nāmnām</strong><br>
<strong>prati-kṣaṇāsvādana-lolupasya</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I worship the lotus feet of the Divine Master, whose heart is insatiably desirous of tasting the infinite Holy Names, Forms, Qualities, and Pastimes of Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava that are full of the sweetness of love divine.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nikuñja-yūno rati-keli-siddhyair</strong><br>
<strong>yā yālibhir yuktir apekṣaṇīyā</strong><br>
<strong>tatrāti-dākṣyād ati-vallabhasya</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I worship the lotus feet of the Divine Master, who is extremely dear to the Supreme Divine Couple who sport in the groves of Vraja, because he is greatly expert in those particular engagements that are performed by the sakhīs in order to serve the amorous Pastimes of the Divine Couple.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sākṣād dharitvena samasta-śāstrair</strong><br>
<strong>uktas tathā bhāvyata eva sadbhiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>kintu prabhor yaḥ priya eva tasya</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇāravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I worship the lotus feet of the Divine Master (who is the simultaneously inconceivably distinct and nondistinct manifest embodiment of the Supreme Personality of Godhead): all the scriptures sing the glories of his being the direct non-different form of the Supreme Lord Śrī Hari, and the pure saints also concede this; yet, he is the most beloved of the Lord.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yasya prasādād bhagavat-prasādo</strong><br>
<strong>yasyāprasādān na gatiḥ kuto ’pi</strong><br>
<strong>dhyāyaṁ stuvaṁs tasya yaśas tri-sandhyaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande guroḥ śrī-charaṇaravindam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I worship the lotus feet of the Divine Master, ever singing his glories and meditating upon him at the three junctions of the day. By his grace alone one gains the grace of the Lord, and if he is displeased, the soul has no shelter anywhere.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrīmad guror aṣṭakam etad uchchair</strong><br>
<strong>brāhme muhūrte paṭhati prayātnāt</strong><br>
<strong>yas tena vṛndāvana-nātha-sākṣāt</strong><br>
<strong>sevaiva labhyā januṣo ’nta eva</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who, during the Brāhma-muhūrta (ninty-six minutes before sunrise) with meticulous regularity wholeheartedly sings these eight prayers to the Divine Master, attains, upon leaving the body, the qualification to engage in the direct service of the Lord of Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-krishna-chaitanya-prabhu-jive-daya-kori" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Invocation to the book Śaraṇāgati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya prabhu jīve dayā kari’</strong><br>
<strong>svapārṣada svīya dhāma saha avatari’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bestowing mercy upon all souls, Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya Mahāprabhu descends with His associates and abode.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>atyanta durlabha prema karibāre dāna</strong><br>
<strong>śikhāya śaraṇāgati bhakatera prāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To distribute the most rare form of divine love, He teaches surrender, which is the life of the devotees:</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dainya, ātma-nivedana, goptṛtve varaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>‘avaśya rakṣibe kṛṣṇa’ – viśvāsa pālana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Humility, self-submission, embracing the Lord’s guardianship, maintaining the faith that Kṛṣṇa will certainly protect me,</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakti-anukūla mātra kāryera svīkāra</strong><br>
<strong>bhakti-pratikūla bhāva – varjanāṅgīkāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Commitment to engage only in activities that are favourable to devotion, and promising to reject any disposition unfavourable to devotion,</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ṣaḍ-aṅga śaraṇāgati ha-ibe yā̐hāra</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐hāra prārthanā śune śrī-nanda-kumāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The divine son of Nanda hears the prayers of those who surrender in this six-fold way.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpa-sanātana-pade dante tṛṇa kari’</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-vinoda paḍe dui pada dhari’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod places a straw between his teeth and falls before Śrī Rūpa and Śrī Sanātan, grasping their feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kā̐diyā kā̐diyā bale, āmi ta’ adhama</strong><br>
<strong>śikhāye śaraṇāgati karahe uttama</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Crying and crying, he prays, “I am so fallen! Please teach me surrender and uplift me.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhaja-re-bhaja-re-amar-mana-ati-manda" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Bhajan-gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaja re bhaja re āmār mana ati manda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(bhajan vinā gati nāi re)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(vraja-vane rādhā-kṛṣṇa bhajan vinā gati nāi re)<br>
(bhaja)<strong> vraja-vane rādhā-kṛṣṇa-charaṇāravinda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(jñāna-karma parihari’ re)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(śuddha rāga-patha dhari’ jñāna-karma parihari’ re)</p>
<p class="translation">O my mind, how foolish you are. Just worship the lotus feet of Rādhā and Krishna in the forest of Vraja. Without such worship there is no means of spiritual advancement. Give up all speculative knowledge and materialistic activities and just worship Rādhā and Krishna in the forests of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse">(bhaja)<strong> gaura-gadādharādvaita-guru-nityānanda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(gaura-kṛṣṇe abheda jene re)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(guru kṛṣṇa-priya jene gaura-kṛṣṇe abheda jene re)<br>
(smara)<strong> śrīnivāsa, haridāsa, murāri, mukunda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(gaura-preme smara smara re)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(śrīnivāsa, haridāse, gaura-preme smara smara re)</p>
<p class="translation">You should worship and serve Lord Gaura, Gadādhara, Advaita, and the original Spiritual Master, Lord Nityānanda. Knowing Lord Gaura and Lord Krishna to be the same, and knowing the Spiritual Master to be very dear to Krishna, you should remember the dear associates of Lord Chaitanya, such as Śrīvās Ṭhākur, Haridās Ṭhākur, Murāri Gupta, and Mukunda Datta. In deep love for Lord Gaura, you should remember the two great personalities Śrīvās Ṭhākur and Haridās Ṭhākur.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse">(smara)<strong> rūpa-sanātana-jīva-raghunātha-dvandva</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(yadi bhajan karbe re)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(rūpa-sanātane smara yadi bhajan karbe re)<br>
(smara)<strong> rāghava-gopāla-bhaṭṭa-svarūpa-rāmānanda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(kṛṣṇa-prema yadi chāo re)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(svarūpa-rāmānande smara kṛṣṇa-prema yadi chāo re)</p>
<p class="translation">You should remember the Goswāmīs of Vṛndāvan: Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Sanātan Goswāmī, Jīva Goswāmī, Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, and Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī. If you worship Lord Krishna, then you should remember the two great souls Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī and Sanātan Goswāmī. You should also remember Rāghava Paṇḍit, Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Swarūp Dāmodar Goswāmī, and Rāmānanda Rāya. If you really seek love of Krishna, then you must remember Swarūp Dāmodar Goswāmī and Rāmānanda Rāya.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse">(smara)<strong> goṣṭhī-saha karṇapura sena-śivānanda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(ajasra smara smara re)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(goṣṭhī-saha sena śivānande ajasra smara smara re)<br>
(smara)<strong> rūpānuga sādhu-jana bhajana-ānanda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(vraje vās yadi chāo re)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(rūpānuga sādhu-jane smara vraje vās yadi chāo re)</p>
<p class="translation">You should remember Śrīla Kavi Karṇapura and all his family members, who are all sincere servants of Lord Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu. You should also remember the father of Kavi Karṇapura, Śivānanda Sena. Always remember, always remember Śrī Karṇapura and his family. You should remember all those Vaiṣṇavas who strictly follow the path of Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī and who are absorbed in the ecstasy of bhajan. If you actually want residence in the land of Vraja, then you must remember all of the Vaiṣṇavas, who are followers of Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-vibhavari-shiesha-aloka-praveshia" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Vibhāvarī Śeṣa <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vibhāvarī śeṣa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āloka praveśa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nidrā chhāḍi’ uṭha jīva</strong><br>
<strong>bala hari hari&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mukunda murāri</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rāma kṛṣṇa hayagrīva</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The night has come to an end and the light of dawn is entering. O jīva-soul, arise and give up your sleep. Chant the Holy Names of Lord Hari, who is the giver of liberation; the enemy of the Mura demon; the Supreme Enjoyer; the all-attractive one; and the horse-headed incarnation, Hayagrīva.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nṛsiṁha vāmana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-madhusūdana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vrajendra-nandana śyāma</strong><br>
<strong>pūtanā-ghātana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kaiṭabha-śātana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya dāśarathi-rāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Hari incarnated as the half-man, half-lion, Nṛṣiṁha. He appeared as the dwarf-brāhmaṇa Vāmana, and as the killer of the Madhu demon. He is the beloved son of the King of Vraja, Nanda Mahārāj, and is blackish in complexion. He is the slayer of the Pūtanā witch, and the destroyer of the demon Kaiṭabha. All glories to Lord Hari, who appeared as Lord Rāma, the son of King Dāśarath.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yaśodā-dulāla&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;govinda gopāla</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndāvana-purandara</strong><br>
<strong>gopī-priya-jana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhikā-ramaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhuvana-sundara-vara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the darling of Mother Yaśodā; the giver of pleasure to the cows, land, and spiritual senses; and the protector of the cows. He is the Lord of the Vṛndāvan forest; the gopīs’ beloved; the lover of Rādhikā; and the most beautiful personality in all the worlds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rāvāṇāntakara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mākhana-taskara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopī-jana-vastra-hārī</strong><br>
<strong>vrajera rākhāla&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopa-vṛnda-pāla</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chitta-hārī vaṁśī-dhārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As Rāmachandra He brought about the end of the demoniac King Rāvaṇa; as Kṛṣṇa He stole the older gopīs’ butter; He stole the younger gopīs’ clothes while they were bathing in the Yamunā. He is a cowherd boy of Vraja and the protector of the cowherd boys. He steals the hearts of all and always holds a flute.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yogīndra-vandana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-nanda-nandana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vraja-jana-bhaya-hārī</strong><br>
<strong>navīna nīrada&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rūpa manohara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mohana vaṁśī-vihārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Kṛṣṇa is worshipped by the best of yogīs and is the son of Nanda. He removes all the fears of the inhabitants of Vraja. He is the colour of a fresh rain cloud, and His form is enchanting. When He wanders about, playing His flute, He looks very charming.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yaśodā-nandana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kaṁsa-nisūdana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nikuñja-rāsa-vilāsī</strong><br>
<strong>kadamba-kānana-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rāsa-parāyaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndā-vipina-nivāsī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the son of Yaśodā, and the killer of King Kaṁsa, and He sports in the Rāsa Dance among the groves of Vraja. Kṛṣṇa engages in this Rāsa Dance underneath the kadamba trees, and He resides in the forest of Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ānanda-vardhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-niketana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;phula-śara-yojaka kāma</strong><br>
<strong>gopāṅgaṇā-gaṇa-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chitta-vinodana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;samasta-guṇa-gaṇa-dhāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He increases the ecstasy of His devotees. He is the reservoir of all love and is the transcendental Cupid who uses His flowered arrows to increase the loving desires of the gopīs. He is the pleasure of the gopīs’ hearts and the abode of all wonderful qualities.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yāmuna-jīvana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keli-parāyaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mānasa-chandra-chakora</strong><br>
(hari)<strong> nāma-sudhā-rasa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gāo kṛṣṇa-yaśa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rākha vachana mana mora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Kṛṣṇa is the life of the River Yamunā. He is always absorbed in amorous Pastimes and He is the moon of the gopīs’ minds, which are like the chakora birds that subsist only upon moonlight. O mind, obey these words of mine and sing the glories of Śrī Kṛṣṇa in the form of these Holy Names, which are full of nectarean mellows.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-thakura-vaishnava-gana-kori-ei-nivedana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ṭhākura Vaiṣṇava-gaṇa <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ṭhākura vaiṣṇava-gaṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kari ei nivedana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mo baḍa adhama durāchāra</strong><br>
<strong>dāruṇa-saṁsāra-nidhi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe ḍubāila vidhi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keśe dhari’ more kara pāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O saintly Vaiṣṇavas, although I am very fallen and misbehaved, I nevertheless place this appeal before you. My destiny has immersed me in this fearsome ocean of repeated birth and death. Please grab me by the hair, and pull me to the opposite shore.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vidhi baḍa balavān&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā śune dharama-jñāna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadāi karama-pāśe bāndhe</strong><br>
<strong>nā dekhi tāraṇa leśa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yata dekhi saba kleśa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anātha kātare te̐i kānde</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My destiny is very powerful. I am not able to hear the knowledge of spiritual life, and I am always bound by the ropes of karma. I cannot see any way to escape my condition. I see only suffering. Helpless and stricken with grief, I cry.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kāma, krodha, lobha, moha,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mada, abhimāna saha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āpana āpana sthāne ṭāne</strong><br>
<strong>aichhāna āmāra mana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;phire yena andha jana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;supatha vipatha nāhi jāne</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am just like a blind person unable to see what path is auspicious or inauspicious. I am helplessly dragged from here to there by lust, anger, greed, bewilderment, madness, and pride.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nā la-inu sat mata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;asate majila chitta</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tuyā pāye nā karinu āśa</strong><br>
<strong>narottama dāse kaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhi’ śuni’ lāge bhaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tarāiyā laha nija pāśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My mind became immersed in the impermanent satisfaction of the senses, and I could not understand the real goal of life. For this reason I did not aspire to attain Your lotus feet. Narottam Dās says, “Now that I am able to see and hear the Vaiṣṇavas, I have become free from all fear.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hari-haraye-namah-krishna-yadavaya-namah" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Nāma-saṅkīrtan <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari haraye namaḥ kṛṣṇa yādavāya namaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>yādavāya mādhavāya keśavāya namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord Hari, O Lord Kṛṣṇa, I offer my obeisances to You, who are known as Yādava, Hari, Mādhava, and Keśava.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gopāla govinda rāma śrī madhusūdana</strong><br>
<strong>giridhārī gopīnātha madana-mohana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gopāla, Govinda, Rāma, Śrī Madhusūdan, Giridhārī, Gopīnāth, Madana-mohan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-chaitanya nityānanda śrī advaitachandra</strong><br>
<strong>gadādhara śrīvāsādi gaura-bhakta-vṛnda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Śrī Chaitanya and Nityānanda. All glories to the moonlike Śrī Advaita Āchārya. All glories to Śrī Gadādhar Paṇḍit and all the devotees of Śrī Chaitanya headed by Śrīvās Ṭhākur.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya rūpa sanātana bhaṭṭa raghunātha</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-jīva gopāla-bhaṭṭa dāsa raghunātha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Sanātan Goswāmī, Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, Gopāla Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, and Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei chhaya gosāñi kari charaṇa vandana</strong><br>
<strong>yāhā haite vighna-nāśa abhīṣṭa-pūraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my obeisances to the Six Goswāmīs. By offering them my obeisances all obstacles to devotion are destroyed and all spiritual desires are fulfilled.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei chhaya gosāñi yā̐ra mu̐i tā̐ra dāsa</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐-sabāra pada-reṇu mora pañcha-grāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am a servant of that person who is a servant of these six Goswāmīs. The dust of their holy feet is my five kinds of foodstuffs.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tā̐dera charaṇa sevi bhakta sane vāsa</strong><br>
<strong>janame janame mora ei abhilāṣa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This is my desire, that birth after birth I may live with those devotees who serve the lotus feet of these six Goswāmīs.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei chhaya gosāñi yabe vraje kailā vāsa</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-kṛṣṇa-nitya-līlā karilā prakāśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When these six Goswāmīs lived in Vraja, they revealed and explained the eternal Pastimes of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ānande bala hari bhaja vṛndāvana</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-guru-vaiṣṇava-pade majāiyā mana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Absorbing your mind in meditation upon the divine feet of the Spiritual Master and the holy Vaiṣṇavas, chant the Names of Lord Hari in ecstasy, and worship the transcendental realm of Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-guru-vaiṣṇava-pāda-padma kari āśa</strong><br>
(hari)<strong> nāma-saṅkīrtana kahe narottama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Desiring the lotus feet of Śrī Guru and the Vaiṣṇavas, Narottam Dās sings the saṅkīrtan of the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhaja-bhakata-vatsala-shri-gaurahari" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Bhoga Ārati Gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaja bhakata-vatsala śrī-gaurahari</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-gaurahari sohi goṣṭha-vihārī</strong><br>
<strong>nanda-yaśomatī-chita-hārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Just worship Śrī Gaurahari, who is always affectionate toward His devotees. He is the same Supreme Personality of Godhead, Kṛṣṇa, who sported in the cowherd pastures of Vraja and stole the hearts of Nanda and Yaśodā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>“belā halo, dāmodara, āisa ekhana</strong><br>
<strong>bhoga-mandire vasi’ karaha bhojana”</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Mother Yaśodā calls to Kṛṣṇa, “My dear Dāmodar, it is very late. Please come right now, sit down in the dining hall, and take your lunch.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nandera nirdeśe vaise giri-vara-dhārī</strong><br>
<strong>baladeva-saha sakhā vaise sāri sāri</strong></p>
<p class="translation">On the direction of Nanda Mahārāj, Kṛṣṇa, the holder of Govardhan Hill, sits down, and then all the cowherd boys, along with Kṛṣṇa’s elder brother, Śrī Baladeva, sit down in rows to take their lunch.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuktā-śākādi bhāji nālitā kuṣmāṇḍa</strong><br>
<strong>ḍāli ḍālnā dugdha-tumbī dadhi mochā-ghaṇṭa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">They are then served a feast of śuktā and various kinds of green leafy vegetables, then nice fried things, and a salad made of the green leaves of the jute plant. They are also served pumpkin, baskets of fruit, small square cakes made of lentils and cooked down milk, followed by thick yogurt, squash cooked in milk, and vegetable preparations made from the flower of the banana tree.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mudga-baḍā māṣa-baḍā roṭikā ghṛtānna</strong><br>
<strong>śaṣkulī piṣṭaka kṣīr puli pāyasānna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Then they receive fried squares of mung dal patties and urad dal patties, chapātīs, and rice with ghee. Next, sweets made with milk, sugar, and sesamum; rice flour cakes; thick cooked-down milk; cakes floating in milk; and sweet rice.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>karpūra amṛta-kelī rambhā kṣīra-sāra</strong><br>
<strong>amṛta rasālā, amla dvādaśa prakāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There is also sweet rice that tastes just like nectar due to its being mixed with camphor. There are bananas, and condensed milk which is nectarean and delicious. They are also served twelve kinds of sour preparations made with tamarinds, limes, lemons, oranges, and pomegranates.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>luchi chini sarapurī lāḍḍu rasāvalī</strong><br>
<strong>bhojana karena kṛṣṇa haye kutūhalī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There are purīs made with white flour and sugar; purīs filled with cream; laḍḍus; and dal patties boiled in sugared rice. Kṛṣṇa eagerly eats all of the food.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhikāra pakka anna vividha vyañjana</strong><br>
<strong>parama ānande kṛṣṇa karena bhojana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In great ecstasy and joy Kṛṣṇa eats the rice, curried vegetables, sweets, and pastries cooked by Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chhale-bale lāḍḍu khāya śrī-madhumaṅgala</strong><br>
<strong>bagala bājāya āra deya haribola</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kṛṣṇa’s funny brāhmaṇa friend Madhumaṅgal, who is very fond of laḍḍus, gets them by hook or by crook. Eating the laḍḍus he shouts, “Haribol! Haribol!” and makes a funny sound by slapping his sides under his armpits with his hands.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhikādi gaṇe heri’ nayanera koṇe</strong><br>
<strong>tṛpta haye khāya kṛṣṇa yaśodā-bhavane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Beholding Rādhārāṇī and Her gopī friends out of the corners of His eyes, Kṛṣṇa eats at the house of Mother Yaśodā with great satisfaction.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhojanānte piye kṛṣṇa suvāsita vāri</strong><br>
<strong>sabe mukha prakṣālaya haye sāri sāri</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After lunch, Kṛṣṇa drinks rose-scented water. Then all of the boys, standing in lines, wash their mouths.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hasta-mukha prakṣāliyā yata sakhā-gaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>ānande viśrāma kare baladeva-sane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After all the cowherd boys wash their hands and mouths, in great bliss they take rest with Lord Balarām.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jambula rasāla āne tāmbula-maśālā</strong><br>
<strong>tāhā kheye kṛṣṇachandra sukhe nidrā gelā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The two cowherd boys Jāmbula and Rasāla then bring Kṛṣṇa pān made with betel nuts, fancy spices, and catechu. After eating that pān, Śrī Kṛṣṇachandra then happily goes to sleep.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viśālākṣa śikhi-puchchha chāmara ḍhulāya</strong><br>
<strong>apūrva śayyāya kṛṣṇa sukhe nidrā yāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">While Kṛṣṇa happily takes His rest on an excellent bedstead, His servant Viśālākṣa cools Him with a fan of peacock feathers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yaśomatī-ājñā peye dhaniṣṭhā-ānīta</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-prasāda rādhā bhuñje haye prīta</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At Mother Yaśodā’s request the gopī Dhaniṣṭhā brings remnants of food left on Kṛṣṇa’s plate to Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī, who eats them with great delight.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>lalitādi sakhī-gaṇa avaśeṣa pāya</strong><br>
<strong>mane mane sukhe rādhā-kṛṣṇa-guṇa gāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lalitā Devī and the other gopīs then receive the remnants, and within their hearts they sing the glories of Rādhārāṇī and Kṛṣṇa with great joy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="17">
<h5>17</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-līlā eka-mātra yāhāra pramoda</strong><br>
<strong>bhogārati gāya ṭhākur bhakati-vinoda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Ṭhākur Bhakti Vinod, whose one and only joy is the Pastimes of Lord Hari, sings this Bhoga Ārati song.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse">(bhaja govinda govinda gopāla)<br>
(giridhārī gopīnātha nanda-dulāla)<br>
(bhaja govinda govinda gopāla)<br>
(nanda-dulāla jaya jaya śachī-dulāla)<br>
(śachī-dulāla jaya jaya gaura gopāla)</p>
<p class="translation">(Serve Govinda, serve Gopāl. Serve Giridhārī, Gopīnāth, Nanda Dulāl. Serve Govinda, serve Gopāl. All glories to Nanda’s son! All glories to Śachī’s son! All glories to golden Gopāl!)</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-mahaprasade-govinde-nama-brahmani" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Honouring Prasādam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>[Sanskrit]</h3>
<p class="translation"><i>The following verses are recited before honouring Prasādam:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>mahāprasāde govinde nāma-brahmaṇi vaiṣṇave</strong><br>
<strong>svalpa-puṇya-vatāṁ rājan viśvāso naiva yāyate</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“O King, one whose fortune is meagre never develops faith in mahāprasād, Govinda, the Name, the Vedas, or the Vaiṣṇavas.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhai-re-shorira-avidya-jal-jodendriya-tahe-kal" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Prasāda-sevāya Gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhāi re!</strong><br>
<strong>śarīra avidyā-jāla&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaḍendriya tāhe kāla</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīve phele viṣaya-sāgare</strong><br>
<strong>tāra madhye jihvā ati&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lobhamaya sudurmati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāke jetā kaṭhina saṁsāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O brother! The body is a net of illusion, and the material senses are one’s enemies; they throw the soul into the ocean of mundanity. Among the senses, the tongue is the most voracious and wicked. It is very difficult to conquer the tongue in this world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa baḍa dayāmaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karibāre jihvā jaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;svaprasāda anna dila bhāi</strong><br>
<strong>sei annāmṛta pāo&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-kṛṣṇa-guṇa gāo</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;preme ḍāka chaitanya nitāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kṛṣṇa is very merciful: to conquer the tongue He has given us His remnants. Accept this nectarean food, sing the glories of Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, and with love call out, “Chaitanya! Nitāi!”<br>
N.B. On Harivāsar-tithis (days on which devotees fast from grains) the above song beginning <i>Bhāi re! Śarīra avidyā-jāla</i> is not customarily sung because it mentions grain prasādam (anna).</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-eka-dina-shiantipure" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śāntipure Prasāda-sevana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="translation"><i>The following three compositions are also recited occasionally:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhāi re!</strong><br>
<strong>eka-dina śāntipure&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prabhu advaitera ghare</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dui prabhu bhojane basila</strong><br>
<strong>śāka kari’ āsvādana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prabhu bale, “bhakta-gaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ei śāka kṛṣṇa āsvādila</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O brother! One day in Śāntipur at the house of Advaita Prabhu, the two Lords sat down to eat. Tasting the śāk, the Lord said, “O devotees, Kṛṣṇa has tasted this śāk.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>“hena śāka āsvādane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-prema āise mane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei preme kara āsvādana</strong><br>
<strong>jaḍa-buddhi parihari’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prasāda bhojana kari’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘hari hari’ bala sarva-jana”</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“By tasting such śāk, divine love for Kṛṣṇa arises in the heart. With such love, taste this śāk. Everyone should give up mundane conceptions, take this prasād, and chant, ‘Hari! Hari!’”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhai-re-shiachira-angane-kabhu" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Nabadwīpe Prasāda-sevana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhāi re!</strong><br>
<strong>śachīra aṅgane kabhu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mādhavendra purī prabhu</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prasādānna karena bhojana</strong><br>
<strong>khāite khāite tā̐ra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āila prema sudurvāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bale, “śuna sannyāsīra gaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O brother! Śrīla Mādhavendra Purīpād once took prasād in Śrī Śachī Devī’s courtyard. As he ate, irresistible divine love arose within him and he said, “O sannyāsīs, listen!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>“mochā-ghaṇṭa phula-baḍi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ḍāli ḍālnā chachchaḍi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śachī-mātā karila randhana</strong><br>
<strong>tāra śuddhā-bhakti heri’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhojana karila hari</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sudhā-sama e anna-vyañjana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Mother Śachī has prepared banana flower curry, fried pumpkin flower, dal, vegetables in soup, and lightly charred vegetables. Seeing her pure devotion, the Lord has eaten them. These grains and vegetables are thus equal to nectar.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>“yoge yogī pāya yāhā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhoge āja habe tāhā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari bali’ khāo sabe bhāi</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇera prasāda-anna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;trijagata kare dhanya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tripurāri nāche yāhā pāi’”</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Today we will attain by eating, what yogīs attain through yoga. Brothers! Chant ‘Hari’ and eat! Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s prasād makes the three worlds fortunate, and Lord Śiva dances upon receiving it.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhai-re-eka-dina-nilachale-prasada-sevana-kale" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Nīlāchale Prasāda-sevana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhāi re!</strong><br>
<strong>eka-dina nīlāchale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prasāda-sevana-kāle</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mahāprabhu śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya</strong><br>
<strong>balilena bhakta-gaṇe,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;“khecharānna śuddha-mane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sevā kari’ hao āja dhanya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O brother! One day in Nīlāchal at the time of honouring prasādam, Mahāprabhu Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya said to the devotees, “Be fortunate today by honouring khichuḍi with pure hearts.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>“khecharānna piṭhā-pānā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;apūrva prasāda nānā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jagannātha dila tomā sabe</strong><br>
<strong>ākaṇṭha bhojana kari’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bala mukhe hari hari</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avidyā-durita nāhi rabe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Lord Jagannātha has given you khichuḍi, pancakes, sherbet, and various other types of wonderful prasād. Eat up to your neck and chant the Name of Hari! Ignorance and sin will disappear.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>“jagannātha-prasādānna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viriñchi-śambhura mānya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khāile prema ha-ibe udaya</strong><br>
<strong>emana durlabha dhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāiyāchha sarva-jana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya jaya jagannātha jaya”</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Lord Jagannātha’s prasād is honoured by Lord Brahmā and Lord Śiva. If you eat it, divine love will arise within you. You all have now attained such a precious treasure. Glory! Glory! Glory to Lord Jagannātha!”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-jaya-gaurachander-arotiko-shobha" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Gaura Ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse">(kibā)<strong> jaya jaya gorāchā̐der āratika śobhā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāhnavī-taṭa-vane jaga-mana-lobhā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(jaga-janer mana lobhā)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(gaurāṅger ārati śobhā jaga-janer mana lobhā)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(nitāi gaura haribol)</p>
<p class="translation">All glories, all glories to the beautiful ārati ceremony of worship to Lord Gaurachandra in a grove on the banks of the Jāhnavī River! It is attracting the minds of all living entities in the universe.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse">(kibā)<strong> dakṣiṇe nitāichā̐da bāme gadādhara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nikaṭe advaita śrīnivāsa chhatra-dhara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">On Lord Gaurachandra’s right side is Lord Nityānanda, and on His left is Śrī Gadādhar. Before Him stands Śrī Advaita, and Śrīvās Ṭhākur holds an umbrella over the Lord’s head.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse">(kibā)<strong> basiyāchhe gorāchā̐da ratna-siṁhāsane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(ratna-simhāsana alo karere)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(basiyāchhen gorāchā̐d ratna-siṁhāsane)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ārati karena brahmā ādi-deva gaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(gaurāṅger ārati kare)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(brahmā ādi-deva gaṇe gaurāṅger ārati kare)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(gaurāṅgera bhakta sane gaurāṅger ārati kare)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(nitāi gaura haribol)</p>
<p class="translation">Lord Chaitanya is seated upon a jewelled throne while the demigods headed by Lord Brahmā perform the ārati ceremony.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse">(kibā)<strong> narahari-ādi kari chāmara ḍhulāya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sañjaya-mukunda-vāsu-ghoṣa-ādi gāya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(gaura-guṇa gāna kare)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(sañjaya mukunda vāsu-ghoṣa gaura-guṇa gāna kare)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(nitāi gaura haribol)</p>
<p class="translation">Narahari Sarakār and other associates of Lord Chaitanya fan Him with whisks and the devotees headed by Sañjaya Paṇḍit, Mukunda Datta, and Vāsu Ghoṣa sing sweet kīrtan along with the other devotees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse">(kibā)<strong> śaṅkha bāje ghaṇṭā bāje bāje karatāla</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;madhura mṛdaṅga bāje parama rasāla</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(madhur madhur madhur bāje)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(gaurāṅger ārati kāle madhur madhur madhur bāje)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(śaṅkha bāje ghaṇṭā bāje madhur madhur madhur bāje)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(madhura mṛdanga bāje madhur madhur madhur bāje)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(nitāi gaura haribol)</p>
<p class="translation">Conchshells, bells, and karatālas resound, and the mṛdaṅgas play very sweetly. This kīrtan music is supremely sweet and relishable to hear.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse">(kibā)<strong> bahu koṭi chandra jini vadana ujjvala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gala-deśe vana-mālā kare jhalamala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(jhalmal jhalmal jhalmal kare)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(gaura-gale vana-phuler mālā jhalmal jhalmal jhalmal kare)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(bhakta-vṛṇder ratna-mālā jhalmal jhalmal jhalmal kare)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(nitāi gaura haribol)</p>
<p class="translation">The brilliance of Lord Chaitanya’s face conquers millions upon millions of moons, and the garland of forest flowers around His neck shines.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse">(kibā)<strong> śiva, śuka, nārada preme gada-gada</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakati-vinoda dekhe gorāra sampada</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Śiva, Śukadeva Goswāmī and Nārada Muni are all present and their voices are choked with the ecstasy of transcendental love. Thus Bhakti Vinod envisions the glorious worship of Lord Gaurachandra.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse">(ei bār āmāy dayā kara)<br>
(ṭhākura śrī-bhakti-vinoda ei bār āmāy dayā kara)<br>
(ṭhākura śrī-sarasvatī ei bār āmāy dayā kara)<br>
(patit-pāvan śrī-gurudev ei bār āmāy dayā kara)<br>
(sapārṣada gaurahari ei bār āmāy dayā kara)<br>
(nitāi gaura haribol)</p>
<p class="translation">(This time please give me your grace. O Śrī Bhakti Vinod, Śrī Saraswatī Ṭhākur, O Gurudev, saviour of the fallen, O Lord Gaurahari along with Your associates, this time please give me Your grace.)</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jayare-jayare-jaya-gaura-sarasvati" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Sāraswat Ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h4>Translated by Devamayī Devī Dasī of U.S.A.</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jayare jayare jaya gaura-sarasvatī</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-vinodānvaya-karuṇā-murati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories, all glories, all glories to Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur, who is intimately connected with Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur and is an embodiment of Śrī Gaurāṅga’s mercy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prakāśile gaura-sevā bhūvana-maṅgala</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-siddhānta-śuddha-prajñāna-ujjvala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You manifested devotional service to Lord Gaurāṅga, which is beneficial to the whole universe. Your pure devotional conclusions are effulgent with Absolute Knowledge.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-śyāma eka-tanu dakṣe gorā rāya</strong><br>
<strong>bāme rādhā madhye svayaṁ śyāma-gopa jaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Śrī Rādhā and Śrī Śyāma (Kṛṣṇa) who are combined in the one body of Śrī Gaurāṅga, who is on the right side. Glories to Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī, who is on the left side; and glories to Him, who is in the middle and who is none other than the Supreme Cowherd Boy, Śrī Śyāmasundar Himself.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vraja-rasa nava-bhāve navadvīpe rāje</strong><br>
<strong>udāre madhura-rāga abhinava sāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The mellow of Vraja (Vṛndāvan) is present in Nabadwīp in a new mood; when this sweet loving sentiment is mixed with magnanimity, then it shows itself as uniquely beautiful.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mādhurya-kaivalya-rāga vrajera niryāsa</strong><br>
<strong>prāpti parākāṣṭhā tāhe gaurāṅga-vilāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Mādhurya-rasa (conjugal love) is the essence of Vraja Dhām, and the Pastimes of Mahāprabhu take it to its highest perfection.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-bhāva-kānti aṅgikari’ bhāla-mate</strong><br>
<strong>dakṣiṇe āsana rasa-garimā dekhāte</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Earnestly accepting Śrī Rādhā’s mood and beauty, He has taken His place on the right to show His glorious position.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-rasa-traya-svāda rahasya prayāsa</strong><br>
<strong>nirakhi’ praphulla rādhā mukhe manda-hāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Seeing Kṛṣṇa’s mischievous desire to understand the glories of Her love, Kṛṣṇa’s wonderful sweetness that only She can relish through Her transcendental qualities, and the happiness She feels from Kṛṣṇa’s sweet love for Her, Rādhārāṇī is very pleased and there is a subdued smile on Her face.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>madhye rahi’ vaṁśī-rave ghoṣe vaṁśī-dhara</strong><br>
<strong>rādhāra sampade āmi gaurāṅga-sundara!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the middle, Vaṁśīdhara (the Flute Player) is playing His flute, thinking that, “Only by the wealth of Rādhārāṇī do I become Gaurāṅga Sundar.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mad-abhīṣṭha rūpa rādhāra hṛdaya-mandire</strong><br>
<strong>gaurāṅga bhajile suṣṭhu sphūrti pāya tāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“My most cherished form is revealed in the temple of Rādhārāṇī’s heart, and that highest manifestation of Myself can be attained only by worshiping Gaurāṅga.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nadīyā-prakāśe mahāprabhu gaura-nidhi</strong><br>
<strong>patita-pāvana deve milāila vidhi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By the Lord’s divine arrangement, the Supreme Savior of the fallen souls appeared in Nadīyā as the most beloved Śrī Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e-rūpa ārati brahmā-śambhu-agochara</strong><br>
<strong>gaura-bhakta-kṛpā-pātra mātra siddhi-sāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This type of divine worship of Mahāprabhu is beyond the vision of even Lord Brahmā or Lord Śiva. Only by receiving the mercy of a devotee of Lord Gaurāṅga can one attain such perfection.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī svarūpa, rāmānanda, rūpa, sanātana</strong><br>
<strong>śrī raghu, jīvādi-kṛpāya dekhe bhakta-jana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Only by the mercy of Śrī Svarūp Dāmodar, Śrī Rāmananda Rāya, Śrī Rūpa, Śrī Sanātan, Śrī Raghunāth, Śrī Jīva, and their associates do the devotees attain that vision.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya guru-gaura-rādhā-govinda-sundara</strong><br>
<strong>jaya dāo bhakta-vṛnda nitya nirantara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to Śrī Guru, Gaurāṅga, Rādhā-Govindasundar. O devotees, always sing Their eternal glories.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-kobe-shri-chaitanya-more-koribena-daya" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Dainya Prārthanā Gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabe śrī-chaitanya more karibena dayā</strong><br>
<strong>kabe āmi pāiba vaiṣṇava-pada-chhāyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O when will Śrī Chaitanya bestow upon me His grace? When will I get shelter in the shade of the holy feet of the Vaiṣṇavas?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabe āmi chhāḍiba e viṣayābhimāna</strong><br>
<strong>kabe viṣṇu-jane āmi kariba sammāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I give up the false ego of being an enjoyer and when will I offer all respects to the sincere devotees of Lord Viṣṇu?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gala-vastra kṛtāñjali vaiṣṇava-nikaṭe</strong><br>
<strong>dante tṛṇa kari’ dā̐ḍāiba niṣkapaṭe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I submit myself by the feet of the Vaiṣṇava with the cloth of surrender around my neck and with straw between my teeth?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kā̐diyā kā̐diyā jānāiba duḥkha-grāma</strong><br>
<strong>saṁsāra-anala haite māgiba viśrāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Weeping and weeping when will I submit my sorrowful tale at the feet of the Vaiṣṇava, and when will I seek relief from the fire of material suffering praying to that Vaiṣṇava for shelter and rest?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuniyā āmāra duḥkha vaiṣṇava ṭhākura</strong><br>
<strong>āmā’ lāgi’ kṛṣṇe āvedibena prachura</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Upon hearing the tale of my worldly sorrow, that saintly Vaiṣṇava will petition Kṛṣṇa on my behalf in prayer.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaiṣṇavera āvedane kṛṣṇa dayāmaya</strong><br>
<strong>e hena pāmara prati habena sadaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Then satisfied by that Vaiṣṇava’s prayer, Kṛṣṇa will be merciful upon this wicked and sinful wretch.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vinodera nivedana vaiṣṇava-charaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpā kari’ saṅge laha ei akiñchane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur submits this prayer at the lotus feet of the Vaiṣṇavas: “Give me your grace and accept this lowly soul in your association.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-namo-namah-tulasi-maharani" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Tulasī-parikramā Gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Braj Bhāṣā composition by Śrīla Chandraśekhara Kavi</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>namo namaḥ tulasī mahārāṇī</strong><br>
<strong>vṛnde mahārāṇī namo namaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Tulasī Mahārāṇī, O Vṛnda Devī, I offer my obeisances unto you again and again.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>namo re namo re mā̐iyā namo nārāyaṇī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Nārāyaṇi I offer you my obeisances again and again.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yā̐ko daraśe paraśe agha-nāśa hoi</strong><br>
<strong>mahimā veda-purāṇe vākhāni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By seeing or touching you, all sins are destroyed. Your glories are sung in the Vedas and Purāṇas.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yā̐ko patra mañjarī komala</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-pati-charaṇa-kamale lepaṭāni</strong><br>
<strong>dhanya tulasī pūraṇa tapa kiye</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-śālagrāma-mahāpāṭarāṇī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your leaves and soft mañjarīs are entwined at the lotus feet of Lord Nārāyaṇa. O Tulasī, by your performance of austerity you have become the worshipful consort of Śrī Śālagrām-śilā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhūpa, dīpa, naivedya, ārati</strong><br>
<strong>phulanā kiye varakhā varakhāni</strong><br>
<strong>chhāppānna bhoga, chhatriśa vyañjana</strong><br>
<strong>vinā tulasī prabhu eka nāhi māni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You shower your mercy upon anyone who offers you incense, lamp, foodstuffs, worship and flowers. The Lord does not accept even one of fifty-six food offerings or thirty-six curries offered without Tulasī leaves.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śiva śuka nārada āura brahmādika</strong><br>
<strong>ḍhu̐ḍata phirata mahāmuni jñānī</strong><br>
<strong>chandraśekhara mā̐iyā, terā yaśa gāoye</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati dāna dījiye mahārāṇī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Śiva, Śukadeva Goswāmī, Nārada Muni, all the demigods headed by Lord Brahmā, the mystics, and great munis are all circumambulating you, O Tulasī Mahārāṇī. Thus Chandraśekhara sings of your glories. Please bestow upon him the gift of devotion.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-guru-charana-padma-kevala-bhakati" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī-Guru-Vaiṣṇava Māhātmya Gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-guru-charaṇa-padma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kevala-bhakati-sadma</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vando̐ muñi sāvadhāna-mate</strong><br>
<strong>yā̐hāra prasāde bhāi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;e bhava tariyā yāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-prāpti haya yā̐hā haite</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The lotus feet of the Spiritual Master are the abode of pure devotional service. I bow down to those lotus feet with great care and attention. My dear brother (my dear mind) it is through the grace of the Spiritual Master that we cross over this material world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>guru-mukha-padma-vākya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chittete kariyā aikya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āra nā kariha mane āśā</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-guru-charaṇe rati&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei se uttama-gati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ye prasāde pūre sarva āśā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Make the teachings from the lotus mouth of the Spiritual Master one with your heart, and do not desire anything else. Attachment to the lotus feet of the Spiritual Master is the best means of spiritual advancement. By his mercy all desires for spiritual perfection are fulfilled.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chakṣu-dāna dilā yei&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;janme janme prabhu sei</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;divya-jñāna hṛde prakāśita</strong><br>
<strong>prema-bhakti yā̐hā haite&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avidyā-vināśa yāte</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vede gāya yā̐hāra charita</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He who has given me the gift of transcendental vision is my lord, birth after birth. By his mercy divine knowledge is revealed within the heart, bestowing loving devotion and destroying ignorance. The Vedic scriptures sing of his character.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-guru karuṇā-sindhu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;adhama-janāra bandhu</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lokanātha lokera jīvana</strong><br>
<strong>hā hā prabhu kara dayā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deha more pada-chhāyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;e adhama la-ila śaraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Spiritual Master, ocean of mercy and friend of the fallen souls, you are the teacher of everyone and the life of all people. O master! Be merciful unto me, and give me the shade of your lotus feet. This fallen soul has taken shelter of you.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse">(āmi)<strong> </strong>(śaraṇ nilām)<br>
(abhaya-śrī-pāda-padme śaraṇ nilām)<br>
(āmāy)<strong> </strong>(dayā kara he)<br>
(patit-pāvan śrī-gurudev dayā kara he)<br>
(adhama patita jene dayā kara he)<br>
(jay gurudev)</p>
<p class="translation">(I have surrendered, I have surrendered unto your fearless lotus feet. Please give your mercy. O Śrī Gurudev, saviour of the fallen, please give me your mercy. You are the saviour of the lowly fallen souls. All glories to you Śrī Gurudev.)</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-krishna-chaitanya-prabhu-daya-kara-more" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Sāvaraṇa Śrī-Gaura-pāda-padme Prārthanā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Verses 1 to 7 by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya prabhu dayā kara more</strong><br>
<strong>tomā vinā ke dayālu jagata mājhāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya Mahāprabhu, kindly give Your grace to me; in the whole creation, there’s none as merciful as You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>patita-pāvana-hetu tava avatāra</strong><br>
<strong>mo-sama patita prabhu nā pāibe āra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You descended just to save the fallen souls; O Lord, a soul as fallen as me is nowhere to be found.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hā hā prabhu nityānanda premānanda sukhī</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpāvalokana kara āmi baḍa duḥkhī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, O Lord Nityānanda ever in the joy of Your love; kindly give Your gracious glance, I am very sad.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dayā kara sītā-pati advaita gosāñi</strong><br>
<strong>tava kṛpā-bale pāi chaitanya-nitāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kindly give Your grace, Śrī Advaita Goswāmī, the Lord of Śrīmatī Sītā Ṭhākurāṇī; by the power of Your grace we may gain the service of Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Chaitanya.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-premamaya-tanu paṇḍit gadādhara</strong><br>
<strong>śrīnivāsa haridāsa dayāra sāgara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The embodiment of Śrī Gaura’s love, Paṇḍit Gadādhar; Śrīvās Paṇḍit, Haridās Ṭhākur, you are the ocean of grace personified.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hā hā svarūpa sanātana rūpa raghunātha</strong><br>
<strong>bhaṭṭa-yuga śrī-jīva hā prabhu lokanātha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my Lords, Śrī Svarūp Dāmodar, Śrī Sanātan, Śrī Rūpa and Śrī Raghunāth Dās, Śrī Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa. O my Lord Śrī Lokanāth Goswāmī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dayā kara śrī-āchārya prabhu śrīnivāsa</strong><br>
<strong>rāmachandra-saṅga māge narottama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please grant Your blessings O Lord Śrī Śrīnivās Āchārya, for the company of Śrī Rāmachandra Chakravartī, Narottam Dās does pray.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>dayā kara prabhupāda śrī-gaura-prakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>tava jana-kṛpā māge ei adhama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kindly give Your grace, Śrīla Prabhupād Saraswatī Ṭhākur, manifestation of Śrī Gaura. This fallen servant begs the mercy of your associates. <i>(Verse by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj)</i></p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-vaishnava-thakura-dayara-sagara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Vaiṣṇava-mahimā Gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse">(ohe)<strong> vaiṣṇava ṭhākura&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dayāra sāgara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;e dāse karuṇā kari’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;diyā pada-chhāyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śodhahe āmāre</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra charaṇa dhari</strong></p>
<p class="translation">(O) my Lord, O Vaiṣṇava, ocean of grace, kindly be gracious to this servitor, bestow the shade of your holy feet. Purify me. I clasp your holy lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chhaya vega dami’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhaya doṣa śodhi’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhaya guṇa deha’ dāse</strong><br>
<strong>chhaya sat-saṅga&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deha’ he āmāre</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;basechhi saṅgera āśe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Subjugate the six urges, rectify the six defects, bestow the six good qualities upon this servitor. Please grant me the six kinds of saintly association. I sit at your feet aspiring for that association.*</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ekākī āmāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāhi pāya bala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-nāma-saṅkīrtane</strong><br>
<strong>tumi kṛpā kari’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śraddhā-bindu diyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deha’ kṛṣṇa-nāma-dhane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">On my own I have no strength to chant the Holy Name in congregation. Kindly be pleased to bestow a drop of faith in my heart, giving me the treasure of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s Name.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa se tomāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa dite pāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra śakati āchhe</strong><br>
<strong>āmi ta’ kāṅgāla&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘kṛṣṇa’ ‘kṛṣṇa’ bali’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dhāi tava pāchhe pāchhe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kṛṣṇa is yours. You can give Kṛṣṇa. You have that power. I who am bereft am calling “Kṛṣṇa!” “Kṛṣṇa!” while running desperately behind you.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse">(āmi)<strong> </strong>(dhāi tava pāchhe pāchhe)<br>
(kṛṣṇa dhana pābar āśe dhāi tava pāchhe pāchhe)<br>
(tumi dileo kṛṣṇa dite pāra)<br>
(kṛṣṇa tomār hṛdayer dhan tumi dileo dite pāra)<br>
(kṛṣṇa debār śakti-dhara tumi dileo dite pāra)</p>
<p class="translation">(I run after you. With the hope of attaining the wealth of Kṛṣṇa, I run after you. If you desire to give, you can give Kṛṣṇa. Kṛṣṇa is the wealth of your heart, and if you desire to give, you can give. You have the power to give Kṛṣṇa, and if you desire to give, you can give.)<br>
*<i class="translation__note">Footnote to verse 2:</i><br>
<br>
The six urges are those of speech, the mind, anger, the tongue, the belly, and the genitals.<br>
The six faults are overeating or collecting more monetary funds than required, over-endeavouring for material objectives, unnecessary talking about mundane affairs, being too attached to or too neglectful of Scriptural rules and regulations, associating with worldly-minded persons, and lusting after mundane achievements.<br>
The six good qualities are enthusiasm, patience, confidence, the determination to perform activities favourable to devotional service, the resolution to abandon the association of non-devotees, and the discipline required to always follow in the footsteps of the predecessor āchāryas.<br>
The six kinds of saintly association are offering gifts in charity, accepting charitable gifts, revealing one’s mind in confidence, enquiring confidentially, accepting prasādam and offering prasādam.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-sujanarvuda-radhita-pada-yugam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Prabhupād-padma Stavaka <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Prayer unto the Lotus Feet of my Lord and Master</h3>
