Next, Lord Gaurāṅga’s body, unseen by the devotees, became as light as a piece of dried wood and was gracefully carried away by the wind while lying upon the crest of the ocean’s rolling waves in a divine trance. Realising Lord Kṛṣṇa’s water-sport Pastimes in the Kālindī River with His dearmost cowherd maiden friends, the Lord dove deep into an ocean of divine ecstasy. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Kṛṣṇa-prema.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="59">
<h5>59</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rātri-śeṣa-saumya-veśa-śāyitārdra-saikataṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhinna-sandhi-dīrgha-deha-pelavāti-daivatam</strong><br>
<strong>śrānta-bhakta-chakratīrtha-hṛṣṭa-dṛṣṭi-gocharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After searching for the Lord throughout the night, the exhausted devotees finally found Him at night’s end near Chakratīrtha, His elongated form lying upon the moist sand. The devotees’ eyes were filled with joy as they glanced upon the delicate and serene figure of their Lord. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Kṛṣṇa-prema.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="60">
<h5>60</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ārta-bhakta-kaṇṭha-kṛṣṇa-nāma-karṇa-hṛdgataṁ</strong><br>
<strong>lagna-sandhi-suṣṭhu-deha-sarva-pūrva-sammatam</strong><br>
<strong>ardha-bāhya-bhāva-kṛṣṇa-keli-varṇanāturaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As the anxious devotees began to loudly chant the Holy Names of Kṛṣṇa, this divine vibration entered the Lord’s ears and touched the depths of His heart. Immediately, the Lord’s dislocated joints reunited, and He displayed the beautiful figure which their eyes were accustomed to relishing. In a semiconscious state, the Lord began to describe with deep feelings of separation what He had seen while absorbed in divine trance. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Kṛṣṇa-prema.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-yamunambu-krishna-radhikali-keli-mandalam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="61">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Prema-dhāma-deva Stotram <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Friday</h3>
<h4>Sanskrit composition by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>61</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yāmunāmbu-kṛṣṇa-rādhikāli-keli-maṇḍalaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vyakta-gupta-dṛpta-tṛpta-bhaṅgi-mādanākulam</strong><br>
<strong>gūḍha-divya-marma-moda-mūrchchhanā-chamatkaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the sacred waters of the Yamunā in Vṛndāvan, Śrī Rādhā-Govinda, along with Their dearmost cowherd maiden friends, perform various charming water-sport Pastimes. These divine Pastimes are sometimes revealed, sometimes hidden, sometimes in opposition to each other, and sometimes harmonious. In this way the Lord’s Pastimes are couched in different gestures which bring about different types of divine hankering. The melodious tune of this confidential spiritual treasure which astounds the entire universe was distributed by Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Kṛṣṇa-prema.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="62">
<h5>62</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āsya-gharṣaṇādi-chāṭakādri-sindhu-līlanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhakta-marma-bhedi-tīvra-duḥkha-saukhya-khelanam</strong><br>
<strong>atyachintya-divya-vaibhavāśritaika-śaṅkaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Upon glancing at the Chāṭaka Hill, intolerable feelings of separation overwhelmed the Lord, and different Pastimes in the mood of profound adoration were incited, whereby the Lord began to rub His beautiful face on the ground. When Śrī Gaurāṅga would remember the water sport Pastimes of Lord Kṛṣṇa, He would jump into the ocean and exhibit the divine symptoms of one intoxicated by pure love. By performing these Pastimes, the Lord would inundate the devotees’ hearts with waves from the grand ocean of love of Kṛṣṇa. Those desperate waves carried both transcendental anxiety and joy up onto the shore of the devotees’ hearts. Such inconceivable auspiciousness was bestowed only upon the Lord’s most surrendered associates. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Kṛṣṇa-prema.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="63">
<h5>63</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrotra-netra-gatyatīta-bodha-rodhitādbhutaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-labhya-bhāva-siddha-chetanā-chamatkṛtam</strong><br>
<strong>brahma-śambhu-veda-tantra-mṛgya-satya-sundaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu is not only beyond the mundane experiences of sight and sound, but He also arrests all intellectual endeavours to know Him. Being situated in pure love of Godhead, He remains a mystery and astonishes even those living entities who are fixed in self-realisation. The revealed Vedas of Lord Brahmā and the Tantric literatures of Lord Śiva are simply searching after Śrī Gaurasundar, Reality the Beautiful. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Kṛṣṇa-prema.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="64">
<h5>64</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vipra-śūdra-vijña-mūrkha-yāvanādi-nāmadaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vitta-vikramochcha-nīcha-sajjanaika-sampadam</strong><br>
<strong>strī-pumādi-nirvvivāda-sārvavādikoddharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By freely distributing the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa, Śrī Gaurāṅgadev purified the scholarly intellectual class, the labourer class, the illiterate, and even the lowest sections of society. The Lord alone was the embodiment of the greatest wealth for all sincere and gentle souls, both the rich and the poor, the aristocratic, and the lowborn. Therefore, Lord Chaitanya is respected and honoured by men and women alike as the saviour of all, in both the mundane and spiritual worlds. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Kṛṣṇa-prema.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="65">
<h5>65</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sindhu-śūnya-veda-chandra-śāka-kumbha-pūrṇimā</strong><br>
<strong>sāndhya-chāndrakoparāga-jāta-gaura-chandramā</strong><br>
<strong>snāna-dāna-kṛṣṇa-nāma-saṅga-tat-parātparaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Like a golden moon, Śrī Gaurachandra appeared in the year 1407 Śakābda (1486 A.D.), at the onset of a lunar eclipse on a full-moon evening in the month of March. When the Absolute Truth Śrī Gaurāṅgadev incarnated in the beautiful courtyard of Śachī Devī, the residents of Māyāpur were bathing in the sacred Ganges, congregationally chanting the Holy Names of Hari, and making offerings of various types of jewels to the Lord. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Kṛṣṇa-prema.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="66">
<h5>66</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ātma-siddha-sāvalīla-pūrṇa-saukhya-lakṣaṇaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>svānubhāva-matta-nṛtya-kīrtanātma-vaṇṭanam</strong><br>
<strong>advayaika-lakṣya-pūrṇa-tattva-tat-parātparaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This is the all-conquering conclusion: the highest conception of the Ultimate Reality must also be the highest form of ānanda, ecstasy. Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu is Kṛṣṇa, ecstasy Himself, tasting His own sweetness and dancing in ecstatic joy. His own Holy Name is the cause of His ecstasy, expressed as dancing, and the Holy Name is the effect of His ecstasy, expressed as chanting. The cause is the effect. The dynamo is creating ecstatic energy which makes Him dance, and His chanting distributes that ecstasy to others. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Kṛṣṇa-prema.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="67">
<h5>67</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-purīśvarānukampi-labdha-dīkṣa-daivataṁ</strong><br>
<strong>keśavākhya-bhāratī-sakāśa-keśa-rakṣitam</strong><br>
<strong>mādhavānudhī-kiśora-kṛṣṇa-sevanādaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Īśvara Purī became very fortunate when Lord Gaurāṅga exhibited His causeless mercy by accepting initiation from him. Śrī Keśava Bhāratī was similarly favoured in that, in his presence, Lord Gaurāṅga shaved His beautiful curly hair and accepted from him the garments of a renunciant. Lord Chaitanya gave undivided attention to Śrī Mādhavendra Purī, stating that his attachment for serving Lord Kṛṣṇa’s kiśora form in conjugal love was the highest expression of dedication. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Kṛṣṇa-prema.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="68">
<h5>68</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sindhu-bindu-veda-chandra-śāka-phālgunoditaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>nyāsa-soma-netra-veda-chandra-śāka-bodhitam</strong><br>
<strong>vāṇa-vāṇa-veda-chandra-śāka-lochanāntaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respects unto Śrī Gaurāṅgadev, who rose like a golden moon in Śrī Māyāpur in 1407 Śakābda (A.D. 1486), revealed the Pastime of accepting the garments of the renounced order, sannyās, in 1431 Śakābda in order to benedict the entire material universe, and disappeared beyond the ordinary vision of the people of this world in 1455 Śakābda. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Kṛṣṇa-prema.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="69">
<h5>69</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-svarūpa-rāya-saṅga-harṣa-śeṣa-ghoṣaṇaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śikṣaṇāṣṭakākhya-kṛṣṇa-kīrtanaika-poṣaṇam</strong><br>
<strong>prema-nāma-mātra-viśva-jīvanaika-sambharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">With great joy Śrī Gaurasundar told His dearmost associates Śrī Svarūp Dāmodar and Rāmānanda Rāya that the process of congre­gationally chanting the Holy Names of Kṛṣṇa bestows the highest benediction upon the living entities in this age of Kali:</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>harṣe prabhu kohena, “śuno svarūpa-rāma-rāya</strong><br>
<strong>nāma saṅkīrtana kalau, parama upāya”</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In His famous composition Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam also, Lord Gaurāṅga has placed the chanting of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s Names on the topmost level of importance. The Supreme Lord further taught that chanting Lord Kṛṣṇa’s Names in pure love of Godhead perfectly nourishes and fully satisfies all the living entities in the universe. I offer my obeisances to that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, the divine form of Kṛṣṇa-prema.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="70">
<h5>70</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prema hema-deva dehi dāsareṣa manyatāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>kṣamyatāṁ mahāparādha-rāśireṣa gaṇyatām</strong><br>
<strong>rūpa-kiṅkareṣu rāmānanda-dāsa-sambharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prema-dhāma-devam eva naumi gaura-sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my Golden Lord! (Suvarṇa-varṇa Hemāṅga!) O ocean of prema! Kindly grant me the wealth of Your divine love! Please give some slight attention to this fallen soul and pardon his unlimited offences. There are so many servants of Śrī Rūpa who are so dear to You. My only aspiration is that You count me as one of those servitors. O Gaurasundar, You are the sole protector and embodiment of good fortune for me, Rāmānanda Dās. I offer my obeisances to you, O Gaurāṅga Sundar, my most beautiful Golden Lord, the divine form of Kṛṣṇa-prema.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="71">
<h5>71</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saśraddhaḥ sapta-daśakaṁ prema-dhāmeti-nāmakam</strong><br>
<strong>stavaṁ ko ’pi paṭhan gauraṁ rādhā-śyāma-mayaṁ vrajet</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Whoever faithfully recites this composition of seventy prayers known as the <i>Śrī Śrī Prema-dhāma-deva-stotram</i> will be granted the opportunity to serve that beautiful Golden Lord, Gaurāṅga Sundar, who is none other than Śyāmasundar Himself covered by the heart and halo (bhāva and kānti) of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="72">
<h5>72</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pañchame śata-gaurābde śrī-siddhānta-sarasvatī</strong><br>
<strong>śrīdharaḥ ko ’pi tach chhiṣyas tridaṇḍī nauti sundaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">These prayers have been composed in the fifth century after the appearance of Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu by a certain sannyās disciple of Śrīla Saraswatī Ṭhākur named Śrīdhar (Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj).</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Kirtans</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-gurudeva-kabe-tava-karuna-prakashie" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Gurudeva! Kabe Tava Karuṇā-prakāśe <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gurudeva! kabe tava karuṇā-prakāśe</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-gaurāṅga-līlā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;haya nitya-tattva</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ei dṛḍha viśvāse</strong><br>
<strong>‘hari’ ‘hari’ bali’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;godruma-kānane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhramiba darśana-āśe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gurudev! By a manifestation of your mercy and with firm faith that Śrī Gaurāṅga’s līlā is an eternal reality, when will I wander amidst the forest of Godruma calling out, “Hari!” “Hari!” with the hope of darśan?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nitāi, gaurāṅga,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;advaita, śrīvāsa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gadādhara pañcha-jana</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-nāma-rase&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāsābe jagat</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kari’ mahā-saṅkīrtana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Nitāi, Gaurāṅga, Advaita, Śrīvās, and Gadādhar – these five will inundate the world with the rasa of Kṛṣṇa’s Name by performing grand saṅkīrtan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nartana-vilāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mṛdaṅga-vādana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuniba āpana-kāṇe</strong><br>
<strong>dekhiyā dekhiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se līlā-mādhurī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāsiba premera vāne</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will hear the sounds of the mṛdaṅgas and the play of Their dancing with my own ears. Beholding the beauty of this līlā, I will float in a flood of prema.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nā dekhi’ ābāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se līlā-ratana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐di ‘hā gaurāṅga!’ bali’</strong><br>
<strong>āmāre viṣayī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘pāgala’ baliyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;aṅgete dibeka dhūli</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Again, not seeing those jewel-like Pastimes before me, I cry, calling out, “O Gaurāṅga!” Considering me mad, the worldly will kick dust on my body.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gurudeva-kabe-mora-sei-dina" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Gurudeva! Kabe Mora Sei Dina Habe <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gurudeva! kabe mora sei dina habe</strong><br>
<strong>mana sthira kari’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirjane basiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-nāma gāba yabe</strong><br>
<strong>saṁsāra-phukāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāṇe nā paśibe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deha-roga dūre rabe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Gurudev! When will that day be mine, when I will steady my mind and, sitting in a solitary place, chant Kṛṣṇa’s Name? Then the caco­phony of material existence will no longer enter my ears, and the disease of the body will remain far away.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘hare kṛṣṇa’ bali’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gāhite gāhite</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nayane vahibe lora</strong><br>
<strong>dehete pulaka&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;udita ha-ibe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;premete karibe bhora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Absorbed in constantly calling out, “Hare Kṛṣṇa” as I chant, tears will flow from my eyes, horripilation will appear on my body, and I will become overwhelmed with prema.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gada-gada vāṇī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mukhe vāhiribe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐pibe śarīra mama</strong><br>
<strong>gharma muhur-muhuḥ&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vivarṇa ha-ibe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;stambhita pralaya-sama</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Faltering speech will come from my mouth. My body will tremble, perspire again and again, turn pale, and become stunned as though devastated.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>niṣkapaṭe hena&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;daśā kabe habe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirantara nāma gāba</strong><br>
<strong>āveśe rahiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deha-yātrā kari’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra karuṇā pāba</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I genuinely reach such a state? Then I will chant the Name continuously. Remaining in ecstasy as I maintain the body, I will attain your mercy.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-thakura-vaishnava-pada-avanira" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ṭhākura Vaiṣṇava-pada <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ṭhākura vaiṣṇava-pada&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avanīra susampada</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuna bhāi hañā eka mana</strong><br>
<strong>āśraya la-iyā bhaje&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāre kṛṣṇa nāhi tyaje</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āra saba mare akāraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O brother, hear my words with rapt attention: the lotus feet of the Vaiṣṇavas are the most valuable treasure in the world. Take shelter of those Vaiṣṇavas, and worship them. Kṛṣṇa cannot abandon one who worships Him in this way. All others meet a meaningless death.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaiṣṇava-charaṇa-jala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-bhakti dite bala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āra keha nahe balavanta</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇava-charaṇa-renu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mastake bhūṣaṇa vinu</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āra nāhi bhūṣaṇera anta</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The water which has washed the lotus feet of the Vaiṣṇavas bestows devotional service in pure love of Godhead. There is nothing as effective in attaining this divine love. I place the dust from the lotus feet of the Vaiṣṇavas upon my head. I wear no other ornament.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tīrtha-jala-pavitra-guṇe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;likhiyāchhe purāṇe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se saba bhaktira pravañchana</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇavera pādodoka&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sama nahe ei saba</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yāte haya vāñchhita pūraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The purifying power of the waters of the various places of pilgrimage is described in the Purāṇas, although this is something of a deceptive trick. Actually there is nothing which is as purifying as the water which has washed the lotus feet of the Vaiṣṇavas. This water fulfils all desires.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaiṣṇava-saṅgete mana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānandita anukṣaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā haya kṛṣṇa-parasaṅga</strong><br>
<strong>dīna narottama kānde&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hiyā dhairya nāhi bāndhe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mora daśā kena ha-ila bhaṅga</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Moment by moment my mind finds constant pleasure in the association of the Vaiṣṇavas. I always seek the company of the devotees of Lord Kṛṣṇa. Poor-hearted Narottam Dās cries, “I cannot maintain my composure any longer. Why have I fallen into such a low condition of life that I cannot get the association of the Vaiṣṇavas?”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-je-anilo-prema-dhana-karuna-prachura" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Viraha-gīti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Song of Separation</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ye ānila prema-dhana karuṇā prachura</strong><br>
<strong>hena prabhu kothā gelā āchārya ṭhākura</strong></p>
<p class="translation">That personality who delivered the treasure of prema-bhakti, who was so intense with compassion – where is such a personality to be found as Śrī Āchārya Ṭhākur (Śrīnivās Āchārya)?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kā̐hā mora svarūpa rūpa kā̐hā sanātana</strong><br>
<strong>kā̐hā dāsa raghunātha patita-pāvana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Where are the saviours of the fallen souls? Where is my Svarūp Dāmodar, and where are Rūpa Goswāmī and Sanātan Goswāmī? Where is Raghunāth Dās to be found?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kā̐hā mora bhaṭṭa-yuga kā̐hā kavirāja</strong><br>
<strong>eka-kāle kothā gelā gorā naṭa-rāja</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Where are my Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa and Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmīs? Where am I to find Śrī Kṛṣṇadās Kavirāj now? All at once they have gone to join Lord Gaurāṅga, the great dancer.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pāṣāṇe kuṭiba māthā anale paśiba</strong><br>
<strong>gaurāṅga guṇera nidhi kothā gele pāba</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To reach such a perfect personality as Lord Chaitanya, I can only break my head against a stone in the anguish of separation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>se saba saṅgīra saṅge ye kaila vilāsa</strong><br>
<strong>se saṅga nā pāñā kānde narottama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">They have all gone off together in their own Pastimes. Narottam Dās Ṭhākur says: “Unable to obtain their association, I must simply weep.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ei-vara-karuna-kara-vaishnava-gosani" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Vaiṣṇava Vijñāpti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei-bāra karuṇā kara vaiṣṇava gosāñi</strong><br>
<strong>patita-pāvana tomā vine keha nāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Vaiṣṇava Goswāmī, please be merciful to me this time. You are the saviour of the fallen; without you there is no one.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kāhāra nikaṭe gele pāpa dūre yāya</strong><br>
<strong>emana dayāla prabhu kebā kothā pāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Just by being in your presence, sins go far away. Where can anyone find such kindness?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaṅgāra paraśa haile paśchāte pāvana</strong><br>
<strong>darśane pavitra kara – ei tomāra guṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Merely by the touch of the waters of the sacred Ganges one becomes liberated, even if he is the lowest of mankind; but just by seeing you, the same effect is achieved. Such is your quality.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-sthāne aparādhe tāre hari-nāma</strong><br>
<strong>tomā-sthāne aparādhe nāhika eḍāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If one commits an offence at the feet of Lord Hari, he can be forgiven if he chants the Holy Name. But if one offends you, there is no salvation for him.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra hṛdaye sadā govinda-viśrāma</strong><br>
<strong>govinda kahena mama vaiṣṇava parāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In your heart Govinda is always resting. Śrī Govinda Himself says, “My devotees are My life and soul.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prati janme kari āśā charaṇera dhūli</strong><br>
<strong>narottame kara dayā āpanāra bali’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I hope that in every birth I will obtain the dust of your lotus feet. Śrī Narottam prays, “Please, O Vaiṣṇava Goswāmī, be kind unto me.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-kripa-kara-vaishnava-thakura" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Prārthanā Lālasāmayī <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛpa kara vaiṣṇava ṭhākura</strong><br>
<strong>sambandha jāniyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhajite bhajite,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;abhimāna hau dūra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please give your mercy to me now, oh revered pure devotee, Vaiṣṇava Ṭhākur! Then only, my false ego will go far away by my constant worship in full knowledge of my real eternal position.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘ami ta vaiṣṇava’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;e buddhi haile,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;amānī nā ha’ba āmi</strong><br>
<strong>pratiṣṭhāśā āsi’,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛdaya dūṣibe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;haiba nirayagāmī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If I falsely think that ‘I am a Vaiṣṇava’, then I shall look forward to receiving respect from others. And if the desire for fame and reputation pollutes my heart, then certainly I shall go to live in hell instantly.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra kiṅkara,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āpane jāniba,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘guru’-abhimāna tyaji’</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra uchchhiṣṭha,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;padajala-reṇu,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā niṣkapaṭe bhaji</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Renouncing the false conception that I am a ‘guru’, I will understand myself to be your humble servant. I sincerely worship the remnants of your food, oh pure devotee, as well as the water that has washed the sacred dust of your lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘nije sreṣṭha’ jāni’,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uchchhiṣṭhādi dāne,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;habe abhimāna bhāra</strong><br>
<strong>tāi śiṣya tava,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;thākiyā sarvadā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā laiba pūjā kā’ra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By giving others the remnants of my food, I shall consider myself superior and shall be burdened with the weight of false pride. Therefore, always remaining your surrendered disciple, I shall not accept worship from anyone.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>amānī mānada,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;haile kīrtane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;adhikāra dibe tumi</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra charaṇe,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;niṣkapaṭe āmi,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐diyā luṭiba bhūmi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please bestow upon me the qualities of not desiring honour, of giving honour to others, and thus the capacity to chant the Holy Name. Then, at your feet, I will sincerely weep in ecstasy while rolling on the ground.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nadiya-godrume-nityananda-mahajana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ājñā Ṭahal <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nadīyā-godrume nityānanda mahājana</strong><br>
<strong>pātiyāchhe nāma-haṭṭa jivera kāraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the land of Nadīyā, on the island of Godruma, the magnanimous Lord Nityānanda has opened up the marketplace of the Holy Name, meant for the deliverance of all fallen souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse">(śraddhāvān jana he, śraddhāvān jana he)<br>
<strong>prabhura ājñāya, bhāi, māgi ei bhikṣā</strong><br>
<strong>bala ‘kṛṣṇa,’ bhaja kṛṣṇa, kara kṛṣṇa-śikṣā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">(O people of faith! O people of faith!)<br />By the order of Lord Gaurāṅga, O brothers, I beg these three requests of you: Chant “Kṛṣṇa!”, worship Kṛṣṇa, and teach others about Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aparādha-śūnya ha’ye laha kṛṣṇa-nāma</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa mātā, kṛṣṇa pita, kṛṣṇa dhana-prāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Being careful to remain free of offences, just take the Holy Name of Lord Kṛṣṇa. Kṛṣṇa is your mother, Kṛṣṇa is your father, and Kṛṣṇa is the treasure of your life.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇera saṁsāra kara chhāḍi’ anāchāra</strong><br>
<strong>jīve dayā, kṛṣṇa-nāma – sarva-dharma-sāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Giving up all sinful activities, carry on your worldly duties only in relation to Lord Kṛṣṇa. The showing of compassion to other souls by loudly chanting the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa is the essence of all forms of religion.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nitai-pada-kamala-koti-chandra-sushitala" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Manaḥ Śikṣā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nitāi-pada-kamala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;koṭī-chandra-suśītala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ye chhāyāya jagata juḍāya</strong><br>
<strong>hena nitāi vine bhāi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-kṛṣṇa pāite nāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dṛḍha kari’ dhara nitāira pāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The holy lotus feet of Nitāi, cooling like ten million moons – in the shade of which the world gains soothing relief. Without that Nitāi, O brother, no one can reach Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa – clasp His lotus feet with full conviction.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>se sambandha nāhi yāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛthā janma gela tāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei paśu baḍa durāchāra</strong><br>
<strong>nitāi nā balila mukhe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;majila saṁsāra-sukhe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vidyā-kule ki karibe tāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who never got His connection, passed his life in vain – such an animal is so very wicked. He whose mouth never uttered ‘Nitāi,’ merged in mundane pleasures – how can his aristocratic lineage save him?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ahaṅkāre matta haiñā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitāi-pada pāsariyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;asatyere satya kari’ māni</strong><br>
<strong>nitāiyer karuṇā habe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vraje rādhā-kṛṣṇa pābe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dhara nitāiyer charaṇa du’khāni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Maddened with pride, forgetting the lotus feet of Nitāi – I embrace untruth as truth; but when Nitāi gives His grace you’ll reach Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa in Vraja – O embrace the lotus feet of Nitāi!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nitāiyer charaṇa satya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐hāra sevaka nitya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitāi-pada sadā kara āśa</strong><br>
<strong>narottama baḍa duḥkhī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitāi more kara sukhī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rākha rāṅgā-charaṇera pāśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The lotus feet of Nitāi are truth, His servitors are eternal – ever aspire for the shelter of His feet. Narottam is very sad – O Nitāi make me happy – please keep me at Your lovely lotus feet!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-dhana-mora-nityananda-pati-mora" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Sva-niṣṭha <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhana mora nityānanda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pati mora gaurachandra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇa mora jugala-kiśora</strong><br>
<strong>advaita āchārya bala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gadādhara mora kula</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;narahari vilasai mora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Nityānanda is my wealth. Lord Gaurachandra is my master. The youthful Divine Couple are my life. Advaita Āchārya is my strength. Gadādhar is my family. Narahari Sarakara is my glory.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaiṣṇavera pada-dhūli&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe mora snāna-keli</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tarpaṇa mora vaiṣṇavera nāma</strong><br>
<strong>vichāra koriyā mane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakti-rasa āsvādane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;madhyastha śrī-bhāgavata purāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The dust of the devotees’ lotus feet is my bathing water. The chanting of the devotees’ names is my satisfaction. Considering the merits of all Vedic literatures in the light of devotional service, I have conclud­ed that the <i>Śrīmad Bhāgavatam</i> is the best of all scriptures.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaiṣṇavera uchiṣṭa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe mora mano-niṣṭha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vaiṣṇavera nāmete ullāsa</strong><br>
<strong>vṛndāvane chaturā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe mora mano-gherā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kahe dīne narottama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My mind is firmly convinced of the spiritual benefit obtained by eating the remnants of foodstuff left by the devotees. The names of the devotees are my happiness. The land of Vṛndāvan is the enclosure within which I keep my mind. Poor-hearted Narottam Dās speaks in this way.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nitai-gunamani-amara-nitai-gunamani" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Nitāi Guṇamaṇi Āmāra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Lochan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nitāi guṇamaṇi āmāra nitāi guṇamaṇi</strong><br>
<strong>āniyā premera vanyā bhāsāla avanī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my Lord Nityānanda, the jewel of all good qualities; O my Nitāi, the jewel of all good qualities, has brought a flood of ecstatic love of Godhead that has drowned the whole world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prema-vanyā laye nitāi āila gauḍa-deśe</strong><br>
<strong>ḍubila bhakata-gaṇa dīna-hīna bhāse</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Nityānanda has brought this overwhelming deluge of love of Godhead to the land of Bengal on Lord Chaitanya’s order. The devotees have been inundated by this deluge but the unfortunate non-devotees have remained floating on that ecstatic ocean.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dīna-hīna patita pāmara nāhi bāchhe</strong><br>
<strong>brahmāra durlabha prema sabākāre yāche</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Nityananda freely offered this rare gift of love of Godhead, which is difficult for even Lord Brahmā to attain, to the fallen and wretched souls who were not desirous of receiving it.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ābaddha karuṇā-sindhu kāṭiyā muhāna</strong><br>
<strong>ghare ghare bule prema-amiyāra vāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The ocean of mercy had formerly been tightly sealed, but Lord Nitāi cut a channel in its boundary to allow the great flooding waves of ecstatic love to wash down from house to house.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>lochan bale hena nitāi yebā nā bhajila</strong><br>
<strong>jāniyā śuniyā sei ātma-ghātī haila</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lochan Dās says, “Whoever has not worshipped this Lord Nitāi, or accepted the great gift offered by Him, that person knowingly commits suicide.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-akrodha-paramananda" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Akrodha Paramānanda Nityānanda Rāya <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Lochan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>akrodha paramānanda nityānanda rāya</strong><br>
<strong>abhimāna-śūnya nitāi nagare beḍāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The great personality, Lord Nityānanda Prabhu, is free from anger and full of transcendental ecstasy. He wanders throughout the towns, free from false pride and arrogance.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>adhama patita jīver dvāre dvāre giyā</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāma mahāmantra dena bilāiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He travels from door to door to the homes of the fallen souls, freely distributing the Hare Kṛṣṇa Mahāmantra.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yāre dekhe tāre kahe dante tṛṇa dhari’</strong><br>
<strong>āmāre kiniyā laha bhaja gaurahari</strong></p>
<p class="translation">With straw between His teeth, He begs all who cross his path, “Worship Lord Gaurahari and you will purchase me.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>eta bali’ nityānanda bhūme gaḍi yāya</strong><br>
<strong>sonāra parvata yena dhūlāte loṭāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Saying this, Nityānanda falls to the ground, appearing like a golden mountain rolling in the dust.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hena avatāre yāra rati nā janmila</strong><br>
<strong>lochan bale sei pāpī ela āra gela</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Where attachment for such an incarnation has not arisen, Lochan Dās says that such a sinful person comes and goes in the cycle of repeated birth and death.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse">(yāoyā āsā sāra haila)<br>
(nitāi-charaṇ nā bhajila āoyā āsā sāra haila)</p>
<p class="translation">(“They simply came and went. Those who did not serve the feet of Nitāi simply came and went [took birth and died uselessly].”)</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-parama-karuna-pahu-dui-jana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nityānander Dayā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Lochan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>parama karuṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pahu̐ dui jana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitāi gaurachandra</strong><br>
<strong>saba avatāra-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sāra śiromaṇi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kevala ānanda-kanda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Nitāi and Lord Gaurachandra are the two most merciful lords. They are the crest-jewels of all incarnations and the only source of transcendental ecstatic joy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse">(kevala-i ānanda-kanda)<br>
(nityānanda gaurachandra kevala-i ānanda-kanda)<br>
(sakal)<strong> </strong>(avatārer avatārī nityānanda gaurahari avatārer avatārī)</p>
<p class="translation">(They are the only source of joy. Nityānanda and Gaurachandra are the only source of joy. The source of all avatārs are Nityānanda and Gaurahari.)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaja bhaja bhāi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chaitanya nitāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sudṛḍha viśvāsa kari’</strong><br>
<strong>viṣaya chhāḍiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se rase majiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mukhe bala hari hari</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O brother! I implore you to worship Śrī Chaitanya-Nitāi with firm faith. Give up your attachment to sense gratification and merge into this nectar by chanting the Names of Hari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse">(mukhe bala gaurahari)<br>
(viṣay-kathā parihari’ mukhe bala gaurahari)<br>
(grāmya-kathā parihari’ mukhe bala gaurahari)</p>
<p class="translation">(Chant aloud, “Gaurahari!” Give up talk of worldly affairs and chant aloud “Gaurahari!” Give up village talk and chant aloud “Gaurahari!”)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dekha ore bhāi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tribhuvane nāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;emana dayāla dātā</strong><br>
<strong>paśu pākhī jhure&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāṣāṇa vidare</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuni’ yā̐ra guṇa-gāthā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Look, O brother! There are no benefactors in the three worlds who are as merciful as Them. Even birds and animals are fulfilled and stones melt, by hearing the glories of Their qualities.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse">(eman dayāl ke vā āchhe)<br>
(nitāira gaurer mata eman dayāl ke vā āchhe)<br>
(mar kheyeo nām prema yāche man dayāl ke vā āchhe)</p>
<p class="translation">(Who else is so merciful? Who is as merciful as Nitāi and Gaura? They endure even being beaten, and yet They ask Their aggressors to accept the Holy Name and divine love. Who else is so merciful?)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saṁsāre majiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rahili paḍiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se pade nahila āśa</strong><br>
<strong>āpana karama&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhuñyāya śamana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kahaye lochana dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Absorbed in the cycle of birth and death, having fallen by the wayside without any hope for achieving Their company, the lord of death will come and make you suffer the results of your activities – so sings Lochan Dās.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-dayal-nitai-chaitanya-bole-nach-re" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Dayāl Nitāi Chaitanya <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘dayāl nitāi chaitanya’ ba’le nāch re āmār mana</strong><br>
<strong>nāch re āmār mana, nāch re āmār mana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my mind, just dance! O my mind, just dance! O my mind, please dance, chanting, “Dayāl Nitāi Chaitanya!”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(eman dayāl ta’ nāi he mār kheye prema dey)<br>
(tabe)<strong> aparādha dūre yābe, pābe prema-dhan</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(aparādhera vichāra to nāi he)<br>
(takhan)<strong> kṛṣṇa-nāme ruchi habe, ghuchibe bandhana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">(Such a merciful personality as Nityānanda Prabhu is not to be found anywhere. He suffers a beating from Jagāi and Mādhāi and still gives them love of God.)<br>
(When) offences go far away, you will obtain love of God; (but in these Names of Chaitanya and Nitāi there is no consideration of offences). (Once) you have a taste for the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa, bondage to this world will come to an end.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(anurāg to habe he)<br>
(takhan)<strong> anāyāse saphal habe jīvera jīvana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(naile jīvan to michhe he)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(kṛṣṇa-rati vinā jīvan to michhe he)<br>
<strong>śeṣe vṛndāvane rādhā-śyāmer pābe daraśana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(gaura-kṛpā hale he)</p>
<p class="translation">(When there is attachment to the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa, then,) very easily, the life of a living being becomes successful. (Otherwise, life is indeed in vain. Without attachment to Kṛṣṇa, life is simply false.) Then at the end of life you will obtain the beautiful vision of Rādhā and Śyāma in Vṛndāvan (if the mercy of Lord Gaura is there).</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gaurangera-duti-pada-jar-dhana-sampada" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Sāvaraṇa Śrī-Gaura-mahimā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaurāṅgera duṭi pada&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yāra dhana sampada</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se jāne bhakati-rasa-sāra</strong><br>
<strong>gaurāṅgera madhura-līlā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yāra karṇe praveśilā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛdaya nirmala bhela tāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Anyone who has accepted the two lotus feet of Lord Chaitanya can understand the true essence of devotional service. Whoever hears of the sweet Pastimes of Lord Chaitanya, their heart will become pure.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ye gaurāṅgera nāma laya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāra haya premodaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāre muñi yāi balihāri</strong><br>
<strong>gaurāṅga-guṇete jhure&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitya-līlā tāre sphure</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se jana bhakati-adhikārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In one who takes the Holy Name of Gaurāṅga, there is an awakening of love of Godhead. To such a person I say, “Bravo, excellent!” If one appreciates the transcendental qualities of Lord Gaurāṅga, the eternal Pastimes manifest in their heart. Such a person has real devotional qualification.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaurāṅgera saṅgi-gaṇe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nitya-siddha kari māne</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se yāya vrajendra-suta-pāśa</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-gauḍa-maṇḍala-bhūmi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yebā jāne chintāmaṇi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāra haya vraja-bhūme vāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By considering the associates of Lord Gaurāṅga to be eternally perfect, one can attain the association of Śrī Kṛṣṇa in Vraja Dhām. Whoever considers the holy land of Lord Gaurāṅga, Śrī Nabadwīp Dhām, to be the land of transcendental touchstone, that person takes residence in Vraja Dhām, Śrī Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-prema-rasārṇave&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se taraṅge yebā ḍube</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se rādhā-mādhava-antaraṅga</strong><br>
<strong>gṛhe vā vanete thāke&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘hā gaurāṅga’ bale ḍāke</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;narottama māge tāra saṅga</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Whoever dives into the waves of the nectarean ocean of love of Lord Gaurāṅga becomes an intimate associate of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava. Whether one lives in a house or in the forest, if they chant, “O Lord Gaurāṅga!” Narottam Dās begs to attain their association.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gaura-pahu-na-bhajiya-mainu" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Gaurā Pahu̐ <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaurā pahu̐ nā bhajiyā mainu</strong><br>
<strong>prema ratana-dhana helāya hārāinu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord Gaura! I have not worshipped You and have been robbed of the jewel of divine love by my own negligence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>adhane yatana kari dhana teyāginu</strong><br>
<strong>āpana karama-doṣe āpani ḍubinu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Although I am in need of Your wealth, I have endeavoured to reject Your riches and instead I have dived into sinful material activity.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sat-saṅga chhāḍi’ kainu asate vilāsa</strong><br>
<strong>te-kāraṇe lāgila ye karma-bandha-phā̐sa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Avoiding the association of saintly persons, I have enjoyed sporting with materialistic people. For that reason I have become bound in the noose of fruitive activity and its reactions.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viṣaya-viṣama-viṣa satata khāinu</strong><br>
<strong>gaura-kīrtana rase magana nā hainu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have perpetually drunk the horrible poison of material sense gratification and have never become absorbed in the sweet nectar of the chanting of Lord Gaura’s Names.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kena vā āchhaye prāṇa ki sukha lāgiyā</strong><br>
<strong>narottama-dāsa kena nā gela mariyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O why has my heart not experienced any satisfaction and why has Narottam Dās not simply perished?</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-avatara-sara-gora-avatara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Avatāra Sāra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Lochan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>avatāra sāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gorā avatāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kena nā bhajili tā̐re</strong><br>
<strong>kari’ nīre vāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gela nā piyāsa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āpana karama phere</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The incarnation of Śrī Gaura is the most excellent of all incarnations. Why haven’t I worshipped Him? It is as if I was surrounded by water but denied myself a drink, returning to my own fruitive activity.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kaṇṭakera taru&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadāi sevili </strong>(mana)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;amṛta pābāra āśe</strong><br>
<strong>prema-kalpataru&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-gaurāṅga āmāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhāre bhavili viṣe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I chewed the thorns of the tree of worldly life and considered the blood that flowed through my mouth to be nectar. When offered the fruits of the wish-fulfilling tree of ecstatic love of Lord Gaurāṅga, I rejected them, thinking them to be poison.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saurabhera āśe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;palāśa śu̐kili </strong>(mana)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāsāte paśila kīṭa</strong><br>
<strong>‘ikṣu-daṇḍa’ bhāvi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāṭha chuṣili </strong>(mana)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kemane pāibi miṭha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Desirous of smelling a sweet scent, I sniffed at what I thought to be a fragrant flower. Alas, that flower was odourless like the palāśa flower and instead an insect flew up my nose and bit me. Thinking material life to be sweet like sugarcane, I tried to taste its nectar. Instead, it was like chewing dry wood. In this way all my attempts at enjoyment were false.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hāra’ baliyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;galāya parili </strong>(mana)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śamana kiṅkara sāpa</strong><br>
<strong>‘śītala’ baliyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āguna pohāli </strong>(mana)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāili vajara-tāpa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Admitting defeat, wasted and worn out, I await the snake of death. Declaring it to be cool and soothing, I have embraced the fire of material life, only to suffer intense misery, as if struck by lightning.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saṁsāra bhajili&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-gaurāṅga bhulili</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā śunili sādhura kathā</strong><br>
<strong>iha-parakāla&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dukāla khoyāli </strong>(mana)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khāili āpana māthā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Worshiping my family and my material life, I forgot Lord Gaurāṅga and didn’t listen to the instructions of the saints. Now in my final days, I realise I have died twice, for not only am I leaving this mortal body, but I am also dead while living, having wasted my life in material indulgence.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gay-gora-madhur-svare" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Nāma <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gāy gorā madhur svare</strong><br>
<strong>“hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa hare hare</strong><br>
<strong>hare rāma hare rāma rāma rāma hare hare”</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Gaurasundar sings in a very sweet voice, “Hare Kṛṣṇa Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa Hare Hare, Hare Rāma Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma Hare Hare.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gṛhe thāka, vane thāka, sadā ‘hari’ bale’ ḍāka</strong><br>
<strong>sukhe duḥkhe bhulo nāka, vadane hari-nām kara re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Whether you live at home or in the forest, always chant the Lord’s Holy Name; in happiness or sadness, don’t forget – chant the Holy Name and fill your lips with Harināma.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>māyā-jāle baddha haye, āchha michhe kāja laye</strong><br>
<strong>ekhan-o chetana peye, rādhā-mādhav-nām bala re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bound in māyā’s net, you’ve slaved and toiled in vain. But now that you have a human life and consciousness, chant the Lord’s Name, “Rādhā-Mādhava.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jīvana ha-ila śeṣa, nā bhajile hṛṣīkeśa</strong><br>
<strong>bhakti-vinod-upadeśa, ek-bār nām-rase māta re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This life must surely end soon, and you have not worshipped Hṛṣīkeśa, the master of the senses. Bhakti Vinod advises: at least once, taste the Nāma-rasa, the nectar of the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-kobe-gaura-vane-suradhuni-tate" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Siddhi-lālasā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabe gaura-vane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;suradhunī-taṭe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘hā rādhe hā kṛṣṇa’ ba’le</strong><br>
<strong>kā̐diyā beḍāba&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deha-sukha chhāḍi’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nānā latā-taru-tale</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I wander, weeping, through the forests of Gaura, on the banks of the Ganges River, crying out “O Rādhā! O Kṛṣṇa!” having given up all bodily comforts, and taking shelter beneath the various trees and creepers?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śva-pacha-gṛhete&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;māgiyā khāiba</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;piba sarasvatī-jala</strong><br>
<strong>puline puline&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gaḍāgaḍi diba</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kari’ kṛṣṇa-kolāhala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When, at some outcaste’s home shall I give up all thoughts of caste and beg to share with him humble prasādam and drink the water of the Saraswatī? Along the banks, my voice choked with my ecstasy I’ll chant “Kṛṣṇa” in great delight.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhāma-vāsī jane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;praṇati kariyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;māgiba kṛpāra leśa</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇava-charaṇa-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;reṇu gāya mākhi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dhari’ avadhūta-veśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">And when will I bow down before a resident of the holy dhām and beg from him a drop of mercy; when O when will I smear my body with dust from a Vaiṣṇava’s holy feet and wear the mendicant’s cloth?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gauḍa-vraja-vane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bheda nā heriba</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ha-iba varaja-vāsī</strong><br>
<strong>dhāmera svarūpa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sphuribe nayane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ha-iba rādhāra dāsī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Then I shall see no difference between the forest of Gauḍa and Vraja, and I will be transformed into a resident of the dhām. The true nature of the Lord’s abode will manifest itself to my eyes, and I will become a maidservant of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-kali-ghora-timire-garasala-jagajana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Kali-ghora Timire <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Nayanānanda Dās</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kali-ghora timire&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;garasala jagajana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dharama karama bahu-dūra</strong><br>
<strong>asādhane chintāmaṇi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vidhi milāola āni</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gorā baḍa dayāla ṭhākura</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The people of this world have now been devoured by the dense darkness of this age of Kali and all good activities have gone far away. Thus they are apathetic to the transcendental gems offered by the greatly merciful Lord Gaura.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhāi re bhāi!&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gorā guṇa kahana nā jāya</strong><br>
<strong>kata śata-ānana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kata chaturānana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baraṇiyā ora nāhi pāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“O my brother! O my brother! No one has told you the glories of Lord Gaura? How great is Lord Brahmā, how great is Lord Śeṣa, but they cannot eclipse the greatness of Lord Gaura.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chāri veda ṣaḍa-dara-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śana kari adhyayana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se jadi gaurāṅga nāhi bhaje</strong><br>
<strong>vṛthā tāra adhyayana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lochana vihīna jana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;darpaṇe andhe kibā kāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“What use are the four Vedas and the six systems of philosophy if one does not worship Lord Gaurāṅga? Why study them? What use is a mirror to a blind person?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>veda vidyā dui&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kichui nā jānata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se jadi gaurāṅga jāne sāra</strong><br>
<strong>nayanānanda bhane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei ta sakali jāne</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sarva-siddhi karatale tāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“If one knows the two kinds of Vedic knowledge, he still does not know anything. But if one knows Lord Gaurāṅga, he knows the most precious knowledge.” Nayanānanda says: “Such a person knows everything. All perfections stay in the palm of his hand.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hari-hari-bada-shiela-marame-rahila" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Baḍa Śela Marame Rahila <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari hari!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baḍa śela marame rahila</strong><br>
<strong>pāiyā durlabha tanu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-kṛṣṇa-bhajana vinu</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;janma mora viphala ha-ila</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord! O Lord! A huge spear has pierced my heart. I obtained a rare human body but did not serve Śrī Kṛṣṇa. My birth has now passed in vain.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vrajendra-nandana hari&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;navadvīpe avatari’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jagat bhariyā prema dila</strong><br>
<strong>muñi se pāmara-mati&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśeṣe kaṭhina ati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;te̐i more karuṇā nahila</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Hari, the prince of Vraja, has descended in Nabadwīp and filled the world with divine love, but I am wicked-minded; I am especially and extremely obstinate, so I did not receive His mercy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>svarūpa sanātana rūpa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;raghunātha bhaṭṭa-yuga</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhāte nā haila mora mati</strong><br>
<strong>divya chintāmaṇi dhāma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndāvana hena sthāna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei dhāme nā kainu vasati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Svarūp, Sanātan, Rūpa, Raghunāth Dās, Gopāl Bhaṭṭa, and Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa – I have had no attachment to them, and I have never resided in Śrī Vṛndāvan Dhām, the abode of divine, wish-fulfilling gemstones.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viśeṣa viṣaye mati&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nahila vaiṣṇave rati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirantara kheda uṭhe mane</strong><br>
<strong>narottama dāsa kahe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jībāra uchita nahe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-guru-vaiṣṇava-sevā vine</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am completely absorbed in the mundane, and I have no attachment to the Vaiṣṇavas. Thus, I constantly lament. Narottam Dās says, “I have no reason to live without the service of Śrī Guru and the Vaiṣṇavas.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-yadi-gaura-na-hata-tabe-ki-ha-ita" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Yadi Gaura Nā Hata <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Vāsudeva Ghoṣ</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yadi gaura nā hata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tabe ki ha-ita</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kemane dharitām de</strong><br>
<strong>rādhāra mahimā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-rasa-sīmā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jagate jānāta ke?</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If Lord Gaura had not come, then what would have become of us? How could we have maintained our lives? If He had not come, then how could we know of the glories of Śrī Rādhā and the topmost limit of ecstatic love of Kṛṣṇa?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>madhura vṛndā-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vipina mādhurī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;praveśa chāturī sāra</strong><br>
<strong>varaja-yuvatī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāvera bhakati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śakati ha-ita kāra?</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Who would have had the capacity to render ecstatic devotional service following the mood of the damsels of Vraja? Indeed, the clever expertise of the Vraja-gopīs is essential for entering the supremely sweet forest of Vṛndā, Śrī Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gāo punaḥ punaḥ&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gaurāṅgera guṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sarala kariyā mana</strong><br>
<strong>e bhava-sāgare&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;emana dayāla</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā dekhiye eka-jana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Sing again and again the wonderful qualities of Lord Gaurāṅga while keeping your heart simple. In the ocean of this material world, not a single person has ever seen such a magnanimous and merciful Lord.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse">(āmi)<strong> gaurāṅga baliyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā genu galiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kemane dharinu de</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vāsura-hiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāṣāṇa diyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(vidhi)<strong> kemane gaḍiyāchhe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Even though I am chanting “Gaurāṅga!”, somehow I have not melted in ecstatic love. So how have I maintained the burden of this body? How is it that the creator has given this Vāsu a stone in place of his heart?</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-emona-gauranga-bine-nahi-ara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Emana Gaurāṅga Vine Nāhi Āra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Premānanda Dās</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;emana gaurāṅga vine nāhi āra</strong><br>
<strong>hena avatāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;habe ki hayechhe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hena prema parachāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">No one is like Lord Gaurāṅga! Will there ever be an incarnation like Him, an incarnation that preached ecstatic love of Godhead as He did?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>duramati ati&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;patita pāṣaṇḍī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇe nā mārila kāre</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāma diye&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛdaya śodhila</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yāchi giyā ghare ghare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He did not kill the fallen wicked-hearted blasphemers. Instead, He went from house to house visiting them. He begged them to chant Lord Kṛṣṇa’s Holy Names. He gave them the Holy Names and purified their hearts.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhava-viriñchira&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vāñchhita ye prema</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jagate phelila ḍhāli</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(bhava viriñchi yā vāñchhā kare)<br>
<strong>kāṅgale pāiye&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khāila nāchiye</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bājāiye karatāli</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To the world He freely gave the rare gift of ecstatic love of Godhead, a gift even Brahmā and Śiva attain only with great difficulty. Attaining that gift, the poor people of this world danced and clapped their hands.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hāsiye kā̐diye&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;preme gaḍāgaḍi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pulake vyāpila aṅga</strong><br>
<strong>chaṇḍāle brāhmaṇe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kare kolākuli</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kabe vā chhila e raṅga</strong></p>
<p class="translation">They laughed and wept. Overcome with ecstasy, they rolled on the ground. The hairs of their bodies stood erect. The brāhmaṇas and chaṇḍālas embraced. There was great bliss.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ḍākiye hā̐kiye&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khola-karatāle</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gāiye dhāiye phire</strong><br>
<strong>dekhiyā śamana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tarāsa pāiye</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kapāṭa hānila dvāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Loudly they called out the Holy Names, playing the mṛdaṅga and karatāls. They sang, ran, and danced in a circle. Seeing all this, peaceful composure became afraid, fled, and knocked on people’s doors.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e tina bhuvana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānande bharila</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uṭhila maṅgala-sora</strong><br>
<strong>kahe premānanda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;emana gaurāṅge</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rati nā janmila mora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">An auspicious tumult arose. The three worlds were filled with bliss. Premānanda says: “Alas! Attachment for Lord Gaurāṅga did not arise in me.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ke-jabi-ke-jabi-bhai-bhava-sindhu-para" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ke Yābi Ke Yābi Bhāi? <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Lochan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ke yābi ke yābi bhāi bhava-sindhu pāra</strong><br>
<strong>dhanya kali-yuge re chaitanya-avatāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Who will go, oh brother, who will go to cross the ocean of material existence? The wealth and great fortune of this age of Kali is the incarnation of Lord Chaitanya.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmāra gaurāṅger ghāṭe ujānakheyā vaya</strong><br>
<strong>kaḍipāti nāhi lāge amani pāra haya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My Lord Gaurāṅga stands at the jetty ready to ferry his servants upstream across this material existence. For no charge He is immediately taking them.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-nāmera tarīkhāni śrī-guru-kāṇḍārī</strong><br>
<strong>saṅkīrtana-keroyāla du’bāhu pasāri</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The ferry-boat is the Holy Names of the Lord and the spiritual master is the captain. The oars are the outstretched arms in the saṅkīrtan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarva-jīva uddhāra haila premera vātāse</strong><br>
<strong>lochana paḍiyā raila karamera doṣe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The favourable wind is divine love for Lord Kṛṣṇa, and all the fallen souls have been rescued on this boat. Only Lochan Dās is left behind due to the faults of his own misdeeds.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-vimala-hemajin-tanu-anupama-re" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Vimala Hema Jini <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Vṛndāvan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vimala hema jini&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tanu anupama re!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe śobhe nānā phula-dāma</strong><br>
<strong>kadamba-keśara jini&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ekaṭī pulaka re!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāra mājhe bindu bindu ghāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His incomparable form defeats the purest gold, and is decorated with a garland of many kinds of flowers. His body is covered with goosebumps with hairs standing erect, hairs that defeat the filaments of kadamba flowers, between which are drop after drop of perspiration.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jini mada-matta hāti&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gamana manthara ati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāvāveśe ḍhuli ḍhuli yāya</strong><br>
<strong>aruṇa-vasana chhavi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jini prabhātera ravi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gorā-aṅge laharī khelāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His slow, graceful gait surpasses that of a maddened elephant. Overwhelmed with divine ecstasy, He sways and staggers as He walks. The radiance of His reddish garments eclipses the rising sun, while waves of spiritual emotion ripple across His golden form.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chalite nā pāre gorā-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chā̐d go̐sāñi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;balite nā pāre ādha-bola</strong><br>
<strong>bhāvete āveśa haiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari hari bolāiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āchaṇḍāle dhari’ deya kola</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Overwhelmed, Lord Gaurachandra cannot even walk. Now He cannot properly speak – only broken half-words come. Overcome with ecstatic love, He chants, “Hari! Hari!” He embraces everyone – even the lowest of persons – in His arms.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e sukha-sampada-kāle&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gorā nā bhajinu hele</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hena pade nā karinu āśa</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanyachandra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ṭhākura śrī-nityānanda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;guṇa gāya vṛndāvana dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Alas! At that blissful fortunate time I did not worship Lord Gaura. I did not yearn to attain His feet. In this way Vṛndāvan Dās sings the glories of Lord Chaitanyachandra and Lord Nityānanda.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-anga-upanga-astra-parshada-sange" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Aṅga-upāṅga-astra-pārṣada-saṅge <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>from Gītāvalī by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aṅga-upāṅga-astra-pārṣada-saṅge</strong><br>
<strong>nācha-i bhāva-mūrati gorā raṅge</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the company of His expansions (Nityānanda Prabhu and Advaita Āchārya), sub-expansions (Śrīvās Ṭhākur and other devotees), weapon (Śrī Harināma), and associates (Gadādhar Paṇḍit and other intimate companions), Gorā, the embodiment of divine love, dances in ecstasy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gāota kali-yuga-pāvana-nāma</strong><br>
<strong>bhrama-i śachī-suta nadīyā-dhāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Singing the Name which purifies the Age of Kali, the son of Śachī roams throughout the abode of Nadia.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nagara-bhramiya-amara-gaura-elo-ghore" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>After Parikramā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>nagara bhramiyā āmāra gaura ela ghare</strong><br>
<strong>gaura ela ghare āmāra nitāi ela ghare</strong><br>
<strong>dhūlā jhāri’ śachīmātā gaura kole kare</strong><br>
<strong>ānandete bhakta-gaṇe hari hari bale</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After parikramā of the town, Gaurāṅga returns to His house, and Nitāi also comes to the house. Dusting off His body, Śachī Mātā takes Gaurāṅga upon her lap. Out of great spiritual joy all the devotees are chanting “Hari Hari!”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-aruna-basane-sonara-suraja" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Gaurasundarer Āvirbhāva Vāsare <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>The Holy Appearance Day of Śrī Śrī Gaurasundar</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aruṇa vasane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sonāra sūraja</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;udichhe kena re āja</strong><br>
<strong>vasanta suṣamā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ujāri āpanā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ḍhāle kena jaga-mājha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Why has the Lord appeared suddenly with golden complexion and with golden dress just like the morning sun? Why has He come in the middle of springtime as everything feels so beautiful and wonderful?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>taru-gulma-latā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;apūrva vāratā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vahe kena phale phule</strong><br>
<strong>bhṛṅga o vihaṅge&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kena hena raṅge</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṅgīta taraṅga tule</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Trees, bushes, and creeping vines – all display a wondrous newness. By what cause do they bear such fruits and flowers? Why do the bees and birds in such delight raise waves of melodious song?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
