<p class="verse"><strong>patita durjana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kena re garjana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ullāse phāṭhiyā paḍe</strong><br>
<strong>vidyā-kula-dhana-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;abhimānī jana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kena mlāna duḥkha bhare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am very fallen and unqualified. Why is this sound vibration coming to me, and why am I getting inspiration through it? Those who have high caste, vast knowledge and material wealth, they have so much ego and pride – why are they suffering?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ākāśa-vātāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ghuchāiya trāsa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āśvāse bhāsāye deya</strong><br>
<strong>sādhu-jana-mana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sukha vitaraṇa-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āveśe unmāda haya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The sky and the air are so beautiful in the spring season, and they are naturally nourishing and inspiring everybody. In the same way, the sound vibration of the devotees spreads everywhere automatically as they distribute Kṛṣṇa’s Name.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chaudikete dhvani&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ki apūrva śuni</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bahu-jana-uchcharola</strong><br>
<strong>hare kṛṣṇa rāma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāma divya-dhāma</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari hari hari bola</strong></p>
<p class="translation">What a remarkable sound is heard in all directions bringing jubilation to all the people. The devotees chant the divine Names “Hare, Kṛṣṇa, Rāma”, and the transcendental abode manifests. Chant “Hari Hari Hari!”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>phālgunī pūrṇimā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hindola raṅgimā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sujana bhajana-rāge</strong><br>
<strong>saṅkīrtana sane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;marama gahane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā jāni ki bhāva jāge</strong></p>
<p class="translation">On His holy appearance day, the festival of Holi takes place. All the devotees are chanting as well as all the Hindus. No one can understand the feeling and the substance that comes within the heart from that saṅkīrtan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sandhyā samāgama&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tapana magana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kena hema ghana kole</strong><br>
<strong>aparūpa kata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pūrava parvata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;suvarṇa chandramā bhāle</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As the sunset approaches, the sun shines on the mountain illuminating it like it has golden ornaments. This is especially so in the springtime. Then as the moon rises, it also beautifies that mountain with its rays.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>suvarṇa chandramā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paśichhe nīlimā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se nīla vilīna heme</strong><br>
<strong>ithe kibā bhāya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sādhu-jana gāya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kalaṅka nā rahe preme</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The blue sky is covered by the Golden Moon, and when the devotees glorify the Lord with their discussions, no other conception or motive can exist other than Kṛṣṇa consciousness. At that time, prema, divine love, descends automatically without any impediment.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mahājane bale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;grahaṇera chhale</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṅge nāma-saṅkīrtana</strong><br>
<strong>gaurachandrodaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāpa rāhu kṣaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chandra-śobhā-prema-dhana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The great devotees say, take up your spiritual path with the congregational chanting of the Lord’s Holy Names. With the appearance of Śrī Gaurachandra, all sinfulness is removed and everyone attains the treasure of love of Godhead.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>marmajña sakale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kahe kutūhale</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nīlimā vilīna chā̐de</strong><br>
<strong>chhanna avatāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lukāna kāhāra?</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-ruchi-rūpa-chhā̐de</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Everyone who engages in this Nām-saṅkīrtan of Mahāprabhu comes to enquire sincerely and to know the innermost secret of the Lord’s bluish colour absorbed by His golden features. This is the hidden incarnation of the Lord and the concealed feature of His form is that He is tasting the threefold relish of Śrī Rādhā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ithe hena stuti&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-bhāva-dyuti</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;suvalita śyāma rāo</strong><br>
<strong>udila gaurāṅga&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāma-prema-saṅga</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaya jaya gorā gāo</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Thus I pray to the dark cowherd boy Śyāmasundar who has taken the mood and effulgence of Śrī Rādhā. That Lord, Śrī Chaitanya, appeared with the chanting of the pure divine Name; everyone chant the glories of that Golden Lord, Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-yadi-te-hari-pada-saroja-sudha" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Godruma-chandra-bhajanopadeśa <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Instructions for Worshipping Lord Chaitanya, the Moon of the Island of Godruma-dvīpa</h3>
<h4>Sanskrit compostion by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur (in 1893)</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yadi te hari-pāda-saroja-sudhā-</strong><br>
<strong>rasa-pāna-paraṁ hṛdayaṁ satatam</strong><br>
<strong>parihṛtya gṛhaṁ kali-bhāva-mayaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If your heart wishes to be always absorbed in drinking the ambrosial mellows of the lotus feet of Lord Hari, then give up mundane household life, full of Kali-yuga quarrels and strife, and just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhana-yauvana-jīvana-rājya-sukhaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>na hi nityam anukṣaṇa-nāśa-param</strong><br>
<strong>tyaja grāmya-kathā-sakalaṁ viphalaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Material riches, youth, a long life, and royal happiness – none of these are certain; at every moment they are being destroyed. Give up all useless mundane conversations and just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ramaṇī-jana-saṅga-sukhaṁ cha sakhe</strong><br>
<strong>charame bhaya-daṁ puruṣārtha-haram</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāma-sudhā-rasa-matta-matir</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O friend! The pleasure to be had in the company of beautiful young women ultimately turns to fearfulness, and distracts one from the true goal of human life. With your mind intoxicated by the nectarean mellows of the Holy Name of Lord Hari, just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaḍa-kāvya-raso na hikvya-rasaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>kali-pāvana-gaura-raso hi rasaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>alam anya-kathādy-anuśīlanayā</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The taste of mundane poetry never provides the sweet taste of actual poetry. The sweet mellows of true poetry are verily found in descriptions of Lord Gaura, the savior of all souls in the Kali-yuga. The diligent study of all other topics is useless! Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛṣabhānu-sutānvita-vāma-tanuṁ</strong><br>
<strong>yamunā-taṭa-nāgara-nanda-sutam</strong><br>
<strong>muralī-kala-gīta-vinoda-paraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His left side enhanced by the daughter of Vṛṣabhānu (Rādhārāṇī), Nanda-Suta (Kṛṣṇa), the transcendental paramour, stands on the bank of the Yamunā river absorbed in the pleasure-giving Pastime of playing soft, melodious songs on His flute. Just worship Him, the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-kīrtana-madhya-gataṁ sva-janaiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>pariveṣṭita-jambunadābha-harim</strong><br>
<strong>nija-gauḍa-janaika-kṛpā-jaladhiṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the midst of Hari-kīrtana, surrounded by His loving associates He is the same dark Lord Hari, but now shining with the luster of molten gold. He is the exclusive ocean of mercy for His own Gauḍīya devotees. Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>girirāja-sutā-parivīta-gṛhaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>nava-khaṇḍa-patiṁ yati-chitta-haram</strong><br>
<strong>sura-saṅgha-nutaṁ priyayā sahitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His island home is surrounded by the river Gaṅgā, daughter of the king of mountains. He is the Lord of the nine-fold islands of Nabadwīp and the stealer of the hearts of the renunciants. Along with His beloved wife Viṣṇu Priyā He is glorified by demigods and saintly persons. Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kali-kukkura-mudgara-bhāva-dharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāma-mahauṣadha-dāna-param</strong><br>
<strong>patitārta-dayārdra-sumūrti-dharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His mood is that of wielding a stick for chasing away the mad dog of Kali-yuga. He is absorbed in distributing the Holy Name of Hari, the great remedy for material existence. His heart is melted with compassion for the fallen souls who are suffering, and His transcendental form is very beautiful. Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ripu-bāndhava-bheda-vihīna-dayā</strong><br>
<strong>yadabhīkṣṇam udeti mukhābja-tatau</strong><br>
<strong>tam akṛṣṇam iha vraja-rāja-sutam</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Continually beaming from His lotus face is causeless mercy that makes no distinction between so-called ‘friends’ or ‘enemies.’ Although the son of Mahārāj Nanda, He appears here in a form unlike that of Kṛṣṇa’s dark complexion. Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>iha chopaniṣat-parigīta-vibhur</strong><br>
<strong>dvija-rāja-sutaḥ puraṭābha-hariḥ</strong><br>
<strong>nija-dhāmani khelati bandhu-yuto</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In this world and in the Upaniṣads He is celebrated as the all-pervading Supreme Lord. He appears now as the son of the king of brāhmaṇas and is Lord Hari Himself, with a shining golden complexion. In His own abode He plays eternally with His cowherd boyfriends. Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>avatāra-varaṁ paripūrṇa-kalaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>para-tattvam ihātma-vilāsam ayam</strong><br>
<strong>vraja-dhāma-rasāmbudhi-gupta-rasaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the most excellent of avatāras, and within Him exists the consummate expansions of Godhead. He is the Supreme Truth, enjoying Himself here in great playfulness. He embodies the most confidential mellows flowing within the ocean of rasa in Vraja Dhām. Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śruti-varṇa-dhanādi na yasya kṛpā</strong><br>
<strong>janane balavad-bhajanena vinā</strong><br>
<strong>tam ahaituka-bhāva-pathena sakhe</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O friend! Without the devotional performance of purely intense bhajan, your vast scholarship, noble family lineage, wealth, and so on are completely ineffective for invoking the mercy of the Lord. With a service attitude that is unmotivated, just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>api nakra-gatau hrada-madhya-gataṁ</strong><br>
<strong>kamamochayad ārta-janaṁ tam ajam</strong><br>
<strong>avichintya-balaṁ śiva-kalpa-taruṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This same Lord once liberated the elephant Gajendra from the crocodile’s grasp within the lake. He is the unborn one, possessing inconceivable power, and is truly a desire-tree of pure auspiciousness. Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>surabhīndra-tapaḥ parituṣṭa-manā</strong><br>
<strong>vara-vaṛṇa-dharo harir āvirabhūt</strong><br>
<strong>tam ajasra-sukhaṁ muni-dhairya-haraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Being pleased with the penances of Indra and the surabhi cow, Lord Hari has appeared again in this world possessing a superexcellent complexion. He is endless happiness, and He plunders the patience of the learned sages. Just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>abhilāṣa-chayaṁ tad-abheda-dhiyam</strong><br>
<strong>aśubhaṁ cha śubhaṁ tyaja sarvam idam</strong><br>
<strong>anukūlatayā priya-sevanayā</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The multitude of worldly desires, philosophical meditations on the impersonal oneness of all beings, as well as the relative conceptions of what is auspicious or inauspicious – utterly renounce all of these things. In a favourable mood while rendering loving devotional service, just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-sevaka-sevana-dharma-paro</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāma-rasāmṛta-pāna-rataḥ</strong><br>
<strong>nati-dainya-dayā-para-māna-yuto</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Remaining fixed in the service of Hari’s servants, being addicted to drinking the sweet nectar of the Holy Name of Hari, and endowed with modesty, humility and compassion, offering all respect unto others, just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="17">
<h5>17</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vada yādava mādhava kṛṣṇa hare</strong><br>
<strong>vada rāma janārdana keśava he</strong><br>
<strong>vṛṣabhānusutā-priya-nātha sadā</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Incessantly call out to the Lord, “O Yādava! O Mādhava! O Kṛṣṇa! O Hari!” Chant, “O Rāma! O Janārdana! O Keśava!” “O beloved Lord of Vṛṣabhānu’s daughter!” – and just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="18">
<h5>18</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vada yāmuna-tīra-vanādri-pate</strong><br>
<strong>vada gokula-kānana-puñja-rave</strong><br>
<strong>vada rāsa-rasāyana gaura-hare</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please chant, “O Lord of the forests and hills along the banks of the river Yamunā!” Chant, “O brilliant sun of the groves of Gokula!” Chant, “O elixir of life for the Rāsa Dance!” And chant, “O Gaurahari!” – and just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="19">
<h5>19</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chala gaura-vanaṁ nava-khaṇḍa-mayaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>paṭha gaura-hareś charitāni mudā</strong><br>
<strong>luṭha gaura-padāṅkita-gāṅga-taṭaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please go to the forest of Lord Gaura, divided into nine islands. Please read and recite with great joy the Pastimes of Lord Gaurahari. Please roll about in ecstasy on the bank of the river Gaṅgā marked with the footprints of Lord Gaura – and just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="20">
<h5>20</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>smara gaura-gadādhara-keli-kalāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhava gaura-gadādhara-pakṣa-charaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>śṛṇu gaura-gadādhara-chāru-kathāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja godruma-kānana-kuñja-vidhum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Just remember the artistically playful Pastimes of Gaura and Gadādhar. Just become a loyal follower of Gaura and Gadādhar’s camp. Just listen to the captivating stories of Gaura and Gadādhar – and just worship the beautiful Moon of Godruma’s forest bowers.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-radha-krishna-bol-bol-bolo-re-sobai" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Nagara Kīrtana 4 <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Śrī Nāma from Gītāvalī</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘rādhā-kṛṣṇa’ bal bal balare sabāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(ei)<strong> śikṣā diyā, saba nadīyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;phirchhe neche’ gaura-nitāi</strong><br>
(michhe)<strong> māyāra baśe, jāchchha bhese,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khāchchha hābuḍubu, bhāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Chant, chant “Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa!” Everyone chant! When Lord Chaitanya and Lord Nityānanda came dancing through Nadia, They gave these teachings: Chant, chant “Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa!” Everyone chant! You are caught up in a whirlpool of senseless action and are sinking lower and lower. Chant, chant “Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa!” Everyone chant!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(jīva)<strong> kṛṣṇa-dāsa, e biśvāsa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karle ta āra duḥkha nāi</strong><br>
(kṛṣṇa)<strong> balbe jabe, pulaka habe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jharabe āṅkhi bali tāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If you just understand that the spirit soul is the eternal servant of Kṛṣṇa, you will never have any more sorrows. Chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and your eyes will fill with tears and your body will feel transcendental shivering. Chant, chant “Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa!” Everyone chant!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse">(rādhā)<strong> kṛṣṇa bala, saṅge chala,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ei-mātra bhikhā chāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(jāy)<strong> sakala bipada bhaktivinoda,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bolena jakhana o-nāma gāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Simply chant “Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa!” and join with us. Those are the only alms we beg. Chant, chant “Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa!” Everyone chant! “All dangers will be gone when that Name is chanted,” says Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur. Chant, chant “Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa!” Everyone chant!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hari-bole-modera-gaura-elo" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Nagara Kīrtana 8 <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Śrī Nāma from Gītāvalī</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="verse">(Refrain:)<strong> ‘hari’ bale modera gaura ela</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Our Lord Gaura came chanting “Hari! Hari!”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ela re gaurāṅga-chānda preme elathela</strong><br>
<strong>nitāi-advaita-saṅge godrume paśila</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Gaurāṅga-chānda came looking very disheveled, as if mad, due to ecstatic love of God, and along with Lord Nityānanda and Advaita Āchārya He entered the land of Godruma.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saṅkīrtana-rase mete nama bilāila</strong><br>
<strong>nāmera hāṭe ese preme jagata bhāsāila</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Deeply absorbed in the intoxicating mellows of saṅkīrtan, He distributed the Holy Name. Having come to the marketplace of the Holy Name, He caused the whole universe to swim in ecstatic love of God.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>godruma-vāsīra āja duḥkha dūre gela</strong><br>
<strong>bhakta-vṛnda-saṅge āsi’ hāṭa jāgāila</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Today all the miseries of the residents of Godrumadvīp have gone far away, for Lord Gaurāṅga, having come here along with all His devotees, caused the marketplace of the Holy Name to appear here in Godruma.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nādīya bhramite gorā ela nāmera hāṭe</strong><br>
<strong>gaura ela hāṭe, saṅge nitāi ela hāṭe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Wandering all over the land of Nadia, Lord Gaura came to the marketplace of the Holy Name. Lord Gaura came to the marketplace along with Nitāi.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāche mātoyārā nitāi godrumera māṭhe</strong><br>
<strong>jagata mātāya nitāi premera mālasāṭe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Maddened with ecstasy Lord Nityānanda dances in the fields of Godruma. Due to ecstatic love of God, He moves His powerful arms like a challenging wrestler and thus overwhelms the universe in ecstasy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse">(torā dekhe’ jā’ re)<br>
<strong>advaitādi bhakta-vṛnda nāche ghāṭe ghāṭe</strong><br>
<strong>palāya duranta kali poḍiyā bibhrāṭe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">(O, all of you please go and see!) The Vaiṣṇavas headed by Advaita Āchārya are dancing along the banks of the Gaṅgā from one bathing ghat to the next! At such a sight as this, the wicked personality Kali falls into great danger and thus runs for his life.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ki sukhe bhāsila jīva gorāchāndera nāṭe</strong><br>
<strong>dekhiyā śuniyā pāṣanḍīra buka phāṭe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In what indescribable happiness did all living beings swim while Lord Gaurachandra was dancing? By seeing and hearing of such Pastimes, the hearts of the atheists break.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-prabhu-kohe-kohilan-ei-mahamantra" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Prabhu Kahe – Kahilāṅ ei Mahāmantra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Śrī Chaitanya-bhāgavata: Madhya-khaṇḍa 23.76–78, 28.27–28</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Vṛndāvan Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>“hare kṛṣṇa hare kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa kṛṣṇa hare hare</strong><br>
<strong>hare rāma hare rāma rāma rāma hare hare”</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>prabhu kahe – “kahilāṅ ei mahāmantra</strong><br>
<strong>ihā japa’ giyā sabe kariyā nirbandha</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>ihā haite sarva-siddhi ha-ibe sabāra</strong><br>
<strong>sarva-kṣaṇa bala’ ithe vidhi nāhi āra</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>ki śayane ki bhojane, ki vā jāgaraṇe</strong><br>
<strong>ahar-niśa chinta kṛṣṇa, balaha vadane</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>āma-prati sneha yadi thāke sabākāra</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa vinā keha kichhu nā balibe āra”</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrīman Mahāprabhu said, “Hare Kṛṣṇa Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa Hare Hare, Hare Rāma Hare Rāma, Rāma Rāma Hare Hare.<br>
“I have told you this Mahāmantra. All of you chant it with determination and without fail. By doing so, everyone will attain all spiritual perfection.<br>
“At all times chant this Mahāmantra; there are no other rules. Whether you are resting, eating, or awake – day and night think of Kṛṣṇa and chant His Name aloud.<br>
“If you have any affection for Me, then do not speak of anyone or any­thing other than Kṛṣṇa.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-haribolo-haribolo-haribolo-bhai-re" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Haribala Bhāi Re <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>haribala haribala haribala bhāi re</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāma āniyāchhe gauraṅga-nitāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(modera duḥkha dekhe re)</p>
<p class="translation">O brother! Chant “Hari”! Chant “Hari”! Chant “Hari”! Gaurāṅga and Nitāi have brought Hari’s Name! (Seeing our suffering!)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-nāma vinā jīvera anya dhana nāi re</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāme śuddha hala jagāi-mādhāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(baḍa pāpī chhila re)</p>
<p class="translation">The jīva has no wealth other than Hari’s Name! Jagāi and Mādhāi were purified by Hari’s Name! (They were great sinners!)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>michhe māyā-baddha haye jīvana kāṭāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(āmi āmāra bale re)<br>
<strong>āśā-vaśe ghure’ ghure’ āra kothā yāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(āśāra śeṣa nāi re)</p>
<p class="translation">Bound by Māyā, I pass my life in vain! (Claiming ‘I’ and ‘mine’!) Controlled by desire, wandering and wandering, where else will I go? (Desire has no end!)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘hari-bale’ deo bhāi āśāra mukhe chhāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(nirāśa ta’ sukha re)<br>
<strong>bhoga-mokṣa-vāñchhā chhāḍi’ hari-nāma gāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(śuddha-sattva haye re)</p>
<p class="translation">Brother! Chanting “Hari”, throw ash in the face of desire! (Desireless­ness is happiness!) Giving up desire for enjoyment and liberation, I will chant Hari’s Name! (Being pure in heart!)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nā cheyeo nāmera guṇe o saba phala pāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(tuchchha phaler prayāsa chheḍe re)<br>
<strong>vinoda bale yāi laye nāmera bālāi re</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(nāmera bālāi chheḍe re)</p>
<p class="translation">Even without desiring them, I obtain all those ends (dharma, artha, kāma, and mokṣa) by the power of Hari’s Name! (Give up endeavours for insignificant ends!) Vinod says, “I will remove the obstructions to Hari’s Name! (Give up the obstructions to Hari’s Name!)”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ohe-harinam-tava-mahima-apara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Nāmāṣṭakam 7 <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ohe harināma, tava mahimā apāra</strong><br>
<strong>tava pade nati āmi kari bāra bāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Holy Name! Your glories are boundless! Therefore I bow down at Your lotus feet again and again.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gokulera mahotsava ānanda-sāgara</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra charaṇe poḍi haiyā kātara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O grand festival of Gokula! O ocean of bliss! I fall down at Your lotus feet, for I am feeling very distressed and troubled at heart.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tumi kṛṣṇa, pūrṇa-vapu, rasera nidāna</strong><br>
<strong>tava pade paḍi tava guṇa kari gāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are Lord Kṛṣṇa, Your divine form is fully perfect and complete, and You are the origin of all transcendental mellows. Falling down at Your lotus feet, I sing of Your divine qualities.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>je kare tomāra pade ekānta āśraya</strong><br>
<strong>tā’ra ārti-rāśi nāśa karaha niśchaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You definitely destroy the multitude of afflictions of that person who takes exclusive shelter at Your holy feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarva aparādha tumi nāśa kara tā’ra</strong><br>
<strong>nāma-aparādhāvadhi nāśaho tāhāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You destroy all of their offences, even to the extent of the Nāma-aparādhas (the ten offences against the chanting of the Holy Name).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarva-doṣa dhauta kari’ tāhāra hṛdaya</strong><br>
<strong>siṁhāsane baisa tumi parama āśraya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Cleansing them of all impurities, You are then seated upon the throne of their heart as their supreme shelter.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ati-ramya chida-ghana-ānanda-mūrtimāna</strong><br>
<strong>‘raso vai saḥ’ bali’ veda kare tuyā gāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are delightful and beautiful, the personification of complete cognisance and condensed bliss. The Vedas sing of You, saying, “The Supreme Personality of Godhead is verily the personification of all transcendental mellows.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaktivinoda rūpa-gosvāmī-charaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>māgaye sarvadā nāma-sphūrti sarva-kṣane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At the lotus feet of Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī, Bhakti Vinod constantly begs at every moment for the direct revelation of the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-narada-muni-bajaya-vina" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Nāmāṣṭakam 8 <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nārada muni,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bājāya vīṇā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘rādhikā-ramaṇa’-nāme</strong><br>
<strong>nāma amani,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;udita haya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakata-gīta-sāme</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When the great soul Nārada Muni plays his stringed vīṇā, the Holy Name of Rādhikā-Ramaṇa descends and immediately appears amidst the kīrtan of the Lord’s devotees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>amiya-dhārā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bariṣe ghana,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śravana-jugale giyā</strong><br>
<strong>bhakata-jana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saghane nāche,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhariyā āpana hiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Like a monsoon cloud, the Holy Name showers pure nectar into their ears. All the devotees, due to great ecstasy, enthusiastically dance to their heart’s content.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mādhurī-pūra,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āsaba paśi’,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mātāya jagata-jane</strong><br>
<strong>keha vā kā̐de,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keha vā nāche,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keha māte mane mane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All the inhabitants of the universe become maddened upon entering these intoxicating showers of divine sweetness. Some people cry, some dance and others become fully intoxicated within their minds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pañcha-vadana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nārade dhari’,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;premera saghana rola</strong><br>
<strong>kamalāsana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāchiyā bale,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘bola bola hari bola’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The five-faced Lord Śiva embraces Nārada Muni and repeatedly makes loud screams of ecstatic joy, while Lord Brahmā dances very ecstatically and exclaims, “All of you chant ‘Haribol ! Haribol!’”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sahasrānana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama-sukhe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘hari hari’ bali gāya</strong><br>
<strong>nāma-prabhāve,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mātila viśva,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāma-rasa sabe pāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In supreme happiness the thousand-faced Ananta Śeṣa sings and calls out, “Hari! Hari!” By the influence of the transcendental vibration of that Name, the whole universe becomes ecstatically maddened as everyone tastes and relishes the mellows of the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-nāma,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rasane sphuri’,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pūrā’o āmāra āśa</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-rūpa-pade,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāchaye ihā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakativinoda-dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Holy Name of Śrī Kṛṣṇa has fulfilled all my desires by thus manifesting on everyone’s tongue. Bhakti Vinod, the humble servant of the Lord, therefore prays at the feet of Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī that the chanting of Harināma may always continue in this way.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-krishnotkirtana-gana-nartana-parau" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Ṣaḍ Goswāmī-aṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Sanskrit composition by Śrīla Śrīnivās Āchārya</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇotkīrtana-gāna-nartana-parau premāmṛtāmbho-nidhī</strong><br>
<strong>dhīrādhīra-jana-priyau priya-karau nirmatsarau pūjitau</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-chaitanya-kṛpā-bharau bhuvi bhuvo bhārāvahantārakau</strong><br>
<strong>vande rūpa-sanātanau raghu-yugau śrī-jīva-gopālakau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances unto the Six Goswāmīs, namely Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Śrī Sanātan Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, who are always engaged in chanting the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa and dancing. They are just like the ocean of love of God, and they are popular both with the gentle and with the ruffians, because they are not envious of anyone. Whatever they do, they are all-pleasing to everyone, and they are fully blessed by Lord Chaitanya. Thus they are engaged in missionary activities meant to deliver all the conditioned souls in the material universe.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nānā-śāstra-vichāraṇaika-nipuṇau sad-dharma-saṁsthāpakau</strong><br>
<strong>lokānāṁ hita-kāriṇau tribhuvane mānyau śaraṇyākarau</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-kṛṣṇa-padāravinda-bhajanānandena mattālikau</strong><br>
<strong>vande rūpa-sanātanau raghu-yugau śrī-jīva-gopālakau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances unto the Six Goswāmīs, namely Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Śrī Sanātan Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, who are very expert in scrutinisingly studying all the revealed scriptures with the aim of establishing eternal religious principles for the benefit of all human beings. Thus they are honoured all over the three worlds, and they are worth taking shelter of because they are absorbed in the mood of the gopīs and are engaged in the transcendental loving service of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-gaurāṅga-guṇānuvarṇana-vidhau śraddhā-samṛddhy-anvitau</strong><br>
<strong>pāpottāpa-nikṛntanau tanu-bhṛtāṁ govinda-gānāmṛtaiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>ānandāmbudhi-vardhanaika-nipuṇau kaivalya-nistārakau</strong><br>
<strong>vande rūpa-sanātanau raghu-yugau śrī-jīva-gopālakau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances unto the Six Goswāmīs, namely Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Śrī Sanātan Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, who are very much enriched in the understanding of Lord Chaitanya and who are thus expert in narrating His transcendental qualities. They can purify all conditioned souls from the reactions of their sinful activities by pouring upon them transcendental songs about Govinda. As such, they are very expert in increasing the limits of the ocean of transcendental bliss, and they are the saviours of the living entities from the devouring mouth of liberation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tyaktvā tūrṇam aśeṣa-maṇḍala-pati-śreṇīṁ sadā tuchchhavat</strong><br>
<strong>bhūtvā dīna-gaṇeśakau karuṇayā kaupīna-kanthāśritau</strong><br>
<strong>gopī-bhāva-rasāmṛtābdhi-laharī-kallola-magnau muhur</strong><br>
<strong>vande rūpa-sanātanau raghu-yugau śrī-jīva-gopālakau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances unto the Six Goswāmīs, namely Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Śrī Sanātan Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, who kicked off all association of aristocracy as insignificant. In order to deliver the poor conditioned souls, they accepted loincloths, treating themselves as mendicants, but they are always merged in the ecstatic ocean of the gopīs’ love for Kṛṣṇa and bathe always and repeatedly in the waves of that ocean.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kūjat-kokila-haṁsa-sārasa-gaṇākīrṇe mayūrākule</strong><br>
<strong>nānā-ratna-nibaddha-mūla-viṭapa-śrī-yukta-vṛndāvane</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-kṛṣṇam ahar-niśaṁ prabhajatau jīvārthadau yau mudā</strong><br>
<strong>vande rūpa-sanātanau raghu-yugau śrī-jīva-gopālakau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances unto the Six Goswāmīs, namely Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Śrī Sanātan Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, who were always engaged in worshiping Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa in the transcendental land of Vṛndāvan, where there are beautiful trees full of fruits and flowers which have under their roots all valuable jewels, and which is filled with flocks of kokilas calling melodiously, swans, cranes, and many peacocks – each of them joyfully worship Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa day and night, bestowing the true aim of the goal of life.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saṅkhyā-pūrvaka-nāma-gāna-natibhiḥ kālāvasānī-kṛtau</strong><br>
<strong>nidrāhāra-vihārakādi-vijitau chātyanta-dīnau cha yau</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-kṛṣṇa-guṇa-smṛter madhurimānandena sammohitau</strong><br>
<strong>vande rūpa-sanātanau raghu-yugau śrī-jīva-gopālakau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances unto the Six Goswāmīs, namely Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Śrī Sanātan Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, who were engaged in chanting a fixed number of Holy Names of the Lord and offering obeisances. In this way they utilised their valuable lives. In executing these devotional activities they conquered sleep, eating, recreation, and so on, and were always meek and humble, enchanted by the sweetness arising from remembering the transcendental qualities of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-kuṇḍa-taṭe kalinda-tanayā-tīre cha vaṁśī-vaṭe</strong><br>
<strong>premonmāda-vaśād aśeṣa-daśayā grastau pramattau sadā</strong><br>
<strong>gāyantau cha kadā harer guṇa-varaṁ bhāvābhibhūtau mudā</strong><br>
<strong>vande rūpa-sanātanau raghu-yugau śrī-jīva-gopālakau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances unto the Six Goswāmīs, namely Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Śrī Sanātan Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, who were sometimes on the bank of the Rādhā Kuṇḍa lake or the shores of the Yamunā and sometimes at Vaṁśīvaṭa. There they appeared just like madmen in the full ecstasy of love for Kṛṣṇa, exhibiting different transcendental symptoms in their bodies, and they were merged in the ecstasy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>he rādhe vraja-devīke cha lalite he nanda-sūno kutaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-govardhana-kalpa-pādapa-tale kālindī-vanye kutaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>ghoṣantāv iti sarvato vraja-pure khedair mahāvihvalau</strong><br>
<strong>vande rūpa-sanātanau raghu-yugau śrī-jīva-gopālakau</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my respectful obeisances unto the Six Goswāmīs, namely Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, Śrī Sanātan Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, Śrī Jīva Goswāmī, and Śrī Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī, who were chanting very loudly everywhere in Vṛndāvan, shouting, “Queen of Vṛndāvan, Rādhārāṇī! O Lalitā! O son of Nanda Mahārāj! Where are You all now? Are You just on Govardhan Hill, or are You under the trees on the bank of the Yamunā? Where are You?” These were their moods in executing Kṛṣṇa consciousness.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ki-rupe-paibo-seva-mui-durachara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ki-rūpe Pāiba Sevā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ki-rūpe pāiba sevā muñi durāchāra</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-guru-vaiṣṇave rati nā haila āmāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have no devotional attachment for either the Vaiṣṇavas or my Spiritual Master. How is it possible for a rascal like me to attain devotional service?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aśeṣa māyāte mana magana ha-ila</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇavete leśa-mātra rati nā janmila</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My mind is always drowning in illusion. I have not even the smallest fragment of devotion for the Vaiṣṇavas.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viṣaye bhuliyā andha ha-inu divā-niśi</strong><br>
<strong>gale phā̐sa dite phere māyā se piśāchī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have become blind by constantly meditating on the objects of the senses. The witch Māyā Devī has placed a hangman’s noose around my neck.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>māyāre kariyā jaya chhāḍāna nā yāya</strong><br>
<strong>sādhu-kṛpā vinā āra nāhika upāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Even if I can cure my blindness and defeat Māyā in the form of this hangman, I cannot become free from material illusion without the mercy of the saintly devotees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>adoṣa-daraśi-prabhu patita-uddhāra</strong><br>
<strong>ei-bāra narottame karaha nistāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O master! O purifier of the sinful! O saviour of the fallen souls, you do not see the faults of others; please rescue this Narottam Dās.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-e-ghora-samsare-podiya-manava" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>E Ghora-saṁsāre <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Kālidās</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e ghora-saṁsāre&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paḍiyā mānava</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā pāya duḥkhera śeṣa</strong><br>
<strong>sādhu-saṅga kari’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari bhaje yadi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tabe anta haya kleśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Fallen into this material world of birth and death, there is no end to a person’s unhappiness. Still, if they associate with devotees and worship Lord Hari, then all their sadness and suffering will come to an end.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viṣaya anale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jvalichhe hṛdaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anale bāḍaye anala</strong><br>
<strong>aparādha chhāḍi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;laya kṛṣṇa-nāma</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anale paḍaye jala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The heart burns in the fire of sense gratification and the fire burns more and more. Avoiding offences, chant the Holy Names of Lord Kṛṣṇa, for that will pour water on the fire.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nitāi chaitanya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇa-kamale</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āśraya la-ila yei</strong><br>
<strong>kāli dāsa bale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīvane maraṇe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmāra āśraya sei</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kālidās says, “Anyone who has taken shelter of the lotus feet of Lord Chaitanya and Lord Nityānanda is my shelter in life and death.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-madhava-bahuta-minati-kari-toya" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Mādhava! Bahuta Minati Kari’ Toya <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrī Vidyāpati</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mādhava! bahuta minati kari’ toya</strong><br>
<strong>dei tulasī tila&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deha samarpilu̐</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dayā janu chhāḍabi moya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Mādhava, making numerous earnest prayers, I have offered my body to You with Tulasī and sesame. Please do not withdraw Your mercy from me.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaṇa-ite doṣa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;guṇa-leśa na pāobi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yaba tuhu̐ karabi vichāra</strong><br>
<strong>tuhu̐ jagannātha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jagate kahāosi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaga-bāhira naho̐ muñi chhāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If You examine me, counting my faults, You will not find even a trace of good quality. You are known as Jagannātha, Lord of the Universe; I am not outside that universe – I am one of its fallen souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kiye mānuṣa paśu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pākhī kiye janamiye</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;athavā kīṭa pataṅge</strong><br>
<strong>karama vipāke&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gatāgati punaḥ punaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mati rahu tuyā parasaṅge</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As I come and go again and again, taking birth as a human or an animal, or a bird, or even as a worm or insect as a result of my karma, let my mind remain fixed in attachment to You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaṇaye vidyāpati&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;atiśaya kātara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tara-ite iha bhava-sindhu</strong><br>
<strong>tuyā pada-pallava&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kari avalambana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tila eka deha dīna-bandhu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Vidyāpati says, “I am greatly distressed, and to be delivered from this ocean of material existence, I grasp Your blossom-like feet. O friend of the fallen, please give me one sesame seed of shelter.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-dushta-mana-tumi-kisera-vaishnava" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Vaiṣṇava Ke? <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Who is a Vaiṣṇava?</h3>
<h4>by Bhagavān Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>duṣṭa mana! tumi kisera vaiṣṇava?</strong><br>
<strong>pratiṣṭhāra tare&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirjanera ghare</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tava hari-nāma kevala kaitava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O wicked mind! What kind of Vaiṣṇava do you think you are? Your pretentious show of chanting Lord Hari’s Holy Name in a solitary place is only for the sake of attaining the false prestige of a worldly reputation – it is nothing but pure hypocrisy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaḍera pratiṣṭhā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śūkarera viṣṭhā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāna nā ki tāhā māyāra vaibhava?</strong><br>
<strong>kanaka-kāminī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;divasa yāminī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāviyā ki kāja, anitya se saba</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Such materialistic prestige is as disgusting as the stool of a hog. Do you not know that it is only a mere illusion cast by the potency of Māyā? What is the value of contemplating day and night your plans for enjoying wealth and women? All these things are only temporary.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra kanaka&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhogera janaka</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kanakera dvāre sevaha mādhava</strong><br>
<strong>kāminīra kāma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nahe tava dhāma</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhāra mālika kevala yādava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When you claim wealth as your own, it creates in you ever-increasing desires for material enjoyment. Your riches should be used for serving Mādhava, the Lord of all wealth. Neither is it your proper place to indulge in lust for women, whose only true proprietor is Lord Yādava.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pratiṣṭhāśā-taru&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jaḍa-māyā-maru</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā pela rāvaṇa yujhiyā rāghava</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇavī pratiṣṭhā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāte kara niṣṭhā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhā nā bhajile labhibe raurava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The demon Rāvaṇa (lust-incarnate) fought with Lord Rāmachandra (love-incarnate) in order to gain the tree of worldly reputation – but that oasis turned out to be but a mirage cast in the desert wasteland of the Lord’s illusory material potency. Please cultivate fixed determination to attain only the steady and solid platform whereupon a Vaiṣṇava ever stands. If you neglect worshiping the Lord from this position, then you will ultimately attain a hellish existence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari-jana-dveṣa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pratiṣṭhāśā-kleśa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kara kena tabe tāhāra gaurava</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇavera pāchhe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pratiṣṭhāśā āchhe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāta kabhu nahe anitya-vaibhava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Why do you needlessly suffer the torment of blaspheming the devotees of Lord Hari, attempting to achieve their eminence, thereby only proving your own fruitless foolishness? The desire for spiritual eminence is easily fulfilled when one becomes a devotee of the Lord, for eternal fame automatically follows the heels of a Vaiṣṇava. And that fame is never to be considered a temporary worldly opulence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>se hari-sambandha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śūnya-māyā-gandha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhā kabhu naya jaḍera kaitava</strong><br>
<strong>pratiṣṭhā-chaṇḍālī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirjanatā-jāli</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ubhaye jāniha māyika raurava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The relationship between a devotee and Lord Hari is devoid of even a trace of worldly illusion; it has nothing to do with the materialistic cheating propensity. The prestige of so-called popularity in the material realm is compared to a treacherous dog-eating witch, and the attempt to live in solitude to supposedly engage in unalloyed bhajan is compared to an entangling network of distraction. Please know that anyone striving in either of these ways verily lives in the hell of Māyā’s illusion.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kīrtana chhāḍiba&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pratiṣṭhā māgiba</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ki kāja ḍhu̐ḍiyā tādṛśa gaurava</strong><br>
<strong>mādhavendra-purī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāva-ghare churi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā karila kabhu sadāi jānaba</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“I shall give up chanting the Lord’s Name publicly in kīrtan and retire to solitude, thus smearing myself with worldly honour.” Dear mind, what is the good of seeking such so-called glory? I will always remind you that the great soul Mādhavendra Purī never deceived himself in that regard by committing theft in his own storehouse of perception the way you do.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra pratiṣṭhā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śūkarera viṣṭhā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāra-saha sama kabhu nā mānaba</strong><br>
<strong>matsaratā-vaśe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi jaḍa-rase</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;majechha chhāḍiyā kīrtana-sauṣṭava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your cheap reputation is equal to the stool of a hog. An ordinary ambitious man like you can never be equated with a devotee of Mādhavendra Purī’s eminence. Under the sway of envy, you have drowned yourself in the filthy waters of material enjoyment after having abandoned the excellent perfection of congregational kīrtan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tāi duṣṭa mana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirjana bhajana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prachārichha chhale kuyogī-vaibhava</strong><br>
<strong>prabhu sanātane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama yatane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śikṣā dila yāhā, chinta’ sei saba</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Truly, O wicked mind, the glories of so-called solitary worship are propagated only by false yogīs using unscrupulous means to deceive others. To save yourself from these pitfalls, please contemplate the instructions that the Supreme Lord Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu kindly gave us while addressing Śrīla Sanātan Goswāmī with the utmost care.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sei duṭi kathā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhula’ nā sarvathā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uchchaiḥsvare kara hari-nāma-rava</strong><br>
<strong>phalgu āra yukta&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baddha āra mukta</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kabhu nā bhāviha ‘ekākāra’ saba</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Do not forget for a moment the two most valuable concepts that He taught: 1) the principle of dry, apparent renunciation as opposed to real, appropriate renunciation; and 2) the principle of a soul being trapped in the bondage of matter as opposed to a soul who is liberated. Don’t ever make the mistake of thinking that these conflicting concepts are on the same level. Please remember this while engaging yourself in chanting the Lord’s Holy Names as loud as you possibly can.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kanaka-kāminī-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pratiṣṭhā-vāghinī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhāḍiyāchhe yāre sei ta’ vaiṣṇava</strong><br>
<strong>sei anāsakta&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei śuddha-bhakta</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāra tathā pāya parābhava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One is truly a Vaiṣṇava who has given up the habit of falling victim to the ferocious tigress of wealth, beauty, and fame. Such a soul is factually detached from material life, and is known as a pure devotee. Someone with this consciousness of detachment has thereby become victorious over the mundane world of birth and death.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yathā-yogya bhoga&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāhi tathā roga</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anāsakta sei, ki āra kahaba</strong><br>
<strong>āsakti-rahita&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sambandha-sahita</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viṣaya-samūha sakali mādhava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One is indeed detached who moderately partakes of worldly things that are deemed necessary for living in devotional service; a devotee acting in that manner does not fall prey to the disease of material infatuation. Thus devoid of selfish attachment, and endowed with the ability to see things in relation to the Lord, all sense objects are then directly perceived as being Lord Mādhava Himself.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>se yukta-vairāgya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhā ta’ saubhāgya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhāi jaḍete harira vaibhava</strong><br>
<strong>kīrtane yāhāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pratiṣṭhā-sambhāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhāra sampatti kevala kaitava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This is the standard of befitting renunciation, and one who realises this is most fortunate indeed. Everything involved in such a devotee’s life represents Lord Hari’s personal spiritual opulence as manifest in the world of matter. On the other hand, one who engages in chanting the Lord’s Name with hopes of enhancing his own material reputation finds that all his activities and paraphernalia represent only the riches of hypocrisy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viṣaya-mumukṣu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhogera bubhukṣu</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;duye tyaja mana, dui avaiṣṇava</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇera sambandha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;aprākṛta-skandha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kabhu nāhe tāhā jaḍera sambhava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O mind, please reject the company of two types of persons – those desiring impersonal liberation from the material world, and those who desire to enjoy the pleasure of material sense objects. Both of these are equally non-devotees. The things that are used in relation to Lord Kṛṣṇa are objects belonging directly to the transcendental realm, and thus having nothing to do with matter, they cannot be either owned or forsaken by persons interested in mundane enjoyment or renunciation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>māyāvādī-jana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇetara-mana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mukta abhimāne se ninde vaiṣṇava</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇavera dāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tava bhakti-āśa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kena vā ḍākichha nirjana-āhava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">An impersonal philospher is opposed to thinking of Kṛṣṇa as an object of devotion, and thus being puffed up with the false pride of imaginary liberation, he dares to criticise the true devotees of the Lord. O mind, you are the servant of the Vaiṣṇavas, and you should always hope for attaining devotion. Why then do you make such a loud commotion by calling to me and trying to prove the supposed supremacy of your practice of solitary worship?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ye phalgu-vairāgī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kahe nije tyāgī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se nā pāre kabhu ha-ite vaiṣṇava</strong><br>
<strong>hari-pada chhāḍi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirjanatā bāḍi’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;labhiyā ki phala, phalgu se vaibhava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who falsely gives up things that could actually be used in the Lord’s service proudly calls himself a ‘renunciant’, but unfortunately he can never become a Vaiṣṇava by such an attitude. Abandoning his servitorship to the lotus feet of Lord Hari, and resigning himself to his solitary home – whatever is gained by that exercise can only be the worthless treasure of deception.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="17">
<h5>17</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-dāsye rahi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhāḍa bhoga-ahi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pratiṣṭhāśā nahe kīrtana-gaurava</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-nitya-jana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhā chhāḍi’ mana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kena vā nirjana-bhajana-kaitava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Ever engage yourself in the service of Śrī Rādhā, and keep aloof from the vicious snake of materialistic sense gratification. The glory of participating in the Lord’s kīrtan is not meant to bolster anyone’s ambitions for personal recognition. O mind, why then have you abandoned the identity of being Rādhā’s eternal servant in favour of retiring to a solitary place to practise the cheating process of so-called bhajan?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="18">
<h5>18</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vraja-vāsi-gaṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prachāraka-dhana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pratiṣṭhā-bhikṣuka tārā nahe śava</strong><br>
<strong>prāṇa āchhe tāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se hetu prachāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pratiṣṭhāśā-hīna kṛṣṇa-gāthā saba</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The most valuable treasures amongst the Lord’s preachers are the eternal personalities residing in Vraja Dhām. They never occupy themselves with begging for worthless material reputation, which is cherished only by the living dead. The Vraja-vāsīs are truly infused with life, and therefore they preach in order to give life to the walking corpses of the mundane world. All the songs that the Vraja-vāsīs sing about the glories of Lord Kṛṣṇa are devoid of any tinge of desire for fame.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="19">
<h5>19</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-dayita-dāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kīrtanete āśa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kara uchchaiḥsvare hari-nāma-rava</strong><br>
<strong>kīrtana-prabhāve&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;smaraṇa ha-ibe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se kāle bhajana-nirjana sambhava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This humble servant of Rādhā and Her beloved Kṛṣṇa always hopes for kīrtan, and he begs all to loudly sing the Names of Lord Hari. The transcendental power of congregational chanting automatically awakens remembrance of the Lord and His divine Pastimes in relation to one’s own eternal spiritual form. Only at that time does it become possible to go off to a solitary place and engage in the confidential worship of Their Lordships.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhagavad-bhaktitah-sarvam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>You Will Receive Everything <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<p class="translation">In a verse from his <i>Prapanna-jīvanāmṛtam</i> Śrīla Guru Mahārāj has given us a very nice, clear explanation of śaraṇāgati that is a perfect jewel you cannot find anywhere else. It is the most essential prescription given anywhere for everyone to enter the transcendental service world. If you can memorise just this one verse and put it in your heart, then you will receive everything ever given in any scripture.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>bhagavad-bhaktitaḥ sarvam ity utsṛjya vidher api</strong><br>
<strong>kaiṅkaryaṁ kṛṣṇa-pādaikāśrayatvaṁ śaraṇāgatiḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrī Śrī Prapanna-jīvanāmṛtam: 1.35)</i><br>
Here Śrīla Guru Mahārāj expresses that śaraṇāgati means faith that Bhagavad-bhaktitaḥ sarvam, that through devotion to Bhagavān you will get everything. Bhagavān means the Supreme Personality of Godhead Kṛṣṇa, the Akhila-rasāmṛta-mūrti, the emporium of all rasa, who possesses all worshippable qualities. Everything you want and need – rasa, ecstasy, joy, nourishment, and so on – will automatically come to you through His service. Your life will be glorious, successful, and perfect through devotion to Lord Kṛṣṇa. Not only will you receive everything you need internally, but full facility to serve Kṛṣṇa will also come to you.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhajahu-re-mana-shri-nanda-nandana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Bhajahu̐ Re Mana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Govinda Dās Kavirāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhajahu̐ re mana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-nanda-nandana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;abhaya-charaṇāravinda re</strong><br>
<strong>durlabha mānava&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;janama sat-saṅge</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;taraha e bhava-sindhu re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O mind, just worship the lotus feet of the son of Nanda, which make one fearless. Having obtained this rare human birth, cross over this ocean of worldly existence through the association of saintly persons.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śīta ātapa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vāta variṣaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;e dina yāminī jāgi re</strong><br>
<strong>viphale sevinu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛpaṇa durajana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chapala sukha-lava lāgi’ re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Both in the day and at night I remain sleepless, suffering the pains of the heat and cold, the wind and the rain. For a fraction of flickering happiness I have uselessly served wicked and miserly men.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e dhana, yauvana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;putra, parijana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ithe ki āchhe paratīti re</strong><br>
<strong>kamala-dala-jala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīvana ṭalamala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(ei āchhe ei nāi jīvana ṭalamala)<br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhajahu̐ hari-pada niti re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">What assurance of real happiness is there in all of one’s wealth, youthfulness, sons, and family members? This life is tottering like a drop of water on a lotus petal (it is here, and then it is gone); therefore, you should always serve and worship the divine feet of Lord Hari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śravaṇa, kīrtana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;smaraṇa, vandana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāda-sevana, dāsya re</strong><br>
<strong>pūjana, sakhī-jana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ātma-nivedana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;govinda-dāsa-abhilāṣa re</strong></p>
<p class="translation">It is the desire and great longing of Govinda Dās to engage himself in the nine processes of bhakti, namely: hearing the glories of Lord Hari and chanting those glories, constantly remembering Him and offering prayers to Him, serving the Lord’s lotus feet, serving the Supreme Lord as a servant, worshiping Him with flowers and incense and so forth, serving Him as a friend, and completely offering the Lord one’s very self.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-manasa-deho-geho-jo-kichu-mora" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Mānasa Deha Geha <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mānasa, deha, geha, yo kichhu mora</strong><br>
<strong>arpilu̐ tuyā pade nanda-kiśora!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Mind, body, and family, whatever may be mine, I have surrendered at Your lotus feet, O youthful son of Nanda!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sampade vipade jīvane maraṇe</strong><br>
<strong>dāy mama gelā tuyā o-pada varaṇe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In good fortune or in bad, in life or at death, all my difficulties have disappeared by choosing those feet of Yours as my only shelter.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mārabi rākhabi – yo ichchhā tohārā</strong><br>
<strong>nitya-dāsa prati tuyā adhikārā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Slay me or protect me as You wish, for You are the master of Your eternal servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>janmāobi maye ichchhā yadi tora</strong><br>
<strong>bhakta-gṛhe jani janma ha-u mora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If it is Your will that I be born again, then may it be in the home of Your devotee.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kīṭa-janma ha-u yathā tuyā dāsa</strong><br>
<strong>bahir-mukha brahma-janme nāhi āśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">May I be born again even as a worm, as long as I may remain Your devotee. I have no desire to be born as a Brahmā averse to You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhukti-mukti-spṛhā-vihīna ye bhakta</strong><br>
<strong>labha-ite tā̐ka saṅga anurakta</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I yearn for the company of the devotee completely devoid of all desire for worldly enjoyment or liberation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>janaka, jananī, dayita, tanaya</strong><br>
<strong>prabhu, guru, pati – tuhu̐ sarva-maya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Father, mother, lover, son, Lord, preceptor, and husband – You are everything to me.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakati-vinoda kahe, śuna kāna!</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-nātha! tuhu̐ hāmāra parāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod says, “O Kṛṣṇa, please hear me! O Lord of Rādhā, You are my life and soul!”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-emona-durmati-samsara-bhitore" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Dainyātmikā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prabhu he!</strong><br>
<strong>emana durmati&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāra bhitare</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paḍiyā āchhinu āmi</strong><br>
<strong>tava nija-jana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kona mahājane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāṭhāiyā dile tumi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord! A wicked mind brought me into this world, but one of Your pure and elevated devotees has come to bring me out.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dayā kari’ more&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;patita dekhiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kahila āmāre giyā</strong><br>
<strong>ohe dīna-jana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuna bhāla kathā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ullasita habe hiyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He saw me so fallen and wretched, took pity, and came to me saying, “O humbled soul, please listen to this good tiding, for it will gladden your heart.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāre tārite&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;navadvīpe avatāra</strong><br>
<strong>tomā hena kata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dīna-hīna jane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karilena bhava-pāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya has appeared in the land of Nabadwīp to deliver you. He has safely conducted many miserable souls such as you across the sea of worldly existence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vedera pratijñā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rākhibāra tare</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rukma-varṇa vipra-suta</strong><br>
<strong>mahāprabhu nāme&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nadīyā mātāya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṅge bhāi avadhūta</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“To fulfil the promise of the Vedas, the son of a brāhmaṇa, bearing the name Mahāprabhu, of golden complexion, has descended with His brother, the avadhūt Nityānanda. Together They have overwhelmed all of Nadia with divine ecstasy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nanda-suta yini&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chaitanya gosāñī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nija nāma kari’ dāna</strong><br>
<strong>tārila jagat&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumio yāiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;laha nija paritrāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Śrī Chaitanya, who is Kṛṣṇa Himself, the son of Nanda, has saved the world by freely distributing His own Holy Name. Go also and receive your deliverance.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>se kathā śuniyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āsiyāchhi, nātha!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra charaṇa-tale</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-vinoda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐diyā kā̐diyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āpana-kāhinī bale</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, hearing those words, Bhakti Vinod has come weeping to the soles of Your lotus feet and tells the story of his life.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ki-jani-ki-bale-tomara-dhamete" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Goptṛtve Varaṇa <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ki jāni ki bale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra dhāmete</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ha-inu śaraṇāgata</strong><br>
<strong>tumi dayāmaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;patita-pāvana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;patita-tāraṇe rata</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By what personal knowledge or strength has one such as I come to Your shelter? Surely it is by Your mercy alone, for You are ever seeking the purification and deliverance of the fallen souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bharasā āmāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ei mātra nātha!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi ta’ karuṇā-maya</strong><br>
<strong>tava dayā-pātra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāhi mora sama</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avaśya ghuchābe bhaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are my only hope, for You are full of compassion and mercy. There is no one who needs Your mercy more than I. You will surely drive away all fear.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmāre tārite&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāhāra śakati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avanī-bhitare nāhi</strong><br>
<strong>dayāla ṭhākura!&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ghoṣaṇā tomāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;adhama pāmare trāhi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">No one else has the power to deliver me. O merciful Lord, by Your declaration, kindly deliver this vile and lowly sinner.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sakala chhāḍiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āsiyāchhi āmi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra charaṇe nātha!</strong><br>
<strong>āmi nitya-dāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi pālayitā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi goptā jagannātha!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have given up everything and come to Your lotus feet. I am Your eternal servant, and You are my protector and maintainer, O Lord of the universe.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra sakala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmi mātra dāsa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmāre tāribe tumi</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra charaṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karinu varaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmāra nahi ta āmi</strong></p>
