<p class="translation">Everything is Yours. I am merely a servant, certain that You will deliver me. I have chosen Your lotus feet as my only shelter. I no longer belong to myself.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakati-vinoda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐diya śaraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;layechhe tomāra pāya</strong><br>
<strong>kṣami’ aparādha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāme ruchi diyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pālana karahe tāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Weeping, Bhakti Vinod takes shelter at Your feet. Forgive his offences, afford him a taste for the Holy Name, and kindly maintain him.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhuliya-tomare-samsare-asiya" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Dainya <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhuliyā tomāre&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāre āsiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;peye nānā-vidha vyathā</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra charaṇe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āsiyāchhi āmi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baliba duḥkhera kathā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, forgetting you and coming to this material world, I have experienced various pains and sorrows. Now I am approaching your lotus feet to submit my tale of woe.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jananī-jaṭhare&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhilāma yakhana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viṣama bandhana-pāśe</strong><br>
<strong>eka-bāra prabhu!&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhā diyā more</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vañchile e dīna dāse</strong></p>
<p class="translation">While I was bound up tightly in the unbearable confines of my mother’s womb, O Lord, You once revealed Yourself before me, but briefly, and then abandoned this poor servant of Yours.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>takhana bhāvinu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;janama pāiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kariba bhajana tava</strong><br>
<strong>janama ha-ila&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paḍi’ māyā-jāle</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā ha-ila jñāna-lava</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At that moment, I promised that having taken birth I would worship you. But after taking birth, I fell into the entangling network of worldly illusion, devoid of even a drop of true knowledge.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ādarera chhele&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;svajanera kole</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hāsiyā kāṭānu kāla</strong><br>
<strong>janaka-jananī-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;snehete bhuliyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāra lāgila bhāla</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As a boy cradled in the lap of relatives, I passed my time smiling and laughing. My parents’ affection helped me to forget You still more, my Lord, and I began to think the material world to be a very nice place.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>krame dina dina&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bālaka ha-iyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khelinu bālaka saha</strong><br>
<strong>āra kichhu-dine&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jñāna upajila</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāṭha paḍi ahar-ahaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Day by day I grew and soon began playing with other boys. My powers of understanding emerged and I read and studied my school lessons incessantly.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vidyāra gaurave&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhrami’ deśe deśe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dhana upārjana kari’</strong><br>
<strong>svajana-pālana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kari eka-mane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhulinu tomāre hari!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Travelling from place to place, proud of my education, I grew wealthy and maintained my family with undivided attention. O Lord Hari, I forgot You!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vārddhakye ekhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakati-vinoda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐diyā kātara ati</strong><br>
<strong>nā bhajiyā tore&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dina vṛthā gela</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ekhana ki ha’be gati?</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Now in old age, this Bhakti Vinod very sadly weeps. I failed to worship You, O Lord, and instead passed my life in vain. What will be my fate now?</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hari-he-prapanche-podiya-agati-hoiya" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Bhajan-lālasā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Kīrtan One</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari he!</strong><br>
<strong>prapañche poḍiyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;agati hoiyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā dekhi upāya āra</strong><br>
<strong>agatira gati,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇe śaraṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāya karinu sāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my Lord Hari! Having fallen into the illusion of this world, thus rendered helpless, I see no other means of deliverance but You. Since You are the only recourse for the helpless, I accept the shelter of Your lotus feet as most essential.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>karama geyāna,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kichhu nāhi mora,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sādhana bhajana nāi</strong><br>
<strong>tumi kṛpā-maya,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmi to’ kāṅgāla,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ahaitukī kṛpā chāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have no background of pious activities, nor any knowledge, nor any regulated devotional practice. But You are full of compassion and kindness; therefore I, being indeed destitute, solicit Your causeless mercy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vākya-mana-vega,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;krodha-jihvā-vega,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;udara-upastha-vega</strong><br>
<strong>miliyā e saba,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāre bhāsāye,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ditechhe paramodvega</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The powerful urges of speech, mind, anger, tongue, belly, and genitals have banded together to cast me adrift on the sea of this material world, thus causing me grievous trouble.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aneka jatane,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se saba damane,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhāḍiyāchhi āśā āmi</strong><br>
<strong>anāthera nātha!&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ḍāki tava nāma,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ekhana bharasā tumi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After numerous endeavours to subdue these material demands, I have completely given up all hope. O Lord of the destitute! I call upon Your Holy Name, for now You are my only hope.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-hari-hari-biphole-janama-goyainu" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Iṣṭa-deve Vijñapti <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hari hari!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viphale janama goṅāinu</strong><br>
<strong>manuṣya-janama pāiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-kṛṣṇa nā bhajiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāniyā śuniyā viṣa khāinu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord Hari, I have spent my life uselessly. Having obtained a human birth and having not worshipped Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, I have knowingly drunk poison.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>golokera prema-dhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-nāma-saṅkīrtana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rati nā janmila kena tāya</strong><br>
<strong>saṁsāra-viṣānale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;divā-niśi hiyā jvale</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;juḍāite nā kainu upāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The treasure of divine love in Goloka Vṛndāvan has descended as the congregational chanting of Lord Hari’s Holy Names. Why did my attraction for that chanting never come about? Day and night my heart burns from the fire of the poison of worldliness, and I have not taken the means to relieve it.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vrajendra-nandana yei&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śachī-suta haila sei</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;balarāma ha-ila nitāi</strong><br>
<strong>dīna-hīna yata chhila&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-nāme uddhārila</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāra śākṣī jagāi mādhāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Kṛṣṇa, who is the son of the King of Vraja, became the son of Śachī (Lord Chaitanya), and Balarām became Nitāi. The Holy Name delivered all those souls who were lowly and wretched. The two sinners Jagāi and Mādhāi are evidence of this.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hā hā prabhu nanda-suta&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛṣabhānu-sutā-yuta</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karuṇā karaha ei-bāra</strong><br>
<strong>narottama-dāsa kaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā ṭheliha rāṅgā pāya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomā vine ke āchhe āmāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord Kṛṣṇa, son of Nanda, accompanied by the daughter of Vṛṣabhānu, please be merciful to me now. Narottam Dās says, “O Lord, please do not push me away from Your reddish lotus feet, for who is my beloved except for You?”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-atma-nivedana-tuya-pade-kori" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ātma-nivedana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ātma-nivedana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tuyā pade kari</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ha-inu parama sukhī</strong><br>
<strong>duḥkha dūre gela&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chintā nā rahila</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chaudike ānanda dekhi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have become supremely joyful by surrendering myself at Your holy feet. Unhappiness has gone away, and there are no more anxieties. I see joy in all directions.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aśoka-abhaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;amṛta-ādhāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra charaṇa-dvaya</strong><br>
<strong>tāhāte ekhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśrāma labhiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhāḍinu bhavera bhaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Your two lotus feet are reservoirs of immortal nectar where one may live free from sorrow and fear. I have found peace there now and have given up the fear of worldly existence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra saṁsāre&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kariba sevana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nahiba phalera bhāgī</strong><br>
<strong>tava sukha yāhe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kariba yatana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ha’ye pade anurāgī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I shall render service in Your household and not endeavour to enjoy the fruits of that service, but rather I shall strive for whatever pleases You, fully devoted to Your lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra sevāya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;duḥkha haya yata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;seo ta’ parama sukha</strong><br>
<strong>sevā-sukha-duḥkha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama sampada</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāśaye avidyā-duḥkha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Troubles encountered in Your service shall be the cause of great happiness, for in Your devotional service joy and sorrow are equally great riches. Both destroy the misery of ignorance.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pūrva itihāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhulinu sakala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sevā-sukha pe’ye mane</strong><br>
<strong>āmi ta’ tomāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi ta’ āmāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ki kāja apara dhane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have completely forgotten all past history by feeling great joy in my mind. I am most certainly Yours, and You are indeed mine. What need is there of any other treasure?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakati-vinoda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānande ḍubiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra sevāra tare</strong><br>
<strong>saba cheṣṭā kare&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tava ichhā-mata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;thākiyā tomāra ghare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod, diving into the ocean of bliss, devotes all his efforts for Your service and dwells in Your house according to Your wishes.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-amara-jivana-sada-pape-rata" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Āmāra Jīvana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmāra jīvana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā pāpe rata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāhika puṇyera leśa</strong><br>
<strong>parere udvega&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;diyāchhi ye kata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;diyāchhi jīvere kleśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My life is always attached to sinful activity and there is not even a small part of goodness in it. I have caused others anxiety and given trouble to other souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nija sukha lāgi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāpe nāhi ḍari</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dayā-hīna svārtha-para</strong><br>
<strong>para-sukhe duḥkhī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā mithya-bhāṣī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;para-duḥkha sukha-kara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">For the sake of my own happiness I have openly performed sinful activity. Devoid of compassion, I am concerned only with my own selfish interests. Always telling lies, I become miserable to see the happiness of others, while the misery of others brings great happiness to me.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aśeṣa kāmanā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hṛdi mājhe mora</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;krodhī, dambha-parāyaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>mada-matta sadā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viṣaye mohita</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hiṁsā-garva vibhūṣana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There are limitless material desires within the core of my heart. I am full of anger, attached to arrogance, intoxicated by vanity, and always bewildered by enjoying sense objects. Violence and pride are my ornaments.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nidrālasya hata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sukārye virata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;akārye udyogī āmi</strong><br>
<strong>pratiṣṭhā lāgiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śāṭhya-ācharaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lobha-hata sadā kāmī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Ruined by laziness and sleep, I resist all pious deeds, yet am very enthusiastic to perform wicked acts. For the sake of worldly fame and reputation I engage in the practice of deceitfulness. I am always lustful and a victim of my own greed.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e hena durjana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sajjana-varjita</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;aparādhi nirantara</strong><br>
<strong>śubha-kārya-śūnya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadānartha-manāḥ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nānā duḥkhe jara jara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">A vile, wicked person such as this, rejected by saintly persons, is a constant offender, devoid of auspicious works, always in the mentality of separate interest, and wasted away by various kinds of miseries.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vārdhakye ekhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;upāya-vihīna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā’te dīna akiñchana</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-vinoda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prabhura charaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kare duḥkha nivedana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Now in old age, deprived of all means of relief, thus humbled and destitute, this Bhakti Vinod submits his tale of grief at the feet of the Supreme Lord.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-tumi-sarveshvareshvara-vrajendra-kumara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Tumi Sarveśvareśvara <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tumi sarveśvareśvara, vrajendra-kumāra!</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra ichchhāya viśve sṛjana saṁhāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O youthful son of the King of Vraja, You are the Lord of all lords. According to Your will, creation and destruction take place in the universe.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tava ichchhā-mata brahmā karena sṛjana</strong><br>
<strong>tava ichchhā-mata viṣṇu karena pālana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">According to Your desire Lord Brahmā creates and according to Your desire Lord Viṣṇu maintains.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tava ichchhā-mata śiva karena saṁhāra</strong><br>
<strong>tava ichchhā-mate māyā sṛje kārāgāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">According to Your will Lord Śiva destroys, according to Your will Māyā constructs the prison house of this world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tava ichchhā-mata jīvera janama-maraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>samṛddhi-nipāta duḥkha sukha-saṁghaṭana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">According to Your will the living beings take birth and die, and according to Your will they meet with prosperity and ruin, happiness and sorrow.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>michhe māyā-baddha jīva āśā-pāśe phire</strong><br>
<strong>tava ichchhā vinā kichhu karite nā pāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The tiny soul bound up by Māyā vainly struggles in the fetters of worldly desire. Without Your sanction the soul is unable to do anything.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tumi ta’ rakṣaka āra pālaka āmāra</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra charaṇa vinā āśā nāhi āra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are my only protector and maintainer. Except for Your lotus feet there is no other hope for me.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nija-bala-cheṣṭā prati bharasā chhāḍiyā</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra ichchhāya āchhi nirbhara kariyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">No longer confident of my own strength and endeavour, I have become solely dependent on Your will.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakati-vinoda ati dīna akiñchana</strong><br>
<strong>tomāra ichchhāya tā’ra jīvana maraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod is most poor, and his pride has been levelled. Now in accordance with Your will he lives and dies.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-sarvasva-tomara-charane-sanpiya" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Sarvasva Tomāra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarvasva tomāra,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇe sa̐piyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paḍechhi tomāra ghare</strong><br>
<strong>tumi ta’ ṭhākura,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra kukkura,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baliyā jānaha more</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Now taking all that I possess and surrendering it unto Your lotus feet, O Lord, I throw myself down before Your house. You are the master of the house; kindly consider me Your own dog.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bā̐dhiyā nikaṭe,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmāre pālibe,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rahiba tomāra dvāre</strong><br>
<strong>pratīpa-janere,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āsite nā diba,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rākhiba gaḍera pāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Chaining me nearby, You will maintain me, and I shall lie at Your doorstep. I will not allow Your enemies to enter, but will keep them outside the bounds of the surrounding moat.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tava nija-jana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prasāda seviyā,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uchchhiṣṭa rākhibe yāhā</strong><br>
<strong>āmāra bhojana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama-ānande,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prati-dina habe tāhā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Whatever food remnants Your devotees leave behind after honouring Your prasād will be my daily sustenance. I will feast on those remnants in great bliss.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>basiyā śuiyā,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāra charaṇa,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chintiba satata āmi</strong><br>
<strong>nāchite nāchite,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nikaṭe yāiba,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yakhana ḍākibe tumi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">While sitting up or lying down, I will constantly meditate on Your lotus feet. Whenever You call me, I will immediately run to You and dance in rapture.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nijera poṣana,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kabhu nā bhāviba,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rahiba bhāvera bhare</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-vinoda,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāre pālaka,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baliyā varaṇa kare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will never even think about arranging for my own nourishment, and will remain absorbed in ever-cherishing love for my master. Bhakti Vinod now accepts You as his only maintainer.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-tatala-saikate-vari-bindu-sama" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Tātala Saikate <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrī Vidyāpati</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tātala saikate&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vāri bindu-sama</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;suta-mita-ramaṇī-samāje</strong><br>
<strong>tohe visari mana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe samarpala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;taba majhu haba kona kāje</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, completely forgetting You, I have offered my mind unto the society of women, children, and friends – but this experience has been just like offering a drop of water unto the burning hot sands of the beach. How can I possibly be relieved of this great misery?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mādhava! hāma pariṇāma-nirāśā</strong><br>
<strong>tuhu̐ jaga-tāraṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dīna-dayāmaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ataye tohāri viśoyāsā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Madhava! As a consequence, I am rendered totally despondent. You are the savior of the universe, and are merciful to the helpless souls. Therefore, I place my hope only in You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ādha janama hāma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nide̐ ga̐yāola</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jarā śiśu kata-dina gelā</strong><br>
<strong>nidhuvane ramaṇī-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rasa-raṅge mātala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tohe bhajaba kona belā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Wandering about in a half-alive condition, I spent my life in utter disgrace. Uncountable days passed as a frivolous child and a useless old man. I have been intoxicated by the pleasure of sharing romantic adventures with beautiful young women. When will I ever get a chance to worship You?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kata chaturānana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mari mari yāota</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;na tuyā ādi avasānā</strong><br>
<strong>tohe janami puna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tohe samāota</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sāgara-laharī samānā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Innumerable Brahmās have died one after another, whereas You are without beginning or end. All of them take birth from You and are again absorbed in You, just like the waves in the ocean.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaṇaye vidyāpati&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śeṣa śamana bhaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tuyā vinu gati nāhi ārā</strong><br>
<strong>ādi anādika&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nātha kahāosi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;aba tāraṇa-bhāra tohārā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Vidyāpati confesses that now, at the end of his life, he is fearful of death. O Lord! There is no shelter other than You. You will always remain celebrated as being the Lord of both that which has beginning and that which is beginningless. Now the responsibility for my deliverance from the material world is entirely Yours.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-atma-samarpane-gela-abhimana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Ātma-samarpaṇe <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ātma-samarpaṇe gelā abhimāna</strong><br>
<strong>nāhi karabu̐ nija rakṣā-vidhāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Surrendering my soul unto You has lifted from me the burden of false pride. No longer will I try to provide for my own safety.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tuyā dhana jāni’ tuhu̐ rākhabi nātha!</strong><br>
<strong>pālya go-dhana jāni kari’ tuyā sātha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I know that You will give protection to Your treasured possessions, O Lord. I now understand the mentality of Your treasured cows safely maintained by Your side.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>charāobi mādhava! yāmuna-tīre</strong><br>
<strong>vaṁśī bājāota ḍakabi dhīre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When You lead Your herds to pasture, O Mādhava, on the banks of the Yamunā river, You will call to them by softly playing on Your flute.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>agha-baka mārata rakṣā vidhāna</strong><br>
<strong>karabi sadā tuhu̐ gokula-kāna!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By slaying great demons such as Aghāsura and Bakāsura, You will always provide full protection, O Kān of Gokul!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rakṣā karabi tuhu̐ niśchaya jāni</strong><br>
<strong>pāna karabu̐ hāma yāmuna-pāni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Fearless and confident of Your protection, I will drink the waters of the Yamunā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kālīya-dokha karabi vināśā</strong><br>
<strong>śodhabi nadī-jala bāḍāobi āśā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Kālīya serpent’s venom poisoned the Yamunā’s waters, yet that poison will be vanquished. You will purify the Yamunā, and by such heroic deeds enhance our faith.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>piyata dāvānala rakhabi moya</strong><br>
<strong>‘gopāla’ ‘govinda’ nāma tava hoya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You will surely protect me by swallowing the forest fire. Thus You are called Gopāl (protector of the cows) and Govinda (pleaser of the cows).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>surapati durmati-nāśa vichāri’</strong><br>
<strong>rākhabi varṣaṇe giri-vara-dhāri!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In order to curb the malice of Indra, king of the demigods, You will protect me from his torrents of rain, O lifter of the mighty Govardhan Hill!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chaturānana karaba yava chori</strong><br>
<strong>rakṣā karabi moye gokula-hari!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When the four-headed Brahmā abducts me along with Your cowherd boyfriends and calves, then also You will surely protect me, O Gokul Hari!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakati-vinoda tuyā gokula-dhana</strong><br>
<strong>rākhabi keśava! karata yatana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod is now the property of Gokul, Your holy abode. O Keśava! Kindly protect him with gentle loving care.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-kobe-hobe-bolo-se-dina-amar" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Kabe Ha’be Bala <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kabe ha’be bala se-dina āmāra</strong><br>
(āmār)<strong> aparādha ghuchi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuddha nāme ruchi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛpā-bale ha’be hṛdaye sañchāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please tell me, when will that day be mine – when (my) offences will end and a taste for the pure Holy Name will be infused within my heart by the power of divine grace?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tṛṇādhika hīna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kabe nije māni</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sahiṣṇutā-guṇa hṛdayete āni’</strong><br>
<strong>sakale mānada&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āpani amānī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;haye āsvādiba nāma-rasa-sāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Considering myself lower than a blade of grass, bringing the quality of forbearance into my heart, showing respect to all, myself being freed from all false pride – when will I taste the essence of the liquid nectar of the Holy Name?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhana jana āra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kavitā, sundarī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baliba nā chāhi deha-sukha-karī</strong><br>
<strong>janme-janme dāo&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ohe gaurahari!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ahaitukī-bhakti charaṇe tomāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Wealth, following, beautiful women, as described in worldly poetry – I do not want any such bodily pleasures. O Lord Gaurahari, please give me unmotivated devotion to Your lotus feet, birth after birth.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse">(kabe)<strong> karite śrī-kṛṣṇa-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāma uchāraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pulakita deha gadgada vachana</strong><br>
<strong>vaivarṇya-vepathu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;habe saṅghaṭana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirantara netre va’be aśru-dhāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">(When,) while articulating the divine Name of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, will my body be thrilled in ecstatic rapture and my words choked with emotion? When will pallor and ecstatic trembling occur, and when will streams of tears flow constantly from my eyes?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabe navadvīpe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;suradhunī-taṭe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘gaura-nityānanda’ bali’ niṣkapaṭe</strong><br>
<strong>nāchiyā gāiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;beḍāiba chhuṭe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vātulera prāya chhāḍiyā vichāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When, in the land of Nabadwīp, on the banks of the Ganges, will I run about, guilelessly calling, “O Gaura! O Nityānanda!” dancing and singing like a madman, giving up all considerations?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabe nityānanda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;more kari’ dayā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhāḍāibe mora viṣayera māyā</strong><br>
<strong>diyā more nija-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇera chhāyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāmera hāṭete dibe adhikāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will Lord Nityānanda be merciful to me and release me from the illusion of worldliness? Giving me the shade of His lotus feet, when will He allow me to enter the marketplace of the Holy Name?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kiniba, luṭiba&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-nāma-rasa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāma-rase māti’ ha-iba vivaśa</strong><br>
<strong>rasera rasika-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇa paraśa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kariyā majiba rase anibāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I shall buy and plunder the mellow of the Name Hari, and becoming thoroughly intoxicated by those liquid mellows of the Holy Name, I shall become stunned. By touching the feet of those great souls who are able to relish those mellows, I will be constantly immersed in the sweet nectar of the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabe jīve dayā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ha-ibe udaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nija-sukha bhuli’ sudīna-hṛdaya</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-vinoda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kariyā vinaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-ājñā-ṭahala karibe prachāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will there be an awakening of compassion for all fallen souls, and when will this Bhakti Vinod, forgetting his own happines, with a meek heart set out to propagate by humble entreaty the sacred order of Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu?</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-durlabha-manava-janma-labhiya-samsare" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Durlabha Mānava-janma <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>durlabha mānava-janma labhiyā saṁsāre</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa nā bhajinu duḥkha kahiba kāhāre?</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The human form of life is the rarest opportunity for attaining spiritual perfection. Now I am lamenting because I’ve somehow or other been born with such an opportunity, but I wasted it by never worshiping Lord Kṛṣṇa. O, to whom shall I tell the tale of this misery?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘saṁsāra’ ‘saṁsāra’ kari’ michhe gela kāla</strong><br>
<strong>lābha nā ha-ila kichhu, ghaṭila jañjāla</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Having married and entered into the entanglements of materialistic family life, I passed my time in vain. I never got any tangible gain or permanent benefit, only trouble and botheration.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kisera saṁsāra ei chhāyābāji prāya</strong><br>
<strong>ihāte mamatā kari vṛthā dina yāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">What kind of world is this anyway? It seems to be just like a magic lantern show that I saw at a carnival, wherein so many shadows and optical illusions dance magically before my eyes. I feel great attachment and identification with such a world, and thus day after day passes by fruitlessly, without any purpose whatsoever.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e deha patana ha’le ki rabe āmāra?</strong><br>
<strong>keha sukha nāhi dibe putra paribāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When this body drops dead on the ground, then what will remain mine? At that moment, all of my sons and dearest loved ones will not be able to give me any happiness.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gardabhera mata āmi kari pariśrama</strong><br>
<strong>kāra lāgi’ eta kari, nā ghuchila bhrama</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I work hard just like an ass every day, and now I am wondering for whom am I working so hard? I am still surrounded by so many illusions.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dina yāya michhā kāje, niśā nidrā-vaśe</strong><br>
<strong>nāhi bhāvi maraṇa nikaṭe āchhe vase</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I waste every day in useless, insignificant work, and I waste every night controlled by sleep. And in every twenty-four hours I never for one second consider that death is sitting very close by my side.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhāla manda khāi, heri, pari, chintā-hīna</strong><br>
<strong>nāhi bhāvi, e deha chhāḍiba kona dina</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I live a very carefree life-style, eating, going out, wearing nice clothes, as I please, but I never consider that one day I will have to give up this body.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>deha-geha-kalatrādi-chintā avirata</strong><br>
<strong>jāgichhe hṛdaye mora buddhi kari hata</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My poor heart is plagued by constant anxieties about the maintenance and daily turmoils created by my body, my house, my wife, my family members, and my social obligations. All these anxieties are pinching me and destroying all my intelligence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hāya, hāya! nāhi bhāvi – anitya e saba</strong><br>
<strong>jīvana vigate kothā rahibe vaibhava?</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Alas, alas! What a remorseful situation has arisen! I am absorbed in all this trouble, but I never consider that all these things are temporary and subject to perish very soon. After I’m dead and gone, where will all of my material opulences remain?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śmaśāne śarīra mama paḍiyā rahibe</strong><br>
<strong>vihaṅga-pataṅga tāya vihāra karibe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When my body will be thrown in the pit at the cremation grounds, it will simply lie there motionlessly. Then many crows, vultures, ants, and worms will come and playfully sport there.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kukkura sṛgāla saba ānandita ha’ye</strong><br>
<strong>mahotsava karibe āmāra deha la’ye</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All the stray dogs and jackals will then become very much delighted, and in great ecstasy they will make a festival ground out of my body and will have a huge celebration and feast.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ye dehera ei gati, tāra anugata</strong><br>
<strong>saṁsāra-vaibhava āra bandhu-jana yata</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Just see, this is the ultimate destination of this material body. And the most amazing thing is that all of my material opulences, house, family and friends have exactly the same destination.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ataeva māyā-moha chhāḍi buddhimāna</strong><br>
<strong>nitya-tattva kṛṣṇa-bhakti karuna sandhāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Therefore I ask of anyone who has any sharp intelligence: please give up all of these temporary illusions presented by Māyā, and kindly search after the means to get pure devotion to Lord Kṛṣṇa, for this is the only really tangible eternal truth.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-anya-abhilasha-chadi-jniana-karma-parihari" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Anya-abhilāṣa Chhāḍi <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>anya-abhilāṣa chhāḍi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jñāna-karma parihari’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāya-mane kariba bhajana</strong><br>
<strong>sādhu-saṅge kṛṣṇa-sevā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā pūjiba devī-devā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ei bhakti parama-kāraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Worship the Lord with body and mind, giving up the desire for material endeavour and mundane knowledge. Perform service in the association of devotees and do not worship the various demigods. These activities are the prime cause of devotion to Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mahājanera yei patha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāte haba anurata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pūrvāpara kariyā vichāra</strong><br>
<strong>sādhana-smaraṇa līlā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ihāte nā kara helā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāya-mane kariyā susāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The essence of devotional practice is to carefully consider and follow the path revealed by the great devotees. One should not neglect the practice of remembering the Pastimes of the Lord, for devotional service should be performed with the mind as well as the body.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>asat-saṅga sadā tyāga&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhāḍa anya gīta-rāga</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karmī jñānī parihari’ dūre</strong><br>
<strong>kevala bhakata saṅga&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-kathā-rasa-raṅga</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;līlā-kathā vraja-rasa-pure</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Always give up bad association and avoid singing mundane songs. Keep at a distance from materialistic people as well as those absorbed with mundane knowledge. Associate only with the devotees and merge in the happiness of topics of the loving Pastimes of Kṛṣṇa in Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yogī, nyāsī, karmī, jñānī,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anya-deva-pūjaka, dhyānī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;iha-loka dūre parihari’</strong><br>
<strong>karma, dharma, duḥkha, śoka,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yebā thāke anya yoga</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhāḍi’ bhaja girivara-dhārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please give up the association of the yogīs, sannyāsīs, karmīs, jñānīs, meditators, and the worshippers of the demigods. Also abandon attachment for all varieties of fruitive activities, religious duties, distresses, lamentations, and material objects. Just worship Kṛṣṇa, the lifter of Govardhan Hill.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tīrtha-yātrā pariśrama&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kevala manera bhrama</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sarva-siddhi govinda-charaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>dṛḍha-viśvāsa hṛde dhari’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mada-mātsarya parihari’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā kara ananya-bhajana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Travelling to the holy places is only so much hard labour to attain some mental illusion when really the lotus feet of Śrī Govinda are the perfection of one’s life. One should give up pride and envy and with firm determination in the heart, one should always perform unalloyed worship of the Lord.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa-bhakta-saṅga kari’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-bhakta-saṅga heri’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śraddhānvita śravaṇa, kīrtana</strong><br>
<strong>archana, vandana, dhyāna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nava-bhakti mahājñāna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ei bhakti parama-kāraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The best process of devotional service is to hear, chant, worship, glorify and meditate with faith on the Names, Forms, Qualities, and Pastimes of Kṛṣṇa in the association of the devotees of Kṛṣṇa through the nine forms of bhakti.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hṛsīke govinda-sevā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā pūjiba devī-devā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ei ta ananya-bhakti-kathā</strong><br>
<strong>āra yata upālambha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśeṣa sakali dambha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhite lāgaye mane vyathā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Engage the senses in the service of Śrī Govinda and do not worship the various demigods. This principle is in the line of unalloyed devotion. All other activities are simply done through pride, and to see them gives great pain to one’s heart.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dehe vaise ripu-gaṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yateka indriya-gaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keha kāra bādhya nāhi haya</strong><br>
<strong>śunile nā śuna kāṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jānile nā jāne prāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dā̐ḍāite nā pāre niśchaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The six enemies: lust, anger, greed, illusion, pride, and envy, and the five senses of sight, sound, smell, taste, and touch reside in my body, but I am unable to control them. Although I hear and understand repeatedly that one should serve Kṛṣṇa with all his senses, still I cannot accept this fact with firm determination.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kāma, krodha, moha, lobha,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mada, mātsarya, dambha-saha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sthāne sthāne niyukta kariba</strong><br>
<strong>ānanda kari’ hṛdaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ripu kari’ parājaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anāyāse govinda bhajiba</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will engage lust, anger, greed, illusion, envy, and pride in their proper places. In this way, I will defeat the enemies and with ecstasy in my heart, I will worship Govinda without difficulty.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>‘kāma’ kṛṣṇa-karmārpaṇe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘krodha’ bhakta-dveṣi-jane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘lobha’ sādhu-saṅge hari-kathā</strong><br>
<strong>‘moha’ iṣṭa-lābha vine&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘mada’ kṛṣṇa-guṇa-gāne</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;niyukta kariba yathā tathā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I will engage my lust in eagerness to serve Kṛṣṇa and I will use my anger against those who are envious of the devotees. I will be greedy to hear the topics of Hari in the association of the devotees. I will be illusioned if I fail to achieve my worshipable Lord and I will feel proud to chant the glories of Kṛṣṇa. In this way, I will engage them in their respective duties.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>anyathā svatantra kāma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anarthādi yāra dhāma</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakti-pathe sadā deya bhaṅga</strong><br>
<strong>kibā vā karite pāre&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāma-krodha sādhakere</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yadi haya sādhu-janāra saṅga</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Otherwise, independent lusty desires, which are the source of all unwanted things, will always disturb the path of devotional service. What harm can lusty desires and anger do to a practitioner of devotional service if they associate with devotees?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>krodhe vā nā kare kibā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;krodha-tyāga sadā dibā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lobha moha ei ta kathana</strong><br>
<strong>chhaya ripu sadā hīna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kariba manera adhīna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇachandra kariyā smaraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Anger spoils everything. Therefore, I will always give up anger, greed and illusion. I will control the six enemies with the help of my mind while remembering Lord Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āpani pālābe saba&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuniyā ‘govinda’-rava</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;siṁha-rave yena kari-gaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>sakali vipatti yābe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mahānanda sukha pābe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yāra haya ekānta bhajana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All the enemies will run away by hearing the sound vibration of Govinda’s Name just as a deer flees upon hearing the roar of a lion. One who performs unalloyed devotional service will feel great happiness and all his dangers will be vanquished.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nā kariha asat-cheṣṭā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lābha pūjā pratiṣṭhā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā chinta govinda-charaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>sakala santāpa yābe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parānanda sukha pābe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-bhakti parama-kāraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Therefore my dear mind, do not endeavour for bad association, profit, adoration, and distinction, but always remember the lotus feet of Govinda. Please engage in loving devotional service with great happiness and all your dangers will be destroyed.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>asat-saṅga kuṭināṭi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhāḍa anya paripāṭi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anya deve nā kariha rati</strong><br>
<strong>āpana āpana sthāne&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pirīti sabāi ṭāne</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakti-pathe paḍaye vigati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please give up all duplicity, illusory activities, and the search for bodily happiness. Do not be attached to the worship of various demigods. Mundane love and affection for material relationships attracts everyone to their respective illusions, and thus advancement on the path of devotional service is checked.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āpana bhajana-patha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe haba anurata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;iṣṭa-deva-sthāne līlā-gāna</strong><br>
<strong>naiṣṭhika-bhajana ei&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāre kahilu bhāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hanumāna tāhāte pramāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Be continuously engaged on the path of devotional worship and be attached to glorifying the Pastimes of Śrī Kṛṣṇa in Vṛndāvan. This process of devotional service is known as steady devotional service. The prime example of this is Hanumān.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nama-chintamanih-krishna-chaitanya-rasa-vigrahah" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Glories of the Holy Name <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Quotations from the booklet given out by Śrīla Gurudev at the time of Harināma initiations</h3>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāma-chintāmaṇiḥ kṛṣṇa-chaitanya-rasa-vigrahaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>pūrṇaḥ śuddho nitya-mukto ‘bhinnatvān nāma-nāminoḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Padma-purāṇa)</i><br>
The Holy Name ‘Kṛṣṇa’ is the wish-fulfilling gem, Kṛṣṇa Himself, the living form of ecstasy, the absolute whole, transcendental to māyā, the eternal independent; because, the Name and the Named are identical.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>harer nāma harer nāma harer nāmaiva kevalam</strong><br>
<strong>kalau nāsty eva nāsty eva nāsty eva gatir anyathā</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Bṛhan-Nāradīya-Purāṇa)</i><br>
The Holy Name, the Holy Name, the Holy Name alone – in this age of Kali there is no other shelter, no other shelter, no other shelter.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>hare keśava govinda vāsudeva jaganmaya</strong><br>
<strong>itīrayanti ye nityaṁ na hi tān bādhate kaliḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Bṛhan-Nāradīya-Purāṇa)</i><br>
The influence of Kali no longer affects one who constantly chants the Holy Names of the Lord, such as Hare, Keśava, Govinda, and Vāsudeva.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>go-koṭi-dānaṁ grahaṇe bhagasya</strong><br>
<strong>prayāga-gaṅgodaka-kalpa-vāsaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>yajñāyutaṁ meru-suvarṇa-dānaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>govinda-kīrtter na samaṁ śatāṁśaiḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Skanda-Purāṇa)</i><br>
Making a gift of ten million cows on a solar eclipse, residing for a millennium at holy places like Prayāg, where the sacred Ganges flows, performing ten thousand sacrifices and giving away a mountain of gold – hundreds of such pious activities can never equal the slightest attempt to sing the Holy Name of the Supreme Lord Govinda.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-pita-varana-kali-pavana-gora" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse One</h3>
<h4>Eight Sanskrit verses of instruction by Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>cheto-darpaṇa-mārjanaṁ bhava-mahādāvāgni-nirvāpaṇaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śreyaḥ-kairava-chandrikā-vitaraṇaṁ vidyā-vadhū-jīvanam</strong><br>
<strong>ānandāmbudhi-vardhanaṁ prati-padaṁ pūrṇāmṛtāsvādanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>sarvātma-snapanaṁ paraṁ vijayate śrī-kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtanam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa thoroughly cleanses the mirror of the heart and extinguishes the fire of misery in the forest of birth and death. As the evening lotus blooms in the moon’s cooling rays, the heart begins to blossom in the nectar of the Name. And at last the soul awakens to its real inner treasure – a life of love with Kṛṣṇa.<br>
Again and again tasting nectar, the soul dives and surfaces in the ever-increasing ocean of ecstatic joy. All phases of the self of which we may conceive are fully satisfied and purified, and at last conquered by the all-auspicious influence of the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa.<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pīta-varaṇa kali-pāvana gorā</strong><br>
<strong>gāoya-i aichhana bhāva-vibhorā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Gaurāṅga, whose complexion is golden and who is the deliverer of the fallen souls of Kali-yuga, sings as follows, overwhelmed with spiritual ecstasy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chitta-darpaṇa-parimārjanakārī</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-kīrtana jaya chitta-vihārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“All glories to the chanting of the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa! It thoroughly cleanses the mirror of the heart (or mind) and is the delight of the soul.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>helā-bhava-dāva-nirvāpaṇa-vṛtti</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-kīrtana jaya kleśa-nivṛtti</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“All glories to the chanting of the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa! It extinguishes the forest fire of material existence and removes all material tribulations.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śreyaḥ-kumuda-vidhu-jyotsnā-prakāśa</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-kīrtana jaya bhakti-vilāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“All glories to the chanting of the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa! It appears like the moon in the heart and distributes its cooling moonlight, making the white lotus of the soul’s good fortune blossom and bloom. Kṛṣṇa-kīrtan is the bhakti-vilāsa, the beautiful Pastime of devotion.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viśuddha-vidyā-vadhū-jīvana-rūpa</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-kīrtana jaya siddha-svarūpa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“All glories to the chanting of the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa! It reveals one’s pure identity in relationship with the Lord even up to divine consorthood. This chanting is the real perfection of life.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ānanda-payonidhi-vardhana-kīrti</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-kīrtana jaya plāvana-mūrti</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“All glories to the chanting of the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa! Kṛṣṇa-kīrtan causes the ocean of ecstatic joy to overflow. It is an inundation of divine love.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pade pade pīyūṣa-svāda-pradātā</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-kīrtana jaya prema-vidhātā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“All glories to the chanting of the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa! Kṛṣṇa-kīrtan gives one a taste of fully satisfying nectar at every step; ultimately it bestows ecstatic love of Godhead.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakati-vinoda svātma-snapana-vidhāna</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-kīrtana jaya prema-nidāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories to the chanting of the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa! It bathes the soul of Bhakti Vinod. This chanting is a storehouse of love of Godhead.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-tuhu-daya-sagara-tarayite-prani" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Two</h3>
<h4>by Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāmnām akāri bahudhā nija-sarva-śaktis</strong><br>
<strong>tatrārpitā niyamitaḥ smaraṇe na kālaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>etādṛśī tava kṛpā bhagavan mamāpi</strong><br>
<strong>durdaivam īdṛśam ihājani nānurāgaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“O my Lord, Your Holy Name bestows auspiciousness upon all. And You have unlimited Names such as Kṛṣṇa and Govinda by which You reveal Yourself. In Your many Holy Names You have kindly invested all Your transcendental potency. And in chanting these Names, there are no strict rules concerning time or place. Out of Your causless mercy, You have descended in the form of divine sound, but my great misfortune is that I have no love for Your Holy Name.”<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tuhu̐ dayā-sāgara tārayite prāṇī</strong><br>
<strong>nāma aneka tuyā śikhāoli āni’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord! You are an ocean of mercy, and so you have brought the teachings of the glories of your unlimited Holy Names to deliver all souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sakala śakati dei’ nāme tohārā</strong><br>
<strong>grahaṇe rākhali nāhi kāla-vichārā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You have conferred all Your power upon Your Name. And there are no rules governing the appropriate time or place for chanting the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-nāma-chintāmaṇi tohāri samānā</strong><br>
<strong>viśve bilāoli karuṇā-nidānā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Holy Name is a chintāmaṇi gem, a divine touchstone, and is non-different from You. Out of Your kindness for all souls, You have distributed Your Holy Name throughout the universe.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tuyā dayā aichhana parama udārā</strong><br>
<strong>atiśaya manda nātha! bhāga hāmārā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This is Your mercy, O Lord. You are supremely kind. But, O Lord, I am most wicked and unfortunate.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāhi janamala nāme anurāga mora</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-vinoda-chitta duḥkhe vibhora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have never known any attraction for the Holy Names. And so, the heart of Bhakti Vinod is filled with sadness.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-krishna-kirtane-jodi-manasa-tohara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Three</h3>
<h4>by Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>tṛṇād api sunīchena taror iva sahiṣṇunā</strong><br>
<strong>amāninā mānadena kīrtanīyaḥ sadā hariḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who is humbler than a blade of grass, more forbearing than a tree, who gives due honour to others without desiring it for himself is qualified to always chant the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa.<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-kīrtane yadi mānasa tohāra</strong><br>
<strong>parama yatane ta̐hi labha adhikāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If your mind is always carefully absorbed in the chanting of the Holy Name, then you will become qualified for chanting the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tṛṇādhika hīna dīna akiñchana chhāra</strong><br>
<strong>āpane mānabi sadā chhāḍi’ ahaṅkāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Humbler than a blade of grass, considering yourself low, poor, and meek, give up your false ego.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛkṣa-sama kṣamā-guṇa karabi sādhana</strong><br>
<strong>pratihiṁsā tyaji’ anye karabi pālana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Tolerant as a tree, you should forgive and forbear, giving up retaliation. Practise nurturing and protecting others.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jīvana-nirvāhe āne udvega nā dibe</strong><br>
<strong>para-upakāre nija-sukha pāsaribe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Live without causing anxiety to others; do good unto others without considering your own happiness.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ha-ileo sarva-guṇe guṇī mahāśaya</strong><br>
<strong>pratiṣṭhāśā chhāḍi’ kara amānī hṛdaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">A great soul who has all these qualities should not become proud of his saintly qualities, but, giving up position and prestige, he should always remain prideless within his heart.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa-adhiṣṭhāna sarva-jīve jāni’ sadā</strong><br>
<strong>karabi sammāna sabe ādare sarvadā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Always knowing that Kṛṣṇa dwells within the hearts of all living beings, you should always properly respect and honour others.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dainya, dayā, anye māna, pratiṣṭhā-varjana</strong><br>
<strong>chāri guṇe guṇī ha-i’ karaha kīrtana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Humility, mercy, consideration of others, and giving up position are the four qualities needed to perform kīrtan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakati-vinoda kā̐di’ bale prabhu-pāya</strong><br>
<strong>hena adhikāra kabe dibe he amāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod, crying at the Lord’s holy feet says, “O my Lord, when will You bestow upon me the qualifications for Kṛṣṇa-kīrtan?”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-prabhu-tava-pada-juge-mora-nivedana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Four</h3>
<h4>by Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>na dhanaṁ na janaṁ na sundarīṁ</strong><br>
<strong>kavitāṁ vā jagad-īśa kāmaye</strong><br>
<strong>mama janmani janmanīśvare</strong><br>
<strong>bhavatād bhaktir ahaitukī tvayi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“O Lord, I have no desires to accumulate wealth, followers, beautiful women, or salvation. My only prayer is for Your causeless devotional service, birth after birth.”<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prabhu tava pada-yuge mora nivedana</strong><br>
<strong>nāhi māgi deha-sukha vidyā dhana jana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord! This is my humble submission at Your lotus feet: I do not ask from You sensual pleasure, learning, wealth, or followers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāhi māgi svarga āra mokṣa nāhi māgi</strong><br>
<strong>nā kari prārthanā kona vibhūtira lāgi’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I do not beg for residence on the celestial planets, nor do I wish liberation from this mundane existence. Nor do I pray for the attainment of any mystic powers.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nija karma-guṇa-doṣe ye ye janma pāi</strong><br>
<strong>janme janme yena tava nāma guṇa gāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Whatever birth I may obtain due to the results of my previous worldly activities, I pray that I may sing the glories of Your Holy Name birth after birth.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ei mātra āśā mama tomāra charaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>ahaitukī-bhakti hṛde jāge anukṣaṇe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This alone is my cherished hope, my aspiration, my prayer at Your lotus feet: Let causeless and uninterrupted devotion awaken within my heart and flow towards You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viṣaye ye prīti ebe āchhaye āmāra</strong><br>
<strong>sei mata prīti ha-uk charaṇe tomāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Let me love Your lotus feet as much as I now love sense gratification; transfer my affection from the objects of the senses to Your lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vipade sampade tāhā thākuk samabhāve</strong><br>
<strong>dine dine vṛddhi ha-uk nāmera prabhāve</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In danger or success, good fortune or disaster, let me remain equipoised, and let my affection for You increase day by day by the influence of the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>paśu pakṣī haye thāki’ svarge vā niraye</strong><br>
<strong>tava bhakti rahu bhakti-vinoda-hṛdaye</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Whether I live as bird or beast, in heaven or in hell, let the humble Bhakti Vinod always cherish devotion to You in his heart of hearts.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-anadi-karama-phole" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Five</h3>
<h4>by Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>ayi nanda-tanuja kiṅkaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>patitaṁ māṁ viṣame bhavāmbudhau</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpayā tava pāda-paṅkaja-</strong><br>
<strong>sthita-dhūlī-sadṛśaṁ vichintaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“O son of Nanda Mahārāj, I am Your eternal servant, yet because of my own karma, I have fallen into this terrible ocean of birth and death. Please accept this fallen soul and consider me a particle of dust at Your holy lotus feet.”<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>anādi karama-phale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paḍi’ bhavārṇava-jale</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;taribāre nā dekhi upāya</strong><br>
<strong>e-viṣaya-halāhale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;divā-niśi hiyā jvale</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mana kabhu sukha nāhi pāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As a result of my selfish fruitive activities, which are without beginning, I have fallen into the ocean of material existence. And now I can see no means of deliverance. Day and night my heart burns from the vicious poison of these worldly affairs, and due to this my mind never finds any true happiness.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āśā-pāśa śata śata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kleśa deya avirata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pravṛtti-ūrmira tāhe khelā</strong><br>
<strong>kāma-krodha-ādi chhaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bāṭapāḍe deya bhaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avasāna haila āsi’ belā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am bound by hundreds and hundreds of desires that give unending distress, and these desires play in the waves of ever-increasing materialistic tendency. Lust, anger, greed, pride, illusion, and envy are like six highway robbers making me greatly fearful, and in this way my life is coming to an end.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jñāna-karma ṭhaga dui&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;more pratāriyā la-i’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avaśeṣe phele sindhu-jale</strong><br>
<strong>e hena samaye bandhu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tumi kṛṣṇa kṛpā-sindhu</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛpā kari’ tola more bale</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The two highway robbers named intellectual knowledge and fruitive activity have cheated and misled me, and finally they have thrown me into the ocean of misery. At such a time as this, dear Kṛṣṇa, You are my only friend. You are an ocean of mercy. Please be kind upon me and uplift me from this condition of suffering by Your divine power.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>patita kiṅkare dhari’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāda-padma-dhūli kari’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deha bhakti-vinode āśraya</strong><br>
<strong>āmi tava nitya-dāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhuliyā māyāra pāśa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baddha haye āchhi dayāmaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am Your eternal servant, O merciful Lord. But, having forgotten this since time immemorial, I have become bound up in this network of illusion. Take this fallen servant of Yours, give him a place in the dust of Your lotus feet; give your shelter to this humble Bhakti Vinod.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-tunde-tandavini-ratim" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>The Nectarean Holy Name <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<p class="verse"><strong>tuṇḍe tāṇḍavinī ratiṁ vitanute tuṇḍāvalī-labdhaye</strong><br>
<strong>karṇa-kroḍa-kaḍambinī ghaṭayate karṇārbudebhyaḥ spṛhām</strong><br>
<strong>cetaḥ-prāṅgaṇa-saṅginī vijayate sarvendriyāṇāṁ kṛtiṁ</strong><br>
<strong>no jāne janitā kiyadbhir amṛtaiḥ kṛṣṇeti varṇa-dvayī</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrī Chaitanya-charitāmṛta: Antya-līlā, 1.99)</i><br>
“I do not know how much nectar the two syllables ‘Kṛṣ-ṇa’ have produced. When the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa is chanted, it appears to dance within the mouth. We then desire many, many mouths. When that Name enters the holes of the ears, we desire many millions of ears. And when the Holy Name dances in the courtyard of the heart, it conquers the activities of the mind, and therefore all the senses become inert.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-aparadha-phole-mama" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Six</h3>
<h4>by Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>nayanaṁ galad-aśru-dhārayā</strong><br>
<strong>vadanaṁ gadgada-ruddhayā girā</strong><br>
<strong>pulakair nichitaṁ vapuḥ kadā</strong><br>
<strong>tava nāma-grahaṇe bhaviṣyati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“O Lord, when will tears flow from my eyes like waves, and my voice tremble in ecstasy? When will the hairs of my body stand on end while chanting Your Holy Name?”<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aparādha-phale mama&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chitta bhela vajra-sama</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tuyā nāme nā labhe vikāra</strong><br>
<strong>hatāśa ha-iyā hari&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tava nāma uchcha kari</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baḍa duḥkhe ḍāki bāra bāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord Hari, as a result of my offences incurred in previous lifetimes, my heart has become as hard as a thunderbolt, and feels no change upon chanting Your Holy Name. Disappointed, hopeless, and in great distress, I loudly call Your Name again and again.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dīna-dayāmaya karuṇā-nidāna</strong><br>
<strong>bhāva-bindu dei’ rākhaha parāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, you are compassionate towards the fallen souls and the origin of mercy. Please give me a drop of divine ecstasy and thereby save my life!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabe tuyā nāma-uchchāraṇe mora</strong><br>
<strong>nayane jharaba dara dara lora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will an incessant stream of tears flow from my eyes as I chant Your Name in divine ecstasy?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gadagada-svara kaṇṭhe upajaba</strong><br>
<strong>mukhe bola ādha ādha bāhirāba</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will my voice be choked with divine emotion, and when will words garbled due to ecstasy come from my mouth?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pulake bharaba śarīra hāmāra</strong><br>
<strong>sveda-kampa-stambha habe bāra bāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will the hairs of my body stand up in ecstasy? When will I become stunned with ecstasy, shiver, and perspire out of the ecstasy of divine love? When will these symptoms of ecstasy overwhelm me again and again?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vivarṇa-śarīre hārāobu̐ jñāna</strong><br>
<strong>nāma-samāśraye dharabu parāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will my body turn colours and become pale out of ecstasy? When will I lose consciousness? And when will my very life be under the shelter of the Holy Name?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>milaba hāmāra kiye aichhe dina</strong><br>
<strong>ro-oye bhakati-vinoda mati hīna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod, who is devoid of good intelligence, cries, “When will I meet with such a day?”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-matala-hari-jana-kirtana-range" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Honouring Rāga-mārg From Afar <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<p class="translation">Śrīla Prabhupād Saraswatī Ṭhākur preached:</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>mātala hari-jana kīrtana-raṅge</strong><br>
<strong>pūjala rāga-patha gaurava-bhaṅge</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>Gaurava-bhaṅge</i> means the honourable guideline of the scriptures, and from that position we are always to give honour from afar to rāga-mārg. Rāga-mārg is a very high plane. Why? Because in that plane there is only affectionate attachment to Kṛṣṇa. That is the plane of affectionate service.<br>
<i>Mātala Hari-jana kīrtana-raṅge</i> and <i>pūjala rāga-patha gaurava-bhaṅge</i>: this is our life’s goal. Kīrtan is the first stage and also the last stage. Kīrtan can take us from the lowest level to the highest level, rāga-mārg. We start our practising life through kīrtan.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gaite-gaite-nama-ki-doshia-hoilo" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Seven</h3>
<h4>by Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>yugāyitaṁ nimeṣeṇa chakṣuṣā prāvṛṣāyitam</strong><br>
