<strong>śūnyāyitaṁ jagat sarvaṁ govinda-viraheṇa me</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“O Govinda! Without You, the world is empty. Tears are flooding my eyes like rain, and a moment seems like forever.”<br>
<i>Corresponding Bengali composition by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gāite gāite nāma ki daśā ha-ila</strong><br>
<strong>“kṛṣṇa-nitya-dāsa mui” hṛdaye sphurila</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Chanting the Name again and again, what was my condition? Within my heart I realised, “I am an eternal servant of Kṛṣṇa.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jānilāma māyā-pāśe e jaḍa jagate</strong><br>
<strong>govinda-virahe duḥkha pāi nānā-mate</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I realised that I was bound by Māyā’s ropes in this dull mundane universe, and I simply experience misery in various ways due to separation from Lord Govinda.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āra ye saṁsāra mora nāhi lāge bhāla</strong><br>
<strong>kā̐hā yāi’ kṛṣṇa heri e chintā viśāla</strong></p>
<p class="translation">And I don’t much like this world of birth and death. Where shall I go to see Kṛṣṇa? This was my biggest worry.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kā̐dite kā̐dite mora ā̐khi variṣaya</strong><br>
<strong>varṣā-dhārā hena chakṣe ha-ila udaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I began weeping and weeping; my tears fell. Torrents of rain poured from my eyes just like the rains of the monsoon season.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nimeṣa ha-ila mora śata yuga-sama</strong><br>
<strong>govinda-viraha āra sahite akṣama</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Now, a moment for me is like a hundred ages. I cannot bear this separation from Lord Govinda.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śūnya dharā-tala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chaudike dekhiye</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parāṇa udāsa haya</strong><br>
<strong>ki kari ki kari&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sthira nāhi haya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīvana nāhika raya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The world is empty; as I survey the four directions, the entire surface of the world is void. My life is empty, my mind is listless; I am indifferent and apathetic towards everything. Depressed, melancholy and forlorn, my life has no meaning now. What do I do now? I cannot remain steady. I can no longer maintain my life.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vraja-vāsi-gaṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mora prāṇa rākha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhāo śrī-rādhā-nāthe</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-vinoda-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;minati māniyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;laohe tāhāre sāthe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O residents of Vṛndāvan, save my life and show me Rādhānāth, the Lord of Śrī Rādhā. Considering this humble prayer of Bhakti Vinod, please give him Your association and take him with You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-viraha āra sahite nā pāri</strong><br>
<strong>parāṇa chhāḍite āra dina dui chāri</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am unable to further tolerate this separation from my Lord Kṛṣṇa and am ready to give up my life within a few days (lit. two or four days).</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gaite-govinda-nam-upajilo-bhava-gram" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Seven (b)</h3>
<p class="translation"><i>Second related composition by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gāite govinda-nāma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;upajila bhāva-grāma</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhilāma yamunāra kūle</strong><br>
<strong>vṛṣabhānu-sutā-saṅge&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śyāma naṭavara raṅge</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vā̐śarī bājāya nīpa-mūle</strong></p>
<p class="translation">While I sang the Name of Govinda a host of ecstasies arose within me. I saw Sundar-śyāma, the best of dancers, on Yamunā’s shores, with Śrī Rādhā, Vṛṣabhānu’s daughter, sporting in Pastimes of love playing His flute beside the water while a kadamba tree above cast shade upon the Divine Couple.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dekhiyā yugala-dhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;asthira ha-ila mana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jñāna-hārā ha-ilu̐ takhana</strong><br>
<strong>kata-kṣane nāhi jāni&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jñāna-lābha ha-ila māni</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āra nāhi bhela daraśana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">On seeing this, my mind was stunned. I lost my senses. Unaware of time, I fainted. When I awoke after immeasurable time, the treasured couple had gone. My vision of Vraja had been broken.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-sakhi-go-kemote-dhoribo-paran" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Seven (c)</h3>
<p class="translation"><i>Third related composition by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>*sakhi go kemate dhariba parāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>nimeṣa ha-ila yugera samāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my dear girlfriend! How will I maintain my life? The passing of a mere moment has become like a vast age of time.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrāvaṇera dhārā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ā̐khi variṣaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śūnya bhela dharā-tala</strong><br>
<strong>govinda-virahe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇa nāhi rahe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kemane vā̐chiba bala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Tears flow from my eyes like torrents of rain in the month of Śrāvaṇa, and the face of the earth has become totally void. In separation from Govinda my life cannot go on. Please, tell me how I will be able to live.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakati-vinoda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;asthira ha-iyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;punaḥ nāmāśraya kari’</strong><br>
<strong>ḍāke rādhānātha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;diyā daraśana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇa rākha, nahe mari</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod has become very restless and again takes shelter of the Holy Name, calling out, “O Lord of Rādhā! By bestowing Your audience, please save my life! By Your mercy alone I shall not perish!”<br>
*<i class="translation__note">Footnote:</i><br>
 Śrīla Guru Mahārāj, Śrīla Śrīdhar Mahārāj, advised not to sing ‘sakhi go’ but ‘bandhu he’ instead.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bondhu-gon-shiunoho-vachana-mora" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Eight</h3>
<h4>by Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>āśliṣya vā pāda-ratāṁ pinaṣṭu mām</strong><br>
<strong>adarśanān marma-hatāṁ karotu vā</strong><br>
<strong>yathā tathā vā vidadhātu lampaṭo</strong><br>
<strong>mat-prāṇa-nāthas tu sa eva nāparaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Kṛṣṇa may embrace me in love or trample me under His feet. He may break my heart by hiding Himself from me. Let that debauchee do whatever He likes, but He will always be the only Lord of my life.”<br>
<i>Corresponding Bengali composition by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bandhu-gaṇa! śunaha vachana mora</strong><br>
<strong>bhāvete vibhora&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;thākiye yakhana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhā deya chitta-chora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my friends! Listen to my words. I am filled to overflowing with devotional ecstasy when I have a divine vision of that thief who has stolen my heart.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vichakṣaṇa kari’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhite chāhile</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;haya ā̐khi-agochara</strong><br>
<strong>punaḥ nāhi dekhi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐daye parāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;duḥkhera nā thāke ora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">And when I want to see Him more clearly, He disappears from sight and becomes invisible. Again, not seeing Him, my soul weeps and my grief knows no bounds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jagatera bandhu sei kabhu more laya sātha</strong><br>
<strong>yathā tathā rākhu more āmāra se prāṇanātha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Sometimes Kṛṣṇa, the friend of the universe, takes me as one of His own, and sometimes He ignores me, but however He keeps me, He is the only Lord of my life.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>darśana-ānanda-dāne&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sukha deya mora prāṇe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bale more praṇaya-vachana</strong><br>
<strong>punaḥ adarśana diyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dagdha kare mora hiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇe more māre prāṇa-dhana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When He gives His blissful audience to me, He gives happiness to my soul. He gives me great ecstasy by allowing me to see Him and by speaking affectionately to me. Again He goes away, removing Himself from my sight. And by not letting me see Him, He burns my heart with the fire of separation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yāhe tāra sukha haya, sei sukha mama</strong><br>
<strong>nija sukhe duḥkhe mora sarvadāi sama</strong></p>
<p class="translation">But even though He pains my soul in this way, He is still the Lord of my life. Whatever is His happiness is also my happiness. And so, for me, my own happiness and distress is all the same.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakati-vinoda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁyoge viyoge</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhe jāne prāṇeśvara</strong><br>
<strong>tāra sukhe sukhī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei prāṇanātha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se kabhu nā haya para</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In this way, Bhakti Vinod, in both union and separation, knows that Kṛṣṇa is the only Lord of his soul. Kṛṣṇa’s happiness is my happiness. He is the Lord of my life; there is no other Lord for me than Him.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-yoga-pithopari-sthita-ashta-sakhi-subeshtita" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śikṣāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Eight (b)</h3>
<p class="translation"><i>Second related composition by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur:</i><br>
(Regarding this, Śrīla Śrīdhar Mahārāj advised not to sing this composition because “it is too high.”)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yoga-pīṭhopari-sthita&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;aṣṭa-sakhī-suveṣṭita</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndāraṇye kadamba-kānane</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-saha vaṁśī-dhārī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśva-jana-chitta-hārī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇa mora tā̐hāra charaṇe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At the yoga-pīṭha, surrounded by the aṣṭa-sakhīs, in Vṛndāvan Forest, in a grove of kadamba trees, by the side of Śrī Rādhā, holding His flute, Śrī Kṛṣṇa enchants all souls within the universe. I fall at His feet and surrender my life to Him.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sakhī-ājñāmata kari do̐hāra sevana</strong><br>
<strong>pālya-dāsī sadā bhāvi do̐hāra charaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Following the orders of a sakhī, I engage in service to the holy pair, Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, always considering myself a dependent maidservant at Their lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabhu kṛpā kari’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mama hasta dhari’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;madhura-vachana bale</strong><br>
<strong>tāmbula la-iyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khāya dui jane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mālā laya kutūhale</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Sometimes being kind, They take me by the hand and speak sweet words to me out of affection. Taking the betel nuts I have prepared, the two of Them eat and accept a garland from me with great delight.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>adarśana haya kakhana ki chhale</strong><br>
<strong>nā dekhiyā do̐he hiyā mora jvale</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Again, They disappear from my vision by some trick. Not seeing Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, my heart burns in agony.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yekhāne sekhāne&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;thākuka du’jane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āmi ta charaṇa-dāsī</strong><br>
<strong>milane ānanda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;virahe yātanā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sakala samāna vāsi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Here or there, wherever They stay, I am the maidservant of Their lotus feet. Through the happiness of union and the agony of separation, I am Their maidservant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-kṛṣṇa prāṇa mora jīvane maraṇe</strong><br>
<strong>more rākhi’ māri’ sukhe thākuka du’jane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa are my life and soul in life and in death; whether They protect me or take my life, may They always be happy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakati-vinoda&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āna nāhi jāne</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paḍi’ nija sakhī-pāya</strong><br>
<strong>rādhikāra gaṇe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;thākiyā satata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yugala-charaṇa chāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinoda says: “I know nothing else. Falling at the feet of my own sakhī (confidante), remaining always among the associates of Rādhikā, I take shelter in the shade of the Divine Couple’s feet.”</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Śrī Śrī Upadeśāmṛta</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-guru-kripa-bale-labhi-sambandha-vijniana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Upadeśāmṛta <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Sweet Upward Guidance to Eternity</h3>
<h3>Verse One</h3>
<h4>Eleven Sanskrit verses by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>vācho vegaṁ manasaḥ krodha-vegaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>jihvā-vegam udaropastha-vegam</strong><br>
<strong>etān vegān yo viṣaheta dhīraḥ</strong><br>
<strong>sarvām apīmāṁ pṛthivīṁ sa śiṣyāt</strong></p>
<p class="translation">A sober person who can tolerate (1) the urge to speak, (2) the mind’s demands, (3) the actions of anger, and the urges of (4) the tongue, (5) the belly, and (6) the genitals, is qualified to instruct the entire world.<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>guru-kṛpā-bale labhi’ sambandha-vijñāna</strong><br>
<strong>kṛti-jīva hayena bhajane yatnavān</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Attaining proper sambandha-jñān by the mercy of Śrī Guru, fortunate jīvas engage in bhajan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sei jīve śrī-rūpa-gosvāmi mahodaya</strong><br>
<strong>‘upadeśāmṛte’ dhanya karena niśchaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī has certainly blessed such jīvas with <i>Śrī Upadeśāmṛta</i>.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gṛhī gṛha-tyāgī bhede dvi-prakāra jane</strong><br>
<strong>upadeśa bheda vichāribe vijña-gaṇe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There are two types of practitioners – householders and renunciants, and the wise will conclude that there are different instructions for each of them.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gṛhī prati ei saba upadeśa haya</strong><br>
<strong>gṛha-tyāgī prati ihā parākāṣṭhāmaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All these instructions are for householders, and they are of utmost importance for renunciants.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5-6">
<h5>5-6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vākya-vega mano-vega krodha-vega āra</strong><br>
<strong>jihva-vega udara-upastha-vega chhāra</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>ei chhaya vega sahi’ kṛṣṇa-nāmāśraye</strong><br>
<strong>jagat śāsite pāre parājiyā bhaye</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Those who tolerate the six base urges – the urges of speech, the mind, anger, the tongue, the belly, and the genitals – by taking the shelter of Kṛṣṇa’s Name can instruct the world and conquer fear.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kevala śaraṇāgati kṛṣṇa-bhaktimaya</strong><br>
<strong>bhakti-pratikūla tyāga tāra aṅga haya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Pure surrender is made up of Kṛṣṇa-bhakti. Rejecting that which is unfavourable to bhakti is one of its limbs.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chhaya vega sahi’ yukta-vairāgya-āśraye</strong><br>
<strong>nāme aparādha-śūnya ha-ibe nirbhaye</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Those who tolerate these six urges in the shelter of yukta-vairāgya will fearlessly become free from the offences to the Holy Name.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-krishna-lilamrita-sara-tara-shiata-shiata-dhara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>The Real Essence of Nectar <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa-līlāmṛta-sāra,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāra śata śata dhāra,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;daśa-dike vahe yāhā haite</strong><br>
<strong>se chaitanya-līlā haya,&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sarovara akṣaya,</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mano-haṁsa charāha tāhāte</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrī Chaitanya-charitāmṛta: Madhya-līlā, 25.271)</i><br>
“What is Kṛṣṇa-līlā? It is the real essence of nectar. It is the gist of sweetness, happiness, and ecstasy. The sweetness of the sweetest thing that can ever be conceived of is represented in Kṛṣṇa-līlā. Then what is Chaitanya-līlā? In Chaitanya-līlā, that sweet nectar of Kṛṣṇa-līlā is flowing in all ten directions in hundreds of streams as if from a fountain. That fountain is Chaitanya-līlā.”<br>
All the devotees like to read <i>Śrī Chaitanya-charitāmṛta</i> and I have also read it many times, but the meaning is not always apparent to us. The real meaning comes through our service, so we must try wholeheartedly and happily to serve, even though sometimes service may not come so happily to us. Sometimes it may give us pain or difficulty.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-atyahara-prayasa-prajalpa-jana-sanga" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Upadeśāmṛta <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Two</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>atyāhāraḥ prayāsaś cha prajalpo niyamāgrahaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>jana-saṅgaś cha laulyañ cha ṣaḍbhir bhaktir vinaśyati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One’s devotional service is spoiled when one becomes too entangled in the following six activities: (1) eating more than necessary or collecting more funds than required; (2) over-endeavouring for mundane things that are very difficult to obtain; (3) talking unnecessarily about mundane subject matters; (4) practising the scriptural rules and regulations only for the sake of following them and not for the sake of spiritual advancement, or rejecting the rules and regulations of the scriptures and working independently or whimsically; (5) associating with worldly-minded persons who are not interested in Kṛṣṇa con­sciousness; and (6) being greedy for mundane achievements.<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>atyāhāra prayāsa prajalpa jana-saṅga</strong><br>
<strong>laulyādi niyamāgraha hale bhakti-bhaṅga</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When over-accumulation, over-endeavour, unnecessary talk, association with non-devotees, fickleness, and misapplication of rules occur, bhakti becomes obstructed.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gṛha-tyāgī janera sañchaya atyāhāra</strong><br>
<strong>adhika sañchayī gṛhī vaiṣṇavera chhāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">For renunciants, any accumulation is excessive (atyāhāra), and for householder devotees, excessive accumulation is bad.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakti-anukūla naya se saba udyama</strong><br>
<strong>prayāsa nāmete tāra prakāśa viṣama</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All endeavours that are not favourable to bhakti are called prayāsa; occurrences of them are condemnable.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>grāmya-kathā prajalpa nāmete parichaya</strong><br>
<strong>matera chāñchalya laulya asat-tṛṣṇāmaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Village talk is called prajalpa. Restlessness of thought produced by material desire is called laulya.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5-6">
<h5>5-6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viṣayī yoṣit-saṅgī tat-tat-saṅgī āra</strong><br>
<strong>māyāvādī dharma-dhvajī nāstika prakāra</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>se saba asat-saṅga bhakti-hānikara</strong><br>
<strong>viśeṣa yatane sei saṅga parihara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Materialists, those who are attached to women, their companions, māyāvādīs, religious hypocrites, and atheists – the association of such persons is destructive to bhakti. With great care, avoid all such association.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>niyama agraha āra niyama āgraha</strong><br>
<strong>dvi-prakāra doṣa ei bhakta-galagraha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The faults of both being negligent of rules and being obsessive about rules are burdens to devotees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>eke svādhikāragata niyama varjana</strong><br>
<strong>āre anya-adhikāra niyama grahana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In one, there is rejection of the rules meant for one’s own stage, and in the other, there is adherence to the rules meant for stages other than one’s own.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-shrimad-bhagavat-padambuja" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>The Highest Śloka <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-śrīmad-bhagavat-padāmbuja-madhu-svādotsavaiḥ ṣaṭ-padair</strong><br>
<strong>nikṣiptā madhu-bindavaś cha parito bhraṣṭā mukhāt guñjitaiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>yatnaiḥ kiñchid ihāhṛtaṁ nija-paraśreyo ’rthinā tan mayā</strong><br>
<strong>bhūyobhūya ito rajāṁsi pada-saṁlagnāni teṣāṁ bhaje</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Śrī Śrī Prapanna-jīvanāmṛtam: 10.14)</i><br>
The bees, intoxicated in the festival<br />of drinking the honey of the Lord’s lotus feet,<br />busily humming the glories of the Lord,<br />drops of honey from their mouths fall and scatter all around;<br />for my own divine prospect, I’ve carefully collected<br />some of those drops herein –<br />and thus do I worship the dust of the holy lotus feet<br />of those saints, again and again.<br>
Śrīla Gurudev, Śrīla Govinda Mahārāj: “I think that this is the highest śloka I have heard in my life. I am always quoting this, and whenever I have a chance I include it in a book.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-anukulya-sankalpera-chhaya-anga-sara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Upadeśāmṛta <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Three</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>utsāhān niśchayād dhairyāt tat-tat-karma-pravartanāt</strong><br>
<strong>saṅga-tyāgāt sato vṛtteḥ ṣaḍbhir bhaktiḥ prasidhyati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There are six principles favourable to the execution of pure devotional service: (1) being enthusiastic, (2) endeavouring with confidence, (3) being patient, (4) acting according to regulative principles (such as śravaṇaṁ kīrtanaṁ viṣṇoḥ smaraṇam, etc. – hearing, chanting, remembering Kṛṣṇa, etc.), (5) abandoning the association of nondevotees, and (6) following in the footsteps of the previous āchāryas. These six principles undoubtedly assure the complete success of pure devotional service.<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1-2">
<h5>1-2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ānukūlya-saṅkalpera chhaya aṅga sāra</strong><br>
<strong>utsāha viśvāsa dhairya tat-tat-karma āra</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>saṅga-tyāga sādhu-vṛtti karile āśraya</strong><br>
<strong>bhakti-yoga-siddhi labhe sarva-śāstre kaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All the scriptures say that if one takes shelter in the six most important aspects of accepting the favourable – enthusiasm, conviction, perseverance, engaging in favourable activities, abandoning bad association, and following the conduct of the sādhus – then one will attain perfection in bhakti-yoga.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3-4">
<h5>3-4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakti-anuṣṭhāne utsāhera prayojana</strong><br>
<strong>bhaktite viśvāsa dṛḍha dhairyāvalambana</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>ye karma karile haya bhaktira ullāsa</strong><br>
<strong>ye karma jīvana-yātrā nirvāhe prayāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Enthusiasm for the practice of bhakti, firm conviction in bhakti, perseverance, activities by which bhakti develops, and activities by which one’s life is maintained are all necessary.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>asat-saṅga tyāge haya saṅga-vivarjana</strong><br>
<strong>sadāchāra sādhu-vṛtti sarvadā pālana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Give up association by rejecting bad association and always follow the conduct and practices of the sādhus.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tyāgī bhikṣā-yoge āra gṛhī dharmāśraye</strong><br>
<strong>karibe jīvana yātrā sāvadhāna haye</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Renunciants should carefully maintain their lives by begging, and householders should maintain their lives by following the codes for proper conduct.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-asat-sanga-tyaji-sadhu-sanga-kara-bhai" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Upadeśāmṛta <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Four</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>dadāti pratigṛhṇāti guhyam ākhyāti pṛchchhati</strong><br>
<strong>bhuṅkte bhojayate chaiva ṣaḍ-vidhaṁ prīti-lakṣaṇam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">(1) Offering gifts, (2) accepting gifts, (3) revealing one’s mind in confidence, (4) enquiring confidentially, (5) accepting prasād, and (6) offering prasād, are the six symptoms of love shared by one devotee and another.<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>asat-saṅga tyāji’ sādhu-saṅga kara bhāi</strong><br>
<strong>prītira lakṣaṇa chhaya vichāri’ sadāi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Brother, give up bad association, associate with the sādhus, and always engage in the six exchanges of affection.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dāna-graha sva-sva-guhya jijñāsā varṇana</strong><br>
<strong>bhuñjana bhojana-dāna saṅgera lakṣaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Giving and accepting gifts, asking about and describing confidential matters, and eating and distributing food are the characteristics of such association.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-asat-lakshana-hina-gaya-krishna-nama" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Upadeśāmṛta <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Five</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇeti yasya giri taṁ manasādriyeta</strong><br>
<strong>dīkṣāsti chet praṇatibhiś cha bhajantam īśam</strong><br>
<strong>śuśrūṣayā bhajana-vijñam ananyam anya-</strong><br>
<strong>nindādi-śūnya-hṛdam īpsita-saṅga-labdhyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One should (1) mentally honour the devotee who chants the Holy Name of Lord Kṛṣṇa, one should (2) offer humble obeisances to the devotee who has undergone spiritual initiation (dīkṣā) and is engaged in service to the Lord, and one should (3) associate with and faithfully serve that pure devotee who is advanced in undeviated devotional service and whose heart is completely devoid of the propensity to criticise others.<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>asat lakṣaṇa-hīna gāya kṛṣṇa-nāma</strong><br>
<strong>manete ādara tā̐te kara aviśrāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Always honour within the mind those who are free from bad qualities and chant the Name of Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>labdha-dīkṣā kṛṣṇa bhaje yei mahājana</strong><br>
<strong>praṇami’ ādara tā̐re kara sarva-kṣaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Always honour with obeisances great souls who are initiated and serve Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3-4">
<h5>3-4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhajana-chatura yei tā̐ra kara sevā</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇamaya sabe dekhe suvaiṣṇava yebā</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>śatru mitra sad asat kichhu nā vichāre</strong><br>
<strong>sarvottama saṅga bali’ sevaha tā̐hāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Serve those who are expert in bhajan. Consider the association of such great Vaiṣṇavas, who see Kṛṣṇa within everything, and do not conceive of enemies and friends or good and bad, to be the best of all, and serve them.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nira-dharma-gata-phena-pankadi-samyukta" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Upadeśāmṛta <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Six</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>dṛṣṭaiḥ svabhāva-janitair vapuṣaś cha doṣair</strong><br>
<strong>na prākṛtatvam iha bhakta-janasya paśyet</strong><br>
<strong>gaṅgāmbhasāṁ na khalu budbuda-phena-paṅkair</strong><br>
<strong>brahma-dravatvam apagachchhati nīra-dharmaiḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Being situated in one’s original Kṛṣṇa conscious position, a pure devo­tee does not identify with the body. Such a devotee should not be seen from a materialistic point of view. Indeed, one should overlook a devotee’s having a body born in a low family, a body with a bad complexion, a deformed body, or a diseased or infirm body. According to ordinary vision, such imperfections may seem prominent in the body of a pure devotee, but despite such seeming defects, the body of a pure devotee cannot be polluted. It is exactly like the waters of the Ganges, which sometimes during the rainy season are full of bubbles, foam, and mud. The Ganges waters do not become polluted. Those who are advanced in spiritual understanding will bathe in the Ganges without considering the condition of the water.<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nīra-dharma-gata phena-paṅkādi-saṁyukta</strong><br>
<strong>gaṅgā-jala brahmatā ha-ite nahe chyuta</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Ganges water does not lose its spiritual nature because it contains foam, mud, and so on, which are natural in water.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sei-rūpa śuddha-bhakta jaḍa-deha-gata</strong><br>
<strong>svabhāva-vapura doṣe na haya prākṛta</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Similarly, pure devotees are not mundane because of faults within their nature and form that are natural in the material body.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3-4">
<h5>3-4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ataeva dekhiyā bhaktera kadākāra</strong><br>
<strong>svabhāvaja varṇa kārkaśyādi doṣa āra</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>prākṛta baliyā bhakte kabhu nā nindibe</strong><br>
<strong>śuddha-bhakti dekhi’ tā̐re sarvadā vandibe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Therefore, upon seeing the faults produced by a devotee’s nature or occupation, such as ugliness or apparent roughness, never consider a devotee mundane and criticise them. Rather, see their pure bhakti and always revere them.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-avidya-pittera-doshe-dushta-rasanaya" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Upadeśāmṛta <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Seven</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>syāt kṛṣṇa-nāma-charitādi-sitāpy avidyā-</strong><br>
<strong>pittopatapta-rasanasya na rochikā nu</strong><br>
<strong>kintv ādarād anudinaṁ khalu saiva juṣṭā</strong><br>
<strong>svādvī kramād bhavati tad-gada-mūla-hantrī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Holy Name, Character, Pastimes and Activities of Kṛṣṇa are all transcendentally sweet like sugar candy. Although the tongue of one afflicted by the jaundice of avidyā (ignorance) cannot taste anything sweet, it is wonderful that simply by carefully chanting these sweet Names every day, a natural relish awakens within one’s tongue, and one’s disease is gradually destroyed at the root.<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>avidyā pittera doṣe duṣṭa rasanāya</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtane ruchi nāhi haya hāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There is no taste for chanting about Kṛṣṇa on a tongue that is defiled by the jaundice of ignorance. Alas!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sitapala prāya kṛṣṇa-kathā anudina</strong><br>
<strong>ādare sevite ruchi dena samīchīna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Like sugar candy, daily engaging in discussion of Kṛṣṇa with faith produces genuine ruchi.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa-kāmya-vismṛti avidyā-gada-mūla</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrtana-krame haya ta’ nirmūla</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The root of the disease – ignorance, forgetfulness that Kṛṣṇa is our desired end – is gradually uprooted by chanting about Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sei krame kṛṣṇa-nāmādite āsvādana</strong><br>
<strong>anudina bāḍe, ruchi haya anukṣaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By chanting, the taste of Kṛṣṇa’s Name, Pastimes, and so on, increases day by day, and one’s ruchi becomes constant.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-namadira-smriti-ara-kirtana-niyame" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Upadeśāmṛta <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Eight</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>tan-nāma-rūpa-charitādi-sukīrtanānu-</strong><br>
<strong>smṛtyoḥ krameṇa rasanā-manasī niyojya</strong><br>
<strong>tiṣṭhan vraje tad-anurāgi-janānugāmī</strong><br>
<strong>kālaṁ nayed akhilam ity upadeśa-sāram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The essence of all advice is that one should utilise one’s full time – twenty-four hours a day – in nicely chanting and remembering the Lord’s divine Name, transcendental Form, Qualities, and eternal Pastimes, thereby gradually engaging one’s tongue and mind. In this way one should reside in Vraja (Goloka Vṛndāvan Dhām) and serve Kṛṣṇa under the guidance of devotees. One should follow in the footsteps of the Lord’s beloved devotees, who are deeply attached to His devotional service.<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nāmādira smṛti āra kīrtana niyame</strong><br>
<strong>niyojita kara jihvā chitta krame krame</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Gradually engage the tongue and mind in the practices of remembrance and chanting of Kṛṣṇa’s Names and so forth.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vraje vasi’ anurāgīra sevā-anusāra</strong><br>
<strong>sarva-kāla bhaja ei upadeśa sāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Reside in Vraja and serve at all times, following the services of those who have deep attachment to Kṛṣṇa. This is the essence of all instructions.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-vaikuntha-apeksha-shreshtha-mathura-mandala" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Upadeśāmṛta <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Nine</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaikuṇṭhāj janito varā madhu-purī tatrāpi rāsotsavād-</strong><br>
<strong>vṛndāraṇyam udāra-pāṇi-ramaṇāt tatrāpi govardhanaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-kuṇḍam ihāpi gokula-pateḥ premāmṛtāplāvanāt</strong><br>
<strong>kuryād asya virājato giri-taṭe sevāṁ vivekī na kaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The holy place known as Mathurā is spiritually superior to Vaikuṇṭha, the transcendental world, because the Lord appeared there. Superior to Mathurā-purī is the transcendental forest of Vṛndāvan because of Kṛṣṇa’s Rāsa-līlā Pastimes. And superior to the forest of Vṛndāvan is Govardhan Hill, for it was raised by the divine hand of Śrī Kṛṣṇa and was the site of His various loving Pastimes. And, above all, the superexcellent Śrī Rādhā Kuṇḍa stands supreme, for it is overflooded with the ambrosial nectarean prema of the Lord of Gokul, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Where, then, is that intelligent person who is unwilling to serve this divine Rādhā Kuṇḍa, which is situated at the foot of Govardhan Hill?<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaikuṇṭha apekṣā śreṣṭha māthura-maṇḍala</strong><br>
<strong>tad apekṣā vṛndāvan yathā rāsa-sthala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Higher than Vaikuṇṭha is Māthura Maṇḍal, and higher than that is Vṛndāvan, the place of the Rāsa Dance.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tad apekṣā govardhan nitya keli-sthāna</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-kuṇḍe tad apekṣā premera vijñāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Higher than Vṛndāvan is Govardhan, the place of Kṛṣṇa’s daily play, and higher than that is Rādhā Kuṇḍa, where there is full realisation of prema.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-chid-anveshi-jniani-jada-karmi-ha-ite-shreshtha" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Upadeśāmṛta <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Ten</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>karmibhyaḥ parito hareḥ priyatayā vyaktiṁ yayur jñāninas</strong><br>
<strong>tebhyo jñāna-vimukta-bhakti-paramāḥ premaika-niṣṭhās tataḥ</strong><br>
<strong>tebhyas tāḥ paśu-pāla-paṅkaja-dṛśas tābhyo ’pi sā rādhikā</strong><br>
<strong>preṣṭhā tadvad iyaṁ tadīya-sarasī tāṁ nāśrayet kaḥ kṛtī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the śāstra it is said that of all types of fruitive workers, one who is advanced in knowledge of the higher values of life is favoured by the Supreme Lord Hari. Out of many such people who are advanced in knowledge (jñānīs), one who is practically liberated by virtue of his knowledge may take to devotional service. He is superior to the others. However, one who has actually attained prema, pure love of Kṛṣṇa, is superior to him. The gopīs are exalted above all the advanced devotees because they are always totally dependent upon Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the transcendental cowherd boy. Among the gopīs, Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī is the most dear to Kṛṣṇa. Her kuṇḍa (lake) is as profoundly dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa as this most beloved of the gopīs. Who, then, will not reside at Rādhā Kuṇḍa and, in a spiritual body surcharged with ecstatic devotional feelings (aprākṛta-bhāva), render loving service to the Divine Couple Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda, who perform Their aṣṭa-kālīya-līlā, Their eternal eightfold daily Pastimes. Indeed, those who execute devotional service on the banks of Rādhā Kuṇḍa are the most fortunate people in the universe.<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chid-anveṣī jñānī jaḍa-karmī ha-ite śreṣṭha</strong><br>
<strong>jñāni-chara bhakta tad apekṣā kṛṣṇa-preṣṭha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Jñānīs who seek the spiritual plane are higher than mundane karmīs. Devotees who were formerly jñānīs are more dear to Kṛṣṇa than them.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prema-niṣṭha bhakta tad apekṣā śreṣṭha jāni</strong><br>
<strong>gopī-gaṇe tad apekṣā śreṣṭha bali’ māni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">We know that devotees fixed in prema are higher than them, and we consider the gopīs higher than all other devotees.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarva-gopī śreṣṭhā rādhā kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhā sadā</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐hāra sarasī nitya kṛṣṇera prītidā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Rādhā is the highest of all the gopīs. She is forever Kṛṣṇa’s favourite, and Her lake always pleases Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e hena premera sthāna govardhana-taṭe</strong><br>
<strong>āśraya nā kare kebā kṛtī niṣkapaṭe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">What fortunate soul would not take shelter in this abode of prema at the base of Govardhan Hill with all sincerity?</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-sakala-preyasi-shreshtha-vrishabhanu-suta" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Upadeśāmṛta <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Verse Eleven</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu in Sanskrit</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇasyochchaiḥ praṇaya-vasatiḥ preyāsibhyo ’pi rādhā</strong><br>
<strong>kuṇḍaṁ chāsyā munibhir abhitas tādṛg eva vyadhāyi</strong><br>
<strong>yat preṣṭhair apy alam asulabhaṁ kiṁ punar bhakti-bhājāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>tat premedaṁ sakṛd api sarāḥ snātur āviṣkaroti</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Of the many objects of favoured delight and of all the lovable damsels of Vraja-bhūmi, Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī is certainly the most treasured object of Kṛṣṇa’s love. And, in every respect, Her divine kuṇḍa is described by great sages as similarly dear to Him. Undoubtedly Rādhā Kuṇḍa is very rarely attained even by the great devotees; therefore it is even more difficult for ordinary devotees to attain. If one simply bathes once within those holy waters, one’s pure love of Kṛṣṇa is fully aroused.<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sakala preyasī-śreṣṭhā vṛṣabhānu-sutā</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐hāra sarasī nitya śrī-kṛṣṇa-dayitā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The daughter of Vṛṣabhānu is the best of all Kṛṣṇa’s lovers, and Her lake is always dear to Śrī Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>muṇi-gaṇa śāstre ei-rūpa nirdhārila</strong><br>
<strong>vraja-madhye śreṣṭha bali’ kuṇḍe sthira kaila</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the scriptures, the sages have ascertained this and determined that this lake is supreme within all of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sādhana bhaktira kathā ki baliba āra</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-preṣṭha-gaṇera durlabha prema-sāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">What more can I say of sādhana-bhakti? The highest form of prema is difficult to attain even for those who are dear to Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>niṣkapaṭe sei kuṇḍe ye kare majjana</strong><br>
<strong>kuṇḍa tā̐re sei prema kare vitaraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Yet Rādhā Kuṇḍa gives such prema to those who bathe within it sincerely.</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
<section class="category">
<h1>Other</h1>
<section class="song" id="song-krishna-hoite-chatur-mukha" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Guru Paramparā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Verses 1-9 composed by Bhagavān Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṛṣṇa haite chatur-mukha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hana kṛṣṇa-sevonmukha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;brahmā haite nāradera mati</strong><br>
<strong>nārada haite vyāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;madhva kahe vyāsa-dāsa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pūrṇaprajña padmanābha-gati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the beginning of creation the science of devotional service was received by the four-headed Brahmā from the Supreme Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Devarṣi Nārada’s understanding of this divine science was obtained from Brahmā. The great sage Kṛṣṇa Dvaipāyana Vyāsa, who was empowered to compile the Vedic literatures, became a disciple of Devarṣi Nārada. Śrīpād Madhva Āchārya, the founder of the Śuddha-dvaita school of Vedānta philosophy, who visited Vyāsadev at Badarīkāśram in the thirteenth century to learn from him Vedānta philosophy, calls himself a servant of Kṛṣṇa Dvaipāyana Vyāsa. Pūrṇaprajña Tīrtha (Madhva) is the guru and sole refuge of Padmanābha Tīrtha</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nṛhari mādhava vaṁśe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;akṣobhya paramahaṁse</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śiṣya bali’ aṅgīkāra kare</strong><br>
<strong>akṣobhyera śiṣya jaya-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tīrtha nāme parichaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐ra dāsye jñānasindhu tare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The two other principal disciples of Madhva are Nṛhari Tīrtha and Mādhava Tīrtha. Mādhava Tīrtha accepted the great paramahaṁsa Akṣobhya Tīrtha as a disciple. The principal disciple of Akṣobhya Tīrtha was known as Jayatīrtha. Jayatīrtha’s service was for his disciple Jñānasindhu.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tā̐hā haite dayānidhi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐ra dāsa vidyānidhi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rājendra ha-ila tā̐hā haite</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐hāra kiṅkara jaya-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dharma nāme parichaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paramparā jāna bhāla mate</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Dayānidhi received the science of devotional service from Jñānasindhu, and the servant of Dayānidhi was Vidyānidhi (Vidyādhirāja Tīrtha). Rājendra Tīrtha became a disciple of Vidyādhirāja Tīrtha. Rājendra Tīrtha’s servant was known as Jayadharma or Vijayadhvaja Tīrtha. In this way you should properly understand this disciplic succession.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya-dharma-dāsye khyāti&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-puruṣottama-yati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐hate brahmaṇya-tīrtha sūri</strong><br>
<strong>vyāsatīrtha tā̐ra dāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lakṣmīpati vyāsa-dāsa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐hā haite mādhavendra purī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The great sannyāsī Śrī Puruṣottama Tīrtha received his knowledge in the service of his guru, Vijayadhvaja Tīrtha (Jayadharma). The principal disciple of Puruṣottama Tīrtha was Subrahmaṇya Tīrtha. His servant was the great Vyāsatīrtha (Vyāsa Rāya). Vyāsatīrtha’s servant was Lakṣmīpati Tīrtha, whose disciple was Mādhavendra Purī Goswāmī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mādhavendra purī-vara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śiṣya-vara śrī-īśvara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nityānanda, śrī-advaita vibhu</strong><br>
<strong>īśvara-purīke dhanya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karilena śrī-chaitanya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jagad-guru gaura mahāprabhu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The chief disciple of Mādhavendra Purī was Īśvara Purī, and two of his other disciples were the renowned incarnations of Godhead Śrī Nityānanda and Śrī Advaita Āchārya. Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu, the spiritual preceptor of all the worlds, made Īśvara Purī greatly fortunate by accepting him as His spiritual master.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mahāprabhu śrī-chaitanya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-kṛṣṇa nahe anya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rūpānuga-janera jīvana</strong><br>
<strong>viśvambhara-priyaṅkara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-svarūpa-dāmodara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-gosvāmī rūpa-sanātana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Mahāprabhu Śrī Chaitanya is non-different from Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa and is the very life of those Vaiṣṇavas who follow Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī. Śrī Svarūp Dāmodar Goswāmī, Rūpa Goswāmī and Sanātan Goswāmī were the givers of great happiness to Viśvambhara (Śrī Chaitanya).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpa-priya mahājana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jīva-raghunātha hana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐ra priya kavi kṛṣṇadāsa</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-dāsa-priya-vara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;narottama sevāpara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yā̐ra pada viśvanātha-āśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The great souls Jīva Goswāmī and Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī became very dear to Rūpa Goswāmī. Jīva Goswāmī was a disciple of Rūpa Goswāmī. Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī, a disciple of Advaita Āchārya’s disciple Jadunandan Āchārya, was accepted by Rūpa and Sanātan as their third brother. Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī’s beloved student was Kṛṣṇadās Kavirāj Goswāmī. Kṛṣṇādās Kavirāj was an intimate friend of Lokanāth Goswāmī. They lived together in Vṛndāvan and always discussed the topics of Kṛṣṇa with one another. Lokanāth Goswāmī, a disciple of Gadādhar Paṇḍit, had only one disciple, whose name was Narottam Dās. Narottam Dās was always engaged in the service of his guru, and he also engaged himself in the service of his guru’s intimate friends. Thus he became very dear to Kṛṣṇadās Kavirāj Goswāmī. To serve the feet of Narottam Dās Ṭhākur was the only desire of Viśvanāth Chakravartī Ṭhākur, who was the fourth āchārya in disciplic succession from Narottam Dās.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viśvanātha-bhakta-sātha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;baladeva jagannātha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐ra priya śrī-bhakti-vinoda</strong><br>
<strong>mahā-bhāgavata-vara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-gaura-kiśora-vara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-bhajanete yā̐ra moda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Viśvanātha Chakravartī Ṭhākur was the śikṣā-guru (instructing spiritual master) of Baladeva Vidyābhūṣaṇa, to whom he taught the precepts of <i>Śrīmad Bhāgavatam</i>. Jagannātha Dās Bābājī was a very prominent Āchārya after Śrī Baladeva Vidyābhūṣaṇa and was the beloved śikṣā-guru of Śrī Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur. Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur’s intimate friend and associate was the eminent mahābhāgavat Śrī Gaura Kiśor Dās Bābājī, whose sole joy was found in Hari-bhajan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tad-anuga-mahājana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-kṛṣṇa-kīrtana-dhana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yebā dila puri’ jaga-kāma</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-vārṣabhānavī-varā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā sevya-sevāparā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐hāra dayita-dāsa nāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The follower of Śrīla Gaura Kiśor Dās Bābājī, the great soul Dayita Dās (Śrīla Saraswatī Ṭhākur), whose wealth is Śrī Kṛṣṇa kīrtan, fulfils the desires of the whole world. He is the dearmost of Śrī Vārṣabhānavī and is always engaged in Her service.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tad-abhinna deha-divya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;svarūpa-rūpa-raghu-jivya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā sevya yā̐ra pādapadma</strong><br>
<strong>susiddhānta-mūrti-dhara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-śrīdhara guru-vara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rupānuga-sādhu-śreya-sadma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The non-different personality and embodiment of bhakti-siddhānta as well as the embodiment of Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur; and who is living within the conception of Śrīla Svarūp Dāmodar, Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī, and Śrīla Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī; and whose lotus feet are always the worshipable happy shelter of the followers of Rūpa Goswāmī, he is Śrīla Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tā̐ra priya mano ’bhīṣṭa-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sthāpane sadā sacheṣṭa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakti-sundara śrī-govinda nāma</strong><br>
<strong>ei paramparā dhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sabe gaura-nija-jana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐dera uchchhiṣṭe mora kāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He who is a very dear disciple of that Śrīla Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj, and who is establishing his divine conception, and who is fulfilling his desire continuously, he is Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī. This is our grand preceptorial line and all of them are very dear to Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu. My sustenance is their remnants alone.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhayabhanjana-jayashiamsana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Dayita Dāsa Praṇati Pañchakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Homage unto Bhagavān Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur</h3>
<h4>Sanskrit composition by</h4>
<h4>Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhayabhañjana jayaśaṁsana-karuṇāyata-nayanam</strong><br>
<strong>kanakotpala janakojjvala-rasasāgara-chayanam</strong><br>
<strong>mukharīkṛta-dharaṇītala-harikīrttana-rasanam</strong><br>
<strong>kṣitipāvana-bhavatāraṇa-pihitāruṇa-vasanam</strong><br>
<strong>śubhadodaya-divase vṛṣaravijā-nija-dayitam</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi cha charaṇāntika-parichāraka-sahitam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He (of divine form) came forth from the birthplace of the golden lotus - the ocean of the mellow of divine consorthood. His large, merciful eyes dispel (the suffering souls’) fear and proclaim (the surrendered souls’) victory. By his tongue (constantly) vibrating the whole Earth planet with Śrī Kṛṣṇa-saṅkīrttan, and by his beauty resplendent in the robes of the sun’s radiance (saffron), he purifies the universe and dispels the suffering of material existence. On his Holy Day of Advent, I again and again bow down unto that beloved associate of Śrī Vṛṣabhānunandinī, and the servitors of his lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śaraṇāgata-bhajanavrata-chirapālana-charaṇam</strong><br>
<strong>sukṛtālaya-saralāśaya-sujanākhila-varaṇam</strong><br>
<strong>harisādhana-kṛtabādhana janaśāsana-kalanam</strong><br>
<strong>sacharāchara-karuṇākara-nikhilāśiva-dalanam</strong><br>
<strong>śubhadodaya-divase vṛṣaravijā-nija-dayitam</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi cha charaṇāntika-parichāraka-sahitam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The devotees surrendered in pure devotion are eternally protected at his lotus feet. He is worshipable by the pure souls endowed with sincerity and good fortune, and he accepts (even) those who obstruct the service of Śrī Hari, just to rectify them. As the very fountainhead of mercy upon all mobile and immobile beings, he crushes the inauspiciousness of the whole universe. On his Holy Day of Advent, I again and again bow down unto that beloved associate of Śrī Vṛṣabhānunandinī, and the servitors of his lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>atilaukika-gatitaulika-ratikautuka-vapuṣam</strong><br>
<strong>atidaivata-mativaiṣṇava-yati-vaibhava-puruṣam</strong><br>
<strong>sasanātana-raghurūpaka-paramāṇugacharitam</strong><br>
<strong>suvichāraka iva jīvaka iti sādhubhiruditam</strong><br>
<strong>śubhadodaya-divase vṛṣaravijā-nija-dayitam</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi cha charaṇāntika-parichāraka-sahitam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Like a miracle, his body moves with a joyful elegance and charm beyond the world’s understanding, fulfilling the artist’s aspiration. (Or, dancing in Pastimes transcendental to the world, his artistic form incites divine love’s hankering). His intellect surpasses that of (even) the demigods, and he is nobility incarnate as the commander in-chief of the Vaiṣṇava sannyāsīs (tridaṇḍi-yatis). The sādhus of profound intellect describe the nature of his personality as meticulously in the line of Śrī Sanātan, Śrī Rūpa, and Śrī Raghunāth, and they speak of him as being on the same plane as Śrī Jīvapāda (being superbly replete in perfect theistic conclusions). On his Holy Day of Advent, I again and again bow down unto that beloved associate of Śrī Vṛṣabhānunandinī, and the servitors of his lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarasītaṭa-sukhadoṭaja-nikaṭapriyabhajanam</strong><br>
<strong>lalitāmukha-lalanākula-paramādarayajanam</strong><br>
<strong>vrajakānana-bahumānana-kamalapriyanayanam</strong><br>
<strong>guṇamañjari-garimā-guṇa-harivāsanavayanam</strong><br>
<strong>śubhadodaya-divase vṛṣaravijā-nija-dayitam</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi cha charaṇāntika-parichāraka-sahitam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">On the bank of Śrī Rādhā Kuṇḍa at Svānanda Sukhada Kuñja, he is devoted to the service of his Beloved, furthermore he is greatly endeared to the divine damsels of Vraja headed by Lalitā. He is most favourite to Kamala-mañjarī who is pre-eminent in Vṛndāvan, and with the glorious qualities of Guṇamañjarī he builds the residence of Śrī Hari. On his Holy Day of Advent, I again and again bow down unto that beloved associate of Śrī Vṛṣabhānunandinī, and the servitors of his lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vimalotsavam amalotkala-puruṣottama-jananam</strong><br>
<strong>patitoddhṛti-karuṇāstṛti-kṛtanūtana-pulinam</strong><br>
<strong>mathurāpura-puruṣottama-samagaurapuraṭanam</strong><br>
<strong>harikāmaka-haridhāmaka-harināmaka-raṭanam</strong><br>
<strong>śubhadodaya-divase vṛṣaravijā-nija-dayitam</strong><br>
<strong>praṇamāmi cha charaṇāntika-parichāraka-sahitam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is immaculate joy incarnate, or, he is the graciousness or the joy of Bimalā Devī. He manifested the Pastimes of his advent at Puruṣottama Kṣetra in the holy land of Orissa, and he revealed his Pastimes of delivering the fallen souls by extending his mercy upon them (by awarding them the gift of divine love). Circumambulating Gaura Dhām in the same way as traditionally done at Vraja Dhām and Puruṣottama Dhām, he continuously propagates the loving desire of the Lord of Vraja, the divine abode of Vaikuṇṭha, and the Holy Name of Kṛṣṇa. On his day of holy advent, I again and again offer my obeisances unto that dear associate of Śrī Vṛṣabhānunandinī, and the servitors of his holy lotus feet.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jayare-jayare-jaya-paramahamsa-mahashiaya" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Āchārya-vandanā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrī Gopāl Govinda Mahānta</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jayare jayare jaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paramahaṁsa mahāśaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-bhakti-siddhānta sarasvatī</strong><br>
<strong>gosvāmī ṭhākura jaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama karuṇāmaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dīna-hīna agatira gati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories, all glories to the topmost personality, Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Goswāmī Ṭhākur, who is an ocean of mercy and the only resort for the fallen souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nīlāchale ha-iyā udaya</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-gauḍa-maṇḍale āsi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-bhakti parakāśi’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jivera nāśilā bhava-bhaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He made his advent in Śrī Nilāchala, Śrī Puri Dhām. He came to the holy dhām of Mahāprabhu, manifesting the line of pure devotion and destroying the fear of material life of the conditioned souls.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra mahimā gāi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hena sādhya mora nāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tabe pāri yadi deha’ śakti</strong><br>
<strong>viśva-hite avirata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āchāra-prachāre rata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśuddha śrī-rūpānugā-bhakti</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have no capacity to sing your glories. Only if you empower me can I do so. You are travelling unceasingly all over India, practising and preaching in a dedicated way the pure and genuine line of devotion in the current of Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrīpāṭa khetari-dhāma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ṭhākura śrī-narottama</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomāte tā̐hāra guṇa dekhi</strong><br>
<strong>śāstrera siddhānta-sāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuni’ lāge chamatkāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kutārkika dite nāre phā̐ki</strong></p>
<p class="translation">We see your qualities are like Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur who in the festival at Khetari Dhām delivered all the wonderful conclusions of the scriptures which defeated the unfair argument and deception of the scholars there.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuddha-bhakti-mata yata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;upadharma-kavalita</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;heriyā lokera mane trāsa</strong><br>
<strong>hāni’ susiddhānta-vāṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;upadharma khāna khāna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sajjanera bāḍāle ullāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By presenting the line of pure devotion he devoured the path of unscriptural practice, and seeing this everybody was afraid. He struck this unscriptural path leaving it in pieces, giving increasing joy to all honest and noble men.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>smārta-mata jaladhara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuddha-bhakti ravi-kara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āchchhādila bhāviyā antare</strong><br>
<strong>śāstra-sindhu-manthanete&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;susiddhānta jhañjhāvāte</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uḍāilā dig-digantare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Pure devotion is like the sun risen in the heart, while the path of logic is like clouds covering that sun. By churning the ocean of the scriptures, you spread perfect conclusions in all directions like a hurricane to disperse the clouds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sthāne sthāne kata maṭha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sthāpiyācha niṣkapaṭa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-sevā śikhāite jīve</strong><br>
<strong>maṭhera vaiṣṇava-gaṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kare sadā vitaraṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-guṇa-kathāmṛta bhave</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You founded many temples in many places without reservation, teaching the fallen souls service in divine love. In these temples the Vaiṣṇavas constantly distribute talks on the nectarean qualities of the Supreme Lord Hari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuddha-bhakti-mandākinī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vimala pravāha āni’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śītala karilā tapta-prāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>deśe deśe niṣkiñchana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prerilā vaiṣṇava-gaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vistārite hari-guṇa-gāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The current of pure devotion descends like the flow of the Ganges, cooling the fire of suffering of material life. Similarly, you sent the Vaiṣṇavas from place to place, widely distributing and chanting the qualities of Lord Hari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pūrve yathā gaurahari&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;māyāvāda chheda kari’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vaiṣṇava karilā kāśī-vāsī</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇava darśana-sukṣma-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vichāre tumi he dakṣa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;temati toṣilā vārāṇasī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Just as in the past, Lord Gaurahari severed the māyāvāda conception, converting the residents of Vārāṇasī into Vaiṣṇavas, similarly by expert analysis of the Vaiṣṇava philosophy you have enraptured all in Vārāṇasī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>daiva-varṇāśrama-dharma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-bhakti yāra marma</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śāstra-yukte karilā niśchaya</strong><br>
<strong>jñana-yoga-karma-chaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mūlya tāra kichhu naya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhaktira virodhī yadi haya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You showed that certainly devotion to Lord Hari at the core of daiva-varṇāśrama-dharma, is the directive of the revealed scriptures, while knowledge, yoga, and material activities, if averse to such devotion are of absolutely no value.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-gauḍa-maṇḍala-bhūmi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakta-saṅge parikrami’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sukīrti sthāpilā mahāśaya</strong><br>
<strong>abhinna vraja-maṇḍala&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gauḍa-bhūmi premojjvala</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prachāra ha-ila viśvamaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You founded parikramā of Śrī Gauḍa Maṇḍal, Śrī Nabadwīp Dhām in the association of the devotees, showing your glories as a great devotee. You showed the whole world the nectarean blissful nature of Śrī Gauḍa Maṇḍal and how it is non-different from Śrī Vraja Maṇḍal, Śrī Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kuliyāte pāṣaṇḍīrā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;atyāchāra kaila yārā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā sabāra doṣa kṣamā kari’</strong><br>
<strong>jagate kaile ghoṣaṇā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘taroriva sahiṣṇunā’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hana ‘kīrttanīyaḥ sadā hariḥ’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You forgave all the atheists of Kuliyā village, despite their blasphemous behaviour. Loudly you proclaimed to one and all, one who is more tolerant than a tree can chant the glories of Lord Hari endlessly.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-viśva-vaiṣṇava-rāja-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sabhā-madhye ‘pātrarāja’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;upādhi-bhūṣaṇe vibhūṣita</strong><br>
<strong>viśvera maṅgala lāgi’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ha-iyācha sarva tyāgī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;viśva-vāsī-jana-hite rata</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the grand assembly of Vaiṣṇava kings you are adorned with the title ‘Pātrarāj’, as the head of the assembly. For the benefit of the whole world, you have renounced everything and devoted yourself to the good of all.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>karitecha upakāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yāte para-upakāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;labhe jīva śrī-kṛṣṇa-sevāya</strong><br>
<strong>dūre yāya bhava-roga&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;khaṇḍe yāhe karma-bhoga</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hari-pāda-padma yāte pāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You untiringly perform benevolent works just to help the souls of this world to attain the service of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa. Then the disease of material suffering would leave them, their bondage to fruitive works would break, and they would attain the lotus feet of the Lord.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jīva moha-nidrā-gata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jāgāte vaikuṇṭha-dūta</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘gauḍīya’ pāṭhāo ghare ghare</strong><br>
<strong>uṭhare uṭhare bhāi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āra ta samaya nāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘kṛṣṇa bhaja’ bale uchchaiḥsvare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Seeing the souls of this world sleeping in illusion, you are sending the <i>Gauḍīya</i> magazine from door to door just like a messenger from the transcendental plane. It calls aloud, “Awake, arise, O brother, there is no more time to waste, serve and worship your Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāra mukhāravinda-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vigalita makaranda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;siñchita achyuta-guṇa-gāthā</strong><br>
<strong>śunile juḍāya prāṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tamo moha antardhāna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dūre yāya hṛdayera vyathā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The nectarean verses of the glorious qualities of the infallible Lord Kṛṣṇa issuing from your lotus mouth soothes our souls, dissipates our ignorance and illusion, and causes the pain in our hearts to go far away.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="17">
<h5>17</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jāni āmi mahāśaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yaśo-vāñchhā nāhi haya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bindu-mātra tomāra antare</strong><br>
<strong>tava guṇa-vīṇā-dhārī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mora kaṇṭha-vīṇā dhari’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avaśete balāya āmāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O great soul! I know that there is no trace of desire for fame in your heart. Your qualities, like an expert musician, take hold of the vīṇā of my voice and make me speak, devoid of any power over myself.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="18">
<h5>18</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaiṣṇavera guṇa-gāna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;karile jīvera trāṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuniyāchhi sādhu-guru-mukhe</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-bhakti samudaya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;janama saphala haya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;e bhava-sāgara tare sukhe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By singing the qualities of Vaiṣṇavas, the soul attains deliverance – this I have heard from the mouths of saints and gurus. Through complete devotion to Kṛṣṇa, one’s life becomes successful, and one happily crosses the ocean of material existence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="19">
<h5>19</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>te-kāraṇe prayāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yathā vāmanera āśa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gaganera chā̐da dharibāre</strong><br>
<strong>adoṣa-daraśī tumi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;adhama patita āmi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nija guṇe kṣamibā āmāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">For this reason I endeavour thus, just like a dwarf aspiring to reach the moon. I am lowly and fallen, but your nature is not to consider any offence, so I beg you to pardon my flaws.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="20">
<h5>20</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-gaurāṅga-pāriṣada&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ṭhākura bhakti-vinoda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dīna-hīna patitera bandhu</strong><br>
<strong>kali-tamaḥ vināśite&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ānilena avanīte</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tomā’ akalaṅka pūrṇa indu</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur, the associate of Śrī Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu and friend of the lowly fallen souls, brought you here, and you came like a spotless full moon to dispel the ignorance of this age of Kali.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="21">
<h5>21</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kara kṛpā vitaraṇa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prema-sudhā anukṣaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mātiyā uṭhuka jīva-gaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>hari-nāma-saṅkīrtane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāchuka jagata-jane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vaiṣṇava-dāsera nivedana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">So please keep incessantly showering your mercy, the nectar of ecstatic love of Godhead, and let all the souls of this world be uplifted by that. May all the people of the world dance in the congregational chanting of the Lord’s Holy Name – this is the prayer of this servant of the Vaiṣṇavas.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-pralaya-payodhi-jale-dhritavan-asi-vedam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Daśāvatār Stotram <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Sanskrit composition by Śrīla Jayadev Goswāmī</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pralaya-payodhi-jale dhṛtavān asi vedaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vihita-vahitra-charitram akhedam</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-mīna-śarīra jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Keśava! O Lord of the universe! O Lord Hari, who have assumed the form of a fish! All glories to You! You easily acted as a boat in the form of a giant fish just to give protection to the Vedas, which had become immersed in the turbulent sea of devastation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kṣitir iha vipulatare tiṣṭhati tava pṛṣṭhe</strong><br>
<strong>dharaṇi-dharaṇa-kina-chakra-gariṣṭhe</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-kūrma-śarīra jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Keśava! O Lord of the universe! O Lord Hari, who have assumed the form of a tortoise! All glories to You! In this incarnation as a divine tortoise the great Mandara Mountain rests upon Your gigantic back as a pivot for churning the ocean of milk. From holding up the huge mountain a large scarlike depression is put in Your back, which has become most glorious.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vasati daśana-śikhare dharaṇī tava lagnā</strong><br>
<strong>śaśini kalaṅka-kaleva nimagnā</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-śūkara-rūpa jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Keśava! O Lord of the universe! O Lord Hari, who have assumed the form of a boar! All glories to You! The earth, which had become immersed in the Garbhodaka Ocean at the bottom of the universe, sits fixed upon the tip of Your tusk like a spot upon the moon.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tava kara-kamala-vare nakham adbhuta-śṛṅgaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>dalita-hiraṇyakaśipu-tanu-bhṛṅgam</strong><br>
<strong>keśava dhṛta-narahari-rūpa jaya jagadīśa hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Keśava! O Lord of the universe! O Lord Hari, who have assumed the form of half-man, half-lion! All glories to You! Just as one can easily crush a wasp between one’s fingernails, so in the same way the body of the wasplike demon Hiraṇyakaśipu has been ripped apart by the wonderful pointed nails on Your beautiful lotus hands.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chhalayasi vikramaṇe balim adbhuta-vāmana</strong><br>
