<p class="translation">(All glories to) Kṛṣṇa, the son of Mother Yaśodā, the cowherd boy and giver of pleasure to the cows! (All glories to) the conquerer of Cupid, Lord Hari, who takes away all inauspiciousness, who is unlimited, and the awarder of liberation!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse">(jaya)<strong> achyuta mādhava rāma vṛndāvana-chandra</strong><br>
(jaya)<strong> muralī-vadana śyāma gopī-janānanda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">(All glories to) the infallible Lord, husband of the goddess of fortune, the supreme enjoyer, and the moon of Vṛndāvan! (All glories to) Kṛṣṇa, who always holds a flute to His mouth, who is the colour of a dark blue raincloud, and is the bliss of the gopīs!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shrita-kamala-kuchha-mandala-dhrita" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrita-kamalā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Sanskrit composition by Śrīla Jayadev Goswāmī</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrita-kamalā-kucha-maṇḍala dhṛta-kuṇḍala he</strong><br>
<strong>kalita-lalita-vana-māla jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Glories, glories to Lord Hari, the Supreme Personality of Godhead, who is bedecked with jeweled earrings and a garland of forest flowers, and whose feet are marked with a lotus!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dinamaṇi-maṇḍala-maṇḍana bhava-khaṇḍana he</strong><br>
<strong>muni-jana-mānasa-haṁsa jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The Lord’s face shines like the whorl of the sun. He removes the miseries of His devotees and is the resting place of the minds of the swan-like sages. Glories! Glories to Lord Śrī Hari!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kāliya-viṣadhara-gañjana jana-rañjana he</strong><br>
<strong>yadu-kula-nalina-dineśa jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Supreme Personality of Godhead who defeated the demoniac Kāliya serpent! O Lord, You are the beloved of all living entities and the sun in the galaxy of the Yadu dynasty. Glories! Glories to Lord Śrī Hari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>madhu-mura-naraka-vināśana garuḍāsana he</strong><br>
<strong>sura-kula-keli-nidāna jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, destroyer of the demons Madhu, Mura, and Naraka! Seated on Garuḍa, You are the source of joy for the demigods. All glories to Hari!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>amala-kamala-dala-lochana bhava-mochana he</strong><br>
<strong>tribhuvana-bhavana-nidhāna jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord Your eyes are like lotus petals, and You destroy the bondage of the material world. You are the maintainer of the three worlds. Glories to Lord Hari!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>janaka-sutā-kṛta-bhūṣaṇa jita-dūṣaṇa he</strong><br>
<strong>samara-śam-ita-daśa-kaṇṭha jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, You are adorned by Sītā, the daughter of Janaka, You were victorious over the demon Dūṣaṇa and all the asuras, and You smashed the greatest asura, the ten-headed Rāvaṇa. Glories, glories, to Lord Hari!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>abhinava-jaladhara-sundara dhṛta-mandara he</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-mukha-chandra-chakora jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Supreme Personality of Godhead who held Govardhan Hill! Your complexion is like a fresh monsoon cloud, and Śrī Rādhārāṇī is like a chakora bird who is nourished by drinking the light of Your moonlike face. Glories! Glories to Lord Hari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tava charaṇe praṇatā vayam iti bhāvaya he</strong><br>
<strong>kuru kuśalaṁ praṇateṣu jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, I offer my humble obeisances at Your lotus feet. Please bless me by Your limitless mercy. Glories! Glories to Lord Śrī Hari!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-jayadeva-kaver idaṁ kurute mudaṁ he</strong><br>
<strong>maṅgalam ujjvala-gīti jaya jaya deva hare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The poet Śrī Jayadeva offers this song of devotion and shining good fortune to Thee. All glories! All glories to Lord Śrī Hari!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nava-nirada-nindita" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Bhaja Kṛṣṇa-nidhiṁ <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>[Sanskrit]</h3>
<h4>unknown composer, from the Maṭh’s Śrī Gauḍīya Gitāñjali</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nava-nīrada-nindita-kānti-dharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>rasa-sāgara-nāgara-bhūpa-varam</strong><br>
<strong>śubha-baṅkima-chāru-śikhaṇḍa-śikhaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja kṛṣṇa-nidhiṁ vraja-rāja-sutam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His lustre defames that of fresh clouds,<br />He is the ocean of rasa, the beloved king of paramours,<br />and His charming peacock-plume crest is pleasantly tilted<br />(down to the left in obeisance to the feet of Śrī Rādhā).<br />Serve the treasure Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the son of the King of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhru-viśaṅkita-vaṅkima-śakra-dhanuṁ</strong><br>
<strong>mukha-chandra-vinindita-koṭi-vidhum</strong><br>
<strong>mṛdu-manda-suhāsya-subhāṣya-yutaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja kṛṣṇa-nidhiṁ vraja-rāja-sutam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His arched, anxious eyebrows resemble rainbows.<br />The moon of His face completely defames ten million moons.<br />He is endowed with fine speech and a fine, soft, and mild smile.<br />Serve the treasure Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the son of the King of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>suvikampad-anaṅga-sad-aṅga-dharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vraja-vāsi-manohara-veśa-karam</strong><br>
<strong>bhṛśa-lāñchhita-nīla-saroja-dṛśaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja kṛṣṇa-nidhiṁ vraja-rāja-sutam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His beautiful limbs tremble with divine love.<br />His dress captivates the minds of all the residents of Vraja.<br />His blue lotus eyes are decorated with sidelong glances.<br />Serve the treasure Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the son of the King of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>alakāvali-maṇḍita-bhāla-taṭaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śruti-dolita-mākara-kuṇḍalakam</strong><br>
<strong>kaṭi-veṣṭita-pīta-paṭaṁ sudhaṭaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja kṛṣṇa-nidhiṁ vraja-rāja-sutam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His forehead is adorned with curling locks.<br />His ears are decorated swaying makara earrings.<br />His waist is wrapped in a fine yellow dhoti.<br />Serve the treasure Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the son of the King of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kala-nūpura-rājita-chāru-padaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>maṇi-rañjita-gañjita-bhṛṅga-madam</strong><br>
<strong>dhvaja-vajra-jhaṣāṅkita-pāda-yugaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja kṛṣṇa-nidhiṁ vraja-rāja-sutam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His charming feet, resplendent with tinkling anklets,<br />are illumined by jewels, and are intoxicating to buzzing bees.<br />His feet are marked with flag, thunderbolt, and fish.<br />Serve the treasure Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the son of the King of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhṛśa-chandana-charchita-chāru-tanuṁ</strong><br>
<strong>maṇi-kaustubha-garhita-bhānu-tanum</strong><br>
<strong>vraja-bāla-śiromaṇi-rūpa-dhṛtaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja kṛṣṇa-nidhiṁ vraja-rāja-sutam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His charming figure is smeared generously with sandalwood paste,<br />and adorned with the Kaustubha jewel. His figure has eclispsed the sun.<br />His form is the crest-jewel of the youths of Vraja.<br />Serve the treasure Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the son of the King of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sura-vṛnda-suvandya-mukunda-hariṁ</strong><br>
<strong>sura-nātha-śiromaṇi-sarva-gurum</strong><br>
<strong>giridhāri-murāri-purāri-paraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja kṛṣṇa-nidhiṁ vraja-rāja-sutam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is most worshipable to the suras (saintly).<br />He is the giver of liberation, and the taker (of the heart).<br />He is the crest-jewel of suras and masters, and the Guru of all.<br />He is the lifter of Govardhan, the enemy of Mura, and Lord Śiva’s superior.<br />Serve the treasure Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the son of the King of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛṣabhānu-sutā-vara-keli-paraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>rasa-rāja-śiromaṇi-veśa-dharam</strong><br>
<strong>jagad-īśvaram īśvaram īḍya-varaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja kṛṣṇa-nidhiṁ vraja-rāja-sutam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is immersed in the finest play with Vṛṣabhānu’s daughter,<br />wearing the dress of the crest-jewel amongst the kings of rasa.<br />He is the Lord of the universe.<br />He is the Lord, the dearmost object of worship.<br />Serve the treasure Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the son of the King of Vraja.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-mayura-mukuta-pitambara-dhari" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Mayūra-mukuṭa <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Sanskrit composition by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse">(jaya)<strong> mayūra-mukuṭa-pītāmbara-dhārī</strong><br>
<strong>muralī-dhara govardhana-dhārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">(All glories to) Śrī Kṛṣṇa, holder of the flute Muralī, and lifter of Govardhan Hill! O Kṛṣṇa, who wears a crown adorned with a pea­cock feather, and who is dressed in golden clothes!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse">(jaya)<strong> śrī-rādhā-mādhava kuñja-vihārī</strong><br>
<strong>muralī-dhara govardhana-dhārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Kṛṣṇa, holder of the flute Muralī, and lifter of Govardhan Hill! O Kṛṣṇa, the Lord of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī, and who plays in the groves of Śrī Vṛndāvan!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse">(jaya)<strong> yaśodā-nandana kṛṣṇa murāri</strong><br>
<strong>muralī-dhara govardhana-dhārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Kṛṣṇa, holder of the flute Muralī, and lifter of Govardhan Hill! All glories to You, Kṛṣṇa, son of Mother Yaśodā, and the killer of the Mura demon!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse">(jaya)<strong> gopī-jana-vallabha vaṁśī-vihārī</strong><br>
<strong>muralī-dhara govardhana-dhārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Kṛṣṇa, holder of the flute Muralī, and lifter of Govardhan Hill! All glories to You, Kṛṣṇa, the dearmost lover of all the cowherd girls of Vṛndāvan, and the player of the flute Vaṁśī!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-jaya-jaya-radha-krishna-jugala-milan" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Jugala Ārati <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jaya jaya rādhā-kṛṣṇa jugala-milana</strong><br>
<strong>ārati karaye lalitādi sakhī-gaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All glories, all glories to the meeting of the transcendental couple, Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. The gopīs headed by Lalitā Devī perform the ceremony of Their worship.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>madana-mohana rūpa tri-bhaṅga-sundara</strong><br>
<strong>pītāmbara śikhi-puchchha-chūḍā-manohara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kṛṣṇa is the attractor of Cupid, and His beautiful form is bending in three places. He wears yellow garments and a charming peacock-feather crown.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>lalita-mādhava-bāme vṛṣabhānu-kanyā</strong><br>
<strong>nīla-basanā gaurī rūpe guṇe dhanyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Dressed in blue garments, Her complexion fair, and Her virtues glorious, King Vṛṣabhānu’s daughter, Śrīmatī Rādhikā is on the left of the charming Lord Mādhava.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nānā-bidha alaṅkāra kare jhalamala</strong><br>
<strong>hari-mano-bimohana vadana ujjvala</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Her various ornaments sparkle and Her effulgent face charms the heart of Lord Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viśākhādi sakhī-gaṇa nānā rāge gāya</strong><br>
<strong>priya-narma-sakhī jata chāmāra ḍhulāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Viśākhā and the other girls sing songs in various tunes and moods as Kṛṣṇa’s intimate playful girlfriends fan the Divine Couple with whisks.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-rādhā-mādhava-pada-sarasija-āśe</strong><br>
<strong>bhakativinoda sakhī-pade sukhe bhāse</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Yearning to attain the lotus feet of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa, Bhakti Vinod floats in happiness at the gopīs’ feet.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bandhu-sange-yadi-tava-ranga-parihasa" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Keśi Tīrtha Upakaṇṭhe <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Sanskrit composition by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>smerāṁ bhaṅgī-traya-parichitāṁ sāchi-vistīrṇa-dṛṣṭiṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vaṁśī-nyastādhara-kiśalayām ujjvalāṁ chandrakeṇa</strong><br>
<strong>govindākhyāṁ hari-tanum itaḥ keśi-tīrthopakaṇṭhe</strong><br>
<strong>mā prekṣiṣṭhās tava yadi sakhe bandhu-saṅge ’sti raṅgaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>(Bhakti-rasāmṛta-sindhu: 1.2.239)</i><br /><i>(Śrī Chaitanya-charitāmṛta: Ādi-līlā, 5.224)</i><br>
My dear friend, if you are indeed attached to your worldly friends, do not look at the smiling face of Lord Govinda as He stands on the bank of the Yamunā at Keśī Ghāṭa. Casting sidelong glances, He rests the tender blossoms of His lips upon His flute. His transcendental body, bending in three places, appears very bright in the moonlight.<br>
<i>Corresponding Bengali composition by unknown composer, from the Maṭh’s Śrī Gauḍīya Gitāñjali</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h3>Bandhu-saṅge</h3>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bandhu-saṅge yadi tava&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;raṅga parihāsa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;thāke abhilāṣa – thāke abhilāṣa</strong><br>
<strong>tabe mora kathā rākha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yeyo nāka yeyo nāka</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mathurāya keśī-tīrtha-ghāṭera sakāśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If you have desire – if you have any desire – to laugh and enjoy in the company of friends, then remember my words: do not go, do not go, near the ghāṭ at Keśī Tīrtha in Mathurā!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>govinda-vigraha dhari’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tathāya āchhena hari</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nayane baṅkima-dṛṣṭi mukhe manda-hāsa</strong><br>
<strong>kibā tri-bhaṅgima ṭhāma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;varṇa samujjvala śyāma</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nava-kiśalaya śobhā śrī-aṅge prakāśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bearing the figure of Govinda, Hari is present there with a side-long glance in His eyes and a mild smile on His face. What a threefold pose! His complexion is a radiant dark blue, and the beauty of His divine limbs resembles tender new leaves.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>adhare vaṁśīṭi tāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;aniṣṭera mūlādhāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śikhi-chūḍākeo bhāi karo nā viśvāsa</strong><br>
<strong>se mūrti nayane here&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;keha nāhi ghare phire</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsārī gṛhīra ye go haya sarva-nāśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The flute at His lips is the cause of disaster! O brother, no one can believe His peacock feather crest, and after seeing His figure with their own eyes, no one returns home! A family man and householder who does so meets with utter ruin.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāi mora mane baḍa trāsa</strong><br>
<strong>ghaṭibe vipada bhāri&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yeyo nāko he saṁsārī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mathurāya keśī-tīrtha-ghāṭera sakāśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Thus I feel great fear in my mind that grave danger will befall you. So do not go, O family man, near the ghāṭ at Keśī Tīrtha in Mathurā!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-radha-madhava-kunja-bihari" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Jaya Rādhā-Mādhava <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="verse">(jaya)<strong> rādhā-mādhava&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(jaya)<strong> kuñja-vihārī</strong><br>
(jaya)<strong> gopī-jana-vallabha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(jaya)<strong> giri-vara-dhārī</strong><br>
(jaya)<strong> yaśodā-nandana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(jaya)<strong> vraja-jana-rañjana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</strong>(jaya)<strong> yāmuna-tīra-vana-chārī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">(All glories to) Kṛṣṇa is the lover of Rādhā. He displays many amorous Pastimes in the groves of Vṛndāvan, He is the lover of the cowherd maidens of Vraja, the holder of the great hill named Govardhan, the beloved son of Mother Yaśodā, the delighter of the inhabitants of Vraja, and He wanders in the forests along the banks of the River Yamunā.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-he-deva-bhavantam-vande" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Kṛṣṇa-vandanā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Sanskrit composition by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;he deva bhavantaṁ vande</strong><br>
<strong>man-mānasa-madhukara marpaya nija-pada-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;paṅkaja-makarande</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, I offer my respectful obeisances to You. May you place the bumblebee of my mind in the honey of Your lotus feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yady api samādhiṣu vidhir api paśyati</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;na tava nakhāgra-marīchim</strong><br>
<strong>idam ichchhāmi niśamya tavāchyuta</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tad api kṛpādbhuta-vīchim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Although Brahmā in his samādhi cannot obtain a glimpse of even a ray of the tips of the nails of Your feet, still, O Achyuta, having heard of the waves of Your astonishing mercy, I yearn to see You.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaktir udañchati yady api mādhava</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;na tvayi mama tila-mātrī</strong><br>
<strong>parameśvaratā tad api tavādhika-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;durghaṭa-ghaṭana-vidhātrī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Mādhava, although I do not have even a sesame seed of devotion for You, Your supreme power can make even the impossible become possible.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ayam avilolatayādya sanātana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kalitādbhuta-rasa-bhāram</strong><br>
<strong>nivasatu nityam ihāmṛta-nindini</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vindan madhurima-sāram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O eternal Lord, may the bumblebee of my mind, finding there the most wonderful sweetness, eternally stay in the honey of Your lotus feet, which rebuke the sweetest nectar.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-namamishvaram-sachchidananda-rupam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Dāmodarāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Sanskrit composition by Satyavrata Muni in Śrī Padma-purāṇa</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>namāmīśvaraṁ sachchidānanda-rūpaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>lasat-kuṇḍalaṁ gokule bhrājamānam</strong><br>
<strong>yaśodā-bhiyolūkhalād dhāvamānaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>parāmṛṣṭam atyantato drutya gopyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To the Supreme Lord, whose form is the embodiment of eternal existence, knowledge, and bliss, whose shark-shaped earrings are swinging to and fro, who is beautifully shining in the divine realm of Gokul, who (due to the offence of breaking the pot of yoghurt that his mother was churning into butter and then stealing the butter that was kept hanging from a swing) is quickly running from the wooden grinding mortar in fear of Mother Yaśodā, but who has been caught from behind by her, who ran after Him with greater speed – to that Supreme Lord, Śrī Dāmodar, I offer my humble obeisances.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rudantaṁ muhur netra-yugmaṁ mṛjantaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>karāmbhoja-yugmena sātaṅka-netram</strong><br>
<strong>muhuḥ śvāsa-kampa-trirekhāṅka-kaṇṭha-</strong><br>
<strong>sthita-graiva-dāmodaraṁ bhakti-baddham</strong></p>
<p class="translation">(Seeing the whipping stick in His mother’s hand,) He is crying and rubbing His eyes again and again with His two lotus hands. His eyes are filled with fear, and the necklace of pearls around His neck, which is marked with three lines like a conchshell, is shaking because of His quick breathing due to crying. To this Supreme Lord, Śrī Dāmodar, whose belly is bound not with ropes but with His mother’s pure love, I offer my humble obeisances.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>itīdṛk svalīlābhir ānanda-kuṇḍe</strong><br>
<strong>svaghoṣaṁ nimajjantam ākhyāpayantaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>tadīyeṣita-jñeṣu bhaktair jitatvaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>punaḥ prematas taṁ śatāvṛtti vande</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By such childhood Pastimes as this He is drowning the inhabitants of Gokul in pools of ecstasy, and is revealing to those devotees who are absorbed in knowledge of His supreme majesty and opulence that He is only conquered by devotees whose pure love is imbued with intimacy and is free from all conceptions of awe and reverence. With great love I again offer my obeisances to Lord Dāmodar hundreds and hundreds of times.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>varaṁ deva mokṣaṁ na mokṣāvadhiṁ vā</strong><br>
<strong>na chānyaṁ vṛṇe ’haṁ vareśād apīha</strong><br>
<strong>idan te vapur nātha gopāla-bālaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>sadā me manasy āvirāstāṁ kim anyaiḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, although You are able to give all kinds of benedictions, I do not pray to You for the boon of impersonal liberation, nor the highest liberation of eternal life in Vaikuṇṭha, nor any other boon (which may be obtained by executing the nine processes of bhakti). O Lord, I simply wish that this form of Yours as Bāla Gopāl in Vṛndāvan may ever be manifest in my heart, for what is the use to me of any other boon besides this?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>idan te mukhāmbhojam avyakta-nīlair</strong><br>
<strong>vṛtaṁ kuntalaiḥ snigdha-raktaiś cha gopyā</strong><br>
<strong>muhuś chumbitaṁ bimba-raktādharaṁ me</strong><br>
<strong>manasy āvirāstām alaṁ lakṣa-lābhaiḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, Your lotus face, which is encircled by locks of soft black hair tinged with red, is kissed again and again by Mother Yaśoda, and Your lips are reddish like the bimba fruit. May this beautiful vision of Your lotus face be ever manifest in my heart. Thousands and thousands of other benedictions are of no use to me.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>namo deva dāmodarānanta viṣṇo</strong><br>
<strong>prasīda prabho duḥkha-jālābdhi-magnaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpā-dṛṣṭi-vṛṣṭyātidīnaṁ batānu-</strong><br>
<strong>gṛhāṇeśa mām ajñam edhy akṣi-dṛśyaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Supreme Godhead, I offer my obeisances unto You. O Dāmodar! O Ananta! O Viṣṇu! O Master! O my Lord, be pleased with me. By showering Your glance of mercy upon me, deliver this poor ignorant fool who is immersed in an ocean of worldly sorrows, and become visible to my eyes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kuverātmajau baddha-mūrtyaiva yadvat</strong><br>
<strong>tvayā mochitau bhakti-bhājau kṛtau cha</strong><br>
<strong>tathā prema-bhaktiṁ svakāṁ me prayachchha</strong><br>
<strong>na mokṣe graho me ’sti dāmodareha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord Dāmodar, just as the two sons of Kuvera – Maṇigrīva and Nalakīśvara – were delivered from the curse of Nārada and made into great devotees by You in Your form as a baby tied with rope to a wooden grinding mortar, in the same way, please give to me Your own prema-bhakti. I only long for this and have no desire for any kind of liberation.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>namas te ’stu dāmne sphurad-dīpti-dhāmne</strong><br>
<strong>tvadīyodarāyātha viśvasya dhāmne</strong><br>
<strong>namo rādhikāyai tvadīya-priyāyai</strong><br>
<strong>namo ’nanta-līlāya devāya tubhyam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord Dāmodar, I first of all offer my obeisances to the brilliantly effulgent rope which binds Your belly. I then offer my obeisances to Your belly, which is the abode of the entire universe. I humbly bow down to Your most beloved Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī, and I offer all obeisances to You, the Supreme Lord, who displays unlimited Pastimes.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-amara-samana-hina-nahi-e-samsare" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Āmāra Samāna Hīna Nāhi <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>from Kalyaṇa-kalpa-taru by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmāra samāna hīna nāhi e saṁsāre</strong><br>
<strong>asthira hayechhi paḍi’ bhava-pārāvāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">There is no one as wretched as I am in this world. I have fallen into this ocean of material existence and become distraught.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kula-devī yogamāyā more kṛpā kari’</strong><br>
<strong>āvaraṇa samvaribe kabe viśvodarī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O family Goddess! O Yogamāyā! When will you bestow your mercy upon me and remove the veil (of illusion) O You from whose womb manifests the universe?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śunechhi āgame vede mahimā tomāra</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-vimukhe bā̐dhi’ karāo saṁsāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have heard of your glories in the Āgamas and Vedas. You bind souls who are averse to Śrī Kṛṣṇa and subject them to saṁsāra.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-sāmmukhya yāra bhāgya-krame haya</strong><br>
<strong>tāre mukti diyā kara aśoka abhaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">To those who by good fortune have become inclined to Śrī Kṛṣṇa, you grant liberation and make them free from lamentation and fear.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e dāse janani kari’ akaitava dayā</strong><br>
<strong>vṛndāvane deha’ sthāna tumi yogamāyā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Mother! Please bestow your non-deceiving grace upon this servant and give me a place in Vṛndāvan. You are Yogamāyā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tomāke laṅghiyā kothā jīve kṛṣṇa pāya</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa rāsa prakaṭila tomāra kṛpāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">How can a jīva who neglects you attain Kṛṣṇa? Kṛṣṇa manifested the Rāsa Dance by your mercy.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tumi kṛṣṇa-sahacharī jagata-jananī</strong><br>
<strong>tumi dekhāile more kṛṣṇa-chintāmaṇi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are Kṛṣṇa’s companion and the mother of the world. You have shown me Kṛṣṇa, the wish-fulfilling jewel.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>niṣkapaṭa haye mātā chāo mora pāne</strong><br>
<strong>vaiṣṇave viśvāsa vṛddhi haka prati-kṣaṇe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O mother, please non-deceivingly glance upon me. May my faith in the Vaiṣṇava increase at every moment.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaiṣṇava-charaṇa vinā bhava-pārāvāra</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-vinoda nāre ha-ibāre pāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Without (shelter at) the feet of the Vaiṣṇavas, Bhakti Vinod cannot reach the shore of the ocean of material existence.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-janama-saphala-tara-krishna-darashiana-jara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Rūpa-kīrtan <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>janama saphala tāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-daraśana yāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāgye ha-iyāchhe eka-bāra</strong><br>
<strong>vikaśiyā hṛn-nayana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kari’ kṛṣṇa daraśana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhāḍe jīva chitera vikāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His birth is successful whose good fortune dawns, allowing him to have the vision of Lord Kṛṣṇa just once. When the soul gives up all delusions of the mind, then only will they see the vision of Kṛṣṇa blooming within the eyes of their heart.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndāvana-keli chatur vanamālī</strong><br>
<strong>tribhaṅga-bhaṅgimā-rūpa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vaṁśī-dhārī aparūpa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rasamaya nidhi, guṇa-śālī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One sees Kṛṣṇa there fully decorated with garlands of forest flowers as the most expert connoisseur of all the amorous love-sports in Vṛndāvan. His transcendental form bent in three places, and playing wonderfully on His flute, He is the reservoir of all relishable mellows and is the abode of all virtuous qualities.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>varṇa nava jaladhara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śire śikhi-pichchha-vara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;alakā tilaka śobhā pāya</strong><br>
<strong>paridhāne pīta-vāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vadane madhura hāsa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hena rūpa jagat mātāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By such a beautiful form He is maddening the entire universe. His complexion is like that of a fresh, new rain cloud, His head is decorated with a big peacock feather, and the sandalwood tilak on His forehead is most becoming. Wearing brilliant yellow-coloured garments, He stands with His face decorated by a wide, sweet smile.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>indra-nīla jini&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-rūpa-khāni</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;heriyā kadamba-mūle</strong><br>
<strong>mana uchāṭana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā chale charaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁsāra gelāma bhule</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Beholding Him standing at the base of a kadamba tree, I can see that Kṛṣṇa’s beauty is conquering the lustre of an entire mine of sapphires. Seeing this, my mind has become so restless that my feet will move no longer, and I’ve completely forgotten about my family and home life in this world.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse">(sakhī he)<strong> sudhāmaya se rūpa mādhurī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dekhile nayana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;haya achetana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jhare premamaya vāri</strong></p>
<p class="translation">(O sakhī!) O dear girlfriend! Seeing that sweet form abounding in nectar, I have fallen unconscious as a fountain of tears gushing with ecstatic love cascades from my eyes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kibā chūḍā śire&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kibā vaṁśī kare</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kibā se tribhaṅga ṭhāma</strong><br>
<strong>charaṇa-kamale&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;amiyā uchhale</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhāte nūpura dāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">What a wonderful crown upon His head! What a wonderful flute He is holding in His hand! What a wonderfully beautiful form as He stands in His three-fold bending posture! From His lotus feet, nectar is overflowing, and a tinkling sound emanates from clusters of ankle bells decorating them.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sadā āśā kari&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhṛṅga-rūpa dhari</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇa-kamale sthāna</strong><br>
<strong>anāyāse pāi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-guṇa gāi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āra nā bhajiba āna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Accepting the form of a honeybee, I always hope for a residence near His lotus feet. And I will get it very easily because I always sing the glories of Kṛṣṇa, adoring no one else.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shiuno-he-rasika-jon-krishna-guna-aganon" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Guṇa-kīrtana <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śuna he rasika-jana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-guṇa-agaṇana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ananta kahite nāhi pāre</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa jagatera guru&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa vāñchhā-kalpa-taru</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nāvika se bhava-pārāvāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Listen to this, oh wisest relishers of mellows! The transcendental qualities of Śrī Kṛṣṇa are innumerable; indeed, it is not possible to describe such unlimited divine attributes. Kṛṣṇa is the spiritual master of the entire universe, He is like a wish-fulfilling desire-tree, and He is the helmsman of the boat on the ocean of material existence.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hṛdaya pīḍita yāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa chikitsaka tāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhava-roga nāśite chatura</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa bahirmukha-jane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;premāmṛta-vitaraṇe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;krame laya nija antaḥpura</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kṛṣṇa is just like a doctor for those whose hearts are ailing, as He is very expert at destroying the diseased condition of material existence. By distributing the nectar of ecstatic love to all the inimical souls, He gradually takes them back into His own confidential realm.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>karma-bandha-jñāna-bandha-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;āveśe mānava andha</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāre kṛṣṇa karuṇā-sāgara</strong><br>
<strong>pāda-padma-madhu diyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;andha-bhāva ghuchāiyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;charaṇe karena anuchara</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kṛṣṇa is an ocean of compassion for those who are bound up in fruitive reactions, as well as for those bound up in philosophical speculation and for those blinded by other human perplexities. Giving the honey of His lotus feet, and thus relieving the darkness of such worldliness, He makes one an attendant of His own feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vidhi-mārga-rata-jane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;svādhīnatā-ratna-dāne</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rāga-mārge karāna praveśa</strong><br>
<strong>rāga-vaśavartī haye&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pārakīya-bhāvāśraye</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;labhe jīva kṛṣṇa-premāveśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kṛṣṇa eventually bestows the jewel of independence unto those persons who are attached to the path of rules and regulations, thereby allowing them entrance into the path of spontaneous loving service. Becoming influenced by such spontaneity, remaining under the shelter of the mellows of unwedded love (parakīya-bhāva), the soul finally attains all the symptoms of ecstatic love for Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>premāmṛta-vāri-dhārā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā pāna-rata tā̐rā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa tā̐hādera bandhu pati</strong><br>
<strong>sei saba vraja-jana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sukalyāṇa-niketana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dīna-hīna vinodera gati</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kṛṣṇa is the dearmost friend and husband of those who are always attached to drinking the fountain of ecstatic tears of love. Thus, the goal of this most fallen and lowly Bhakti Vinod is to be with all such residents of Vraja within the abode of supreme auspiciousness.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-rupa-manjari-pada-sei-mora-sampada" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī-Rūpa-Mañjarī-pada <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-rūpa-mañjarī-pada&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei mora sampada</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei mora bhajana-pūjana</strong><br>
<strong>sei mora prāṇa-dhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei mora ābharaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei mora jīvanera jīvana</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sei mora rasa-nidhi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei mora vāñchhā-siddhi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei mora vedera dharama</strong><br>
<strong>sei vrata sei tapa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei mora mantra japa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei mora dharama karama</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>anukūla habe vidhi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;se pade ha-ibe siddhi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nirakhiba e dui nayane</strong><br>
<strong>se rūpa-mādhurī-rāśi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāṇa-kūvalaya-śaśī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;praphullita habe niśi-dine</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tuyā adarśana-ahi-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;garale jārala dehi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chira-dina tāpita jīvana</strong><br>
<strong>hā hā prabhu kara dayā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deha more pada-chhāyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;narottama la-ila śaraṇa</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h3>Commentary by Śrīla B.R. Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj:</h3>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="translation"><i>Śrī-Rūpa-Mañjarī-pada, sei mora sampada, sei mora bhajana-pūjana</i> – my everything is Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī’s holy feet. We are to discuss so many classifications and positions of rasa: śānta, dāsya, sakhya, vātsalya, madhura. And in madhura-rasa Rādhārāṇī’s camp is special. Then again there are so many gradations of sakhīs. Then there is the class of the mañjarīs, the younger girls, and they have more freedom to approach: when Rādhā and Govinda are in union in a private environment, the mañjarīs can still approach. They have such freedom to visit Them. If any materials of service are necessary, the sakhīs send the mañjarīs to that place. The sakhīs do not approach there. In that way the mañjarīs enjoy the best confidence.<br>
The most secret service of both can be supplied through the mañjarīs. In the highest position they have free entrance, and their leader is Rūpa Mañjarī. She is understood to be the leader of the whole group of younger girls, the mañjarīs, therefore in madhura-bhajan she is all-in-all. This has been taught to us by Narottam Ṭhākur. For us – and the younger batch – she is our highest resort.<br>
<i>Sei mora sampada</i> – my wealth is there in her feet. <i>Sei mora bhajana-pūjana</i> – my worship and service is also in her. <i>Sei mora prāṇa-dhana, sei mora ābharaṇa</i> – the very wealth and gist of my life is there; and the ornaments of my life, if any there may be, that is within her grace. <i>Sei mora jīvanera jīvana</i> – indeed, the very life of my life, if there is anything, that is also her. I am for her pleasure.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="translation"><i>Sei mora rasa-nidhi</i> – if there is anything, the source of any and all desirable ecstasy is only to be found there. The mine, source, and fountainhead of all rasa is there in her feet. <i>Sei mora vāñchā-siddhi</i> – and if I am to expect any other fulfilment in life, that is also there in her feet. <i>Sei mora vedera dharama</i> – if any duty is recommended by the Vedas for me, I would like that such duty be at her feet.<br>
<i>Sei vrata, sei tapa</i> – if at all there is any desired accumulation from any penance or observance of vows, that is also to be found there – <i>sei mora mantra-japa</i> – and the continuous repetition of a mantra in japa all ends there: all things have only one end in my case, and that is that they all meet in different phases in the feet of Rūpa Mañjarī. If she is satisfied, then the variegated nature of all sorts of my devotional practices are satisfied. <i>Sei mor dharama-karama</i> – and any of my conceptions of duty or activity of any phases or form all have one end and meet in one point: the holy feet of Rūpa Mañjarī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="translation"><i>Anukūla habe viddhi, se-pade ha-ibe siddhi</i> – I only pray to the controller who is at the root of all these arrangements of affairs in this world, “Please connect me there. O Absolute Manager, may you grant my prayer that all my tendencies may go to attain her favour. <i>Nirakhiba e dui nayane</i> – and it will be so very intense and concrete as if I can see it with these eyes. It will not be vague, abstract, or imagination, but I want the most concrete realisation by the grace of the Absolute Manager.” <i>Se Rūpa-mādhurī-rāśi, prāṇa-kuvalaya-śaśī</i> – what sort of high class beauty is there in her holy feet! I want to jump. Let my heart be a lotus.<br>
There are two classes of lotus: the white lotus came from the sun and the red from the moon. The kuvalaya is a mixture of the two. The lustre of her body will feed me day and night as the life of my life which is compared to a kuvalaya. The kuvalaya is fed and nourished by the lustre of the moon, and her holy feet are the moon, and my very vitality is likened to the lotus. May the lustre of her beauty maintain and nourish my heart of hearts. <i>Praphullita habe niśi-dine</i> – and the lotus of my heart will grow by that ray, and dance.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="translation"><i>Tuyā adarśana-ahi</i> – this is my prayer, but what is my present condition? My present condition is in the negative side, my Mistress. My present position is such that without having a sight of you, Your Grace, my heart will burn into ashes. That is my present position. <i>Garale jārala dehī</i> – this is just like the poison of a cobra which produces pain within my heart. I have been bitten by a snake, and what is that snake? It is that I am not getting any direct contact with you, Your Grace. The serpent’s poison has captured my heart and I am going to die. <i>Chira-dina tāpita jīvana</i> – and this is not a sudden thing, but from the beginning I have been suffering this sort of pain of separation from such a beautiful and hopeful life of fulfilment.<br>
<i>Hā hā Prabhu* kara dayā, deha more pada-chāyā, Narottama la-ila śaraṇa</i> – but I have now reached the extreme position and for the last time I am begging for your favour; otherwise I am going to be finished.<br>
This was composed by Narottam Ṭhākur in praise of Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī. In this way he has given us light of the higher quarter of bhajan life. This sampradāya is named as the Rūpānuga-sampradāya, and our Guru-paramparā and all these things are adjusted according to that. He wrote many songs and they are of very substantive firmament, very ecstatic, and of deep faith.<br>
*<i class="translation__note">Footnote:</i><br>
 personally adjusted by Śrīla Gurudev from ‘Rūpa’ to ‘Prabhu’.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-radhika-charana-padma-sakala-shreyera" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Rādhāṣṭaka <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Kīrtan One</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhikā-charaṇa-padma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sakala śreyera sadma</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yatane ye nāhi ārādhila</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-padāṅkita-dhāma&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛndāvana yāra nāma</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhā ye nā āśraya karila</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The lotus feet of Śrī Rādhā are the abode of all auspiciousness, the supreme goal of life. One who in their life has failed to take shelter of that holy dhām named Vṛndāvan which is decorated by the lotuslike Rādhā,…</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhikā-bhāva-gambhīra-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chinte yebā mahādhīra-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gaṇa-saṅga nā kaila jīvane</strong><br>
<strong>kemane se śyāmānanda-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rasa-sindhu-snānānanda</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;labhibe bujhaha eka-mane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">…who in their life has failed to associate with those who are intimate with Śrī Rādhā who are deeply filled with Rādhā’s mood of devotion and who are greatly intelligent – how will such a person bathe in the rasa-sindhu, the ocean of ecstatic joy which is Śyāmasundar? You must singlemindedly understand this point.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhikā ujjvala-rasera āchārya</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-mādhava-śuddha-prema vichārya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Rādhā is the exemplary teacher of the mellows of conjugal love. Pure ecstatic love for Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Mādhava is what is to be considered and discussed.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ye dharila rādhā-pada parama yatane</strong><br>
<strong>se pāila kṛṣṇa-pada amūlya-ratane</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who holds tightly to the lotus feet of Śrī Rādhā with great care will certainly attain the priceless jewels of the lotus feet of Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-pada vinā kabhu kṛṣṇa nāhi mile</strong><br>
<strong>rādhāra dāsīra kṛṣṇa sarva-vede bale</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Without taking shelter of the lotus feet of Śrī Rādhā, one will never meet Kṛṣṇa. All the Vedas say that Kṛṣṇa belongs to the maidservants of Śrī Rādhā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>choḍata dhana-jana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kalatra-suta-mita</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;choḍata karama geyāna</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-pada-paṅkaja-&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;madhurata-sevana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakati-vinoda-paramāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Having given up wealth, followers, wife, children, family, and friends, material activities, and intellectual knowledge, one should become absorbed in the divine sweetness of service to the lotus feet of Śrī Rādhā as one of Her maidservants in madhura-rasa. This is Bhakti Vinod’s solemn declaration.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-virajara-pare-shiuddha-paravyoma-dhama" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Rādhāṣṭaka <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Kīrtan Two</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>virajāra pāre śuddha paravyoma-dhāma</strong><br>
<strong>tad upari śrī-gokula-vṛndāraṇya nāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Beyond the Virajā River lies the pure spiritual sky, and above that Vaikuṇṭha realm lies the divine abode known as Śrī Goloka Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛndāvana chintāmaṇi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chid-ānanda-ratna-khani</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chinmaya apūrva-daraśana</strong><br>
<strong>ta̐hi mājhe chamatkāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa vanaspati-sāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nīla-maṇi tamāla yemana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The land of Vṛndāvan is made of spiritual gems and is therefore likened to a mine of fully cognisant and blissful jewels. This transcendentally conscious realm is certainly a wonderful and extraordinary sight. Within that abode is the most astonishing presence of Lord Kṛṣṇa, who is compared to a tamāl tree, the king of trees possessing the hue of a dark sapphire.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tāhe eka svarṇa-mayī&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;latā sarva-dhāma-jayī</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uṭhiyāchhe parama-pāvanī</strong><br>
<strong>hlādinī-śaktira sāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;‘mahābhāva’ nāma yāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tribhuvana-mohana-mohinī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Entwined upon that blackish tree a beautiful golden creeper has arisen, who is the conqueror of all realms, being the supreme purifier. Her Name is Mahābhāva, being the essence of the supreme pleasure-giving hlādinī potency. She is the enchantress of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, who is Himself the enchanter of the three worlds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-nāme parichita&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tuṣiyā govinda-chitta</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;virājaye parama ānande</strong><br>
<strong>sei latā-patra-phula&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;lalitādi sakhī-kula</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sabe mili’ vṛkṣe dṛḍha bāndhe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Known by the Name of Rādhā, She remains shining there in great ecstasy, always engaged in satisfying Govinda’s heart. The leaves and flowers of that creeper form the assembly of Her girlfriends headed by Lalitā. Together She and all Her friends entwine that blackish tree in a tight embrace.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>latāra paraśe praphulla tamāla</strong><br>
<strong>latā chhāḍi’ nāhi rahe kona kāla</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At the touch of this creeper, the Tamāl Tree blooms; without the embrace of the creeper, He can no longer exist.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tamāla chhaḍiyā latā nāhi bā̐che</strong><br>
<strong>se latā-milana sadā kāla yāche</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The creeper never desires to leave the company of the Tamāl Tree; the creeper perpetually craves Their union.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhakati-vinoda milana do̐hāra</strong><br>
<strong>nā chāhe kakhana vinā kichhu āra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Other than the meeting of these two, Bhakti Vinod desires nothing else.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-radha-bhajane-jodi-moti-nahi-bhela" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Rādhāṣṭaka <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Kīrtan Eight</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādha-bhajane yadi mati nāhi bhelā</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-bhajana tava akāraṇa gelā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">If one does not develop within his heart the desire for Rādhā-bhajan, then his Kṛṣṇa-bhajan is worthless.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ātapa-rahita sūraya nāhi jāni</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā-virahita mādhava nāhi māni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Without sunlight the sun cannot be seen. Similarly I don’t pay any mind to Kṛṣṇa without Rādhā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kevala mādhava pūjaye so ajñānī</strong><br>
<strong>rādhā anādara kara-i abhimānī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One who worships Kṛṣṇa alone is ignorant. Who does not adore Śrī Rādhā is an egotist.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kabahi nāhi karabi tā̐kara saṅga</strong><br>
<strong>chitte ichchhasi yadi vraja-rasa-raṅga</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Never keep company with those who envy Rādhā if within your heart you at all aspire to the loving Pastimes of Vraja.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhikā-dāsī yadi hoya abhimāna</strong><br>
<strong>śīghra-i mila-i tava gokula-kāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Those who consider themselves maidservants of Śrī Rādhā will quickly meet Gokul-Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>brahmā, śiva, nārada, śruti, nārāyaṇī</strong><br>
<strong>rādhikā-pada-raja pūjaye māni’</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Brahmā, Śiva, Nārada, the śrutis, and Nārāyaṇī (Lakṣmī) worship with regard the holy feet of Śrī Rādhā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>umā, ramā, satyā, śachī, chandrā, rukminī</strong><br>
<strong>rādha-avatāra sabe āmnāya-vāṇī</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Umā, Ramā, Satyā, Śachī, Chandrāvālī, and Rukminī are all personal expansions of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī – this is the self-evident verdict of the Vedas.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hena rādhā-paricharyā yā̐kara dhana</strong><br>
<strong>bhakati-vinoda tā̐ra māgaye charaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Bhakti Vinod, whose only wealth is the service of Śrī Rādhā, humbly prays to be a serving maid at Her lotus feet.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-radhakundatata-kunjakutira" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Rādhā-kuṇḍataṭa-kuñja-kuṭīra <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhākuṇḍataṭa-kuñjakuṭīra</strong><br>
<strong>govardhana-parvata, jāmuna-tīra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The grove-dwelling on the banks of Rādhā Kuṇḍa,<br />Govardhan mountain, the banks of River Yamunā;</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kusuma-sarovara, mānasa-gaṅgā</strong><br>
<strong>kalinda-nandinī vipula-taraṅgā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Kusuma-sarovar, Mānasa-gaṅgā,<br />the rolling waves of Yamunā, daughter of the sun;</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vaṁśīvaṭa, gokula, dhīrasamīra</strong><br>
<strong>vṛndāvana-taru, latikā-vānīra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Place of the Rāsa Dance, and Gokula, the gentle breezes,<br />the trees, vines, and vetasa palms of Vraja;</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>khaga-mṛgakula, malaya-bātāsa</strong><br>
<strong>mayura, bhramara, muralī-vilāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">All the birds, deer, and animals, the sweet air of spring,<br />the peacocks, bumblebees, the flute’s sweet song;</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>veṇu, śṛṇga, padachihna, meghamālā</strong><br>
<strong>vasanta, śaśāṅka, śaṅkha, karatālā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Flute, horn, His footsteps, range of clouds in the sky,<br />season of spring, the moon, the conch, the hand-cymbals;</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>jugala vilāse anukūla jāni</strong><br>
<strong>līlā-vilāsa-uddīpaka māni</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I know all these things are nourishing the Pastimes of the Divine Couple, I can feel they arouse the holy Pastimes of the Lord.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e saba choḍata kā̐hā nāhi jāu̐</strong><br>
<strong>e saba choḍata parāṇa hārāu̐</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I can never leave all these things so dear to me,<br />without them I know I’ll surely die.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhaktivinoda kahe, śuna kāna</strong><br>
<strong>tuyā uddīpaka hāmārā parāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Hear me, O Kāna, says Śrī Bhakti Vinod,<br />You are the only light of my life.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-radhe-jaya-jaya-madhava-dayite" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Rādhikā-stutiḥ <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Praise for Śrī Rādhikā</h3>
<h4>Sanskrit composition by Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī Prabhu</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhe jaya jaya mādhava-dayite</strong><br>
<strong>gokula-taruṇī-maṇḍala-mahite</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Rādhā! O beloved of Mādhava! O You who are worshipped by all the young girls of Gokula! All glories unto You! All glories unto You!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dāmodara-rati-vardhana-veśe</strong><br>
<strong>hari-niṣkuṭa-vṛndā-vipineśe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You who dress Yourself in such a way as to increase Lord Dāmodar’s love and attachment for You! O Queen of Vṛndāvan, which is the pleasure grove of Lord Hari!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛṣabhānūdadhi-nava-śaśi-lekhe</strong><br>
<strong>lalitā-sakhi guṇa-ramita-viśākhe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O new moon who has arisen from the ocean of King Vṛṣabhānu! O friend of Lalitā! O You who make Viśākhā loyal to You due to Your wonderful qualities of friendliness, kindness, and faithfulness to Kṛṣṇa!</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>karuṇāṁ kuru mayi karuṇā-bharite</strong><br>
<strong>sanaka-sanātana-varṇita-charite</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O You who are filled with compassion! O You whose divine characteristics are described by the great sages Sanaka and Sanātan! O Rādhā, please be merciful to me!</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-radha-krishna-prana-mora-jugala-kishora" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Sakhi-vṛnde Vijñaptiḥ <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Prayer to the Gopīs</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-kṛṣṇa prāṇa mora jugala-kiśora</strong><br>
<strong>jīvane maraṇe gati āra nāhi mora</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The youthful Divine Couple Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa are my life and soul. In life or death I have no other refuge but Them.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kālindīra kūle keli-kadambera vana</strong><br>
<strong>ratana-bedira upara basāba dujana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I shall install the Divine Couple on a jewelled throne in a pleasant forest of kadamba trees on the shore of the Yamunā river.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śyāma-gaurī-aṅge dia chandanera gandha</strong><br>
<strong>chāmara ḍhulāba kabe heriba mukha-chandra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I place aromatic sandalwood paste on the limbs of Lord Śyāmasundar and Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī? When will I fan Them with a chāmara wisk? When will I see Their moonlike faces?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gāṅthiyā mālātira mālā diba doṅhāra gale</strong><br>
<strong>adhare tuliyā diba karpūra-tāmbule</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I string garlands of malati flowers and place them on the necks of the Divine Couple? When will I place betel nuts mixed with camphor in Their lotus mouths?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>lalitā visākhā-ādi jata sakhī-vṛnda</strong><br>
<strong>ājñāya kariba sevā charaṇāravinda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When, following the orders of Lalitā, Viśākhā, and the other gopīs, will I serve the lotus feet of the Divine Couple?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya-prabhura dāsera anudāsa</strong><br>
<strong>sevā abhilāṣa kare narottama-dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Narottam Dās, the servant of the servant of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya Mahāprabhu, longs for this service to the Divine Couple.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-gauranga-bolite-hobe-pulaka-shorira" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>‘Gaurāṅga’ Balite Habe <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>by Śrīla Narottam Dās Ṭhākur</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>“gaurāṅga” balite habe pulaka śarīra</strong><br>
<strong>“hari hari” balite nayane vabe nīra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will there be shivering of the body upon chanting Lord Gaurāṅga’s Holy Name? When will there be tears in my eyes while chanting the Lord’s Holy Name?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>āra kabe nitāichā̐da karuṇā karibe</strong><br>
<strong>saṁsāra-vāsanā mora kabe tuchchha habe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I obtain the mercy of Lord Nītyānanda? When, by His mercy, will my desire for material enjoyment become insignificant?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viṣaya chhāḍiyā kabe śuddha habe mana</strong><br>
<strong>kabe hāma heraba śrī-vṛndāvana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will my mind be completely purified, having given up all material desires? When will I attain the vision of Śrī Vṛndāvan Dhām?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpa-raghunātha-pade ha-ibe ākuti</strong><br>
<strong>kabe hāma bujhaba se yugala-pīriti</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When will I be eager to follow in the footsteps of Śrī Rūpa and Śrī Raghunāth, and when will I properly understand the loving affairs of the Divine Couple?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rūpa-raghunātha-pade rahu mora āśa</strong><br>
<strong>prārthanā karaye sadā narottama dāsa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My only aspiration is to attain the lotus feet of Śrī Rūpa and Śrī Raghunāth. Narottam Dās continually submits this prayer.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-yabe-rupa-sanatana-vraje-gela-dui-jana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrīla Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī Śochaka <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Śrīla Raghunāth Dās’ Deep Sorrow</h3>
<h3>Kīrtan One</h3>
<h4>unknown composer, from the Maṭh’s Śrī Gauḍīya Gitāñjali</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yabe rūpa-sanātana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vraje gelā dui jana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śuna-ite raghunātha-dasa</strong><br>
<strong>nija-rājya-adhikāra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;indra-sama sukha yāra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhaḍiyā chalilā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prabhu-pāśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When Raghunāth Dās heard that Rūpa and Sanātan went to Vraja, he gave up the proprietorship of his estate and he gave up pleasures equal to those of Indra, and came to the Lord’s side.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>uṭhi’ rātre niśā-bhāge&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;duyāre praharī jāge</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;patha chhāḍi’ vipathe gamana</strong><br>
<strong>kṣudhā tṛṣṇā nāhi pāya&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;manodvege chali’ yāya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadā chinte chaitanya-charaṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He arose in the dark very early in the morning, avoided the main road, and travelled on an alternate path. He felt no hunger or thirst as he ran anxiously. He thought constantly of Śrī Chaitanya’s feet.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>eka-dina eka grāme&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sandhyā-kāle go-vāthāne</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;“hā chaitanya” baliya basilā</strong><br>
<strong>eka gopa dugdha dilā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhā kheye viśrāmilā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sei rātre tathāi rahilā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">One day in a village in the evening he sat in a cowshed chanting, “O Chaitanya!” and a cowherd boy gave him some milk. He drank it, rested, and remained there for the night.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ye aṅga pālaṅka vine&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhūmi-śayyā nāhi jāne</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kaṇṭake hā̐ṭaye sei pāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">His body had never experienced lying on the ground without a bed­stead, but his feet now walked over thorns.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yi̐ho belā daṇḍa chāri&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tolā jale snāna kari’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ṣaḍ-rasa karita bhojana</strong><br>
<strong>ebe yadi kichhu pāna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sandhyā-kāle tāhā khāna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā pāile amani śayana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He used to bathe with raised water at mid-morning and eat meals comprised of the six tastes, but now he would eat in the evening if he found something, and would lie down without eating anything if he did not.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bāra dinera patha yāna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tina sandhyā anna khāna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;praveśilā nīlāchala-pure</strong><br>
<strong>dekhiyā se śrī-mandira&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;du-nayane vahe nīra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;“hā chaitanya” bale uchchaiḥsvare</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After travelling the path for twelve days and eating solid food on only three of these days in the evening, he entered the city of Nīlāchal. Seeing the Temple, tears poured from his eyes, and he called out, “O Chaitanya!”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>e rādhā-vallabha-dāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mane kari abhilāṣa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kothā mora raghunātha-dāsa</strong><br>
<strong>tā̐hāra prasaṅga-mātra&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pulakita haya gātra</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tā̐ra pada-reṇu kari āśa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">This Rādhā Vallabha Dās longs, “Where is my Raghunāth Dās? Just by his association, the hair on my body stands on end. I pray for the dust of his feet.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-chaitanya-kripa-haite-raghunatha-dasa-chitte" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrīla Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī Śochaka <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Śrīla Raghunāth Dās’ Deep Sorrow</h3>
<h3>Kīrtan Two</h3>
<h4>unknown composer, from the Maṭh’s Śrī Gauḍīya Gitāñjali</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-chaitanya-kṛpā haite&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;raghunātha-dāsa-chitte</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama vairāgya upajila</strong><br>
<strong>kalatra gṛha sampada&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nija-rājya-adhipada</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mala-prāya sakala tyajila</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By the mercy of Śrī Chaitanya, extreme detachment arose in the mind of Raghunāth Dās. Wife, home, wealth, proprietorship of his estate – he left all of this as though it were stool.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>puraścharyā kṛṣṇa-nāme&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;giyā se puruṣottame</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gaurāṅgera pada-yuga seve</strong><br>
<strong>ei mane abhilāṣa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;punaḥ raghunātha dasa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nayana-gochara habe kabe</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After completing his preparatory duties with the chanting of Kṛṣṇa’s Name, he went to Śrī Puruṣottam Kṣetra and served the feet of Śrī Gaurāṅga. My heart longs, “When will I see Raghunāth Dās again?”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gauraṅga doyālu haiyā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-kṛṣṇa mantra diyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;govardhana-śilā guñjā-hāre</strong><br>
<strong>vraja-vane govardhane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-rādhāra śrī-charaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;samarpaṇa karila yāhāre</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Gaurāṅga mercifully gave him the Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa mantra, a Govardhan-śilā, and a guñja-mālā. He thus offered him to the divine feet of Śrī Rādhā at Govardhan in Vṛndāvan.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaurāṅgera agochare&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nija-keśa chhi̐ḍi kare</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;virahe vyākula vraje gelā</strong><br>
<strong>deha-tyāga kari’ mane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gelā giri-govardhane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;du-go̐sāi tāhāre rākhilā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After the disappearance of Śrī Gaurāṅga, Raghunāth Dās began to pull out his hair, and overcome with separation, he went to Vraja. Intending to give up his body, he went to Govardhan Hill, but two goswāmīs saved him.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhari rūpa-sanātana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rākhila tā̐ra jīvana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;deha-tyāga karite nā dilā</strong><br>
<strong>dui go̐sāira ājñā peye&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-kuṇḍera taṭe giyā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;niyama kariyā vāsa kailā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Rūpa and Sanātan received him and saved his life. They did not allow him to give up his body. Receiving instructions from these two goswāmīs, Raghunāth went to the bank of Rādhā Kuṇḍa and lived there strictly observing various vows.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chhi̐ḍā vastra paridhana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vraja-phala-gavya pāna</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anna ādi nā kare āhāra</strong><br>
<strong>tina sandhyā snānāchāri&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;smaraṇa kīrtana kari’</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-pada bhajana yāhāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He wore torn cloth and ate only the fruits and milk products found in Vraja. He did not eat rice or other grains. He bathed at the three junctions of the day, engaged in remembrance and chanting, and served the feet of Śrī Rādhā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ṣāṭ daṇḍa rātri dine&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-kṛṣṇa-līlā-gāne</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;smaraṇete sadāi go̐yāya</strong><br>
<strong>chāri daṇḍa śuye thāke&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;svapne rādhā-kṛṣṇa dekhe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tilārdheka vyartha nāhi yāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Night and day, twenty-four hours a day, he would sing about the Pastimes of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa and constantly engage in remembrance of Them. He lay down for an hour and a half each day, but in his dreams he saw Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. He never wasted a single moment.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chaitanyera padāmbuje&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rākhe manobhṛṅga-rāje</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;svarūpera sadāi āśrāya</strong><br>
<strong>bhinna-deha rūpa-sane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gati yā̐ra sanātane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhaṭṭa go̐sāyera priya mahāśaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He would fix the bumble bee of his mind on the lotus feet of Śrī Chaitanya. He remained always in the shelter of Svarūp Dāmodar. He was always with Śrī Rūpa, and Sanātan was his shelter. He was dear to Raghunāth Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī and Gopāl Bhaṭṭa Goswāmī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-rūpera gaṇa yata&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;yā̐hāra pade āśrita</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;atyanta vātsalya yā̐ra jīve</strong><br>
<strong>sei ārta-nāda kari’&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kā̐di’ bale, “hari hari</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prabhura karuṇā habe kabe”</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Sheltered at the feet of all the followers of Śrī Rūpa, who are most affectionate to all souls, he would piteously cry out, “O Lord! O Lord! When will I receive my Lord’s mercy?”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>he rādhikāra vallabha&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gāndharvikāra bāndhava</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhikā-ramaṇa rādhānātha</strong><br>
<strong>he he vṛndāvaneśvara&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hā hā kṛṣṇa dāmodara</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛpā kari’ kara ātmasātha</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“O Beloved of Rādhikā! O Friend of Gāndharvikā! O Delight of Rādhikā! O Lord of Rādhā! O Lord of Vṛndāvan! O Kṛṣṇa! O Dāmodar! Please mercifully accept me.”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prabhu rūpa sanātana&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tina hailā adarśana</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;andha haila e dui nayana</strong><br>
<strong>vṛthā ā̐khi kāhā̐ dekhi&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vṛthā dehe prāṇa rākhi</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sevāchāra bāḍāya dviguṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When the Lord (Śrī Chaitanya), Śrī Rūpa, and Śrī Sanātan had disappeared, his two eyes became blind. “What can I see with these useless eyes? I just maintain my life in this useless body.” This was his feeling, yet his engagement in service doubled.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa śrī-śachī-suta&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāra guṇa yata yata</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;avatāra śrī-vigraha nāma</strong><br>
<strong>gupta vyakta līlā-sthāna&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dṛṣṭa śruta vaiṣṇava-gaṇa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sabhākāre karaye praṇāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He would offer obeisances to Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the son of Śrī Śachī, all of His qualities and avatārs, His Deity, His Name, the hidden and manifest places of His Pastimes that are seen or heard about, and all the Vaiṣṇavas.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>rādhā-kṛṣṇera viyoge&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;chhāḍila sakala bhoge</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rūkhā śukhā anna-mātra sāra</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-chaitanyera vichchhedete&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;anna chhāḍi’ sei haite</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;phala gavya karena āhāra</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In separation from Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, Raghunāth Dās gave up all enjoyment and ate nothing but dry, stale rice. In separation from Śrī Chaitanya, he gave up rice and ate only fruits and milk products.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sanātanera adarśane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tāhā chhāḍi sei dine</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kevala karena jala pāna</strong><br>
<strong>rūpera vichchheda yabe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;jala chhāḍi’ dila tabe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;rādhā-kṛṣṇa bali’ rākhe prāṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">After the disappearance of Śrī Sanātan, he gave this up, and from that day on drank only water. When he was separated from Śrī Rūpa, he then gave up water and maintained his life simply by calling out to Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
