<p class="verse"><strong>svarūpera adarśane&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nā dekhe rūpera gaṇe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;virahe vikala haiyā kānde</strong><br>
<strong>kṛṣṇa-kathālapa vine&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śravaṇe nāhika śune</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;uchchaiḥsvare ḍāke ārta-nāde</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When Śrī Rūpa disappeared, and Raghunāth could not see any of Śrī Rūpa’s followers, then he sorrowfully wept in separation. Without any discussion of Kṛṣṇa, he did not listen to anything with his ears and piteously cried out aloud:</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hā hā rādhā-kṛṣṇa kothā&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kothā āchha he lalitā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;he viśākhe deha daraśana</strong><br>
<strong>hā chaitanya mahāprabhu&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hā svarūpa mora prabhu</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;hā hā prabhu rūpa-sanātana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“O Rādhā! O Kṛṣṇa! Where are You? O Lalitā, where are you? O Viśākhā! Please appear before me. O Chaitanya Mahāprabhu! O Svarūp, my master! O my masters Rūpa and Sanātan!”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="17">
<h5>17</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kā̐de go̐sāi rātra-dine&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;puḍi’ yāya tanu-mane</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;virahe ha-ila jara jara</strong><br>
<strong>manda manda jihvā naḍe&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;preme aśru netre paḍe</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mane kṛṣṇa karaye smaraṇa</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Dās Goswāmī cried night and day. His mind and body burned in sepration and became frail. As his tongue moved slowly and tears of love poured from his eyes, he remembered Kṛṣṇa within his heart.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="18">
<h5>18</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sei raghunātha-dāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pūribe manera āśa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ei mora baḍa āchhe sādha</strong><br>
<strong>e rādhā-vallabha-dāsa&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mane kare abhilāṣa</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sabe more karaha prasāda</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Raghunāth Dās will fulfil the desires of my heart. This is my great hope.” This Rādhā Vallabha Dās longs within his heart, “May you all bless me.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ha-ha-bhaktivinoda-thakkura-guroh" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrīmad Bhakti Vinod Viraha Daśakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>*Prayer in Separation of Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h3>
<h4>Sanskrit composition</h4>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hā hā bhakti-vinoda-ṭhākura! guroḥ! dvāviṁśatis te samā</strong><br>
<strong>dīrghād duḥkha-bharād aśeṣa-virahād duḥsthīkṛtā bhūr iyam</strong><br>
<strong>jīvānāṁ bahu-janma-puṇya-nivahākṛṣṭo mahī-maṇḍale</strong><br>
<strong>āvirbhāva-kṛpāṁ chakāra cha bhavān śrī-gaura-śaktiḥ svayam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Alas, alas! O Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur, O supreme guru, for twenty-two years this world has been plunged into misfortune, long grief-stricken in your intolerable separation. You are the divine potency of Śrī Gaura, and you graciously made your advent in this earthly plane, being attracted by the living beings’ virtuous deeds performed throughout many lifetimes.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dīno ’haṁ chira-duṣkṛtir nahi bhavat-pādābja-dhūli-kaṇā-</strong><br>
<strong>snānānanda-nidhiṁ prapanna-śubhadaṁ labdhuṁ samartho ’bhavam</strong><br>
<strong>kintv audārya-guṇāt tavāti-yaśasaḥ kāruṇya-śaktiḥ svayaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-śrī-gaura-mahāprabhoḥ prakaṭitā viśvaṁ samanvagrahīt</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Since I am lowly and very wretched, it was not my luck to reach the ocean that bestows the fortune of surrender – the ocean of the ecstasy of bathing in a particle of the dust of your holy lotus feet. Yet due to your magnanimous nature, you gave your grace to the whole universe, personally revealing your super-eminence as the personification of the mercy potency of Śrī Gaurāṅga. (That is, by coming into this world, I received his grace.)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>he deva! stavane tavākhila-guṇānāṁ te viriñchādayo</strong><br>
<strong>devā vyartha-manorathāḥ kim u vayaṁ martyādhamāḥ kurmahe</strong><br>
<strong>etan no vibudhaiḥ kadāpy atiśayālaṅkāra ity uchyatāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śāstreṣv eva “na pāraye ’ham” iti yad gītaṁ mukundena tat</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord, even all the demigods headed by Lord Brahmā feel frustrated by their inability to (fittingly) sing the glories of all your divine qualities. What, then, can be said of the attempt of a fallen soul, a mere human like me? Surely, the learned will never waste their eloquent words eulogising this statement, since even the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa Himself, has sung in the scriptures His celebrated confession, “<i>Na pāraye ’ham</i> – I am unable (to reciprocate your devotion).”</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dharmaś charma-gato ’jñataiva satatā yogaś cha bhogātmako</strong><br>
<strong>jñāne śūnya-gatir japena tapasā khyātir jighāṁsaiva cha</strong><br>
<strong>dāne dāmbhikatā ’nurāga-bhajane duṣṭāpachāro yadā</strong><br>
<strong>buddhiṁ buddhimatāṁ vibheda hi tadā dhātrā bhavān preṣitaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At a time when religion was evaluated in terms of bodily relationship, saintliness was determined by ignorance, and yoga practice was motivated by sensual pleasure; when scholarship was cultivated just for voidism, japa was conducted for the sake of fame, and penance was performed out of vindictiveness; when charity was given out of pride, and on the pretext of spontaneous devotion the most gross, sinful acts were perpetrated – and in all such anomalous situations even the intelligentsia were at loggerheads with one another – at that very time, you were sent by the Almighty Creator.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>viśve ’smin kiraṇair yathā himakaraḥ sañjīvayann oṣadhīr</strong><br>
<strong>nakṣatrāṇi cha rañjayan nija-sudhāṁ vistārayan rājate</strong><br>
<strong>sach-chhāstrāṇi cha toṣayan budha-gaṇaṁ sammodayaṁs te tathā</strong><br>
<strong>nūnaṁ bhūmi-tale śubhodaya iti hlādo bahuḥ sātvatām</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As the universally cooling moon is beautified by diffusing its nectarean rays, nourishing the plants and inciting the twinkling of the stars, your holy advent in this world is similarly ascertained as satisfying the pure devotional scriptures (through profound study) and blessing the learned with full-blown happiness (by the perfect axiomatic conclusions descending in divine succession). With your appearance, the ecstasy of the devotees knows no bounds.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>lokānāṁ hita-kāmyayā bhagavato bhakti-prachāras tvayā</strong><br>
<strong>granthānāṁ rachanaiḥ satām abhimatair nānā-vidhair darśitaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>āchāryaiḥ kṛta-pūrvam eva kila tad rāmānujādyair budhaiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>premāmbhonidhi-vigrahasya bhavato māhātmya-sīmā na tat</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By writing many books and by multifarious methods recognised by the pure devotees, you have demonstrated the preaching of pure devotional service unto the Supreme Lord, for the benefit of the whole world. We have heard of similar achievements in previous times by stalwart scholars such as Śrī Rāmānuja and many other Āchāryas; but the glory of you – the very embodiment of the nectar of divine love – does not end (cannot be confined) here.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yad dhāmnaḥ khalu dhāma chaiva nigame brahmeti saṁjñāyate</strong><br>
<strong>yasyāṁśasya kalaiva duḥkha-nikarair yogeśvarair mṛgyate</strong><br>
<strong>vaikuṇṭhe para-mukta-bhṛṅga-charaṇo nārāyaṇo yaḥ svayaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>tasyāṁśī bhagavān svayaṁ rasa-vapuḥ kṛṣṇo bhavān tat pradaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Merely the effulgence of His divine abode has been designated by the title ‘Brahman’ in the Vedas, and only the expansion of an expansion of His expansion is sought after with great tribulation by the foremost yogīs. The most exalted of the liberated souls shine resplendent as bumblebees at His lotus feet. The primeval origin of even the original Śrī Nārāyaṇa who is the Lord of the spiritual sky above Brahman: He is the original Supreme Lord, the personification of all nectarean mellows – Śrī Kṛṣṇa – and He is the one that you give.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarvāchintyamaye parātpara-pure goloka-vṛndāvane</strong><br>
<strong>chil-līlā-rasa-raṅginī parivṛtā sā rādhikā śrī-hareḥ</strong><br>
<strong>vātsalyādi-rasaiś cha sevita-tanor mādhurya-sevā-sukhaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>nityaṁ yatra mudā tanoti hi bhavān tad dhāma-sevā-pradaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Situated in the topmost region of the absolutely inconceivable spiritual sky is the holy abode of Śrī Vṛndāvan Dhām in the spiritual planet known as Goloka. There, surrounded by sakhīs, Śrīmatī Rādhikā revels in the mellows of divine Pastimes. With great ecstasy, She expands the joy of loving sweetness in the service of Śrī Kṛṣṇachandra, Who is otherwise served in four relationships up to parenthood. You, O Ṭhākur Bhakti Vinod, can give us the service of that holy dhām.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-gaurānumataṁ svarūpa-viditaṁ rūpāgrajenādṛtaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>rūpādyaiḥ pariveśitaṁ raghu-gaṇair āsvāditaṁ sevitam</strong><br>
<strong>jīvādyair abhirakṣitaṁ śuka-śiva-brahmādi-sammānitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-rādhā-pada-sevanāmṛtam aho tad dātum īśo bhavān</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The internal purport is known to Śrī Svarūp Dāmodar by the sanction of Śrī Gaurachandra. That, which is adored by Śrī Sanātan Goswāmī and distributed by the preceptors realised in transcendental mellows, headed by Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī; that which is tasted and enhanced by Śrī Raghunāth Dās Goswāmī and followers, and carefully protected by the votaries headed by Śrī Jīva Prabhu; and that which (from a respectful distance) is venerated by great personalities such as Śrī Śuka, Lord Śiva, the chief of the demigods, and Lord Brahmā, the grandfather of all beings – O wonder of wonders! the nectarean rapture of servitude unto Śrī Rādhikā – that, too, you can give us.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kvāhaṁ manda-matis tv atīva-patitaḥ kva tvaṁ jagat-pāvanaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>bho svāmin kṛpayāparādha-nichayo nūnaṁ tvayā kṣamyatām</strong><br>
<strong>yāche ’haṁ karuṇā-nidhe! varam imaṁ pādābja-mūle bhavat-</strong><br>
<strong>sarvasvāvadhi-rādhikā-dayita-dāsānāṁ gaṇe gaṇyatām</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Where am I, so lowly and fallen, and where are you, the great soul who delivers the universe! O Lord, by your grace, you are sure to forgive my offences. O ocean of mercy, in the dust of your lotus feet I pray for just this benediction: kindly make my life successful by recommending me for admission into the group of Śrī Vārṣabhānavī Dayita Dās, who is the dearmost one in your heart.<br>
*<i class="translation__note">Translator’s Note:</i><br>
 The preceding prayer to Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur (1838-1914), the pioneer of pure devotion in the recent age, was composed within the lifetime of the author’s Guru, Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur Goswāmī Prabhupād. Upon reading the original Sanskrit verse, just the year before his own passing away, Śrīla Prabhupād commented, “Now I am confident that we have one man who is qualified to uphold the standard of our sampradāya (divine succession).”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-gauda-dhamashrita-shiuddha-bhaktam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrīla Gaura Kiśor Dās Bābājī Mahārāj Aṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Eight Verses in Obeisance to Śrīla Gaura Kiśor Dās Bābājī Mahārāj</h3>
<h4>Sanskrit composition</h4>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-gauḍa-dhāmāśrita-śuddha-bhaktaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>rūpānugādyaṁ niravady arūpam</strong><br>
<strong>vairāgya-dharmojjvala-vigrahaṁ taṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande prabhuṁ gaura-kiśora-saṁjñam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">A pure devotee sheltered in the holy land of Gauḍa, the best of the followers of Śrī Rūpa, he has a faultless ascetic form, and is an effulgent embodiment of renunciation. I offer obeisances to him, the master Gaura Kiśor.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>asat-prasaṅgaṁ parihāya nityaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>gaurāṅga-sevā-vrata-magna-chittam</strong><br>
<strong>gauḍa-vrajābheda-viśiṣṭa-prajñaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande prabhuṁ gaura-kiśora-saṁjñam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Rejecting all bad association, his heart is ever immersed in the vow of service to Śrī Gaurāṅga, and he is filled with realisation of the non-difference of Gauḍa and Vraja. I offer obeisances to the master Gaura Kiśor.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-dhāma-māyāpura-divya-gūḍha-</strong><br>
<strong>māhātmya-gītonmukhaṁ vareṇyam</strong><br>
<strong>dhanyaṁ mahābhāgavatāgragaṇyaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande prabhuṁ gaura-kiśora-saṁjñam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Intent upon chanting the secret divine glories of Śrī Dhām Māyāpur, he is exalted, admirable, and the foremost of advanced devotees. I offer obeisances to the master Gaura Kiśor.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pūtāvadhūta-vraja-śīrṣa-ratnaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-rādhikā-kṛṣṇa-nigūḍha-bhaktam</strong><br>
<strong>sadā vrajāveśa-sarāga-cheṣṭaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande prabhuṁ gaura-kiśora-saṁjñam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The crown jewel of the pure mendicants and the residents of Vraja, he is a confidential devotee of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa and in the ecstatic mood of Vraja is ever engaged in rāgānugā-bhakti. I offer obeisances to the master Gaura Kiśor.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śokāspadātīta-prabhāva-ramyaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>mūḍhair avedyaṁ praṇatābhigamyam</strong><br>
<strong>nityānubhūtāchyuta-sad-vilāsaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande prabhuṁ gaura-kiśora-saṁjñam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He has a delightful splendour beyond the reach of sorrow. Incom­prehensible to the ignorant, but attainable by the surrendered, he constantly tastes the infallible Lord’s eternal Pastimes. I offer obeisances to the master Gaura Kiśor.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kāpaṭya-dharmānvita-chaṇḍa-daṇḍa-</strong><br>
<strong>vidhāyakaṁ sajjana-saṅga-raṅgam</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-chaitanya-padābja-bhṛṅgaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande prabhuṁ gaura-kiśora-saṁjñam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is a vehement subduer of the deceitful, is cheerful in the association of the sincere, and is a bee in the lotus feet of Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanya. I offer obeisances to the master Gaura Kiśor.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dāmodarotthāna-dine pradhāne</strong><br>
<strong>kṣetre pavitre kuliyābhidhāne</strong><br>
<strong>prapañcha-līlā-parihāravantaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande prabhuṁ gaura-kiśora-saṁjñam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the month of Dāmodar, on the principal day of Utthān Ekādaśī, in the holy place known as Kuliyā, he withdrew his manifest Pastimes. I offer obeisances to the master Gaura Kiśor.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tava hi “dayita-dāse” satya-sūrya-prakāśe</strong><br>
<strong>jagati durita-nāśe prodyate chid-vilāse</strong><br>
<strong>vayam anugata-bhṛtyāḥ pāda-padmaṁ prapannā</strong><br>
<strong>anudinam anukampāṁ prārthayāmo nagaṇyāḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Manifest in your Dayita Dās as the sun of truth to destroy the sin in this world and manifest the Lord’s spiritual Pastimes, we, his insignificant followers and servants, take shelter at your lotus feet and pray continuously for your mercy.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-guror-guro-me-paramo-gurus-tvam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrīmad Gaura Kiśora Namaskāra Daśakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Ten Verses of Obeisances to Śrīla Gaura Kiśor Dās Bābājī Mahārāj</h3>
<h4>Sanskrit composition</h4>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>guror guro me paramo gurus tvaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vareṇya! gaurāṅga-gaṇāgragaṇye</strong><br>
<strong>prasīda bhṛtye dayitāśrite te</strong><br>
<strong>namo namo gaura-kiśora tubhyam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Divine Master of my Divine Master, my most venerable preceptor, you are supremely worshipable in the group of the foremost associates of Śrī Gaurāṅga. May you be gracious upon this servitor surrendered unto your loving servitor (Dayita Dās). O Gaura Kiśor, again and again do I offer my obeisances unto you.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sarasvatī-nāma-jagat-prasiddhaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>prabhuṁ jagatyāṁ patitaika-bandhum</strong><br>
<strong>tvam eva deva! prakaṭī chakāra</strong><br>
<strong>namo namo gaura-kiśora tubhyam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O worshipful one, you alone revealed my Lord and Master, who is renowned throughout the universe as Śrī Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī, and who is the only friend of the fallen souls of the world. O Gaura Kiśor, again and again do I offer my obeisances unto you.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kvachid vrajāraṇya-vivikta-vāsī</strong><br>
<strong>hṛdi vraja-dvandva-raho-vilāsī</strong><br>
<strong>bahir virāgī tv avadhūta-veṣī</strong><br>
<strong>namo namo gaura-kiśora tubhyam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Living in solitude at Vraja Dhām, your heart was absorbed in the most secret Pastimes of the Divine Youthful Couple of Vraja, whilst externally you maintained the strictures of a renunciant, and sometimes you appeared to transcend all recognised strictures. O Gaura Kiśor, again and again do I offer my obeisances unto you.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kvachit punar gaura-vanāntachārī</strong><br>
<strong>surāpagā-tīra-rajo-vihārī</strong><br>
<strong>pavitra-kaupīna-karaṅka-dhārī</strong><br>
<strong>namo namo gaura-kiśora tubhyam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Sometimes you roam about the edge of Gauravana (the boundary of Śrī Nabadwīp Dhām), wandering along the beach near the banks of the Gaṅgā. O Gaura Kiśor, who dons the holy loincloth and carries the mendicant’s waterpot, again and again do I offer my obeisances unto you.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sadā harer nāma mudā raṭantaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>gṛhe gṛhe mādhukarīm aṭantam</strong><br>
<strong>namanti devā api yaṁ mahāntaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>namo namo gaura-kiśora tubhyam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Ever singing the Holy Name of Śrī Hari with great ecstasy and accepting alms from house to house like a bee collecting honey from flower to flower, you are the great soul unto whom even the demigods bow down. O Gaura Kiśor, again and again do I offer my obeisances unto you.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kvachid rudantañ cha hasan naṭantaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>nijeṣṭa-deva-praṇayābhibhūtam</strong><br>
<strong>namanti gāyantam alaṁ janā tvāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>namo namo gaura-kiśora tubhyam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Becoming overwhelmed with love for your worshipful Lord, sometimes you dance, sometimes you cry, sometimes you laugh; and again, you sing aloud. The people profusely offer their respects unto you, O Gaura Kiśor; and again and again do I offer my obeisances unto you.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mahāyaśo-bhakti-vinoda-bandho!</strong><br>
<strong>mahāprabhu-prema-sudhaika-sindho!</strong><br>
<strong>aho jagannātha-dayāspadendo!</strong><br>
<strong>namo namo gaura-kiśora tubhyam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O friend of the glorious Ṭhākur Bhakti Vinod, O matchless ocean of the nectar of loving devotion for Mahāprabhu Śrī Chaitanyadev, O moon that received the grace of Vaiṣṇava Sārvabhauma Śrī Jagannātha, O Gaura Kiśor, again and again do I offer my obeisances unto you.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>samāpya rādhā-vratam uttamaṁ tvam</strong><br>
<strong>avāpya dāmodara-jāgarāham</strong><br>
<strong>gato ’si rādhādara-sakhya-riddhiṁ</strong><br>
<strong>namo namo gaura-kiśora tubhyam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Completing the great holy vow of Ūrja-vrata, you selected the day of the awakening of Śrī Dāmodar to achieve the cherished treasure of your internal identity as a sakhī devoted to the service of Śrī Rādhikā. O Gaura-Kiśor, again and again do I offer my obeisances unto you.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vihāya saṅgaṁ kuliyā-layānāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>pragṛhya sevāṁ dayitānugasya</strong><br>
<strong>vibhāsi māyāpura-mandira-stho</strong><br>
<strong>namo namo gaura-kiśora tubhyam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Forsaking the company of the residents of Kuliyā town to accept the service of your servitor Śrī Dayita Dās, your divine presence is now found in a holy temple at Śrī Dhām Māyāpur. O Gaura Kiśor, again and again do I make obeisances unto you.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sadā nimagno ’py aparādha-paṅke</strong><br>
<strong>hy ahaitukīm eṣa kṛpāñ cha yāche</strong><br>
<strong>dayāṁ samuddhṛtya vidhehi dīnaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>namo namo gaura-kiśora tubhyam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Although I remain deep in the mud of offences, I (a fallen soul) am begging you for your causeless mercy. Please be gracious and deliver this soul bereft. O Gaura Kiśor, again and yet again do I offer my obeisances unto you.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nite-yasmin-nishiante-nayana-jala-bharaih" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrī Dayita Dāsa Daśakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Prayer unto Bhagavān Śrī Śrīmad Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur after his manifest Pastimes.</h3>
<h4>Sanskrit composition</h4>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nīte yasmin niśānte nayana-jala-bharaiḥ snāta-gātrārbudānāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>uchchair utkrośatāṁ śrī-vṛṣakapi-sutayādhīrayā svīya-goṣṭhīm</strong><br>
<strong>pṛthvī gāḍhāndhakārair hṛta-nayana-maṇīvāvṛtā yena hīnā</strong><br>
<strong>yatrāsau tatra śīghraṁ kṛpaṇa-nayana he nīyatāṁ kiṅkaro ’yam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At the end of the night, Śrī Śrī Vṛṣabhānunandinī suddenly took him (my Divine Master), to Her entourage, withdrawing him from the company of throngs of griefstricken souls. A great cry of lamentation arose and their bodies were bathed by their tears. When he was thus stolen away, this world was plunged into the deep darkness of one whose eyes have been stolen away (hṛta – stolen away; nayana-maṇi – jewel of the eye – the internal name of Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur is ‘Nayana-maṇi’).<br>
(Bereft of the vision of my Divine Master,) O my sorrowful eyes – or, O saviour of the fallen (dīna-nayana) – wherever that great soul may be, please quickly take this servitor there! (He is my Master named ‘Nayana,’ or ‘one who brings us near,’ but he shows his mercy by not taking me into his company for the time being.)</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yasya śrī-pāda-padmāt pravahati jagati prema-pīyūṣa-dhārā</strong><br>
<strong>yasya śrī-pāda-padma-chyuta-madhu satataṁ bhṛtya-bhṛṅgān vibhartti</strong><br>
<strong>yasya śrī-pāda-padmaṁ vraja-rasika-jano modate sampraśasya</strong><br>
<strong>yatrāsau tatra śīghraṁ kṛpaṇa-nayana he nīyatāṁ kiṅkaro ’yam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">From his lotus feet, the nectarean river of divine love flows throughout the universe; his servitors, like bees, maintain their lives drinking the honey that falls from his lotus feet; and the pure devotees in the shelter of the confidential mellows of Vraja revel in the bliss of singing the glories of his lotus feet: O Dīna-nayana, wherever that great soul may be, please quickly take this servitor there.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vātsalyaṁ yach cha pitro jagati bahumataṁ kaitavaṁ kevalaṁ tat</strong><br>
<strong>dāmpatyaṁ dasyutaiva svajana-gaṇa-kṛtā bandhutā vañchaneti</strong><br>
<strong>vaikuṇṭha-sneha-mūrteḥ pada-nakha-kiraṇair yasya sandarśito ’smi</strong><br>
<strong>yatrāsau tatra śīghraṁ kṛpaṇa-nayana he nīyatāṁ kiṅkaro ’yam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Parental affection, so highly esteemed in the world, is a colossal hoax (as an obstacle to Hari-bhakti); socially recognised pure matrimonial love is nothing but dacoity (in that it plunders away both the husband’s and the wife’s eagerness for the chance to acquire the treasure of love unadulterated by the superficiality of familial prejudice); and common friendship is merely deception. I have gleaned these thoughts from the rays of light that emanate from the toenails of the holy feet of that great personality, the embodiment of supramundane affection. O Dīna-nayana, wherever that great soul may be, please quickly take this servitor there.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yā vāṇī kaṇṭha-lagnā vilasati satataṁ kṛṣṇa-chaitanyachandre</strong><br>
<strong>karṇa-kroḍāj janānāṁ kim u nayana-gatāṁ saiva mūrtiṁ prakāśya</strong><br>
<strong>nīlādrīśasya netrārpaṇa-bhavana-gatā netra-tārābhidheyā</strong><br>
<strong>yatrāsau tatra śīghraṁ kṛpaṇa-nayana he nīyatāṁ kiṅkaro ’yam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The divine message sung by Śrī Kṛṣṇa Chaitanyachandra had its continuous play in the ears of the people. From the ear, did he, just to fulfil the purport of the name ‘Nayana-maṇi,’ reveal his form to the eye, making his advent in the mansion (temple) favoured by the glance of Śrī Nīlāchalachandra (at the time of the Ratha Yātrā festival)? O Dīna-nayana, wherever that great soul may be, please swiftly take this servitor there.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaurendor asta-śaile kim u kanaka-ghano hema-hṛj-jambu-nadyā</strong><br>
<strong>āvirbhūtaḥ pravarṣair nikhila-jana-padaṁ plāvayan dāva-dagdham</strong><br>
<strong>gaurāvirbhāva-bhūmau rajasi cha sahasā saṁjugopa svayaṁ svaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>yatrāsau tatra śīghraṁ kṛpaṇa-nayana he nīyatāṁ kiṅkaro ’yam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Drawing up the pure golden waters of the Jambu River mentioned in <i>Śrīmad Bhāgavatam</i>, has this golden cloud (Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur) arisen on the mountain where the golden moon (Śrī Gaurachandra) descended (set), just to shower down torrents of rain upon the whole countryside that was scorched by the forest fire (of the threefold sufferings), then suddenly concealed himself in the dust of the land of the advent of Śrī Gaurāṅga? O Dīna-nayana, wherever that Great Master may be now, please quickly take this servitor to that place.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gauro gaurasya śiṣyo gurur api jagatāṁ gāyatāṁ gaura-gāthā</strong><br>
<strong>gauḍe gauḍīya-goṣṭhy-āśrita-gaṇa-garimā drāviḍe gaura-garvī</strong><br>
<strong>gāndharvā gauravāḍhyo giridhara-parama-preyasāṁ yo gariṣṭho</strong><br>
<strong>yatrāsau tatra śīghraṁ kṛpaṇa-nayana he nīyatāṁ kiṅkaro ’yam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is of the same hue as Śrī Gaura, and although he, who sings the tidings of Śrī Gaura, is the (natural) Guru of the whole universe, he accepted discipleship to a great devotee called Śrī Gaura Kiśor (‘adolescent Gaura’). In the whole Gauḍa Maṇḍal he is the repository of the glory of those who grant entrance into the fold of the pure Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇavas. He proudly ascended the lofty throne of preaching the glories of the gift of the Golden Lord Śrī Gaura (the service of Śrī Rādhā-Govinda in Vraja) to the Vaiṣṇavas of Draviḍa (the devotees in South India, who are generally worshippers of Lakṣmī-Nārāyaṇa). The glory of his dignity shines even in the group of Śrī Gāndharvā, and he holds a pre-eminent position in the intimate circle of Śrī Giridhārī – that is, he is the most beloved of Lord Mukunda. O Dīna-nayana, wherever that great soul is now, please swiftly take this servitor there.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yo rādhā-kṛṣṇa-nāmāmṛta-jala-nidhināplāvayad viśvam etad</strong><br>
<strong>āmlechchhāśeṣa-lokaṁ dvija-nṛpa-vaṇijaṁ śūdra-śūdrāpakṛṣṭam</strong><br>
<strong>muktaiḥ siddhair agamyaḥ patita-jana-sakho gaura-kāruṇya-śaktir</strong><br>
<strong>yatrāsau tatra śīghraṁ kṛpaṇa-nayana he nīyatāṁ kiṅkaro ’yam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He inundated the whole universe of countless living beings – whether brāhmaṇa, kṣatriya, vaiśya, śūdra, less than śūdra, and even mlechchha – with the oceanic nectar of the Holy Name of Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. Although unapproachable by the liberationists and yogic perfectionists, he is known as the friend of the fallen, the mercy potency of Śrī Gaurāṅga. O Dīna-nayana, wherever that great soul may be, please swiftly take this servitor there.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>apy āśā varttate tat puraṭa-vara-vapur lokituṁ loka-śandaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>dīrghaṁ nīlābja-netraṁ tila-kusuma-nasaṁ ninditārddhendu-bhālam</strong><br>
<strong>saumyaṁ śubhrāṁśu-dantaṁ śata-dala-vadanaṁ dīrgha-bāhuṁ vareṇyaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>yatrāsau tatra śīghraṁ kṛpaṇa-nayana he nīyatāṁ kiṅkaro ’yam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Do I have a hope to ever see that beautiful golden personality who makes everything auspicious for the world? Do I have a hope to ever behold that tall figure once again, his blue lotus eyes, his nose that excels the charm of the tila flower, his forehead that shames the half-moon, his graceful lotus face, his gleaming pearlwhite teeth, his long arms extending to his knees? O Dīna-nayana, wherever that great soul has gone, please swiftly take this servitor there.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaurābde śūnya-vāṇānvita-nigama-mite kṛṣṇa-pakṣe chaturthyāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>pauṣe māse maghāyām amara-gaṇa-guror vāsare vai niśānte</strong><br>
<strong>dāso yo rādhikāyā atiśaya-dayito nitya-līlā-praviṣṭo</strong><br>
<strong>yatrāsau tatra śīghraṁ kṛpaṇa-nayana he nīyatāṁ kiṅkaro ’yam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the year of our Lord Gaurāṅga 450, in the month of Pauṣa, on the fourth day of the dark fortnight of the moon in the star of Maghā, at the closing of the night of Bṛhaspativara (5.30 a.m., 1st January, 1937), that most beloved attendant of Śrīmatī Vṛṣabhānunandinī entered into the eternal Pastimes. O Dīna-nayana, wherever that great soul may be, quickly take this servitor there to him.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>hā-hā-kārair janānāṁ guru-charaṇa-juṣāṁ pūritābhūr nabhaś cha</strong><br>
<strong>yāto ’sau kutra viśvaṁ prabhupada-virahād dhanta śūnyāyitaṁ me</strong><br>
<strong>pādābje nitya-bhṛtyaḥ kṣaṇam api virahaṁ notsahe soḍhum atra</strong><br>
<strong>yatrāsau tatra śīghraṁ kṛpaṇa-nayana he nīyatāṁ kiṅkaro ’yam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The entire earth and skies were filled with the cries of anguish of the people and of the disciples devoted to the service of Śrī Gurudev’s lotus feet. Where has that Great Master gone? Alas! today the whole universe seems empty, in the separation of Prabhupād. This servitor of Gurudev cannot endure even a moment of his separation. O Dīna-nayana, wherever, wherever that great soul may be, please quickly take this servitor there to him.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shrimach-chaitanyapadau-chara" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrīmad Rūpa-pāda-rajaḥ-prārthanā-daśakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Aspiring for the Dust of Śrīmad Rūpa Goswāmī’s Lotus Feet</h3>
<h4>Sanskrit composition</h4>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrīmach-chaitanya-pādau chara-kamala-yugau netra-bhṛṅgau madhu dyau</strong><br>
<strong>gauḍe tau pāyayantau vraja-vipina-gatau vyāja-yuktau samutkau</strong><br>
<strong>bhātau sabhrātṛkasya svajana-gaṇa-pater yasya saubhāgya-bhūmnaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>sa śrī-rūpaḥ kadā māṁ nija-pada-rajasā bhūṣitaṁ saṁvidhatte</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrīla Rūpa Goswāmī is the mine of the most precious treasure, the leader of the associates of Lord Chaitanya (the divine succession known as the Rūpānuga Sampradāya, ‘the followers of Śrī Rūpa’). In the company of his brothers, he was resplendent in (the province of) Gauḍa, causing the fervent twin bumblebees in the form of the lotus eyes of Śrī Chaitanyadev to drink nectar, as the Lord’s lotus feet moved on the pretext of visiting Vṛndāvan. When will that Śrīmad Rūpa Prabhu grace me with the dust of his holy feet?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>pīta-śrī-gaura-pādāmbuja-madhu-madironmatta-hṛd-bhṛṅga-rājo</strong><br>
<strong>rājyaiśvaryaṁ jahau yo jana-nivaha-hitādatta-chitto nijāgryam</strong><br>
<strong>vijñāpya svānujena vraja-gamana-rataṁ chānvagāt gaurachandraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>sa śrī-rūpaḥ kadā māṁ nija-pada-rajasā bhūṣitaṁ saṁvidhatte</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At Śrī Rāmakeli Dhām, the king of bees – the heart of Śrī Rūpa – became intoxicated drinking the honey-wine of the lotus feet of Śrī Gaurachandra, and sacrificed his life (in Hari-kīrtan) for the benefit of all the people of the world, summarily abandoning a life of kingly opulence. After informing his elder brother Śrī Sanātan, Śrī Rūpa and his younger brother Śrī Vallabha followed the footsteps of Śrī Chaitanyadev, whose heart was absorbed in going (from Nīlāchala) to Śrī Vṛndāvan. When will that Śrī Rūpa Prabhu grace me with the dust of his holy lotus feet?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛndāraṇyāt prayāge hari-rasa-naṭanair nāma-saṅkīrtanaiś cha</strong><br>
<strong>lebhe yo mādhavāgre jana-gahana-gataṁ prema-mattaṁ janāṁś cha</strong><br>
<strong>bhāvaiḥ svair mādayantaṁ hṛta-nidhir iva taṁ kṛṣṇa-chaitanyachandraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>sa śrī-rūpaḥ kadā māṁ nija-pada-rajasā bhūṣitaṁ saṁvidhatte</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Chaitanyadev returned from Vṛndāvan to Prayāg Dhām and performed Nām-saṅkīrtan surrounded by crowds of hundreds of thousands. Intoxicated with divine love, dancing, He melted the hearts of hundreds of faithful souls with His astonishing tran­scendental ecstasies. Śrī Rūpa, as though regaining his most precious treasure, thus found Śrī Chaitanyadev in the direct presence of the Deity Śrī Bindu Mādhava. When will that Śrī Rūpa Prabhu grace me with the dust of his holy lotus feet?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>ekāntaṁ labdha-pādāmbuja-nija-hṛdaya-preṣṭha-pātro mahārttir</strong><br>
<strong>dainyair duḥkhāśru-pūrṇair daśana-dhṛta-tṛṇaiḥ pūjayāmāsa gauram</strong><br>
<strong>svāntaḥ kṛṣṇañ cha gaṅgā-dinamaṇi-tanayā-saṅgame sānujo yaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>sa śrī-rūpaḥ kadā māṁ nija-pada-rajasā bhūṣitaṁ saṁvidhatte</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At the sacred spot on the confluence of the Gaṅgā and the Yamunā, Śrī Rūpa achieved intimate union with the lotus feet of the dearmost Lord of his life – the Lord who is Kṛṣṇa within and Gaura without. In grave humility, with straw in mouth and with tears of sorrow, Śrī Rūpa along with his younger brother, worshipped Śrī Gaura-Kṛṣṇa in intense love. When will that Śrī Rūpa Prabhu grace me with the dust of his holy lotus feet?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>svasya prema-svarūpaṁ priya-dayita-vilāsānurūpaika-rūpaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>dūre bhū-luṇṭhitaṁ yaṁ sahaja-sumadhura-śrī-yutaṁ sānujañ cha</strong><br>
<strong>dṛṣṭvā devo ’titūrṇaṁ stuti-bahu-mukham āśliṣya gāḍhaṁ rarañje</strong><br>
<strong>sa śrī-rūpaḥ kadā māṁ nija-pada-rajasā bhūṣitaṁ saṁvidhatte</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Chaitanyadev saw Śrī Rūpa and his younger brother rolling on the ground some distance away. Seeing His endeared one, His favourite, His love divine personified in natural beauty and charm, His exclusive alter ego in divine Pastimes, the Lord swiftly approached Śrī Rūpa, profusely singing his glories, and embraced him in ecstasy. When will that Śrī Rūpa Prabhu grace me with the dust of his holy lotus feet?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kaivalya-prema-bhūmāv akhila-rasa-sudhā-sindhu-sañchāra-dakṣaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>jñātvāpy evañ cha rādhā-pada-bhajana-sudhāṁ līlayāpāyayad yam</strong><br>
<strong>śaktiṁ sañchārya gauro nija-bhajana-sudhā-dāna-dakṣañ chakāra</strong><br>
<strong>sa śrī-rūpaḥ kadā māṁ nija-pada-rajasā bhūṣitaṁ saṁvidhatte</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Śrī Gaurahari knew that (as an eternal associate of the Lord) Śrī Rūpa was already proficient in wandering throughout the ambrosial ocean of all mellows, in the land of unalloyed love (in Vraja-rasa). Nonetheless, to expand His own Pastimes, the Lord enabled him to drink the sweet ecstasy of servitude unto Śrī Rādhā, and empowered him with the skill to distribute the nectar of His personal devotional service. When will that Śrīmad Rūpa Prabhu grace me with the dust of his lotus feet?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaurādeśāch cha vṛndā-vipinam iha parikramya nīlāchalaṁ yo</strong><br>
<strong>gatvā kāvyāmṛtaiḥ svair vraja-yuva-yugala-krīḍanārthaiḥ prakāmam</strong><br>
<strong>rāmānanda-svarūpādibhir api kavibhis tarpayāmāsa gauraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>sa śrī-rūpaḥ kadā māṁ nija-pada-rajasā bhūṣitaṁ saṁvidhatte</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When on the order of Śrī Gaurāṅga, Śrī Rūpa visited Śrī Puruṣottama Kṣetra after completing the circumambulation of Śrī Vraja Maṇḍal, he highly gratified Śrī Chaitanyadev and the sagacious devotee assembly headed by Śrī Svarūp Dāmodar and Śrī Rāmānanda Rāya by his ambrosial poetry on the Pastimes of the Divine Couple of Vraja. When will that Śrīmad Rūpa Prabhu grace me with the dust of his lotus feet?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>līlā-saṁgopane śrī-bhagavata iha vai jaṅgame sthāvare ’pi</strong><br>
<strong>saṁmugdhe sāgrajātaḥ prabhu-viraha-hṛta-prāya-jīvendriyāṇām</strong><br>
<strong>yaś chāsīd āśrayaika-sthalam iva raghu-gopāla-jīvādi-varge</strong><br>
<strong>sa śrī-rūpaḥ kadā māṁ nija-pada-rajasā bhūṣitaṁ saṁvidhatte</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When the manifest līlā of the Supreme Lord Śrī Chaitanyadev was withdrawn, all beings including the immobile entities were mortified, deeply afflicted in sorrow. Śrī Rūpa and his elder brother were the only shelter for even the intimate devotees of the Lord, headed by Raghunāth, Gopal Bhaṭṭa, and Śrī Jīva, who had almost lost their lives in the Lord’s separation. When will that Śrīmad Rūpa Prabhu grace me with the dust of his lotus feet?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-mūrtteḥ sādhu-vṛtteḥ prakaṭanam api tal-lupta-tīrthādikānāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-rādhā-kṛṣṇa-pādāmbuja-bhajanamayaṁ rāga-mārgaṁ viśuddham</strong><br>
<strong>granthair yena pradattaṁ nikhilam iha nijābhīṣṭa-devepsitañ cha</strong><br>
<strong>sa śrī rūpaḥ kadā māṁ nija-pada-rajasā bhūṣitaṁ saṁvidhatte</strong></p>
<p class="translation">By writing many, many books, he gave the world all the most cherish­ed desires of his worshipful Lord Śrī Chaitanyadev, principally: revealing the service of the Deity, establishing the pure code of conduct in devotion, revealing the lost holy places, and revealing the path of spontaneous devotion (rāga-mārga) in transcendental loving service to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda. When will that Śrīmad Rūpa Prabhu grace me with the dust of his holy lotus feet?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>līlā-saṁgopa-kāle nirupadhi-karuṇā-kāriṇā svāmināhaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>yat pādābje ’rpito yat pada-bhajanamayaṁ gāyayitvā tu gītam</strong><br>
<strong>yogyāyogyatva-bhāvaṁ mama khalu sakalaṁ duṣṭa-buddher agṛhṇan</strong><br>
<strong>sa śrī-rūpaḥ kadā māṁ nija-pada-rajasā bhūṣitaṁ saṁvidhatte</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Just prior to the withdrawal of his manifest līlā, my causelessly merciful Divine Master, Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur, handed me over to the holy feet of that Divine Personality by having me sing the glorious prayer unto his lotus feet (<i>Śrī Rūpa-mañjarī-pada</i>). Despite my lowliness, when will Śrīmad Rūpa Prabhu – dis­regarding all my various qualifications and disqualifications – grace me with the dust of his holy lotus feet?</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-tat-te-nukampam-susamikshamano" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>The Highest Type of Regulation <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<p class="translation">Even obstacles may improve our position if we can take them in the right way. From a higher angle of vision, it may be seen that everything is coming to help us.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>tat te ’nukampāṁ susamīkṣamāṇo</strong><br>
<strong>bhuñjāna evātma-kṛtaṁ vipākam</strong><br>
<strong>hṛd-vāg-vapurbhir vidadhan namas te</strong><br>
<strong>jīveta yo mukti-pade sa dāya-bhāk</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>Śrīmad Bhāgavatam</i> (10.14.8) gives us a most hopeful suggestion for all stages of life: blame yourself and no one else. Maintain your appreciation for the Lord, seeing everything as His grace. At present we think our circumstances undesirable because they do not suit our present taste. But medicine may not always suit the taste of the patient. Still, it is conducive to health. This verse is the highest type of regulation given by the śāstra. If you can follow this law, then in no time you will have a very good position. We must be careful not to blame the circumstances, but to appreciate that Kṛṣṇa is behind everything. Kṛṣṇa is my best friend; He is in the background of everything. Everything is passing through His attentive eyes. So, there cannot be any defect there.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-yo-nanto-nanta-vaktrair-niravadhi" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Śrīman Nityānanda Dvādaśakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Twelve Prayers revealing the ontological position and Pastimes of Śrī Nityānanda Prabhu</h3>
<h4>Sanskrit composition</h4>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yo ’nanto ’nanta-vaktrair niravadhi hari-saṅkīrtanaṁ saṁvidhatte</strong><br>
<strong>yo vā dhatte dharitrīṁ śirasi niravadhi kṣudra-dhūlī-kaṇeva</strong><br>
<strong>yaḥ śeṣaś chhatra-śayyāsana-vasana-vidhaiḥ sevate te yad arthāḥ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-nityānanda-chandraṁ bhaja bhaja satataṁ gaura-kṛṣṇa-pradaṁ tam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Nityānanda’s representation as Ananta Śeṣa possesses unlimited mouths which propagate and glorify Lord Hari’s Holy Names and attributes. Ananta Śeṣa constantly holds the weight of the Earth planet upon His innumerable hoods as if it were an insignificant particle of dust. Ananta Śeṣa serves the Lord in several ways, manifesting Himself as the Lord’s personal umbrella, resting place, and clothing. Let us eternally worship that great personality Śrī Nityānanda-chandra, who freely grants the association of Śrī Gaurāṅgadev, who is none other than Lord Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>aṁśair yaḥ kṣīraśāyī sakala-bhuvanapaḥ sarva jīvāntarastho</strong><br>
<strong>yo vā garbhodaśāyī daśa-śata-vadano veda-sūktair vigītaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>brahmāṇḍāśeṣa-garbhā prakṛti-pati-patir jīva-saṅghāśrayāṅgaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-nityānanda-chandraṁ bhaja bhaja satataṁ gaura-kṛṣṇa-pradaṁ tam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Nityānanda’s partial manifestation as Kśīrodakaśāyī Viṣṇu functions as the preservation principle of the entire universal creation, and is localised within every soul (Paramātma). Another eminent expansion of Lord Nityānanda is Garbhodakaśāyī Viṣṇu, that all-imposing figure whose thousands of heads are eulogised in the Vedic orations. Lord Nityānanda’s presence is further extended in His expansion as Kāraṇodakaśāyī Viṣṇu, the supplying principle encompassing unlimited universes. Lord Nityānanda is the master of the Lord of material nature, Kāraṇodakaśāyī Viṣṇu, who is the shelter of all living entities. Let us eternally worship that great personality Śrī Nityānanda-chandra, who freely grants the association of Śrī Gaurāṅgadev, who is none other than Lord Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yasyāṁśo vyuha-madhye vilasati paramavyomni saṁkarṣaṇākhya</strong><br>
<strong>ātanvan śuddha-sattvaṁ nikhila-hari-sukhaṁ chetanaṁ līlayā cha</strong><br>
<strong>jīvāhaṅkāra-bhāvāspada iti kathitaḥ kutrachij jīvavad yaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-nityānanda-chandraṁ bhaja bhaja satataṁ gaura-kṛṣṇa-pradaṁ tam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Nityānanda’s presence is further extended within the spiritual abode of Vaikuṇṭha in His partial manifestation known as Saṅkarṣaṇa. In Vaikuṇṭha, Lord Saṅkarṣaṇa performs spiritual Pastimes which are fully transcendental, situated in pure goodness, and all-pleasing to Lord Hari. As mentioned in the revealed scriptures, Lord Nityānanda is the individual temperament of ego existing within the living entity, and He sometimes appears just like an ordinary living entity. Let us eternally worship that great personality Śrī Nityānanda-chandra, who freely grants the association of Śrī Gaurāṅgadev, who is none other than Lord Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yaś chādi vyuha-madhye prabhavati sagaṇo mūla-saṅkarṣaṇākhyo</strong><br>
<strong>dvārāvatyāṁ tad-ūrddhe madhupuri vasati prābhavākhyo vilāsaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>sarvāṁśī rāma-nāmā vrajapuri ramate sānujo yaḥ svarūpe</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-nityānanda-chandraṁ bhaja bhaja satataṁ gaura-kṛṣṇa-pradaṁ tam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Nityānanda who is the fountainhead of the primary Saṅkarṣaṇa, exists in full splendour separate from the original quadruple expansion, and is known as Mūla Saṅkarṣaṇa. That all-attractive personality, Śrī Saṅkarṣaṇa, performs His wonderful Pastimes in the spiritual realm in the regions known as Mathurā and Dvārakā. The trascendental city of Dvārakā is situated just below the city of Mathurā. It is in these spiritual places that Lord Saṅkarṣaṇa and His associates Aniruddha and Pradyumna exhibit Their particular Pastimes scripturally known as prabhāva-vilāsa. Nityānanda Prabhu in the form of Lord Balarāma is the ultimate source of all incarnations, and sportively plays in the charming village of Vrajapura with His younger brother Lord Kṛṣṇa. Let us eternally worship that great personality Śrī Nityānanda-chandra, who freely grants the association of Śrī Gaurāṅgadev, who is none other than Lord Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-kṛṣṇa-prema-nāmā parama-sukhamayaḥ ko ’py achintyaḥ padārtho</strong><br>
<strong>yad gandhāt sajjanaughā nigama-bahumataṁ mokṣam apy ākṣipanti</strong><br>
<strong>kaivalyaiśvarya-sevā-prada-gaṇa iti yasyāṅgataḥ prema-dātuḥ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-nityānanda-chandraṁ bhaja bhaja satataṁ gaura-kṛṣṇa-pradaṁ tam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Pure unalloyed love for Lord Kṛṣṇa, which is inconceivable to all, embodies the most supreme and intense happiness. When the devotees of the Lord realise the joy of such love, they caste away the innumerable doctrines offered by the Vedas, including the soul’s emancipation. Lord Nityānanda openly bestows pure love, automatically including worship of the non-differentiated Brahman and service offered in Vaikuṇṭha, which is dominated by opulence and grandeur. Let us eternally worship that great personality Śrī Nityānanda-chandra, who freely grants the association of Śrī Gaurāṅgadev, who is none other than Lord Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yo bālye līlayaikaḥ parama-madhurayā chaikachakrānagaryāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>mātā-pitror janānām atha nija-suhṛdāṁ hlādayaṁś chitta-chakram</strong><br>
<strong>tīrthān vabhrāma sarvānupahṛta-janako nyāsinā prārthitaś cha</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-nityānanda-chandraṁ bhaja bhaja satataṁ gaura-kṛṣṇa-pradaṁ tam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">When Nityānanda Prabhu was a small boy, He performed sweet and charming Pastimes in the village of Ekachakra. His Pastimes invoked the deepest heartfelt happiness in His mother, father, relatives, and close friends. Once, a renunciant arrived at the house of Nityānanda and prayed to the boy’s father for his son. Lord Nityānanda was placed into the hands of the renunciant, who left with him to visit the holy places of pilgrimage, wandering throughout the length and breadth of India. Let us eternally worship that great personality Śrī Nityānanda-chandra, who freely grants the association of Śrī Gaurāṅgadev, who is none other than Lord Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhrāmaṁ bhrāmañ cha tīrthān yati-mukuṭa-maṇi mādhavendra prasaṅgāt</strong><br>
<strong>labdhollāsaḥ pratikṣya prakaṭita-charitaṁ gaura-dhāmājagāma</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-gauraḥ śrīnivāsādibhir api yamāvāpālaye nandanasya</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-nityānanda-chandraṁ bhaja bhaja satataṁ gaura-kṛṣṇa-pradaṁ tam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Nityānanda travelled from place to place with the crest jewel of the renounced order, Śrī Mādhavendra Purī. In the pure association of Mādhavendra Purī, Lord Nityānanda experienced the most joyful symptoms of ecstasy. After this, the Lord journeyed to Nabadwīp, where He eagerly waited for Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu to reveal His benevolent nature to the world. Shortly following Nityānanda’s arrival, Lord Chaitanyadev Himself, in the company of Śrīnivās Paṇḍit and Haridās Ṭhākur, went to the house of Śrī Nandanāchārya and found Nitāi there. Let us eternally worship that great personality Śrī Nityānanda-chandra, who freely grants the association of Śrī Gaurāṅgadev, who is none other than Lord Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>prāptājño gaura-chandrād akhila-jana-gaṇoddhāra-nāma-pradāne</strong><br>
<strong>yaḥ prāpya dvau surāpau kali-kaluṣa-hatau bhrātarau brahma-daityau</strong><br>
<strong>gāḍha-prema-prakāśaiḥ kṛta-rudhira-vapuś chāpi tāv ujjahāra</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-nityānanda-chandraṁ bhaja bhaja satataṁ gaura-kṛṣṇa-pradaṁ tam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Nityānanda Prabhu was ordered by the golden moon, Śrīman Mahāprabhu, to deliver the masses by the wide distribution of Lord Kṛṣṇa’s Holy Names. One evening, Lord Nityānanda encountered those two drunken and sinful brothers, Jagāi and Mādhāi, who were previously brahma-daitya demons of the first order, acting under the devastating spell of Kali-yuga. Suddenly, Nitāi was attacked by Mādhāi, whereupon His body became smeared with blood. In response, Lord Nitāi exhibited deep affection for the welfare of the two brothers and rescued them from their abominable condition. Let us eternally worship that great personality Śrī Nityānanda-chandra, who freely grants the association of Śrī Gaurāṅgadev, who is none other than Lord Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sākṣād gauro gaṇānāṁ śirasi yad avadhūtasya kaupīna-khaṇḍaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>saṁdhartuñ chādideśāsava-yavana-vadhū-spṛṣṭa-dṛṣṭo ’pi vandyaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>brahmādyānām apīti prabhu-parihṛta-kānām api sveṣṭa-pīṭhaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-nityānanda-chandraṁ bhaja bhaja satataṁ gaura-kṛṣṇa-pradaṁ tam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Lord Gaurāṅga instructed His family members and followers to wear pieces of Lord Nityānanda’s kaupīna respectfully on their bodies. So pure and impeccable is the character of Nitāi that even if He is seen holding the hand of a Yavana’s wife and carrying a bottle of wine, nonetheless He remains supremely worshipable by the great demigods such as Lord Brahmā. Lord Nityānanda is also the desirable object of those exalted devotees who can steal away the heart of the Lord. Let us eternally worship that great personality Śrī Nityānanda-chandra, who freely grants the association of Śrī Gaurāṅgadev, who is none other than Lord Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>uddhartuṁ jñāna-karmādy-apahata-charitān gaurachandro yad āsau</strong><br>
<strong>nyāsaṁ kṛtvā tu māyā mṛga-manusṛtavān grāhayan kṛṣṇa-nāma</strong><br>
<strong>tach chhāyevānvadhāvat sthala-jala-gahane yo ’pi tasyeṣṭa-cheṣṭaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-nityānanda-chandraṁ bhaja bhaja satataṁ gaura-kṛṣṇa-pradaṁ tam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Accepting the renounced order of life and distributing the Lord’s Holy Names, Lord Gaurāṅga chased after those souls who had been spoiled by the doctrines of dry argumentative logic and motivated fruitive activity. Like Lord Gaurāṅga’s own shadow, Nitāi Prabhu accompanied the Lord over land and through rivers, waterways, and jungles. In addition, Lord Nityānanda is the desired goal of all Gaurāṅga’s endeavours. Let us eternally worship that grand personality Śrī Nityānanda-chandra, who can freely grant the association of that golden Lord Śrī Gaurāṅgadev, who is none other than Lord Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-rādhā-prema-lubdho divasa-niśi-tadā svāda-mattaika-līlo</strong><br>
<strong>gauro yañ chādideśa svaparikara-vṛtaṁ kṛṣṇa-nāma-pradātum</strong><br>
<strong>gauḍe ’bādhaṁ dadau yaḥ subhaga-gaṇa-dhanaṁ gaura-nāma-prakāmaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-nityānanda-chandraṁ bhaja bhaja satataṁ gaura-kṛṣṇa-pradaṁ tam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Day and night Lord Chaitanya was intoxicated with the intense desire to taste the sweetness of Śrī Rādhikā’s divine love for Kṛṣṇa. Śrīman Mahāprabhu instructed Lord Nityānanda and His associates to go out and profusely distribute the Holy Names of Lord Kṛṣṇa. However, Lord Nityānanda instead plentifully distributed Lord Gaurāṅga’s Name, the most precious fortune available to the living entities, with no impediment at all. Let us worship that great personality Śrī Nityānanda-chandra, who freely grants the association of Śrī Gaurāṅgadev, who is none other than Lord Kṛṣṇa.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-rādhā-kṛṣṇa-līlā-rasa-madhura-sudhāsvāda-śuddhaika-mūrtau</strong><br>
<strong>gaure śraddhāṁ dṛḍhāṁ bho prabhu-parikara-samrāṭ prayachchhādhame ’smin</strong><br>
<strong>ullaṅghyāṅghriṁ hi yasyākhila-bhajana-kathā svapnavach chaiva mithyā</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-nityānanda-chandraṁ patita-śaraṇa-daṁ gaura-daṁ taṁ bhaje ’ham</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Nityānanda Prabhu, emperor amidst the personal associates of Śrīman Mahāprabhu! Kindly grant this fallen soul deep and resolute faith in that pure and divine figure of Śrī Gaurāṅga. Lord Gaurāṅga’s personality exclusively embodies the sweet nectar relished deep within the devotional mellows of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa’s Pastimes. But if Your lotus feet are over-looked, than all so-called devotional orations and prayers meant for the highest worship become false, like a dream. I worship You, that great personality Nityānanda-chandra, who openly bestows shelter to the most fallen souls, freely granting the association of Śrī Gaurāṅgadev.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-amnayah-praha-tattvam-harim" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Daśa-mūla-tattva <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>The Ten Fundamental Principles</h3>
<h4>Daśa-mūla-tattva-niryāsa by Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>āmnāyaḥ prāha tattvaṁ harim iha paramaṁ sarva-śaktim rasābdhiṁ</strong><br>
<strong>tad-bhinnāṁsāṁś cha jīvān prakṛti-kavalitān tad-vimuktāṁś cha bhāvāt</strong><br>
<strong>bhedābheda-prakāśaṁ sakalam api hareḥ sādhanaṁ śuddha-bhaktiṁ</strong><br>
<strong>sādhyaṁ tat-prītim evety upadiśati janān gaurachandraḥ svayaṁ saḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">“Śrī Gaurachandra personally teaches the souls that<br />(1) revealed knowledge establishes the Truth:<br />(2) Hari is supreme,<br />(3) He is omnipotent,<br />(4) He is an ocean of rasa,<br />(5) the souls are His separated parts,<br />(6) they are either covered by material energy,<br />(7) or liberated from material energy by devotion,<br />(8) everything is a manifestation distinct and non-distinct from Hari,<br />(9) pure devotion is the way, and<br />(10) love for Hari is the goal.”<br>
<i>Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj:</i><br>
“Within this one verse, which conveys the gist of the <i>Daśa-mūla-tattva</i>, Śrīla Bhakti Vinod Ṭhākur has given us everything. He has given us the whole conception of Mahāprabhu Śrī Chaitanyadev and Kṛṣṇa consciousness. If you can read and memorise this verse, then everything will be within you: the full conception of Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu, Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur, Śrīla Guru Mahārāj Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj, and Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh. Everything, the gist of all revealed knowledge (āmnāya), is within this one verse.<br>
“But don’t try to understand this verse by yourself. Hear it from an expert. Everywhere throughout all the scriptures this advice is given: ‘You must hear from an expert.’ So hear this verse from an expert who knows its meaning. If you hear from him with full faith, you will understand properly what is what, and you will get everything.”</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-guru-rupa-virajita-nanda-sutam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Gaurahari-kusuma Stavāṣṭakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Eight Flower Offerings of Praise to Lord Gaurahari</h3>
<h4>Sanskrit composition</h4>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>guru-rūpa-virājita-nanda-sutaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>sutanūnnata-kāñchana-bhūmi-dharam</strong><br>
<strong>dharaṇī-jana-tāraṇa-tuṅga-tariṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja gaurahariṁ bhaja gauraharim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The son of Mahārāj Nanda, Svayaṁ Bhagavān Śrī Kṛṣṇa, is now illustrious in His divine form of Śrī Guru. He is beautiful and highly exalted, bearing His golden Persona. (Or, He is beautiful and highly exalted within the golden land of His divine abode.) He is the eminent lifeboat that rescues the souls of this world. Serve that Lord, Gaurahari, worship Gaurahari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>vṛṣabhānu-sutā-dyuti-bhāva-vṛtaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vṛta-chitta-nijādbhuta-rūpa-madhum</strong><br>
<strong>madhu-vibhrama-nāmada-śaśva-dariṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja gaurahariṁ bhaja gauraharim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Adorned with the heart and halo of Śrīmatī Rādhārāṇī (Vṛṣabhānu-sutā), having dived deeply into the reality of His own wonderful beauty and sweetness, He is sweetly intoxicated in the perpetual distribution of His own Holy Name. Serve that Lord, Gaurahari, worship Gaurahari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>uditākhila-gaurava-gauḍapuraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>puraṭojjvala-maṅgala-mūrta-rasam</strong><br>
<strong>rasanotsava-kīrtana-kṛṣṇa-hariṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja gaurahariṁ bhaja gauraharim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He manifested the full glory of Śrī Nabadwīp Dhām, as He appeared in His radiant golden, all-auspicious form of divine ecstasy, ever engaged in Kṛṣṇa-kīrtan, which is a festival for the tongue. Serve that Lord, Gaurahari, worship Gaurahari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nija-pārṣada-darśana-datta-śubhaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śubha-nāma-sudhā-kṛta-matta-janam</strong><br>
<strong>janatāgha-hara-sthita-chitta-darim</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja gaurahariṁ bhaja gauraharim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He gave the spiritually auspicious darśan of His eternal associates to the world (or, He gave His spiritually auspicious darśan to His eternal associates), He drove the masses of people mad with the nectar of His spiritually all-auspicious Name, vanquishing all sins from the core of their hearts. Serve that Lord, Gaurahari, worship Gaurahari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dadataṁ satataṁ nija-nāma-dhanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>dhana-māna na vai vanitāṁ kavitām</strong><br>
<strong>vitaran vraja-bhaktim-adatta-charīṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja gaurahariṁ bhaja gauraharim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He incessantly distributed the spiritual fortune of His own divine Name. He did not regard as fruitful the paths of endeavouring for material wealth, lusty material desires, or grand material dreams as captured by worldly poetry. He widely made available the fifth end of life which had never been given before in this world: the divine love for Kṛṣṇa found in the Lord’s original divine abode of Goloka Vṛndāvan. Serve that Lord, Gaurahari, worship Gaurahari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>patitādhama-dīna-dayādra-hṛdaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>hṛdayāśrita-yāchaka-veśa-kṛtam</strong><br>
<strong>kṛta-veṣa-yati-śrita-nīla-giriṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja gaurahariṁ bhaja gauraharim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the great benefactor whose heart is melted with compassion for the fallen. Out of that feeling in His heart He accepted the dress of a beggar (the renounced order of life, sannyās). After accepting this dress He took shelter in Nīlāchala (Jagannātha Purī). Serve that Lord, Gaurahari, worship Gaurahari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śruti-kīrtita-pūruṣa-rukṣma-ruchiṁ</strong><br>
<strong>ruchi-rāga-niṣevaṇa-dāna-param</strong><br>
<strong>paramārtha-purāṇa-vigīta-hariṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja gaurahariṁ bhaja gauraharim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He is the resplendent golden Personality glorified by the scriptures and saints. He is the greatest distributor of spontaneous loving divine service (pure transcendental attachment and service to the Lord free from all mundanity). He is the Lord whose glories are sung throughout the scriptures describing transcendental knowledge (Purāṇas), (or, He is the Lord who takes away all apparent discord found within the Purāṇas’ revelation of transcendental knowledge). Serve that Lord, Gaurahari, worship Gaurahari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bahu-nartana-kīrtana-matta-karam</strong><br>
<strong>karatāla-mṛdaṅga-vibhaṅga-param!</strong><br>
<strong>paramādara-pāmara-śāntipurīṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja gaurahariṁ bhaja gauraharim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">He performed greatly maddened dancing and chanting amidst the wonderful sounds of the karatāla and mṛdaṅga as He was shown great affection and honour by the fallen souls. He is the grand abode of divine peace and relief for one and all. Serve that Lord, Gaurahari, worship Gaurahari.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-ma-muncha-muncha-mam-krishna" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Mā Muñcha Pañcha-daśakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Originally published in Śrī Gauḍīya Darśan, 12 January 1967</h3>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj.</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mā muñcha muñcha māṁ kṛṣṇa! dāsaṁ dīna-dayārṇava!</strong><br>
<strong>patitaṁ ghora-saṁsāre hy avaśaṁ prakṛter vaśat</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have fallen into this terrible world and am helplessly subjugated by material nature. O Kṛṣṇa! O Ocean of Mercy for the distressed! Don’t abandon me. Deliver this servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mā muñcha muñcha māṁ kṛṣṇa! dāsaṁ dīna-dayārṇava!</strong><br>
<strong>anādi-bhāgya-vaiguṇyāt bhoga-mugdhaṁ durāśayam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Due to my ‘time-less’ misfortune, I have been bewildered by the wicked desire to selfishly enjoy. O Kṛṣṇa! O Ocean of Mercy for the distressed! Don’t abandon me. Deliver this servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mā muñcha muñcha māṁ kṛṣṇa! dāsaṁ dīna-dayārṇava!</strong><br>
<strong>māyā-krīḍanakaṁ dīnaṁ nirālambaṁ nirāśrayam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am a toy of Māyā Devī. I am distressed. I have no support. I have no shelter. O Kṛṣṇa! O Ocean of Mercy for the distressed! Don’t abandon me. Deliver this servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mā muñcha muñcha māṁ kṛṣṇa! dāsaṁ dīna-dayārṇava!</strong><br>
<strong>janma-mṛtyu-jarā-vyādhi-bhīti-chintāti-kātaram</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am deeply troubled by birth, death, old age, disease, fear, and anxiety. O Kṛṣṇa! O Ocean of Mercy for the distressed! Don’t abandon me. Deliver this servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mā muñcha muñcha māṁ kṛṣṇa! dāsaṁ dīna-dayārṇava!</strong><br>
<strong>jñānājñāna-kṛtānanta-pāpa-bhoga-bhayākulam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am overwhelmed with fear as I suffer the reactions for the unlimited sins I have knowingly and unknowingly committed. O Kṛṣṇa! O Ocean of Mercy for the distressed! Don’t abandon me. Deliver this servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mā muñcha muñcha māṁ kṛṣṇa! dāsaṁ dīna-dayārṇava!</strong><br>
<strong>kāma-krodhādi-dasyubhir nirdayaṁ padamarditam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I have been trampled by the feet of the merciless bandits – lust, anger, and so on. O Kṛṣṇa! O Ocean of Mercy for the distressed! Don’t abandon me. Deliver this servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mā muñcha muñcha māṁ kṛṣṇa! dāsaṁ dīna-dayārṇava!</strong><br>
<strong>antaḥśatru-viparyastaṁ punaḥ punar vilañchhitam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am bewildered and harassed by internal enemies again and again. O Kṛṣṇa! O Ocean of Mercy for the distressed! Don’t abandon me. Deliver this servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mā muñcha muñcha māṁ kṛṣṇa! dāsaṁ dīna-dayārṇava!</strong><br>
<strong>trāṇārthaṁ prārthyamāno ’pi na māṁ paśyati kaśchanaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Although I am praying for deliverance, no one sees me. O Kṛṣṇa! O Ocean of Mercy for the distressed! Don’t abandon me. Deliver this servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mā muñcha muñcha māṁ kṛṣṇa! dāsaṁ dīna-dayārṇava!</strong><br>
<strong>pitaro bāndhavā devā asamarthāḥ parāṅmukhāḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">My ancestors, my friends, and the demigods are all incapable (of delivering me) and opposed to me. O Kṛṣṇa! O Ocean of Mercy for the distressed! Don’t abandon me. Deliver this servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mā muñcha muñcha māṁ kṛṣṇa! dāsaṁ dīna-dayārṇava!</strong><br>
<strong>sva-para-bharasā-hīnaṁ nirupāyaṁ nirākṛtam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am devoid of any hope in myself and in others. I have no means whatsoever (of deliverance), and I am utterly spurned. O Kṛṣṇa! O Ocean of Mercy for the distressed! Don’t abandon me. Deliver this servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mā muñcha muñcha māṁ kṛṣṇa! dāsaṁ dīna-dayārṇava!</strong><br>
<strong>mahāparādha-rāśi-nāmālayaṁ tyakta-sādhanam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I am an abode of a profusion of terrible offences against the Name, and I have abandoned the way of deliverance. O Kṛṣṇa! O Ocean of Mercy for the distressed! Don’t abandon me. Deliver this servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="12">
<h5>12</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mā muñcha muñcha māṁ kṛṣṇa! dāsaṁ dīna-dayārṇava!</strong><br>
<strong>he sad-asad-vichārorddha-prapannārtiharo hare!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord! You remove the distress of the surrendered souls, who are beyond all consideration of good and bad. O Kṛṣṇa! O Ocean of Mercy for the distressed! Don’t abandon me. Deliver this servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="13">
<h5>13</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mā muñcha muñcha māṁ kṛṣṇa! dāsaṁ dīna-dayārṇava!</strong><br>
<strong>dāsatvaṁ dehi dāsānāṁ tava deva dayānidhe!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Lord! O Ocean of Mercy! Please give me the service of Your servants. O Kṛṣṇa! O Ocean of Mercy for the distressed! Don’t abandon me. Deliver this servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="14">
<h5>14</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mā muñcha muñcha māṁ kṛṣṇa! dāsaṁ dīna-dayārṇava!</strong><br>
<strong>niyuñkṣva nityadāsye māṁ pradāya prema-vartanam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please engage me in Your eternal service and grant me a salary of prema. O Kṛṣṇa! O Ocean of Mercy for the distressed! Don’t abandon me. Deliver this servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="15">
<h5>15</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mā muñcha muñcha māṁ kṛṣṇa! dāsaṁ dīna-dayārṇava!</strong><br>
<strong>svāntaraṅga-sevāṁ dehi he gopījana-vallabha!</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Please bestow Your intimate service upon me, O Beloved of the gopīs! O Kṛṣṇa! O Ocean of Mercy for the distressed! Don’t abandon me. Deliver this servant.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="16">
<h5>16</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mā muñcha-pañcha-daśakaṁ! kṛṣṇa dīna-dayārṇava!</strong><br>
<strong>tridaṇḍi-śrīdharodgītaṁ gṛhānedaṁ stavāmṛtam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Tridaṇḍi Bhikṣu Śrī Śrīdhar sings aloud this Mā Muñcha Pañcha-Daśakam. O Ocean of Mercy for the distressed, Śrī Kṛṣṇa! Please accept this nectarean prayer.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-shri-svarupa-raya-rupa-jiva-bhava" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Praṇām Mantras <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>to Śrīla Guru Mahārāj, Om Viṣṇupād Aṣṭottara-śata-śrī Śrīmad Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h3>
<h4>Sanskrit verses by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-svarūpa-rāya-rūpa-jīva-bhāva-sambharaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>varṇa-dharma-nirviśeṣa-sarva-loka-nistaram</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-sarasvatī-priyañ cha bhakti-sundarāśrayaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śrīdharaṁ namāmi bhakti-rakṣakaṁ jagad-gurum</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>sindhu-chandra-parvatendu-śāka-janma-līlanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śuddha-dīpta-rāga-bhakti-gauravānuśīlanam</strong><br>
<strong>bindu-chandra-ratna-soma-śāka-lochanāntaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śrīdharaṁ namāmi bhakti-rakṣakaṁ jagad-gurum</strong></p>
<p class="translation">I offer my humble obeisances unto the Guru of the entire world, Oṁ Viṣṇupād Śrī Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj, who carries the conception of Śrī Svarūp Dāmodar, Śrī Rāmānanda Rāy, Śrī Rūpa Goswāmī, and Śrī Jīva Goswāmī; who delivers everyone irrespective of caste or creed; who is the most beloved of Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Goswāmī Prabhupād; and who is the abode of transcendental loving service (the only shelter of Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda).<br>
I offer my humble obeisances unto the Guru of the entire world, Oṁ Viṣṇupād Śrī Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj, who revealed the Pastimes of his holy advent in the world in the year 1817 Śaka era (1895 A.D.); who constantly practises the wealth of pure loving devotion in divine consorthood; and who disappeared from our eyes in the year 1910 Śaka era (1988 A.D.).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<p class="verse"><strong>kanaka suruchi rāngaṁ sundaraṁ saumya-mūrtiṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vibudha-kula-vareṇyaṁ śrī-guruṁ siddhi-pūrtim</strong><br>
<strong>taruṇa-tapana-vāsaṁ bhaktidañ chid-vilāsaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>bhaja bhaja tu mano re śrīdharaṁ śambidhānam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my dear mind! Please engage yourself eternally in the worship of Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj, who has manifested his form as Śrī Gurudev; who is the giver of all perfection; whose charming golden form is beautifully reposed with sweetness and tranquility, and dressed in radiant saffron robes. He is rich with perfect devotional conceptions, highly learned and pre-eminent among the followers of Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-naumi-shri-gurupadabjam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Praṇati Daśakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Ten prayers unto the holy lotus feet of Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h3>
<h4>Offered on his 94th holy day of advent, 1988.</h4>
<h4>Sanskrit composition</h4>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>naumi śrī-gurupādābjaṁ yatirājeśvareśvaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-bhaktirakṣakaṁ śrīla-śrīdhara-svāminaṁ sadā</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sudīrghonnata-dīptāṅgaṁ supībya-vapuṣaṁ paraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>tridaṇḍa-tulasīmālā-gopīchandana-bhūṣitam</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>achintya-pratibhā-snigdhaṁ divyajñāna-prabhākaraṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vedādi-sarvaśāstrānāṁ sāmañjasya-vidhāyakam</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gauḍīyāchārya-ratnānām ujjvalaṁ ratna-kaustubhaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-chaitanya-mahāpremon-mattālīnāṁ śiromaṇim</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gāyatry-artha-viniryyāsaṁ gītā-gūḍhārtha-gauravaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>stotraratnādi-samṛddhaṁ prapanna-jīvanāmṛtam</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>apūrvvagrantha-sambhāraṁ bhaktānāṁ hṛd-rasāyanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>kṛpayā yena dattaṁ taṁ naumi kāruṇya-sundaram</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>saṅkīrtana-mahārāsarasābdheś chandramānibhaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>saṁbhāti vitaran viśve gaura-kṛṣṇaṁ gaṇaiḥ saha</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dhāmani śrī-navadvīpe guptagovardhane śubhe</strong><br>
<strong>viśvaviśruta-chaitanya-sārasvata-maṭhottamam</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sthāpayitvā gurūn gaura-rādhā-govindavigrahān</strong><br>
<strong>prakāśayati chātmānaṁ sevā-saṁsiddhi-vigrahaḥ</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="10">
<h5>10</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-śrī-rūpa-siddhānta-divya-dhārādharaṁ guruṁ</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-bhakti-rakṣakaṁ devaṁ śrīdharaṁ praṇamāmy aham</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śraddhayā yaḥ paṭhen nityaṁ praṇati-daśakaṁ mudā</strong><br>
<strong>viśate rāgamārgeṣu tasya bhakta-prasādataḥ</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="translation">I eternally offer my humble obeisances unto the lotus feet of my most worshipful Divine Master, the supreme emperor of the sannyāsī kings – Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Swāmī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2-4">
<h5>2-4</h5>
<p class="translation">He possesses a lofty, dignified, divinely effulgent, incomparable holy form, so charming to behold. Adorned with gopī-chandan and a necklace of tulasī beads, he holds the triple staff of the Vaiṣṇava ascetics. Although he possesses the qualification of inconceivable potency, he is full of the utmost affection. All the ten directions are illuminated by the effulgence of his supramundane, unalloyed perception. He is the dispenser of the genuine proper harmonious adjustment of all the scriptures – the Vedas, the Vedānta, the Upaniṣads, the Purāṇas, etc., headed by the <i>Śrīmad Bhāgavata-purāṇa</i> (<i>Śrīmad Bhāgavatam</i>) which holds equal importance with the Vedas.
