In the necklace of the jewels of Āchāryas in the Śrī Gauḍīya sampradāya, he shines resplendently like the brilliant Kaustubha gem, and he is radiant in his glory as the crown-jewel of the bumblebee-devotees who are mad in the Supreme Lord Śrī Chaitanya Mahāprabhu’s love supreme. I eternally offer my obeisances unto him, my most worshipful Divine Master.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5-6">
<h5>5-6</h5>
<p class="translation">By his grace, he brought to full bloom the deepest underlying purport of Gāyatrī, the mother of the Vedas; by his grace he opened the hidden treasure-house of the glorious internal purport of <i>Śrīmad Bhāgavad-gītā</i> – and he distributed these gifts to one and all, even the lowest of the low. He revealed the supreme holy book <i>Śrī Prapanna-jīvanāmṛtam</i>, which is a treasure of many types of precious gems of holy stanzas (the wealth of holy stanzas compiled from <i>Stotra-ratna</i> by Śrī Yāmunā Āchārya and many other valuable sources) sung by the Lord and His devotees; he revealed an unprecedented collection of holy books (<i>Search for Śrī Kṛṣṇa – Reality the Beautiful, Śrī Guru and His Grace, The Golden Volcano of Divine Love</i>, etc.) that are the very life-nectar for the devotees’ hearts and spiritual senses – he gave these gifts to the world. I eternally offer my obeisances unto him, my most worshipful Divine Master, the beautiful Deity of mercy incarnate.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="translation">He revels in his full-blown splendour, and, along with and through his intimate servitors he distributes throughout the entire universe the Supreme Personality of Godhead Śrī Gaura-Kṛṣṇa, the moon risen from the nectar ocean of the grand dance of the congregational chanting of Śrī Kṛṣṇa’s Holy Name.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8-10">
<h5>8-10</h5>
<p class="translation">In Śrī Nabadwīp Dhām, which is non-different from the holy abode of Śrī Vṛndāvan, His Divine Grace established the world-renowned Śrī Chaitanya Sāraswat Maṭh at Śrī Koladwīp, the selfsame ‘Hidden Govardhan Hill’ (Gupta-Govardhan), which is the sacred place where all offences are absolved (aparādha-bhañjan-pāṭ). There, he revealed the beautiful devotional service of the worshipful Deities Śrī Śrī Guru Gaurāṅga Gāndharvā Govindasundar, divinely manifesting his very self as the Deity of service in perfection. Eternally do I offer my obeisances unto the holy lotus feet of my Divine Master Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj who carries the divine current of Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī coming in the line of Śrī Rūpa, the object of Śrī Gaurāṅga Mahāprabhu’s divine affection.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="11">
<h5>11</h5>
<p class="translation">One who with pure faith happily sings this <i>Praṇati Daśakam</i> every day, gains the qualification to serve the Supreme Lord on the path of spontaneous love (rāga-mārga), by the grace of the associate-servitors of that Śrīla Gurudev.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-samudra-sambhava-gabhi-surabhi-apana" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Śrī Govinda Kuṇḍa Māhātmya <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>The Glories of Śrī Govinda Kuṇḍa</h3>
<h4>Sanskrit verses from Śrī Girirāj-khaṇḍam, Śrī Garga-saṁhitā.</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>atha govardhane ramye surabhir gauḥ samudra-jā</strong><br>
<strong>snāpayām āsa gopeśaṁ dugdha-dhārābhir ātmanaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>surabher dugdha-dhārābhir govinda-snānato nṛpa</strong><br>
<strong>jāto govinda-kuṇḍo ’drau mahā-pāpa-haraḥ paraḥ</strong><br>
<strong>kadāchit tasmin dugdhasya svādutvaṁ pratipadyate</strong><br>
<strong>tatra snātvā naraḥ sākṣād govinda-padam āpnuyāt</strong><br>
<strong>govinda-kuṇḍe viśade yaḥ snāti kṛṣṇa-mānasaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>prāpnoti kṛṣṇa-sārūpyaṁ maithilendra na saṁśayaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation"><i>Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj’s corresponding Bengali composition:</i></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>samudra-sambhavā gābhī surabhī āpana</strong><br>
<strong>dugdhe abhiṣeka kaila vrajendra-nandana</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Surabhī, the divine cow who appeared from churning the milk ocean, lovingly bathed Vrajendra Nandan Kṛṣṇa with her own milk.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>sei dugdhe pūrṇa kuṇḍa ‘śrī govinda’ nāma</strong><br>
<strong>ramya giri govardhane śobhe abhirāma</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The pond filled with that milk, ‘Śrī Govinda Kuṇḍa’, graces beautiful Govardhan with exquisite beauty.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>kuṇḍabāri mahāpāpa-hārī se chinmaya</strong><br>
<strong>snāne pāne bhava-bhaya tritāpa nāśaya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The transcendental water of this kuṇḍa can banish even the greatest sins. The threefold miseries – ādhyātmik, ādhidaivik, and ādhibhautik – and all fears of this material world are destroyed by bathing in or drinking this water.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bahu-bhāgye keha sei dugdhāsvāda pāya</strong><br>
<strong>chidānanda-deha labhi kṛṣṇa-loke yāya</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Sometimes, by great fortune, someone will get to taste the water of Govinda Kuṇḍa as that milk, and, attaining a transcendentally joyful form, go to Kṛṣṇaloka.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī kṛṣṇa-mānase yebā kare hethā snāna</strong><br>
<strong>gupta-govardhane rādhā-kṛṣṇa-sevā pāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Anyone who bathes in Śrī Govinda Kuṇḍa desiring to serve Kṛṣṇa, attains the divine service of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa in Gupta Govardhan (hidden Govardhan).</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-devadidevam-ahibhushanam-indukashiam" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="">
<h2>Gaṅgādhara Sadāśiva Praṇām-mantram <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h4>Sanskrit composition</h4>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<p class="verse"><strong>devādidevam-ahibhūṣaṇam-indukāśaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>panchānanaṁ paśupatiṁ varadaṁ prasannam</strong><br>
<strong>gaṅgādharaṁ praṇatapālakamāśutoṣaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>vande sadāśiva-haripriya-chandramaulim</strong></p>
<p class="translation">The chief of the Demigods, whose body is decorated by snakes, whose body shines like the moon, who has five heads, who carries his weapon known as Paśupat, who gives boons, who is of satisfied nature, who holds the Gaṅgā on his head, who maintains those who give obeisances to him, who is easily pleased – I offer my obeisances to Sadāśiva who is dear to Hari, and whose crown is the moon.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-bhagyadhishia-tvadiyo-vimala-sukhamayah" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Śrī Guru-praśastiḥ <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>In honour of Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj’s holy appearance day.</h3>
<h4>Sanskrit composition</h4>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>bhāgyādhīśa! tvadīyo vimala-sukhamayaḥ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;samprakāśastu-nityo</strong><br>
<strong>gauḍaṁ rāḍhaṁ tathedaṁ tribhūvanam-akhilaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dhanya-dhanyan chakāra</strong><br>
<strong>khaṇḍe kāle dṛśāṁ no gagaṇa-rasamitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pūrayitvā budhānā –</strong><br>
<strong>mānandaṁ bardhayan vai sva-parijana-gaṇair-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dhāmani tvaṁ vibhāsi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Fortune-maker! Divine Master! Your unalloyed blissful appearance is eternal. The people of Gauḍa, Rāḍha and all the universe speak of your virtuous and auspicious appearance. Time appears to stand still at your appearance, as the sky is filled with your wonderful qualities. Your manifestation gives joy to all the saintly persons and that joy increases unlimitedly due to the illumination of the holy dhām by you, along with your divine associates.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>devādyāste ’khila guṇa-gaṇān-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;naiva gātuṁ samarthāḥ</strong><br>
<strong>kvāhaṁ jīvo ’tiśaya-patito</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;manda-bhāgyo ’ti kṣudraḥ</strong><br>
<strong>bho ārādhya! stavana-viṣaye</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kintu dīnādhamasya</strong><br>
<strong>pratyāśā tat-sukaruṇatayā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vīrachandrābhidastvam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Even gods and goddesses are unable to sing your praiseworthy qualities and attributes. Where am I? A very fallen soul, an unfortunate and tiny living entity. O adorable, venerable Gurudev! Your blessings and mercy can raise me to sing with expectation, as you are nondifferent from Birachandra Prabhu.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>dṛṣṭvā viśvasya jīvān khalu hari-vimukhān</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gaura-devo dayāyā</strong><br>
<strong>rūpaṁ gauḍe bhavastaṁ parama-karuṇayā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prāhinod-dīna bandho!</strong><br>
<strong></strong><br>
<strong>etaj jñātvā prakāśāt sudina-samudayaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;smāramāśāḥ saharṣā</strong><br>
<strong>jayante chaiva māyā-nigaḍa-nikara-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;saṁmochane ’smakamaddhā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Seeing the people of the universe to be averse to Śrī Hari, the Lord, Śrī Gaurahari mercifully sent you to Śrī Rūpa’s own Gauḍa-deśa as their protector and guardian. By the remembrance of your auspicious appearance day we can cut the bondage of Māyā, the nescience of material ignorance.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yadvad-bhānuḥ kiraṇa-nikarair-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāsayan viśvam etan</strong><br>
<strong>nāśaṁ kṛtvā nikhila-tamasāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tejasā saṁvibhāti</strong><br>
<strong>kṛtvā nāśaṁ prakṛti-tamasāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;satya-sūryaṁ prakāśya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;divya-jñānair-hari-guṇa-gaṇais</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tvan cha tadvad-vibhāsi</strong></p>
<p class="translation">As the sun eradicates all the darkness of the universe by spreading its rays and shining forth, similarly you are shining and eradicating the darkness of Māyā as the true sun of divine knowledge, full of the all-powerful qualities of Śrī Hari.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>duḥkhaiḥ pūrṇaṁ vibudha-hṛdayaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kāla-dharmāch cha dṛṣṭvā</strong><br>
<strong>māyāvādān kalijakumatān</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;duṣkṛtān śāsitun cha</strong><br>
<strong>deśe deśe bhramasi vitaran</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gaura-vāṇīn cha nāma</strong><br>
<strong>dhṛtvā deva! tribhuvana-jayaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vajra-kalpaṁ tridaṇḍam</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my Lord! Taking the thunder-like mendicant’s (<i>sannyasī’s</i>) triple staff (<i>tridaṇḍa</i>) and conquering the universe, you travelled from place to place distributing the teachings of Gaurāṅga (<i>Gaura-vāṇī</i>) to please the saddened hearts of the intelligentsia and to destroy the impersonalist conceptions and demoniac ideas that have grown by the influence of the age of Kali.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="6">
<h5>6</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>varṣāyāṁ vai sajala-jalado</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vādayan mandrabheriṁ</strong><br>
<strong>yadyad viśve bhramati bahudhā</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vāridhārān cha varṣan</strong><br>
<strong>tadvad-bhūmau bhramasi sagaṇair-</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;ghoṣayan gaura-gāthā</strong><br>
<strong>nityaṁ divyāmṛta-sukaruṇāṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tvaṁ hi deva! pravarṣan</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In the rainy season, the clouds filled with water move through the sky with a thunderous sound and shower their rains. Similarly, you shower your merciful divine rains as you travel throughout this universe. Your travelling throughout the earth, along with your associates, is like the rumble of thunder as you distribute the glories of Lord Gaurāṅga. O my Lord, in this way you eternally shower supremely merciful nectar over all.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="7">
<h5>7</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-chaitanya-vilāsa-dhāmani</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;navadvīpāśrame sundare</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-gaurāṅga-vidhos-tathā vraja yunoḥ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sevā-sudhā-sampadam</strong><br>
<strong>tadvan gāṅga-taṭe dayāmaya-vibho!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;sadhūn samāhlā-dayan</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-rūpānuga-sampradāya-vibhavānud</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhāsayan bhāsase</strong></p>
<p class="translation">At Nabadwīp, the manifestation place of Śrī Chaitanya, on the banks of the Ganges River you built a beautiful hermitage filled with the wealth of the conception of Śrī Gaurachandra: the worship of the youthful son of Vraja, Śrī Govindasundar. O merciful Lord! Here you adore the saintly devotees and give them joy by perfectly explaining the supreme teachings of Śrī Rūpa, appearing personally to give this merciful current of divinity as his full representative.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="8">
<h5>8</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>chārvākānta-kṛtāntako ’khila</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;guruḥ pāṣaṇḍa-śailāśanir</strong><br>
<strong>bauddha-dhvānta-matānta-dāyaka</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mahāmārtaṇḍa-chūḍāmaṇiḥ</strong><br>
<strong>māyāvāda-mahāvivarta</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gahaṇāj-jīvān samuddhā-vayan</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-gaurendu-jaya-dhvajo vijayate</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;svāmin bhavān nityaśaḥ</strong></p>
<p class="translation">You are the divine master and protector of all by destroying the philo­sophy of Chārvāk Ṛṣi; you are a stone-like weapon to the miscreants of this world; you are like the bright sun to the darkness of Buddhism; you protect the souls of this world from the horrible hole of impersonalist thought (māyāvāda); and you fly the victory flag of Śrī Gaurachandra (or you are the victory flag for Śrī Gaurendu – the poet).</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="9">
<h5>9</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>śrī-gaurāṅga-sarasvatī-dhunidhara!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śrī-bhakti-saṁrak-ṣaka!</strong><br>
<strong>śrī-siddhānta-sarasvatī priyavara!</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;nyāsīśvara! śrī-guro!</strong><br>
<strong>devādyeha! bhavat-śubhodaya</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;dine saṁprārthaye ’haṁ vibho!</strong><br>
<strong>pādābje khalu nitya-bhṛtya iti</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;me kāruṇyam-ātan-vatām</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my Lord! O divine guardian of the current of the teachings of Śrī Gaurāṅga coming through Śrīla Bhakti Siddhānta Saraswatī Ṭhākur, you are the protector of pure devotion, you are the dearmost of Śrī Saraswatī Ṭhākur, you are the senior-most sannyasī, you are the great Divine Master. O my Lord! Today, your advent day, this eternal servant of your lotus feet prays to always bathe in the shower of your mercy.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-nava-nalina-ganjitam-kusuma-dala" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Praṇati-pañchakam <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>In honour of Śrīla Bhakti Rakṣak Śrīdhar Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj.</h3>
<h4>Sanskrit composition</h4>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nava-nalina-gañjitaṁ&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kusuma-dala-maṇḍitaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;pāda-yugam-abhaya-varadānam</strong><br>
<strong>kanaka-ghana-ninditaṁ vapura-mara-vanditaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;naṭana-gati-gaurava-nidānam</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhaje śrīdhara-svāminam-aniśam</strong><br>
<strong>śarada-vidhu-nindi-mukha-padma-mati-śobhanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;gopī-tilaka-charchita-subhālam</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gamana-jita-vāraṇaṁ&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;śamana-bhaya-tāraṇaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prakṛti-ruchi-nindi-kṛta-veśam</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;prakṛti-ruchi-nambhrama-suveśam</strong><br>
<strong>phulla-kara-pallavaṁ&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakta-jana-vallabhaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;tuṅga-pṛthulaṁ hṛdaya-deśam</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>gaura-puraṭa-saurabhaṁ&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇa-puraṭa-gauravaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;vibudha-kula-padma-naliśam</strong><br>
<strong>bhakta-kula-daṇḍanaṁ&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhaṇḍa-mata-khaṇḍanaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;bhakta-kula-nandana-yatīśam</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>tribhuvana-nivāsine&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;kṛṣṇam-upadeśinaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;asura-kula-dāmbhika-kṛtāntam</strong><br>
<strong>naumi budha-nāyakaṁ&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;parama-gati-dāyakaṁ</strong><br>
<strong>&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;mama śaraṇa-maraṇa-gaṇa-chintyam</strong></p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1-2">
<h5>1-2</h5>
<p class="translation">(1-2) His two feet resemble fresh petals of a lotus flower and distribute fearless blessings; his divine glowing form puts to shame the glow of gold, and is glorified for its beautiful dancing by all the demigods; his lotus face is so charming it puts to shame the charm of the autumnal moon; his broad forehead is decorated with gopī-chandan tilak; I adore that Śrīdhara-deva Swāmī always.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3-4">
<h5>3-4</h5>
<p class="translation">(3-4) He can put to an end our wandering in this material world; he can protect us from fear and death; his beautiful form dressed in saffron robes puts to shame the attraction and taste for mundane beauty; his hands like fresh blossoms distribute bliss; he is the mutually beloved by the devotees; his heart is broad and strong; he preaches the glories of Śrī Govardhan; he spreads the sweet aroma of the abode of Śrī Kṛṣṇa; he punishes the deceivers and refutes their faulty conceptions thereby giving pleasure to the devotees and revealing himself as a pre-eminent sannyāsī preacher. I always adore that Śrīdhara-deva Swāmī.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="translation">(5) He is a death-sentence to arrogant, haughty demons; he is the saviour of everyone in all the three worlds for he distributes the teachings of Śrī Kṛṣṇa; he is pre-eminent amongst the learned, and the most supreme merciful shelter. I adore that Śrīdhara-deva until my death.</p>
</div>
</section>
<section class="song" id="song-maha-bhaya-shiankula-e-bhava-tarangini" data-translated="1">
<div class="verse-block" data-number="1">
<h2>Māyā Duratyayā <span class="nav-links">&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#toc">↑</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<a href="#first-lines">↓</a></span></h2>
<h3>Insurmountable Illusion</h3>
<h4>Composition about surrendering to Māyā’s Master,</h4>
<h4>by Śrīla Bhakti Sundar Govinda Dev-Goswāmī Mahārāj</h4>
<h5>1</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mahābhaya śaṅkula e-bhava taraṅginī</strong><br>
<strong>janama-maraṇa mālā-dale</strong><br>
<strong>niśi-diśi gatāgatikarata̐hi jīva-kula</strong><br>
<strong>mohita mahāmāyā bale</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Filled with tremendous trepidation (mahā-bhaya), the jīva-souls are carried away by the waves of the ocean of material existence along the chain of birth and death, perpetually coming and going in all directions throughout the darkness of the confines of the illusory environment, captivated by the power of Mahāmāyā.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="2">
<h5>2</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>karabi ki mana abhāgiyā –</strong><br>
<strong>bhuvana-mohana-māyā kemane re utarabi</strong><br>
<strong>labhibi kemane kānāiyā?</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O Mind! What can you do about my great misfortune? Mahāmāyā bewilders the whole universe, how will you ever overcome her? How will you attain Kānāi-lāl – the youthful Kṛṣṇa of Goloka Vṛndāvan?</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="3">
<h5>3</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>nija-rūpe vitāri’ tri-guṇa-jāle phā̐da rachi’</strong><br>
<strong>ta̐hi māhe gaṇikā pratiṣṭhā –</strong><br>
<strong>kanaka varāṅganā dāniyā āmiṣa sama</strong><br>
<strong>sa̐pitechhe kāla-kare dhṛṣṭā</strong></p>
<p class="translation">In your own form you are trying to cross over the net of the three modes of material nature but, in doing so they have bound you in a noose. You have treated Māyā like a prostitute. She has been feeding you kanak, kāminī, and pratiṣṭha just like meat, and you have become immersed in the darkness of arrogance.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="4">
<h5>4</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>mana majhu vāta avadhāna</strong><br>
<strong>soi dayālu prabhu charaṇa saroje mana</strong><br>
<strong>dhana-tanu, sabahu̐ dāna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">O my mind, your attention is like the wind. Your real wealth will be found at the lotus feet of the all-merciful Lord, so give yourself completely there.</p>
</div>
<div class="verse-block" data-number="5">
<h5>5</h5>
<p class="verse"><strong>yā̐kara māyā tachhu pade paraṇami</strong><br>
<strong>leha śaraṇa hoi dīna</strong><br>
<strong>svapaneha nā bhāviha tā̐ka charaṇa vinu</strong><br>
<strong>dharama yukati samīchīna</strong></p>
<p class="translation">Offer yourself to the lotus feet of the Lord who is the owner and controller of Māyā, taking shelter there in all humility. Do not even dream that without His lotus feet you can properly adjust with the environment or practise spiritual life (dhārma).</p>
</div>
</section>
</section>
